eṉṟurittiruppatu tolkāppiyampōlum tamiḻilakkaṇa muṉṉūlkaḷiṉ āṇivarampu kaṭippiṭittuykkum āciriyar iyalpiṉip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu. toṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ mulliyi aṇinturikkumiṭattē attiṇikkuriya teyvattiṉ iruppiyuṭaṉpaṭṭuc civattiṉ mēṉmi terikkum camaṟkāram peritum pōṟṟaṟpāla toṉṟākum. itaṉāl civaperumāṉ ennilattukkum iṟiyeṉpatu kuṟippittavāṟumākum. tirunāṭṭuc ciṟappilē 'moyvariyulakam pōlum muḷarinīr marutavippu' eṉpatiluḷḷa 'moyvariyulakam' eṉṉum coṟṟoṭar tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa 'cēyōṉ mēya mivari yulakamum' eṉpataṉi niṉintu eḻuntatātal vēṇṭum. purāṇattil 'mivariyulakam' eṉṟē pāṭaṅkoḷḷutalum ciṟappuṭittātal karutaṟpālatu. 'tirunāṭṭuc ciṟappil' - (pā.12) 'intirateyvatan toḻutu nāṟu naṭuvar' eṉṟatu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟum 'vēntaṉmēya tīmpuṉalulakattiṟku ēṟpuṭittāmāṟu kāṇka.
periyapurāṇattiṟ kaṇṇappanāyaṉār purāṇam mutaliya pala purāṇaṅkaḷil tiṇikaḷiṉ peṟṟi virivākavum curukkamākavum kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, tirukkuṟipput toṇṭanāyaṉār purāṇat tiṟṟāṉ āciriyar aintiṇikaḷiyum oruṅkē ārāyntu aḻaku peṟak kūṟivittirukkiṉṟaṉar. civañāṉamuṉivar tamatu kāñcip purāṇattil 'tiruttoṇṭi naṉṉāṭṭu nāṉilattin tiṇi vaḷamun terittuk kāṭṭa maruttoṇṭivāycciyar vāḻkuṉṟi nakarkkulak kaviyē vallāṉ' eṉṟu pārāṭṭiyatu ippakutiyi nōkkiyēyāku poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kaṟpāre varum itaṉ cuviyi nukarntiṉpuṟalvēṇṭum. itiluḷḷa tiṇippakutiyellām eṭutteḻutiṉ mikaviriyumākaliṉ oṉṟiraṇṭu ceytikaḷē iṅkē kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
"	kuṟavar paṉmaṇi yarittiti vitippaṉa kuṟiñci
kaṟavi yāṉiri māṉuṭaṉ payilvaṉa kāṉam
paṟavi tāmari yiruntiṟa varuntuva paḻaṉam
cuṟava muṇmarup paṇaṅkayar vaṉakaḻic cūḻal."2
'	tēṉi ṟintacen tiṉiyiṭi tarumalic cīṟūr
pāṉi ṟintapuṟ patattaṉa mullinīḷ pāṭi
tūne laṉṉaney kaṉṉaliṉ kaṉiyataṇ ṭuṟiyūr
mīṉi ṟintapē ruṇaviṉa vēlivip piṭaṅkaḷ.'1
ivviru pāṭṭilum kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉṉum nāṉku nilaṅkaḷiṉ peṟṟi naṉku kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. muṟpāṭṭil neytalukkuk kūṟiya 'cuṟavamuṇmarup paṇaṅkayarvaṉa' eṉṉuñ ceyti 'ciṉiccuṟaviṉ kōṭu naṭṭu maṉiccērttiya vallaṇaṅkiṉāl'2 eṉṟu paṭṭiṉappāliyiṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. 'neytaṉilattil nuḷiyarkku valivaḷantappiṉ ammakaḷir kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic cuṟavukkōṭu nāṭṭip paravukkaṭaṉ koṭut taliṉ āṇṭu varuṇaṉ veḷippaṭumeṉṟār' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tolkāppiya uriyiṟ kūṟutalum iṅkē karutaṟpālatu.
iṉi, tiṇi aintāyiṉum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār pālittiṇikku nilam vēṇṭiṟṟila reṉpatu,
"	naṭuva ṇintiṇi naṭuvaṇa toḻiyap

paṭutiri viyam pāttiya paṇpē"3
eṉṉum akattiṇiyiyaṟ cūttirattāṉ aṟiyalākum, pālit
tiṇikku iyaṟkiyil nila miṉṟāyiṉum vēṉiṟkālattu naṇpakaṟ poḻutil mulliyum kuṟiñciyum cārnta iṭaṅkaḷ vemmi mikkup pāliyeṉappaṭum eṉpatu tollāciriyar koḷki. itaṉi,
"	vēṉiṟ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
tāṉa lanti rukat taṉmiyiṟ kuṉṟi
mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟimiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pāli yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum"4
eṉṉum cilappatikārattuk kāṭukāṇkātiyāṉ aṟika. itu,
"	kōla mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyu maṭutta cilliṭaṅkaḷ
nīla vāṭpaṭi nīlikōṭ ṭaṅkaḷu nirantu
kāla vēṉiliṟ kaṭumpakaṟ poḻutiṉip paṟṟip
pāli yuñcola lāvaṉa vuḷaparaṉ murampu"5
eṉṉum pāṭṭil cēkkiḻār perumāṉāl evvaḷavu aḻaku peṟak kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu. cilliṭaṅkaḷ, poḻutiṉip paṟṟi, pāliyuñ colalāvaṉa eṉṉun toṭarkaḷellām āḻnta karuttiṉik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟu ippāṭṭil pālikkut teyvaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu āciriyaratu teḷiviṉik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. 'māyōṉ mēya' eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiravuriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, 'pālikku ñāyiṟṟit teyvamākki avaṉiṟṟōṉṟiya maḻiyiṉiyum kāṟṟiṉiyum atteyvap pakutiyākkik kūṟupavāleṉiṉ, ellāt teyvattiṟkum antaṇar avikoṭukkuṅkāl aṅki ātittaṉkaṭkoṭukkum eṉpatu vētamuṭipākaliṉ ātittaṉ ellā nilattiṟkum potuveṉamaṟukka' eṉṟum, 'avvannilattiṟṟeyvaṅkaḷē pālikkun teyvamāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, 'teyvamuṇāvē' eṉṉum cūttiravuriyil 'itaṉāṉē pālikkut teyvamum iṉṟāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum tam tuṇipurittup pōntaṉar. uriyāciriyarākiya iḷampūraṇavaṭikaḷō 'teyvam koṟṟavi' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. ikkoḷkikkē ilakkiya mēṟkōḷkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭum. cēkkiḻār perumāṉum 'nīla vāṭpaṭinīli' eṉak koṟṟaviyik kūṟivittiruppatu nām pōṟṟaṟ pālatoṉṟākum.


9. tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ
"	eḻutteṉap paṭupa,
akaramutal
ṉakara viṟuvāy muppa ḵteṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē."1
"	avitām,
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āyta meṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa,"2
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṉmarapuc cūttiraṅkaḷ tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ ivi yeṉavum, ittuṇiya veṉavum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. 'eḻutteṉappaṭupa' eṉṉum ittalic cūttirattāṉum, ivviyaliṟ piṉvarum pala cūttiraṅkaḷāṉum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi neṭuṅkaṇakkil vaḻaṅkivarum muṟiyē yāmeṉpatu aṟiyap paṭum. atu virivañci viḷakkātu viṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. akaram mutal oḷakāram iṟutiyākavuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttukkaḷum, kakaram mutal ṉakaram iṟutiyākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttuk kaḷum, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟu cārpeḻuttukkaḷum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷāmeṉka. eḻuttuk kaḷi uruveḻuttu, uṇarveḻuttu, oliyeḻuttu, taṉmi yeḻuttu eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum pakuttukkoṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar. iḷampūraṇarum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, 'taṉmiyum vaṭivum namakkuṇarttalarumiyiṉ āciriyar kūṟiṟṟilar' eṉa iṅṅaṉa murippatu taṉmiyeḻutteṉa ōrpirivu koḷḷutalum uṇṭeṉ pataṟkup paḻañcāṉṟākum. veḷippaṭiyākavuḷḷa oliyeḻuttu, vaṭiveḻuttu eṉpaṉavē iṅku ārāyccikkuriyaṉa.
eḻuttu eṉṉum peyariṉ kāraṇam
cuvāmināta tēcikar tamatu ilakkaṇak kotturiyil eḻutteṉṉum peyar paṟṟi yeḻutuvatu piṉvarumāṟu:- "eḻutteṉṉun toḻiṟ peyar apporuḷi viṭṭup  pālpakāvaḵṟiṇip poruṭpotup peyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu ōviya mutaliyaṉa pōlaṉṟi akaram ṉakaram mutaliyaṉa vaṭivi yuṇarttuñ ciṟappup peyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu oliyi yuṇarttum ākupeyarāy, apporuḷiviṭṭu avvoliyiṉa tilakkaṇatti yuṇarttum irumaṭiyāku peyarāy, apporuḷiviṭṭu avvilakkaṇatti yuṇarttum nūliṉiyuṇarttum mummaṭiyāku peyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṟṟeḻuttu  iṅṅaṉa maṟivitta teḻuttu eṉak karumakaruttāviyum karuvik karuttāviyum uṇarttum nāṉmaṭi yākupeyarāy niṉṟu palaporuḷ paṭṭatu kāṇka".
itaṉāṉē, eḻutteṉṉuñ col toḻiṟ peyareṉpatum, atu mutaṟkaṇ eḻutaluṟum vaṭiviyuṇarttip piṉpu oliyi yuṇarttaluṟṟa teṉpatum avar karuttātal peṟappaṭum. yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōṭ cūttiramoṉṟu 'eḻutappaṭu taliṉeḻuttē' eṉṟu  kūṟuvatum, naṉṉūl urikkaṇ 'eḻutap paṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉak koḷka' eṉṟu mayili nātar kūṟuvatum eḻutteṉpatu eḻutu eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟanta ceyappaṭu poruṭpeyar eṉṉum karuttuṭiyaṉavām. civañāṉa muṉivar tolkāppiyac cūttira viruttiyil eḻutappaṭuvateṉṉum poruṭkaṇ eḻuteṉṉu mutaṉiliyuriccolliṉ muṉṉarc ceyappaṭu poruṇmi yuṇarttum aikāravikuti puṇarntu..... avvikāraṅ keṭṭuk keṭṭa vaḻit takaramiraṭṭittu eḻutteṉa muṭintatu' eṉṟu kūṟuvatu ataṉit teḷivākkukiṟatu. maṟṟum avar, 'ivvāṟaṉṟi eḻuteṉṉu mutaṉili eḻutteṉattāṉē tirintu niṉṟa teṉṟum, aḵtāku peyarāṟ ceyappaṭu poruḷi yuṇarttiṟ ṟeṉṟuṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar; atu poruntātu, eṉṟurippatu cuvāmināta tēcikar kūṟṟi maṟuppatu pōṉṟuḷatu. eṉiṉum, ivaraṉivarum eḻutteṉpataṟku eḻutu eṉpatē mutaṉiliyeṉak koṇṭaṉar. iṉṉōr karuttiṉpaṭi eḻutteṉpatu eḻutappaṭum vaṭiviyē mutaṟkaṭ kuṟittatākum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, oliyiyuṇarntu kōṭaṟkuk karuviyākavē vaṭivu vēṇṭappaṭṭateṉpatu ellārkkum uṭampāṭākaliṉ vaṭivākiya eḻuttuṇṭātaṟku muṉpu oliyik kuṟippatoru peyar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyattuyāṇṭum eḻutteṉṉum peyarē payiṉṟu vantuḷatu. 'ōraḷa picikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttu' 'īraḷa picikku neṭṭeḻuttu' eṉṟāṟpōl varuvaṉa palavum oliyiyē ciṟappākak kuṟittavum, vaṭiviṉic ciṟappākak kuṟikkaluṟum 'uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē' 'meyyi ṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal' eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷil eḻutteṉṉum peyar kāṇappaṭāmiyum cintikkaṟpālaṉa.
ivvaṟṟāl eḻutteṉṉum peyar moḻik karuviyām oliyik kuṟippa toṉṟākavē tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭuḷā reṉak karututal porutta muṭitteṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. tolkāppiya urikārar yārum ippeyarp poruḷi viḷakkiṟṟilarāyiṉum 'ivveḻutteṉappaṭṭa ōciyi aruveṉpār aṟiyātār; ataṉi uruveṉṟēkōṭum' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu oruvāṟu ikkaruttiṟkuc cārpākavuḷatu. iṉi, āciriyar 'ellā veḻuttum' eṉṉum piṟappiyaṟ cūttirattu eḻuttolikaḷiṉ nilivēṟupāṭukaḷi yuṇarttuṅkāl 'eḻutaru vaḷi'yil eṉavum, 'akatteḻu vaḷiyici' eṉavum, 'eḻuntupuṟat ticikkum meyterivaḷiyici' eṉavum, vaḷiyiciyiṉ eḻucciyi avaṟṟukkuk kāraṇamākak kōṭaliṉ eḻu eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu eḻutteṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟākak koḷvatu porutta muṭitteṉat tōṉṟu kiṟatu. atu piṉpu avveḻut tiṉiyuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāka vēṇṭappaṭṭa vaṭiviṉiyum kuṟittatākal vēṇṭum.
uyir mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ kāraṇam
iṉi, akaramutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭukkum uyir eṉṟum, kakara mutaliya patiṉeṭṭukkum mey eṉṟum kuṟiyiṭṭiruppatu paṇṭittamiḻariṉ uṇmi nūluṇarvukkuttakka cāṉṟākum. naṉṉūl uriyil, mayilinātar 'āvi, uyir, mey, uṭampu eṉpaṉa iṭukuṟip potuppeyar' eṉṟu kūṟiyatu poruttamil kūṟṟām. 'am mutalīrā ṟāvi' eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ cūttiravurik kaṇ 'āviyum meyyum pōṟaliṉ ivviruvakiyeḻuttiṟkum āvi meyyeṉpaṉa uvamavāku peyarāyk kāraṇap potup peyarāyiṉa' eṉṟu caṅkara namaccivāyappulavar kūṟuvatum, 'oḷakāra viṟuvāyp, paṉṉīreḻuttu muyireṉa moḻipa' eṉṉun tolkāppiyar cūttira vurikkaṇ 'ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatōr kuṟi; meypatiṉeṭṭiṉiyum iyakkittāṉ aruvāy vaṭiviṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyirāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, 'ṉakāra viṟuvāyp, patiṉeṇṇeḻuttu meyyeṉa moḻipa' eṉṉuñ cūttiravurikkaṇ 'ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi, eṉṉi, paṉṉīruyirkkuntāṉ iṭaṅkoṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkuntaṉmi peṟṟa uṭampāy niṟṟaliṉ' eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urippaṉavum uyir meyyeṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷi viḷakkuvaṉavām. kuṟṟiya likaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpaṉa ikara ukaraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkamāyiṉum avi muṉ oru māttiriyāy niṉṟu piṉ ari māttiriyāyk kuṟukiṉa eṉaṟkiṭaṉiṉṟi, iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭuñ cārntu iyaṟkiyāy arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟṟalāṉum appeyark kuriyavāyiṉa. āytam eṉpataṉ peyark kāraṇattip paḻiya uriyāḷar yārum uḷḷavāṟu pulappaṭuttiṉārallar. 'āyta meṉṟa ōcitāṉ aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉpatu uṇarttaṟku āytameṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meṉṟār' eṉat tolkāppiyavuriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. īṇṭu muppuḷḷi yeṉak kūṟiṉ avvaṭivu ......iṅṅaṉamō, aṉṟi uṅṅaṉamō, vēṟeṅṅaṉamō eḻutap paṭum eṉa aiyam nikaḻumākaliṉ, atu nikaḻāmip poruṭṭu 'muppāṟpuḷḷi' eṉa āciriyar kūṟi vittaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 'aṭuppuk kūṭṭup pōla' eṉa uvamaṅkāṭṭi ataṉi viḷakkiṉar. āytameṉṉum peyarkku avar kāraṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. piṟkālattē naṉṉūṟku uriyeḻutap pukuntār cilar nacciṉārkkiṉiyariṉ karuttiṉiyaṟiya māṭṭārāy ataṉit tiriyakkoṇṭu, 'aṭuppuk kūṭṭup pōla oruvaki āyuta vaṭivāka mūṉṟu puḷḷi vittu eḻutappaṭutalāl āytameṉṟu peyar. āytam - āyutam;  iṅkē pōrk karuviyākiya kēṭakameṉṉum paṭiyum, camittaṟ karuviyākiya aṭuppum.' eṉṟu tammaṉam pōṉavāṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam oru neṟippāṭumiṉṟit taṉittamiḻc collākiya āytam eṉpataṉip paṭikkalap potumi yuṇarttum āyutam eṉṉum vaṭacolleṉak koṇṭu nalintu poruḷ kūṟuvatu ārāyccikkum aṟivukkum ciṟitum ottataṉṟu. āyiṉ, āytameṉpataṉ poruḷtāṉ yātō eṉiṉ, āytal eṉpatu nuṇukutal eṉṉum poruṭṭākaliṉ nuṇukiya ōciyuṭiyatu eṉpatu poruḷātal vēṇṭum. āytal apporuṭṭātali
"	ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam"1
eṉṉum uriyiyal cūttirattāṉaṟika.
 mutal cārpu
iṉi, uyir, mey eṉpavaṟṟi mutaleḻutteṉṟum, kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliyavaṟṟic cārpeḻutteṉṟum piṉṉuḷōr kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyaṉār akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppataṉiyum eḻutteṉṟataṉṟi, mutaleḻutteṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar. kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliya mūṉṟaṉiyum cārntu varuvaṉa eṉakkūṟi, aviyum eḻuttukkaḷōṭu oru nikaraṉa eṉṟār. eṉiṉum 'mūṉṟutali yiṭṭamuppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ' eṉac
cārpiṟṟōṟṟamum eḻutteṉṉum peyarāl āḷappaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppattumūṉṟu eṉak kūṟātu 'muppaḵteṉpa......mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē' eṉak kūṟiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ammūṉṟum taṉittēṉum, cāriyiyoṭu poruntiyēṉum varutaliṉṟi, moḻiyic cārntē varutalākiya ciṟappiṉmiyip paṟṟi eṉka. iṅṅaṉam ciṟappum ciṟappiṉmiyum paṟṟi vēṟu pirittukkūṟuvatu āciriyarkkiyaṟki yeṉpataṉi, 'colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉiyeṉ ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟinticiṉōrē' eṉavum, 'iṭiccoṟ kiḷaviyum uriccoṟkiḷaviyum avaṟṟu vaḻimaruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa', eṉavum coṟkaḷip pirittōtutaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyalākum. kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliya mūṉṟum uyirum meyyumpōla attuṇic ciṟappuṭiyaṉa valla veṉpatē āciriyar karuttātal 'cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟu' eṉa mutaliṟ kūṟiyataṉiyē 'cārntuvariṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat', tērntuveḷippaṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum, eṉap piṟappiyaluḷ valiyuṟuttik kūṟalāṉum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. avvāṟu kūṟiṉā rāyiṉum, mutaṟ cūttirattu 'eḻutteṉappaṭupa ...... muppaḵteṉpa... ...mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē' eṉa, mūṉṟu mallāviṭattu muppatuñ ciṟantaṉa eṉpataṉāl, mūṉṟumē muppatiṉuñ ciṟantaṉa eṉapporuḷ kōṭalum amiyumālō eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉam poruḷ koḷḷāmikkaṉṟē aṭutta cūttirattu avaṟṟi 'eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉa ōtiṉārāyiṟṟeṉpatu. iṉṟēl 'eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉpatu poruḷil kūṟṟāmāṟu kāṇka. ivvuṇmi 'eḻuttōraṉṉa' veṉa vēṇṭā kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, muṉ 'eṉappaṭupa' eṉṟa ciṟappu ammūṉṟaṟkuṅ koḷḷak kiṭantamiyiṉ, atu vilakkutal peṟutu meṉpatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarālum, iṅṅaṉamē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum viḷakkappeṟṟiruttaluṅ kāṇka. aṟṟāyiṉum, cārpiṟṟōṟṟa meṉpaṉa mūṉṟumē evaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟappuṭiyaṉa veṉṟu kāṇkiṉṟāmeṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ atu putiyatōrārāycciyiṉ payaṉeṉṟē koḷḷappaṭum. yāmum avvārāycciyiṉ payaṉi iḻattaṟku oruppaṭuvēmallēm. tamiḻp paṟṟuṭiyār palarum aṉṉar eṉiṉum, ataṉit tolkāppiyaṉār karuttāka vittu nalintu poruḷ kōṭaṟkum, putiya ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāl uḷatām perumiyi ārāycciyāḷarkkaṉṟit tolkāppiyaṉārk kaḷittaṟkum karututal periyatōriḻukkāmeṉka.
iṉi, tolkāppiyaṉār cārpiṟṟōṟṟam mūṉṟeṉak kūṟiyiruppavum, naṉṉūlār cārpeḻuttup patteṉpārāyiṉar.
'	uyirmey āytam uyiraḷapu oṟṟaḷapu- 
aḵkiya i u ai oḷa maḵkāṉ -
taṉinili pattuñ cārpeḻuttākum'1
eṉpatu naṉṉūl.
yāpparuṅkalamuṭiyār uyirmey, aḷapeṭi, ai oḷa makāraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkam ivaṟṟit taṉittaṉi eḻutteṉa vēṇṭiṉarāyiṉum āytakkuṟukkameṉa oṉṟu koṇṭilar. eṉiṉum, yāpparuṅkalavirutti mēṟkōṭ cūttiraṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil āytak kuṟukkamum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkamuṭiyār cārpeḻuttu ōṉpateṉak koṇṭaṉar. āytak kuṟukkaṅ koṇṭilar. ataṉ urikārar, tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiṟṟila rākaliṉ āytak kuṟukkameṉa oṉṟu iṉṟeṉṟiyampiṉar. iṅṅaṉam cārpeḻuttukkaḷ paṟṟik karuttu vēṟupaṭu vārellām tolkāppiyaṉāratu āṇi kaṭippiṭit talip perumiyākak koḷḷum oru peṟṟiyarākaliṉ, yāmum tolkāppiyaratu karuttiṉi ārāyntu kāṇaṟpālam.
tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam, uyiraḷapeṭi, makarakkuṟukkam, uyirmey eṉpavaṟṟi nūṉ marapiṟ potuvakiyālum, moḻimarapiṟ ciṟappu vakiyālum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyaluḷ mēlum ciṟappākak kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ kuṟukumeṉpataṉi moḻi marapilum, oṟṟaḷapeṭiyic ceyyuḷiyalilum kūṟiyuḷḷār. āytaṅ kuṟukumeṉa yāṇṭuṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. iṅṅaṉam piṟar cārpeḻutteṉak koṇṭa oṉpatu tolkāppiyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, avar cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟēyeṉṟum, avaṟṟuṭaṉāka eḻuttukkaḷ muppattumūṉṟē yeṉṟum  variyaṟi ceytu koṇṭār. ataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉi yeṉpatē iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyatu. uyirmeyyi ōreḻuttākak koḷḷutal poruntāteṉac civañāṉa muṉivar tamatu tolkāppiyac cūttira viruttiyil naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār. atu,
"iṉi im mūṉṟumēyaṉṟi uyirmey mutaliyavaṟṟiyum cārpeḻutteṉpāru muḷarālōveṉiṉ, āl eṉpuḻi uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉāṟpōla, lā eṉpuḻiyum meymuṉṉum uyir piṉṉum niṉṟumayaṅkiṉavēyallatu, uyiru meyyumākiya tantaṉmi tirintu vēṟākāmikku 'meyyōṭiyiyiṉu muyiriya ṟiriyā' eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷē cāṉṟākalāṉ, uyirmeyyākiya kālattum kuṟiṉmi neṭiṉmi yeṉṉum uyirttaṉmiyum, vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi yeṉṉum meyttaṉmiyum tammiyalpiṟ ṟirivu paṭāmiyāṉum, uṭaṉ mēluyir vantoṉṟutal poṉmaṇi pōla iyalpu puṇarcciyā meṉpavākalāṉum, oṟṟumi nayattāṉ uyirmey yeṉpataṉit takara ñāḻal pōla ummit toki nilikkaḷattup piṟanta aṉmoḻittoki yeṉpārkkut takaramum ñāḻaluṅ kūṭiya cāntu piṉṉart takaramu ñāḻalu mākātavāṟu pōla meyyuyir nīṅkiṟṟaṉṉuruvātal poruntāmiyāṉum, tuṇaṅki yeṉpatu meymmutal uyirīṟu meymmayakkameṉavum varaku eṉpatu uyirttoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukara meṉavum koḷvataṉṟi, uyirmey mutal uyirmeyyīṟu, uyirmeymayakkam, uyirmeyt toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram. eṉak koḷḷāmiyiṉ oṟṟumi nayampaṟṟi yoṉṟeṉpataṉāloru payaṉiṉmi
yāṉum, meykaḷellām uyirvantoṉṟātu taṉittu niṉṟa vaḻiyē māttiri koḷḷavum eḻutteṉṉavum paṭumeṉpatu avaṟṟiyal pākaliṉ atupaṟṟiyoṉṟeṉal vēṇṭāmi
yāṉum, avviyal paṟintu kōṭaṟkut taṉittu niṉṟameyyi yoṟṟeṉavum puḷḷiyeṉavum uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyi uyirmeyyeṉavum ciṟappup peyariṭṭāḷutal āciriyar karuttākalāṉum, uyirōṭu kūṭiyaviṭattu vari vaṭivuvēṟu
paṭutaliṉ atupaṟṟip 'puḷḷiyillāvellā meyyum' eṉa meymēl vittuc cūttirañ ceytu vaṭiveḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāraṉṟi oliyeḻuttilakkaṇam vēṟupaṭak kūṟāmi
yāṉum - atu poruntāteṉa maṟukka" eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam uyirmey ōreḻuttākāteṉpataṟkuc civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟiya kāraṇaṅkaḷellām mikka valiyuṭiyaṉa veṉpatil aiyamilli. eṉiṉum, kā mutaliyaṉa ōreḻuttoru moḻiyeṉak koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, ceyyuḷiyalil, aci koḷḷumiṭattum piṟāṇṭum uyirmeyyi ōreḻutteṉavē koḷḷavēṇṭi iruttalāṉum uyirmeyyi oruvāṟṟāl ōreḻuttākak koḷḷutalum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārkkuṭaṉ pāṭeṉpatu peṟṟām. ataṉāṟṟāṉ piṉṉūlāciriyarkaḷ uyirmeyyic cārpeḻuttuḷoṉṟākak koṇṭaṉarāvar. eṉiṉum, eḻuttiṉ taṉmiyi nōkkuḻi ataṉkaṇ uyirum meyyum vēṟuvēṟu pulappaṭa niṟṟalāṉum' uyirmeyyeṉa oṉṟu koḷḷātu kā eṉpatu k ā eṉa eḻutappaṭiṉum, eḻututaṟkum payilutaṟkum ciṟitu varuttamāvataṉṟi vēṟoriḻukku
miṉmiyāṉum tolkāppiyaṉār uyirmeyyi vēṟeḻuttāka vitteṇṇiṟṟilar. iṅṅaṉamē vaṭanūlār mutalāyiṉāruñ uyirum meyyuñ cērntataṉi ōreḻuttāka vitteṇṇāmiyum kāṇka.


10. oru kuṟaṭpā
"	nattampōṟ kēṭu muḷatākuñ cākkāṭum
vittakark kallā laritu"1
itu teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvatēvar aruḷiya tirukkuṟaḷiṟ pukaḻ eṉṉum atikārattiluḷḷatu. itaṟkup parimēlaḻakar kūṟiya poḻippuri "pukaḻuṭampiṟku ākkamākum kēṭum, pukaḻuṭampu uḷatākum cākkāṭum caturappāṭuṭiyārkkallatu illi" eṉpatu.
vaṟumiyāl uṭampi meliyac ceytu pukaḻi vaḷarttalum, uṭampit tuṟantu pukaḻi nilipeṟac ceytalum vittakarkkē kūṭumeṉpatu parimēlaḻakar karuttu; itu avarālēyē naṉku viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. "nantu eṉṉun toḻiṟ peyar vikārat tāl nattu eṉṟāy, piṉ am eṉṉum pakutip poruḷ vikuti peṟṟu nattam eṉṟāyiṟṟu. pōl eṉpatu īṇṭu uriyaci eṉa nattam, pōl eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷukkum avar ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kālattāṟ parimēlaḻakarkku muntiyavareṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟa maṇakkuṭavar, ippāṭṭiṟku "ākkam pōlak kēṭum uḷatāṉāṟ pōlac cātalum vallavaṟ kallatu aritu" eṉa uri kūṟi "itu pukaḻpaṭa vāḻtal makkaḷellārkkum ariteṉṟatu" eṉak karutturittuḷḷār. maṇakkuṭavar tam karutti naṉku viḷakkiṟṟila rāyiṉum, iruvar karuttum muraṇpaṭāmi kāṇaṟpālatu. nattam eṉpataṟku ākkameṉṟē iruvarum poruḷ kūṟiṉar. parimēlaḻakar pōl eṉpataṉi uriyaciyeṉak koṇṭu ākum eṉpataṉi muṉṉuṅkūṭṭiṉamiyum, maṇakkuṭavar pōl eṉpataṉi uvamiccollākavē niṟutti ataṉip piṉṉum kūṭṭiṉamiyum  tammuḷ vēṟupāṭām. vittakaṉ vittakar eṉa orumiyum paṉmiyumākap pāṭaṅkoṇṭamiyum ām.
iṉi, peyaraṟiyappaṭāta oruvaraturiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya tirukkuṟaṭ paṉiyēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyoṉṟu eṉṉiṭam uḷatu. avvuri parimēlaḻakar mutalāyiṉāriṉ uriyuṭaṉ cērtteṇṇaṟ kēṟṟa takutiyuṭiyatākat tōṉṟāviṭiṉum, cila kāraṇaṅ kaḷāl aṉṉōr urikkum muṟpaṭṭatāmō eṉa aiyuṟattakka tākavuḷatu. ciṟcila pākkaṭkup piṇṭamākak karuttu māttiramē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēṟupaṭṭa pāṭaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉkaṇ ippāviṟku eḻutappaṭṭirukkum uri:-
"caṅkāyirañ cūḻavāḻum valampuripōlē kiḷiyāṉatu taṉṉic cūḻa vāḻvatu kīrttimāṉukkukkivarum eṉṟavāṟu" eṉṟatē. itiliruntu ippāviluḷḷa 'kēṭu uḷatākuñ cākkāṭum' eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭkup poruḷaṟiyak kūṭavilli; eṉiṉum, 'nattam' eṉpataṟku 'valampuri' eṉap poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭiruppatu veḷiyākiṟatu.
iṉi, cīvakacintāmaṇiyil "caṅkuṭintaṉiya veṇṭāmari malart taṭaṅkaḷ pōlum-naṅkuṭi" (civakacīntā - 547) eṉpataṟku "taṭaṅkaḷilē uṭinta taṉmiyavākiya malarum caṅkum pōlum naṅkuṭi. itu tūymikkuvami; iṉic caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅkeṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmi keṭāta kuṭiyumām; 'nattampōṟ kōṭum' eṉpa" eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri kūṟiyuḷḷār. piṟporuḷil caṅku uṭintaṉiyakuṭi eṉa iyiyum; caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅ keṭātatu pōlak keṭṭāluntaṉṟaṉmi keṭātakuṭi eṉṟu kūṟiyavar ataṟku mēṟkōḷāka  'nattampōṟ kēṭum' eṉpataṉik kāṭṭiyiruppati liruntu, avar nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷami pulaṉām. 'uḷatākuñ cākkāṭum' eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar koṇṭa poruḷ iṉṉateṉa veḷippaṭāviṭiṉum, 'nattam pōṟkēṭum' eṉpataṟku avar koṇṭa poruḷ' pukaḻ kuṉṟutalillāta vaṟumi eṉpatākal vēṇṭum. pukaḻ, kuṭiyiṉṟaṉmi, aṉṟi vittakar taṉmi ākum. eṉavē ikkuṟaḷukku maṇakkuṭavarum parimēlaḻakarum koṇṭa karuttuṭaṉ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu māṟupaṭṭilatu eṉpatum, eṉiṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu ataṉi uvami yākkiyuḷḷāreṉpatum pōtarutal kāṇka.
yāṉ ciṟiyaṉāyt tiṇṇippaḷḷiyil tirukkuṟaḷ paṭittu vanta poḻutu eṉ tantiyār mutalāyiṉōr 'nattam' eṉpataṟku natti yeṉṟum, caṅkeṉṟum ētētō poruḷ kūṟak kēṭṭuḷēṉ. āṉāl appoḻutu avarkaḷ poruntamuṟap poruḷ colliyatāka nāṉ niṉikkavilli aḵtev vāṟāyiṉum nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉum poruḷ uccimēṟ pulavarkoḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷateṉa 
ippoḻutaṟiyalāṉēṉ.


11. nantampōṟkēṭu eṉṉum
 kuṟaḷuri ārāycci
'oru kuṟaṭpā' eṉat talippeyariṭṭu yāṉeḻutiya kaṭṭuri yoṉṟu 'tamiḻp poḻil' tuṇar-1 pakkam 429-431il veḷivantatu. ataṉi maṟuttu, "nattampōṟkēṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷuri" eṉṉun talippiṉkīḻ, tiruvāḷar T.N. appaṉiyaṅkār avarkaḷ tamiḻp poḻilukku eḻutiya kaṭṭuri eṉpārvikku vantatu. ciṟṟaṟivum ciṟu kēḷviyumuṭiya yāṉ palaviṉikaḷuk kiṭippaṭṭa poḻutukaḷil eḻutivarum iṉṉōraṉṉa urikaḷil iṟappavum palavāya piḻikaḷiruttal iyalpē. mūtaṟivuṭiya periyārkaḷ eṉ urikaḷilum karuttic celutti ārāyntu, avaṟṟiluḷḷa piḻikaḷiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvārkaḷāyiṉ, atu eṉakkup periyatōr ūtiyamā meṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyamilli. aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ eḻutiyatu attaṉmiyatāyiṉ avarkaṭku eṉ makiḻcciyiyum naṉṟiyaṟiviyum terivittu amiyum attuṇiyaṉṟiyāṉ ceyyaṟpālatu piṟitilli; eṉ poḻutum vaṟitākātu. āṉāl, avarkaḷ eṉ karuttiṉit teḷiya aṟiyāmalum, maṇakkuṭavar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutalāyiṉāriṉ urip poruḷkaḷik kāṇāmalum, muṉṉoṭupiṉ muraṇumāṟum, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkut taṭipayakku muṟiyilum eḻutiyiruttaliṉ, kaṟpār malivuṟāvaṇṇam avaṟṟi viḷakkutal eṉ kaṭaṉāyiṟṟu.
"	nattampōṟ kēṭu muḷatākuñ cākkāṭum vittakark kallā laritu"1
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷukku maṇakkuṭavarum, parimēlaḻakarum eḻutiya uriyiṉ oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷi nāṉ naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟēṉ. maṇakkuṭavar tam karutti naṉku viḷakkiṟṟila rāyiṉum, iruvar karuttum muraṇpaṭāmi kāṇaṟpālatu' eṉa nāṉ eḻutiyiruppatu aiyaṅkāravarkaḷukkup poruttamākat tōṉṟavilli. avarkaḷ ‘ivviruvaruriyum orē karuttuṭiyaṉa eṉṟal evvāṟu poruntu meṉpatu viḷaṅkavilli' eṉṟum, ‘ākkam pōlakkēṭu' uḷatāṉāṟpōlac cātal eṉpaṉa teḷivāyp poruḷuṇarak kiṭappaṉavāmō' eṉṟum kūṟip pōntu, piṉ, ākkampōlakkēṭu' eṉṟataṉi ‘ākkam pōlat tōṉṟuṅkēṭu' eṉa virittup poruḷ ceytu pārttum, attoṭari muṉpiṉṉāka māṟik ‘kēṭu pōṟ ṟōṉṟum ākkam' eṉa vittup poruḷ ceytu pārttum, muṭivil avviru poruḷum poruntāveṉak kaḻittu, avvaḷaviṉamiyātu, ‘ātalāl ataṉi uvamac collākavē niṟutti uṇmip poruḷ kāṇutal eḷitiṉiyalāta teṉpatu teḷivāyiṟṟeṉka' eṉṟurittaṉar. itiliruntu, maṇakkuṭavaruriyiṉ karutti avar aṟintu koḷḷavilli yeṉpatum, ataṉit tammāl aṟiya muṭiyavilliyeṉa avar ottuk koṇṭiruppatum veḷiyākiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ‘eḷitiṉiyalāta teṉpatu teḷivāyiṟ ṟeṉka' eṉṟu kūṟiyatu avaraḷavil uṇmiyē. piṟarukkum iyalātateṉak kūṟutal muṟiyaṉṟākaliṉ, akkaruttuṭaṉ kūṟiyatākak koḷḷutal vēṇṭā. maṇakkuṭavar kūṟiya ‘ākkampōlakkēṭum' eṉṉun toṭarukku, ākkampōlumāṟu keṭuvatum eṉpatē poruḷ. atu oruvāṟṟāṟkēṭāyiṉum, ākkam payappatākaliṉ ākkampōlak karutappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum viḷakkamākak kūṟiṉ, ākkattuḷ vitteṇṇattakum kēṭum, nilipēṟṟil vitteṇṇattakum cāvum vallavaṟ kallatu ariteṉpatu maṇakkuṭavar urip poruḷākum.
"	oppuravi ṉālvaruṅ kēṭeṉi ṉaḵtoruvaṉ
viṟṟukkōṭ ṭakka tuṭittu"1
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷum,
"	vīntava reṉpavar vīntava rēṉum
īntava ralla tiruntavar yārē"2
eṉṉum kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuṭ pakutiyum mēl ārāynta urik karuttiṉi viḷakkattakuvaṉa. aiyaṅkāravarkaḷukku itu pulaṉākāmiyāl ‘ākkampōlat tōṉṟuṅkēṭu' ‘kēṭupōṟ ṟōṉṟum ākkam' eṉṟiṅṅaṉam iṭarppaṭṭurittu, maṇak kuṭavaruri parimēlaḻakaruriyuṭaṉ muraṇuvateṉṟum, avaruriyiṉ uṇmipporuḷ kāṇaviyalāteṉṟum palavāṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. ‘ākkampōlak kēṭu' eṉṉum toṭariṉ poruḷ avarkku eḷitiṉ viḷaṅkavilliyēṉum, "itu pukaḻpaṭa vāḻtal makkaḷellārkkum ariteṉṟatu' eṉa maṇakkuṭavar karutturittatiṟ karuttūṉṟiyirupparēl ittakiya maruṭci avarkku uṇṭākāmaliruttaluṅ kūṭum. pukaḻip perukac ceytalum pukaḻi niṟuttalum ellārkkum iyalvataṉṟu eṉpatē parimēlaḻakar uriyiṉ karuttu. maṇakkuṭavarum pukaḻpaṭa vāḻtal ellārkkum aritu eṉṟē kūṟiyirukkiṟār. iṅṅaṉamirukka iruvar karuttum muraṇpaṭāmi kāṇaṟpālatu' eṉa yāṉeḻutiyatil iḻukkeṉṉuḷatu?
iṉi, tirukkuṟaṭ paṉiyēṭṭuccuvaṭi yoṉṟiliruntu nāṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭiya maṟṟoruvar urik kuṟippiṉi ārāyvāṉpukku, ataṉit tirukkuṟaḷiṉ nērāṉa patavuri  eṉṟātal aṉṟip poḻippuri yeṉṟātal koḷḷa iṭamilli. maṟṟu atu ētō oruvakiyāṉa karuttiṉip pulappaṭukkiṉṟa oru pōliyuri yeṉavē puṟakkaṇikkattakkatu' eṉṟaṉar. 'avvuri parimēlaḻakar mutalāyiṉāriṉ uriyuṭaṉ cērtteṇṇaṟ kēṟṟa takutiyuṭiyatākat tōṉṟāviṭiṉum' eṉa yāṉ kūṟiyiruppavum avar avvuriyiṉi ‘oru pōliyuri' eṉpataṉāṟpōnta payaṉeṉṉi? avvuriyi eṭutteḻuti "itiliruntu, ippāviluḷḷa ‘kēṭum uḷatākum cākkāṭum' eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭkup poruḷaṟiyakkūṭavilli' eṉa yāṉē kūṟiyiruppavum, avar, ‘tirukkuṟaḷiṉ perumpāṉmiyāṉa coṟkaḷukku avvuriyiṟ poruḷ kāṇumāṟilli' eṉṟurippatu payaṉil ceyalām. "caṅkāyirañ cūḻavāḻum valampuripōlak kiḷiyāṉatu taṉṉic cūḻa vāḻvatu kīrttimāṉukkuk kivarum' eṉṉum avvuriyiyum ūṉṟi nōkkiṉ, atu, kiḷiyāyiṉār mutalāṉōr taṉṉiccūḻa avarkkup poruḷi yīntu vaṟumiyuṟuvatu pukaḻpeṟum paṇpuṭiyāṉukkē iyalvatām eṉa nalintēṉum poruḷ koḷḷa iṭantantu niṟkiṉṟatu. ‘uḷatākuñ cākkāṭum' eṉpataṟkē itiṟ poruḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. ‘nattam pōl' eṉpataṟku ‘valampuripōla' eṉṟu avvuri kūṟuvatu yāvarkkum eḷitiṉ viḷaṅkaṟpālatākavum, aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ ataṉi maṟup pataṟkum periyatōr ārāycciyi mēṟkoḷvatu viyappā kiṉṟatu. maṟṟu, avar 'ātalāl nattameṉṉuñ coṟ poruḷ aṟitaṟku ivvuriyiyē pōtiya cāṉṟākak koḷḷutal iyalāteṉka' eṉkiṉṟaṉar. nattameṉṉuñ coṟporuḷ tuṇitaṟku ivvuriyiṉiyē pōtiya cāṉṟākak koṇṭirun tālaṉṟō ataṉi iṅṅaṉam maṟutturikka vēṇṭum? 'ivvuriyil nattameṉpataṟku valampuriyeṉap poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭiruppatu veḷiyākiṟatu' eṉpatum, "nattameṉṉuñ coṟporuḷ tuṇitaṟku ivvuriyē pōtiya cāṉṟu" eṉpatum orē karuttuṭiyaṉaveṉak koṇṭaṉar pōlum?
iṉi, cīvakacintāmaṇiyil oru toṭarukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uriyiṉi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, avar karuttituveṉa nāṉ viḷakkiyiruntataṟku maṟuppāka aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷavaṟṟi nōkkuḻi, avar nilimikkup peritum iraṅka nērkiṉṟatu. "caṅkuṭintaṉiya veṇṭāmari malarttaṭaṅkaḷ pōlum- naṅkuṭi" eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar "taṭaṅkaḷilē uṭinta taṉmiyavākiya malarum caṅkum pōlum naṅkuṭi; itu tūymikkuvami" eṉa mutaliṟ kūṟip, piṉ, iṉic caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅkeṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmi keṭāta kuṭiyumām; 'nattampōṟ kēṭum' eṉpa" eṉṟurittaṉar. "piṟporuḷil caṅku uṭintaṉiya kuṭi eṉa iyiyum. caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅ keṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmi keṭātakuṭi eṉṟu kūṟiyavar ataṟku mēṟkōḷāka 'nattam pōṟ kēṭum' eṉpataṉik kāṭṭiyiruppatiliruntu, avar nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷami pulaṉām' eṉayāṉ eḻutiyiruntēṉ. "iraṇṭuriyilum ‘uṭintaṉiya' eṉpatu malarukku aṭi" eṉa aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar entavuriyilum coṟkaḷip poruḷ kōḷ muṟiyil muṉṉiṟuttip piṉ poruḷurikkavilli. avar poruḷurittiruppatiliruntē coṟkaḷi iṉṉavāṟu iyiyavittirukkiṉṟaṉaraṉe nām aṟital vēṇṭum. mutaluriyil, taṭaṅkaḷ uṭintaṉiya veṇṭāmari malar caṅkupōlum nam kuṭi eṉa avar coṟkaḷi iyiya vittiruk kiṉṟaṉar  eṉpatu  avvuriyik koṇṭutāṉē aṟiya vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu? avvāṟāka, ‘iṉic caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅkeṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmi keṭāta kuṭiyumām' eṉṉum uriyiliruntu, caṅku uṭintaṉiya kuṭi eṉa avar coṟkaḷi iyittirukkiṉṟaṉareṉpatu aṟivuṭiyār evarkkum eḷitiṟ pulaṉāva toṉṟākavum, ‘piṟa poruḷil caṅku uṭintaṉiya kuṭiyeṉa iyiyum eṉṟal avarurikku māṟukoḷak kūṟalām' eṉa aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟiyateṉṉi? māṟukoḷakkūṟal eṉpataṉi avar yāṅṅaṉam aṟintaṉar? uṭintaṉiya eṉpataṉi malarukkēṟṟi, uṭinta taṉmiyavākiya eṉa iṭarppaṭṭurikkum muṟ poruḷilum, caṅku uṭintāṟ pōlum kuṭi eṉṉum piṟporuḷē ciṟantatāmeṉka. 'uṭinta kālattu niṟam vēṟupaṭāmi maṟṟellāp poruḷkaḷiṭattu miruppavum ataṟkuc caṅkiṉiyē eṭuttuk kāṭṭutal ēṟpuṭiya taṉṟām' eṉkiṉṟaṉar. cuṭṭa kālattu niṟaṅkuṉṟāmi poṉ mutaliya piṟa poruḷkaḷiṭattu miruppavum ataṟkuc caṅkiṉiyē eṭuttukkāṭṭutal ēṟpuṭiyataṉṟām eṉpārkku ivar yātu kūṟuvar? poṉṉiṉ ṟaṉmi atuvātal "cuṭaccuṭarum poṉ pōl" eṉat tamiḻ maṟiyuḷḷum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, "nattampōṟkēṭu" eṉpataṟkup parimēlaḻakaruriyit taḻuviyē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ākkamākuṅkēṭu eṉap poruḷ karutiyuḷḷāreṉṟalum āṇṭik kēṟpuṭiyatākiṉṟatu. keṭṭālum taṉ taṉmi keṭātakuṭi eṉakkuṭiyiṉ perumiyi mēlē kūṟippōntavar. apporuḷi valiyuṟuttaṟku, aṅṅaṉam vaṟumiyāṟ kēṭaṭinta kālattum tam taṉmiyiṟkuṉṟātu ākkamuṟṟu viḷaṅkutal pukaḻpaṭa vāḻuṅkuṭip piṟantār kaṇṇē uḷatām eṉṉuṅ karuttuḷḷa ‘nattam------ laritu' eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷi mutaṟ kuṟippiṉāl niṉi'd2vūṭṭic ceṉṟa vareṉak koḷḷutalil yātōriḻukkumiṉṟu' eṉkiṉṟaṉar. itil 'parimēlaḻakaruriyit taḻuviyē' eṉap puṟappaṭṭavar piṉ avaruriyi naṭṭāṟṟil viṭṭuviṭṭami kāṇalākum. ‘vaṟumiyāṟ kēṭaṭinta kālattum tam taṉmiyiṟ kuṉṟātu ākkamuṟṟu viḷaṅkutal pukaḻpaṭa vāḻum kuṭippiṟantār kaṇṇē uḷatām eṉpatutāṉā parimēlaḻakar koṇṭa poruḷ? vaṟumi yeṉpatu yātu? kēṭeṉpatu yātu? iṅṅaṉam vaḻuppaṭak kūṟiyiruppaṉa pala. avaṟṟi ārāytal vīṇ kālappōkkē. tirukkuṟaḷi mutaṟkuṟippiṉāl niṉippūṭṭic ceṉṟaṉar eṉa aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟuvatu kuṟi kūṟuvatē pōlum. akkuṟitāṉ poyk kuṟiyātal iṅkē kāṭṭappaṭum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mēṟkōḷ eṭuttāḷum muṟimiyic cīvaka cintāmaṇiyiṉ mutaṟ ceyyuḷuriyip paṭitta tuṇiyāṉē aṟintu koḷḷalākum. avar oru ceyyuḷō cūttiramō muḻuvatum mēṟkōḷākakkāṭṭappaṭa vēṇṭiṉ ataṉi aṅṅaṉamē muḻutum eṭutteḻutiyiruppar: aṉṟi ataṉ mutalum iṟutiyum  eḻutiyiruppar: aṉṟi mutaliluḷḷa colliyō toṭariyō eḻuti, eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ, eṉṉuñ cūttiram eṉṟiṅṅaṉam viḷaṅka veḻutiyiruppar: ceyyuḷ allatu cūttirattiṉ oru pakutiyē vēṇṭumāyiṉ, eppakuti vēṇṭumō ataṉi māttiram eḻutiyiruppar; ivvuṇmikaḷi avaruri muḻutuṅ kāṇalākum. itaṉi avar tirukkuṟaṭpākkaḷi yeṭuttāṇṭirup patiliruntē kāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ: cintāmaṇi, muttiyilampakam, 189ām ceyyuḷuriyil "kollāmi mēṟkoṇ ṭoḻukuvāṉ vāḻnāṇ mēṟcellā tuyiruṇṇuṅ kūṟṟu" eṉa muḻutum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. avvilampakattu 292ām ceyyuḷuriyil "ciṟikākkuṅ kāppevaṉ ceyyum" eṉa mutaṟpakutiyik kāṭṭiṉar. vimaliyārilampakattu 3-ām ceyyuḷuriyil. "oṉṉār, aḻutakaṇṇīrumaṉittu" eṉa iṟutip pakutiyik kāṭṭiṉar. ivaṟṟiliruntu nāṉ mēlē kūṟiyaṉa piḻiyā vāymiyātal teḷika. aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ, kūṟṟiṉpaṭi, ‘nattam pōṟkēṭum' eṉṉum mutaṟ kuṟippiṉāl appāṭṭu muḻutiyumē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uṇarttiṉareṉak koḷḷiṉ, cintāmaṇiyiṉ mutaṟ ceyyuḷuriyil, avar "īṇṭu ulakameṉṟatu uyirkkiḻavaṉi; ‘ulakamuvappa' eṉṉum mutaṟkuṟippāl tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi muḻutum uṇarttiṉā reṉṟaṉṟō koḷḷa vēṇṭum? maṟṟum avar pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭuttoki mutaliyavaṟṟiliruntu kāṭṭiya vellām attaṉmiyavātal vēṇṭumē? ivaṟṟiyellām ciṟitum ōrtaliṉṟi avar maṟuppeḻutap pukuntataṉi eṉṉeṉṟurippatu? yāṉ itukāṟum eḻutiyavaṟṟiliruntu aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟṟu muḻutum, ciṟitēṉum uṇmi viravāta veṟum pōliyuri yeṉpatu yāvarkkum iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
iṉi, nattam eṉṉuñ collukkuc caṅkeṉṉum poruḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyilaṉṟi vēṟiṭattuk kāṇappaṭu vatuṇṭō eṉpatu iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyatu tivākarattil "nattupaṇilam vaḷināku curimukam, kampukōṭu caṅkeṉappaṭumē" eṉṟum, piṅkalattil "nantu cutti nāku paṇilam, vaṇṭu kēṭu vaḷiyē curimukam, kampu veḷḷi iṭampuricaṅkē" eṉṟum, cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭil "nalantarunantu cuttinākoṭu vaḷikampukkō, ṭilaṅkiyavāra ṇamvaṇ ṭiṭampuri veḷḷi caṅkām" eṉṟum caṅkiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟil nantu eṉṉum peyar mutaliṭattil niṟkiṉṟatu: ippeyar ammuccāriyi peṟṟu nattam eṉṟāku meṉpatu aṟiñarellārum uṭaṉpaṭaṟ kuriyatē; maṟṟu, cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭil "nattamē yiruḷum ūrum nantuṭaṉ iravu nāṟpēr" eṉa nattam eṉṉum peyarkku nantu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭiruttaluṅ kāṇka. immūṉṟu nikaṇṭukaḷum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkuk kālattāl muṟpaṭṭaṉa veṉpatum cintikkaṟpālatu.
iṉi, aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ tam kaṭṭuriyil ōriṭattil ‘aṉṟiyum, atu ciṟappuṭiyatāyiṉ, maṇakkuṭavarukkuk kālattiṉāṟ piṟpaṭṭa parimēlaḻakar tāmum avvāṟu uriceytalit tavirntu, pōleṉpati uriyaciyākak kūṟiviṭuttateṉṉi?' eṉṟum, piṟitōriṭattil ‘ātalāl ataṉi uccimēṟ pulavar koḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉareṉak kūṟutal attuṇip poruttamuṭiya taṉṟeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu' eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kōṭalē nam muṉaṉōrākiya pulavar perumakkaḷi nām pōṟṟumuṟiyeṉa ikkālattilum karutuvār cilarirukkiṉṟaṉar. itu kuṟittu yāṉ mika viritteḻutuṅ karuttuṭiyēṉāyiṉum, ivvuri viriyāmip poruṭṭu, aḵtu ārāyccimuṟi yaṉṟeṉṟum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kōṭal tamiḻ vaḷarccikku vaḻiyaṭiyākumeṉṟum kūṟumaḷavil niṟuttu kiṉṟēṉ. "caṅkuṭintaṉiya veṇṭāmari malarttaṭaṅkaḷ pōlum, naṅkuṭi"1 eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiya poruḷkaḷ ivvārāyccikku vēṇṭun tuṇiyākavē mēlē taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa. coṟkiṭakki muṟiyi ūṉṟi nōkkiṉ avar kūṟiya iru poruḷumē āciriyar karuttoṭu paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu koḷvataṟku iṭamilli. ‘caṅkuṭintaṉiya' eṉpataṉi malarukku aṭiyākki, caṅku uṭintu virintu niṉṟāṟ pōlum veṇṭāmari malar eṉak kūṟutalē poruttamuṭittām.
"	caṅkuṭin taṉiyatāḻit taṭamalart toṭiyalāṉē"2
eṉac cūḷāmaṇi kūṟuvatum itaṉi valiyuṟuttāniṟkum. maṟṟum taṭaṅkaḷ pōlum kuṭiyeṉṟu nērē poruḷ ceyvatē peritum porutta muṭittātal kāṇka. ikkālattil, tamiḻukku akattiyarāki viḷaṅkum perun tiruviṉarākiya makāmakō pāttiyāya u.vē. cāminātiyaravarkaḷum ik karuttuṭiyavarkaḷeṉpataṉi, avarkaḷeḻutiya ‘cintāmaṇi ārāycci viḷakkattil, ‘caṅkuṭintaṉiya veṇṭāmari malar' eṉpataṉōṭu otta karuttuṭiyatākac, cūḷāmaṇiyiluḷḷa ‘caṅkuṭin taṉiyatāḻi' eṉpataṉi eṭuttukkāṭṭiyiruppatum, taṭaṅkaḷpōlum kuṭi eṉap poruḷ paṭutalkuṟippiṭa,
"pāṟkaṭaṉ muḷittatōr pavaḷap pūṅkoṭi"1
eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷi ilakkattāṟ kuṟittiruppatum naṉku viḷakkum eṉka.


12.  tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpu
ulakilē ēṉiyuyirkaḷiṉiṉṟum makkaḷi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatāy, makkaḷiṉ aṟivu viḷakkattiṟkuc ciṟanta karuviyā vatāy uḷḷatu moḻi. aṉṟiyum moḻiyāṉatu ataṉip pēcivarum makkaḷiṉ karuttup poruḷkaḷittiraṭṭi vittirukkum karu'd2vūlamāyiruttaliṉ, ataṉ amippu mutaliyaṉa koṇṭē ammakkaḷiṉ nākarika niliyi uṇartalum cālum. ippoḻutu ettaṉiyō palanūṟumoḻikaḷ ulakattil vaḻaṅkuvaṉa eṉpa. eṉiṉum, tamiḻ, āriyam, epirēyam, kirikku, ilattīṉ, mutaliya cilamoḻikaḷē mikka paḻamiyum perumiyum uṭiyaṉavāka aṟiñarkaḷāṟ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ iruvaki vaḻakkiṉum niṉṟu nilavuvatu tamiḻ. tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpu eṉṉiṉ piṟa moḻiyiliṉṟit tamiḻiṉ maṭṭumēyuḷḷa taṉmi yeṉṟākum. piṟamoḻi eṉpatu tamiḻ allāta ellā moḻiyiyum kuṟikkumāyiṉum īṇṭut tamiḻukku etirāka niṟutti ārāytaṟkuriyatu caṅkatam eṉappaṭum āriya moḻiyē yākum; parata kaṇṭattu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷukkuḷḷē teṉmoḻi eṉṟum vaṭamoḻi eṉṟum ticip peyaraṭuttu etir niṟīi vaḻaṅkappeṟuvaṉa tamiḻum āriyamumē yaṉṟō? tamiḻiṉ taṉit taṉmiyiyō, uyarviyō kūṟappukiṉ atu maṟṟoṉṟi veṟuppatō iḻivupaṭuttuvatō ākumeṉac cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. attakiya maṉappāṉmiyiṉar ivvaviyiṉ kaṇ irār eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
ellā moḻikkum āriyamē tāyākumeṉṟum, ammuṟiyē tamiḻum āriyattiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatāku meṉṟum koḷḷum ārāyntu tuṇiyāta koḷkiyiṉi yuṭiyār cilar ciṉṉāḷiṉ muṉputoṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. moḻiyārāycci yeṉpatu putiyakali muṟikaḷil oṉṟāki uṇmiyi viḷakki varukiṉṟa innāḷil akkoḷki arukiviṭṭateṉpatil aiyamilli. eṉiṉum akkoḷki aṟavē oḻiyumāṟu, tamiḻiṉ, ciṟappiyalpu kaḷi uḷḷavāṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭit teruṭṭutal nammaṉōr kaṭaṉ. icciṟṟuriyum ikkaruttiṉik koṇṭē eḻunta tākum. moḻinūl ārāycciyāḷar moḻikaḷi avvavaṟṟiṉ paṇpukaḷ koṇṭu vevvēṟu kuṭumpam allatu kuḻuvākap pākupaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivvuripalkāmip poruṭṭu avaṟṟiyellām iṅkuk kūṟātu viṭukiṉṟēṉ.
vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi eṉṉum peyar vaḻakkē avi vevvēṟu taṉi moḻiyāmeṉpatuṇartti niṟkavum, vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟku utittateṉpār pōla vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu teṉmoḻi vantateṉpār cilar tōṉṟiṉami parivuṟaṟpālatē. atilum, tamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷit tuṟipōkak kaṟṟu ilakkaṇak kottu iyaṟṟiya cuvāminātatēcikar 'ainteḻuttālorupāṭi yeṉṟaṟiyavunāṇuvaraṟivuṭiyōrē' eṉṟu kūṟiyatutāṉ peritum iraṅkaṟkuriyatu. tēcikarkkum atu karuttākāteṉpatum, ōr muṉippup paṟṟiyē aṅṅaṉam kūṟiviṭṭaṉareṉpatum 'akattiyar' eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyil muṉ viḷakkiyeḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. vaṭamoḻip paṟṟu mikkārāya cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar tām iyaṟṟiya pirayōka vivēkattil 'vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻukkum vēṟṟumi ciṟitu kūṟi itu vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻ moḻikkum' pētaṅ kōṭi kūṟiṭṭu oru kūṟuṇṭō iṉṟōveṉpatu kūṟukiṉṟatu'. eṉṟaṉar aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiya avar tāmē 'vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻmoḻikkum' ilakkaṇamoṉ ṟeṉpataṟiyātu camñāpētat tālum pāṭivēṟṟumiyālum ikaḻntu vēṟeṉpāri nōkkiyeṉka" eṉṟum kūṟivittaṉar. tēcikar karuttum tīkkitar karuttum āriyamum tamiḻum vevvēṟu moḻikaḷē yāmeṉpatum, eṉiṉum avvirumoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟē yām eṉpatumākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. moḻiyiṉ amitiyē ilakkaṇamākavum avarkaḷ moḻiyiyum ilakkaṇattiyum vēṟu pirittōtiyatu eṅṅaṉam poruntum? avarkaḷ vāḷā ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉa moḻintārēyaṉṟi, eṅṅaṉam oṉṟākumeṉa ārāyntu taṅkōḷ niṟuviṉārallar.
iṉi, irumoḻip pulamiyum oruṅkuṭiya periyārākiya civañāṉa muṉivar tām iyaṟṟiya tolkāppiyap pāyira viruttiyil "aintiram nōkkit tokuttāṉeṉiṉ, tamiḻ moḻip puṇarccikkaṭpaṭuñ ceykikaḷum, kuṟiyīṭukaḷum, viṉikkuṟippu, viṉittoki mutaliya cila collilak kaṇaṅkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi mutaliya coṟpāku pāṭukaḷum, akam puṟameṉṉum poruṭ pākupāṭukaḷum, kuṟiñci veṭci mutaliya tiṇippākupāṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum, veṇpā mutaliya ceyyuḷilakkaṇamum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāmiyāṉum" eṉa eḻutiya uri cintikkaṟpālatu. tolkāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintiram eṉak kūṟiya cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar kūṟṟiyē āciriyar civañāṉa muṉivar iṅṅaṉam maṟutturittārāvar. tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟku vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam mutaṉūlātal evvāṟṟāṉum poruntā teṉpatu itaṉāl valiyuṟuttappaṭṭatu. tēcikar kūṟṟum itaṉāṉē maṟukkappaṭṭami ōrka. vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāta tamiḻc ciṟappiyalpukaḷ iviyeṉat tamiḻilakkaṇap pakutikaḷ palavaṟṟik kiḷanteṭuttōtiya āciriyar, ataṉōṭamiyātu, 'iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum' eṉavum kūṟippōntaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭum tamiḻiyalputāṉ vēṟu yātuḷatu? peyarccol, viṉiccol, viṉimutal, ceyappaṭuporuḷ, payaṉili, mūviṭam, mukkālam, mutaliyaṉa vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭuvaṉa eṉṉalāmō? iviyillāta moḻikaḷē ulakattililli. uvakiyil iṉ collum vekuḷiyil vaṉcollum eḻutal pōla iviyellām ellā moḻiyiṉum iyalpilē amitaṟ kuriyaṉa. ivaṟṟi oṉṟiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟiṟku vantaṉaveṉṟu kūṟuvōṉ "maruntiṟ ṟaṇiyāta pittaṉeṉ ṟeḷḷappaṭuvāṉ" eṉpatu tēṟṟam.
iṉi, ainteḻuttāloru moḻiyeṉavum aṟivuṭiyōr nāṇuvar eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟi iṉṉuñ ciṟitu ārāyātu viṭutal takkataṉṟu. tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ muppattu mūṉṟeṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyiruppavum ataṉic ciṟitum cintiyātu ainteḻutteṉṟu kūṟiyatu eṉ karuti? tamiḻ  mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatil irupattinteḻuttu vaṭamoḻiyiliruttaliṉ avaṟṟik kaḻittu, eñciya aintumē tamiḻukkuriyavākak kūṟalāyiṟ ṟeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷ yāvum piṟamoḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭutaliṉ vaṭamoḻikkeṉa ōreḻuttēṉum iṉṟeṉa ēṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu? iṉi avar eḷḷikkūṟiya ainteḻuttum attuṇi eḷimiyuṭiyaṉa vallaveṉpatiyum ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭil vittu viḷakkukiṉṟēṉ.
"	naṟṟā ṭoḻāa reṉiṉ"1
eṉpatu ōr kuṟaṭpāviṉ piṉṉaṭi. ivvaṭiyi vaṭamoḻiyil eḻututal cālumō? itaṉ mutaliluḷḷa 'na' eṉṉum oṉṟaṉṟi maṟṟiya eḻuttukkaḷ eḻutappaṭāvāyiṉ vaṭamoḻiyaṉṟō peritum eḻuttuk kuṟipāṭuṭiyateṉa itu kāṭṭā niṟkum? ippoḻutu ainteḻuttu ettuṇip perumiyuṭiyaṉa veṉpatu kāṇka.
iṉi, uyir, mey, eṉṉum tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ uyarnta tattuvak koḷkiyuṭaṉ poruntiyaṉa. vaṭaveḻuttuk kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷākiya accu, uṟal eṉpaṉavō yātoru ciṟanta koḷkiyum uṭiyaṉa valla; 'akara mutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy' eṉṟāṟpōla eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mutal iṟutikaḷi uṇarttum attuṇip payaṉṟāṉum uṭiyaṉavalla; mākēccura cūttiram eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mutaliyum iṟutiyiyum cērttup pirattiyākarit taviyē avi. tamiḻāṉatu pakuttaṟivuṭiya makkaḷ mutaliya uyiri uyartiṇi eṉṟum, pakuttaṟivillāta uyiriyum uyiril poruḷiyum aḵṟiṇi eṉṟum aṟivupaṟṟip pākupāṭu ceytiruppatu pōla vaṭamoḻi ceyyavilli; vēṟu emmoḻiyum ceyyavilli. ivviru vakip peyarkaḷē tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uyariya aṟiviyum, koḷkiyiyum, tamiḻiṉ taṉi māṇpiyum viḷakkutaṟkup pōtiyaṉavākum. tamiḻ eḻuttuk kaḷil moḻikku mutalil varuvaṉa ivi, iṟutiyil varuvaṉa ivi, ivaṟṟōṭu mayaṅkutaṟkuriyaṉa. ivi eṉac ceytirukkum variyaṟiyum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷ niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumākap puṇarum poḻutu uṇṭākum mey piṟitātal, mikutal, kuṉṟal eṉṉum tirivukaḷum, iyalpum ellām tamiḻukkuc ciṟappākavuriyavi. oliyiyalpiṟku māṟupaṭātavi. tamiḻ ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟa cantiyum iḷañciṟār uḷḷiṭṭa makkaḷ pēcciluḷḷa cantiyum oṉṟē; vaṭamoḻiccanti aṉṉataṉṟu.
tamiḻiluḷḷa pāl vakuppil muṟippiṟaḻvu ciṟitumilli; vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa pāl vakuppō coṉṉōkkaṅ koṇṭu āṇippeṇṇākavum, peṇṇi āṇākavum, iraṇṭiyum aliyākavum kūṟum piṟaḻcciyuṭiyatu. orumi paṉmi eṉpatu tamiḻ vaḻakku. orumi irumi paṉmi eṉpatu vaṭamoḻi vaḻakku; vaṭamoḻiyilē oru peyaric cārntu niṟṟaṟkuriya vēṟṟumi yurupu ataṉ aṭimoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟoṭum kūṭiyirukkum. aṅṅaṉam varuvatu tamiḻ vaḻakkaṉṟu: piṉ varuvati eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koḷka: 'kaṭaṟkariyil uḷḷatum pāvaṅkaḷi aḻippatum muṉipuṅkavarkaḷukku anukūlamāṉatumākiya cukantam eṉṉum maliyiṉatu kukiyil vacippōṉum taṉ oḷiyāl viḷaṅkuvōṉum makkaḷiṉ tuṉpattip pōkkuvōṉum ākiya murukaṉi vaḻipaṭukiṉṟēm eṉṟu tamiḻil varaṟpālatu, 'kaṭaṟkariyil uḷḷatil pāvaṅkaḷi aḻippatil muṉipuṅkavar kaḷukku anukūlamāṉatil cukantam eṉṉum maliyil kukiyil vacippōṉi taṉ oḷiyāl viḷaṅkuvōṉi makkaḷiṉ tuṉpattip pōkkuvōṉi murukaṉi vaḻipaṭukiṉṟēm' eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil varum. maṟṟum vaṭamoḻiyil vēṟṟumi ēḻēyākat tamiḻiṉ kaṇ eṭṭākum. 'intiraṉ eṭṭām vēṟṟumi yeṉṟaṉaṉ' eṉpār uḷarēṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṉ pōkku viḷiyittaṉi vēṟṟumiyākak koṇṭataṉṟu; tamiḻilē viḷivēṟṟumi parantiruppatoṉṟu; tolkāppiyar 'viḷi marapu' eṉa ōr iyal koṭuttu muppattēḻu cūttiraṅ kaḷālē ataṉi virittu viḷakkutaluṅ kāṇka. tamiḻiluḷḷa viṉiccoṟkaḷ tiṇi pāl iṭam kālam eṉpavaṟṟi oruṅkuṇarttum ciṟappuṭiyaṉa; vēṟu emmoḻiyilum attakiya ciṟappuk kāṇappaṭavilli. kaṇṭāṉ eṉṉum viṉimuṟṟilē mutaṉiliyāṉatu kāṇṭalākiya toḻiliyum, iṟuti nili yāṉatu uyartiṇi āṇpāl paṭarkki viṉi mutaliyum iṭi niliyāṉatu iṟanta kālattiyum veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭi niṟṟal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamāka eḻuttuc col ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilkāṇappaṭum vēṟupāṭukaḷum tamiḻiṉ taṉimāṇpukaḷum palavām.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyiṉum piṟamoḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu eḻuttuc collamiti yaḷavēyākum. vaṭanūlār ilakkaṇattic catta nūl eṉa vaḻaṅkutalum kāṇka. tamiḻilakkaṇamō eḻuttuñ collumākiya oliyaḷavil aṭaṅkuvataṉṟu: makkaḷ uṟutip poruḷ eytutaṟkuriya oḻukalāṟṟiṉi yellām variyaṟi ceytuṇarttum poruṭ pakutiyiyuṭiyatu. poruḷilakkaṇam vēṟem moḻiyilumiṉṟit tamiḻil maṭṭumē yuḷḷateṉpatu aṉiva rālum naṉkaṟiyappaṭṭatu. tamiḻāṉatu piṟa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭiṉum poruḷilakkaṇattiyē ciṟantatākavum iṉṟiyami yātatākavum koṇṭuḷḷa teṉpatu "eḻuttum collum yāppum ārāyvatu poruḷatikārattiṉ poruṭṭaṉṟē poruḷatikāram peṟēmēyeṉiṉ ivi peṟṟum peṟṟilēm" eṉṉum iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ uriyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. poruḷilakkaṇam akam puṟam eṉṉum irukūṟuṭiyatāki aṟam poruḷ, iṉpam vīṭu eṉṉum nāṟporuḷiyum nutaluvatu. akamāṉatu kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli, kikkiḷi, peruntiṇi eṉṉum ēḻu tiṇikaḷiyum, puṟamāṉatu veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki, kāñci, pāṭāṇtiṇi eṉṉum ēḻu tiṇikaḷiyum ovvoru tiṇiyum paṟpala tuṟikaḷiyum uṭiyaṉa. tamiḻiluḷḷa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷellām attiṇi tuṟikaḷōṭu poruntiyaṉavākavē irukkum: oru ceyyuḷēṉum poruḷilak kaṇattiṟku ilakkiyamākāta tāyirātu. poruḷilakkaṇam tamiḻukkēyuriyatu. eṉavē avvilakkaṇattuṭaṉ porunta eḻunta ceyyuṭkaḷellāmum tamiḻukkuriyaṉa veṉpatu kūṟātēyamiyum. ivvuṇmi yuṇarvārkkut tamiḻāṉatu vēṟemmoḻikkum kaṭamippaṭṭirāta taṉic cemmoḻi yeṉpatu pulappaṭum. uṇarātārē palavāṟu pitaṟṟāniṟpar.
iṉi, tamiḻiṉ ciṟappi viḷakkum poruḷilakkaṇak kuṟippukkaḷ cilavaṟṟic curukkamākak kūṟi ivvuriyi muṭippām. puṟapporuḷilakkaṇamāṉatu aracariṉ pōr muṟikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kūṟu mukattāl aṟam poruḷkaḷiyum vīṭṭiyum uṇarttuvatu. ap pōr muṟi yoṉṟē tamiḻar kaḷiṉ uyariya nākarikattik kāṭṭutaṟkup pōtiyatākum. pōr ceytaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa eṉpatum, appōr kaḷi iṉṉiṉṉa muṟiyāṉē āṟṟuvar eṉpatum puṟattiṇi kaḷil aḻakākac collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkē mikavum pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya ceyti yoṉṟuḷatu. aḵtāvatu tamiḻ nāṭṭu vēntarkaḷum vīrarkaḷum pōr nikaḻttumiṭattē appōr nikaḻccikkēṟpa ovvoru vakiyāṉa malarkaḷic cūṭiyiruppar eṉpatē. aṅṅaṉam cūṭumpūkkaḷāṉē avvoḻukkaṅkaḷum veṭci, vañci, mutaliya peyarkaḷip peṟuvaṉavāyiṉa. tām cūṭiyirukkum pūviliruntē appōr iṉṉa kāraṇattāl nikaḻvateṉpati aṟiya vippatu evvaḷavu makiḻccikkuriya ceyalām! itiliruntu, tamiḻaracarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷē potuvāṉa cila oḻuṅkukaḷi yēṟpaṭutti avaṟṟiṉ varampu kaṭavātu oḻukivantaṉa reṉpatum peṟappaṭum. tamiḻnāṭṭāriṉ ippōrmuṟikaḷum vaḻakkaṅkaḷum piṟa nāṭṭavarkaḷālum virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pāratattil vantuḷḷa nirikōṭalum mīṭciyumākiya ceytikaḷ tamiḻaruṭiya pōrmuṟiyi āriyar ēṟṟuk koṇṭamikku aṭiyāḷamākum, "nam pōr vīrar teṉṉāṭṭārip pōlattalikkumalarkaḷi aṇikiṉṟaṉar" eṉap parataṉ kūṟuvatāka vāṉmīki muṉivar tamatu irāmāyaṇattu ayōttiyā kāṇṭattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu tamiḻaruṭiya vaḻakkam āriyarāl virumpi yēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭamiyi naṉku teḷivuṟuttal kāṇka.
iṉi, akapporuḷiṉ māṇpu aḷaviṭaṟ kariyatoṉṟaṉṟu. iyaṟkiyum tūymiyum poruntiya taliyaṉpiṉāl iṉpantuykkum muṟimiyi eḻil peṟa uṇarttuvatu tamiḻukkēyuriya ciṟappiyalpām. ivvuṇmiyip paṇṭic cāṉṟōr palar naṉkuṇarntu kūṟi vittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
"	iṉṟamiḻ iyaṟki yiṉpam nilipeṟa neṟiyiṟ
tuyttār nikartamak kilāta nīrār"1 (cin.curamañcari 69)
eṉṟār tiruttakkatēvar.
akapporuḷ kūṟum kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉappaṭum irukikōḷum uyariya aṉpuṭaṉ poruntiyavē yeṉiṉum, avviraṇṭaṉuḷḷum kaḷavē iyaṟkiyum ciṟappu muṭiyatāmeṉpatu
"kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu kaḷaviṉ vaḻittē"2
eṉpataṉālum iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷukkuk kaḷaviyal eṉap peyar pōntamiyālum aṟiyappaṭum. kaḷaviṉatu ciṟappum, atu tamiḻ āyātavarāl aṟiyappaṭāta māṇpiṉateṉpatum, paripāṭal oṉpatām pāṭṭil naṉku valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. atilē nāṉmaṟip pulavari etirmukamākki,
"	nāṉmaṟi virittu nallici viḷakkum
vāymoḻip pulavīr kēṇmiṉ ciṟantatu
kātaṟ kāmam kāmattuc ciṟantatu
viruppō rottu meyyuṟu puṇarcci
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
taḷḷāp poruḷiyalpiṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ āyvantilār
koḷḷārik kuṉṟu payaṉ"3
eṉak kūṟit tamiḻiṉatu ciṟappi viḷakkiyiruttal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻarkku muḻu urimiyuṭiya kaḷavukkūṭṭam pōlvatorumaṇam āriyariṉ eṇvaki maṇattuḷ oṉṟāka vuḷḷatu. atuvē, yāḻōr kūṭṭam allatu kāntaruvamaṇam eṉpatu. tolkāppiyattilum, iṟiyaṉār. akapporuḷilum tamiḻaruṭiya kaḷavukkūṭṭattiṉ iyalpi āriyaruṭiya yāḻōr kūṭṭattōṭu oppittu viḷakkiyiruttalum,
"	tēvar paṇṇiya tīntoṭi yiṉcuvi mēvar teṉṟamiḻ meypporu ḷātaliṉ"1
eṉat tiruttakkatēvar kūṟiyiruttalum nōkkaṟpālaṉa. āriyar maṇaṅkaḷil oṉṟu tamiḻar maṇattuṭaṉ oruvāṟu ottiruppiṉum tamiḻaruṭiya oḻukkamum nākarikamum mika mēmpaṭṭavi yeṉpataṉip pala ētukkaḷāl niṟuvutal kūṭum. ōr āṭavaṉ tāṉ kātalitta naṅkiyi aṭiyappeṟāvaḻi maṭalūrntēṉum avaḷi aṭitaṟku muyalvaṉ eṉpatu akapporuḷil kūṟappaṭuva toru ceyti maṭalūrvōr nāṇiṉi aṭiyuṭaṉ iḻakka vēṇṭutaliṉ tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir maṭalūrvatāka niṉittaluñ ceyyār. ātaliṉ, tolkāppiyaṉārum
"	ettiṇi maruṅkiṉum makaṭūu maṭaṉmēṟ
poṟpuṭi neṟimi yiṉmiyāṉa"2
eṉa maṭalūrtali makaḷirkku vilakkiṉār. teyvappulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum itukoṇṭē tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷiriṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa māṭciyi viḷakkuvārāy,
"	kaṭalaṉṉa kāma muḻantum maṭalēṟāp
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka til"3
eṉṟār. āṉāl, āriyarukkuḷ makaḷirum ittakiya ceyal ceyyalāmeṉa aṉṉāruṭiya kāmanūl vitippatākum. atu tirumālik kāmuṟṟa peṇṇorutti maṭalūrtal kūṟuvatāka
"	aṉṉa naṭiyār alarēca āṭavarmēl
maṉṉu maṭalūrār eṉpatōr vācakamum
teṉṉuriyiṟ kēṭṭaṟiva tuṇṭataṉi yām teḷiyēm
maṉṉum vaṭaneṟiyē vēṇṭiṉōm"4
eṉattirumaṅki maṉṉaṉ periya tirumaṭaliṟ pāṭiyiruppataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. peṇṇorutti ataṟkut tamiḻ nūl iṭantarāmiyiṉ vaṭanūl muṟiyit taḻuvukiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu itil viḷakkamākac collappaṭṭuḷatu. itiliruntu āriyar oḻukkamum tamiḻar oḻukkamum ittaṉmiyaṉa eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum.
iṉi, tūymiyākiya aṉpiṉ ṟaṉmiyik kāṇavēṇṭiṉ paṇṭi akapporuṭ ceyyuṭkaḷiliruntē ataṉik kāṇalākum. piṉ varuñceyyuḷ aintiṇiyim patil uḷḷatu.
"	cuṉivāyc ciṟunīri yeytāteṉ ṟeṇṇip
piṇimā ṉiṉituṇṇa vēṇṭik kalimāttaṉ
kaḷḷatti ṉūccuñ curameṉpar kātalar
uḷḷam paṭarnta neṟi"1
poruḷvayiṟ ceṉṟa talivaṉ. pirivukku āṟṟātu varuntum taliviyit tōḻi āṟṟuvikkum poruḷiyuṭiyatu. icceyyuḷ kātalar virintu varuvar eṉpatit teḷiyac ceyvatē taliviyi āṟṟuvikkum neṟiyām. ataṉiyē tōḻi iṅṅaṉam kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. talivar ceṉṟa neṟi ōr ariya curaneṟiyākum: accurattilē oṉṟiyoṉṟu piritalillāta aṉpiṉiyuṭiya āṇmāṉum peṇmāṉum nīr vēṭki mikku ōr vaṟuvāyc cuṉiyik kaṇṭaṭikiṉṟaṉa. atiluḷḷa nīrō avaṟṟuḷ yātēṉum oṉṟē parukakkūṭiya ciṟṟaḷaviṉiyuṭiyatu, iraṇṭum parukiṉ oṉṟaṟkēṉum viṭāy taṇivatākātu: innilimiyil eṉceyvatu: āṇmāṉāṉatu tāṉ viṭāyāl varuntiṉum taṉuyirēyākiya mēṉmiyuṭiya peṇmāṉ annīrip paruki iḷippāṟa vēṇṭumeṉa virumpukiṉṟatu; āṉāl ataṉip parukac ceytālō taṉ kātalaṉākiya āṇmāṉ uṇṇātirukkat tāṉ maṭṭum uṇpatu taṉatu kaṟpiṟkum taliyaṉpiṟkum poruntāmiyiṉ, atu parukamāṭṭātu; itaṉik karutiya āṇmāṉ ivvāṟāyatōr cūḻcci ceyyum; aḵtāvatu, peṇmāṉ nīruṇṭu muṭiyuṅkāṟum tāṉum nīruṇpatupōl atil vāy vittu vāḷā uṟiñcik koṇṭirukkum; akkāṭu
tāṉ talivar, karutic ceṉṟa neṟiyākum eṉkiṉṟāḷ tōḻi. itaṉāṟ pōtarum poruḷāvatu tām cellum neṟiyilē vilaṅkukaḷum iṅṅaṉam aṉpuṭiyaṉavāyiruttalik kāṇum talivar tam uyirē pōlum niṉṉip pirintirukkalāṟṟāta
varāy uṭaṉē mīḷvar eṉṟu kūṟit tēṟṟiyavāṟām. eṉṉē kātaliṉ perumi; kātal kaṭavuḷiṉ uruvameṉṟāl vēṟu kūṟuvāṉēṉ. iṟivaṉ tirukkiliyil vīṟṟirukkuṅ kōlatti nam tirunāvukkaracu perumāṉ tiruviyāṟṟil eṅṅaṉam taricittār?
"	kātaṉ maṭappiṭi yōṭuṅ kaḷiṟu varuvaṉa kaṇṭēṉ"2
"	varikkuyil pēṭiyō ṭāṭi viki varuvaṉakaṇṭēṉ"3
"	pēṭi mayiloṭuṅ kūṭip piṇintu varuvaṉakaṇṭēṉ"1
"	karuṅkali pēṭiyō ṭāṭik kalantu varuvaṉakaṇṭēṉ"2
eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam tām kaṇṭavāṟu kūṟi, ivaṟṟikkaṇṭa muṟimiyāl,
"	kaṇṭē ṉavartirup pātam kaṇṭaṟi yātaṉa kaṇṭēṉ"3
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytāraṉṟō?
"	kātal virinilat tārā vaki kāṇār
cātalnaṉ ṟeṉpar takimiyōr - kātalum
ākki yaḷittaḻikkum kantaḻiyiṉ pēruruvē
nōkkilari nōva tevaṉ"4
eṉṉum iṉṉilic ceyyuḷil kātal kaṭavuḷiṉ ciṟanta vuruvameṉak kūṟappaṭṭiruttaluṅ kāṇka. pala kūṟuvāṉēṉ. iṟivaṉi niṉinturukippāṭum meyyaṭiyārkaḷ tam aṉpiṉ mutircciyil avaṉit tammōṭu vēṟṟumiyillāta kātaṟ kiḻamiyuṭiyavaṉāka vittup pāṭuvatoṉṟē kātaliṉ perumiyi naṉku viḷakkuvatākum. aṅṅaṉam pāṭappeṟṟa āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyār tiruppāṭṭil iraṇṭu kūṟi niṟuttutum.
"	poṉṉampūṅ kaḻikkāṉaṟ puṇartu ṇiyōṭuṭaṉvāḻum
aṉṉaṅkāḷ aṉṟilkāḷ akaṉṟumpōy varuvīrkāḷ
kaṉṉaviltōṭ ciṟuttoṇṭaṉ kaṇapatīc caramēya
iṉṉamutaṉ iṇiyaṭikkīḻ eṉatallal uriyīrē"5
"	ciṟiyāru maṭakkiḷiyē yiṅkēvā tēṉoṭupāl
muṟiyālē uṇattaruvaṉ moypavaḷat toṭutaraḷam
tuṟiyāruṅ kaṭaṟṟōṇi purattīcaṉ ṟuḷaṅkumiḷam
piṟiyāḷaṉ ṟirunāmam eṉakkorukāṟ pēcāyē"6

13. tiṇimayakkam
"	tiṇimayak kuṟutaluṅ kaṭinili yilavē
nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkuta liṉṟeṉa moḻipa
pulaṉaṉ kuṇarnta pulami yōrē"1
"	uripporu ḷallaṉa mayaṅkavum peṟumē"2
"	ennili maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum
annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum
vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum"3
eṉṉum tolkāppiya akattiṇiyiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷ tiṇimayakkam uṇartta eḻuntaṉa.
"	muttiṟap poruḷun tattan tiṇiyoṭu
marapiṉ vārātu mayaṅkalu muriya"4
eṉṉum akapporuḷ viḷakkac cūttiramum atu. ivi oru nilattil vēṟu tiṇikkuriya uripporuḷākiya oḻukkamum karupporuḷkaḷum mayaṅkivarutaliyē perumpāṉmi uṇarttuvaṉa eṉpatu ivaṟṟiṉ uriyāl aṟiyalākum. aṅṅaṉam mayaṅki varutaṟkuc caṅkattuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷi liruntu uriyāḷarkaḷ pala utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pāṭṭiṉuḷ vēṟu tiṇikkuriya pūkkaḷ mayaṅki vantavāṟuṅ kāṇka.
ippoḻutu tiṇimayakkameṉṟu palarāl aṟiyap paṭuvatu akapporuḷ kūṟum ittiṇimayakkamaṉṟu: orunilam maṟṟoru nilattuṭaṉ neruṅkiyiruttalāl oṉṟaṟkuriya karup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉa maṟṟoṉṟiṟ ceṉṟu poruntutal eṉpatē.
cēraṉceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṇṇakikkuk kaṟkālkoṇṭu nīrppaṭi ceytu mīḷumuṉ avaṉ maṉivi vañci nakarattiṉ araṇmaṉiyil paḷḷikkaṭṭiliṉ mītu tuyiliṉṟiyiruttali varuṇikkum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
"	kuṟattiyar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pāṇiyum"1
"	toṭuppē ruḻava rōtip pāṇiyum"2
"	kōvala rūtuṅ kuḻaliṉ pāṇiyum"3
"	veṇṭiri poruta vēlivā lukattu"4
"	añcoṟ kiḷaviya rantīm pāṇiyum"5
"	ōrttuṭa ṉirunta kōpperun tēvi"6
eṉṟu kūṟi, nālvaki nilamum vañcikku aṇimiyil iruttali viḷakkiṉār. arumpatavurikārarum ōrttu uṟaṅkāta tēvi yeṉṟatu nālunila aṇimiyuṅ kūṟiṟṟu eṉṟār. iṅṅaṉam oru nāṭṭiṉkaṇ nilaṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu neruṅki avaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷ mutaliyaṉa mayaṅkiyiruttali annāṭṭiṟku ōr ciṟappākak koṇṭu pulavarkaḷ aḻakuṟap puṉintu kūṟi vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. caṅkac ceyyuḷākiya porunarāṟṟuppaṭiyil kaaya-mutal uuru muṭiyavuḷḷa aṭikaḷil ittiṇi mayakkam kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. atil orupakuti piṉvaruvatu:
"	tēṉeyyoṭu kiḻaṅkumāṟiyōr
mīṉeyyoṭu naṟavumaṟukavum
tīṅkarumpō ṭavalvakuttōr
māṉkuṟiyoṭu matumaṟukavum,
kuṟiñci paratavar pāṭa, neytal
naṟumpūṅ kaṇṇi kuṟavar cūṭak,
kāṉavar marutam pāṭa, akavar
nīṉiṟa mullip paḵṟiṇi nuvalak
kāṉakkōḻi katir kutta,
maṉikkōḻi tiṉikkavara,
varimanti kaḻimūḻkak,
kaḻināri variyiṟuppat,
taṇvippiṉ nāṉāṭukuḻīi"7
kuṟiñci nilattuk kuṟavar tēṉiyum kiḻaṅkiyum neytaṉilat tilēviṟṟu aṅkuḷḷa mīṉiṉ neyyum matuvum vāṅkutalum, marutanilattuk kaḷamar karumpiyum avaliyum kuṟiñci nilattil viṟṟu aṅkuḷḷa māṉṟaciyum tēṉum koḷḷutalum, neytaṉilattup paratavar kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭutalum, kuṟiñcinilattuk kuṟavar neytaṟpūc cūṭutalum, mullinilattu āyar marutap paṇṇum maruta nilattārākiya akavar mullippaṇṇum pāṭutalum, mulliyiluḷḷa kōḻi marutattil neṟkatiriyum, marutat tiluḷḷa maṉikkōḻi kuṟiñci cārnta mulliyiluḷḷa tiṉiyiyum kavarntuṇṭalum, kuṟiñciyiluḷḷa manti neytaliṉ kaḻiyil mūḻkutalum, neytaliṉ kaḻiyiluḷḷa nāri kuṟiñci yiṭattu maliyiṟ ceṉṟu taṅkutalum iyaṟki neṟi piṟaḻāmal itiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu kaṟpārkkup periyatōr iṉpam payappatākum.
"	koṭicciyar puṉattayaṟ kuṟiñci neypakar
iṭicciyar katuppayaṟ kamaḻum; ēḻiyaṅ
kaṭicciyar kaḷiyeṟi kuvaḷi kāṉalvāyt
toṭuttalar piṇiyalār kuḻaluṭṭōṉṟumē"1
"	kalavartañ ciṟupaṟi yiciyiṟ kiviṉip
pulavartēm piḻimakiḻ kuravi poṅkumē
kulavukāṟ kōvalar koṉṟit tīṅkuḻal
ulavunī ḷacuṇamā vuṟaṅku meṉpavē"2
ivviraṇṭum cūḷāmaṇic ceyyuṭkaḷ (33, 34). mulli nilattu āycciyar kuṟiñcippūviyum neytaṉilattu nuḷicciyar marutattup pūviyum cūṭikkoḷvar eṉṟum, neytaṉilattu nuḷiyar paṟiyōciyāl maruta nilattu uḻavar kuravi viḷakkamuṟum eṉṟum, mullinilattuk kōvalar kuḻaliciyāl kuṟiñci nilattu acuṇamā uṟaṅkum eṉṟum ivaṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭṭami kāṇka.
iṉi, teyvamaṇakkuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟṟavalla cēkkiḻār perumāṉ tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattē toṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ vaḷamurikkumiṭattu āṟu ceyyuṭkaḷil tiṇimayakkaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu piṉvaruvatu:
"	kavaru mīṉkuvi kaḻiyavar kāṉavark kaḷittuc
civaluñ cēvalu māṟiyuñ ciṟukaḻic ciyarkaḷ
avari yēṉaluk keyiṟṟiyar pavaḷamut taḷantum
uvari neytalum kāṉamuṅ kalantuḷa voḻukkam"1
neytaṉilamākkaḷ mīṉiṉaṅkaḷip pālimākkaḷukku viṟṟu avariṭamiruntu civalum cēvalum vāṅkuvar eṉṟum, pālinila makaḷir avari tiṉikaḷi neytal makaḷirkku viṟṟu avariṭa miruntu pavaḷamum muttum vāṅkuvar eṉṟum itiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. ippāṭṭil amintuḷḷa cila nuṇporuḷkaḷ aṟiyaṟpālaṉa. īṇṭuk kāṉavareṉpār pālimākkaḷ. pāliyāvatu mulliyum kuṟiñciyum kalantu tirintateṉpatu.
"	mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟimiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pāliyeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum"2
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷāl aṟiyalākum. cēkkiḻār kuricilō
"	kōla mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyu maṭuttacil liṭaṅkaḷ
nīla vāṭpaṭi nīlikōṭ ṭaṅkaḷu nirantu
kāla vēṉiliṟ kaṭumpakaṟ poḻutiṉip paṟṟip
pāli yuñcola lāvaṉa vuḷaparaṉ murampu"3
eṉṟu kūṟi vittuḷḷār. iṅṅaṉam mulliyum kuṟiñciyum pāliyāyiṉamiyiṉ avviru tiṇikkumuriya karupporuḷkaḷ pālikkuriyavātal cālum. ikkaruttāṉē mullikkuriya civalum avariyum, kuṟiñcikkuriya kuyiṟcēvalum tiṉiyum itiṟ pālikkuriyavākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉaveṉka. icceyyuḷiluḷḷa maṟṟoru nayam tiṇi mayakkamuṇarttum.  itaṉil, coṟkaḷiyum mayaṅkavittiruppatākum. eyiṟṟiyar avariyēṉalukkuc ciṟukaḻicciyarkaḷ pavaḷamuttaḷantum eṉaniṟkaṟpālavākiya coṟkaḷ māṟi mayaṅki niṟṟal kāṇka.
iṉi, parañcōtimuṉivar, civañāṉamuṉivar, kacciyappa muṉivar, makāvittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ mutalāṉōr pāṭiya tiṇi mayakkaṅkaḷukku aḷavilli. avaṟṟiyellām iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappukiṉ ivvuri mikaviriyumākaliṉ, aṇimik kālattirunta aṟiñar cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiyattiluḷḷa tiṇimayakkap pakutiyoṉṟimāttiram kāṭṭi itaṉi muṭikkutum.
"	vañcinā ṭataṉil naṉcey nāṭeṉac
centamiḻ vaḻaṅkum tēyamoṉ ṟuḷatu: ataṉ
antamil peruvaḷam aṟivār yārō
marutamum neytalum mayaṅki aṅkeṅkum
puriyaṟu celvam nilipeṟa vaḷarum;
maḻali vaṇṭāṉam pularmīṉ kavara,
ōmpupu nuḷicciyar eṟikuḻi, tēṉpoḻi
puṉṉinuṇ ṭātāṟ poṉṉiṟam peṟṟa
erumiyiṉ puṟattirun tiruñciṟi pularttum
alikaṭaṟ kākkikku alakkaṇ viḷikkum;
kētaki malarniḻal iṉameṉak karutit
tārāt taḻuviṭac cārtarac ciritta
āmpalvāy koṭṭiṭum kōṅku alarttātē:
vālvaḷi cūlu ḷintu īṉṟaveṇ muttam
ōtimak kuṭampiyeṉṟu uṉṉupu kālāṟ
paruntiṉam kavarntu ceṉṟu aṭampiṭip putikkum;
karumpaṭu cāliyiṉ perumpuki maṇṭak
kūmpiya neytaṟ pūntaḷir kuḷira
mēyntakal kārā tīmpāl tuḷikkum;
alamukam tākkuḻi alamarum āmi
nuḷicciyar kaṇavarō ṭiḻittiṭum ūṭalil
vaḻitteṟi kuṅkumac cēṟṟiṭi oḷikkum;
pūñciṉi marutiṭi vāḻntiṭum aṉṟil
naḷimīṉ kōṭpaṟi viḷikēṭ ṭuṟaṅkā;
vēyeṉa vaḷarnta cāykulic cāliyil
uppār paḵṟi yoruniṟi piṇippar;
ipperun tēyattu eṅkum irāppakal
tappiṉum māri taṉkaṭaṉ tavaṟā;
koṇmū veṉṉum koḷkalam koṇṭa
amiḻtiṉi avvayiṉ kaviḻttapiṉ celvuḻi
vaṭiyum nīrē nammiṭitīr cāral;
naṉṉīrp perukku munnīr nīttamum
eyyā teṉṟum etirttiṭum piṇakkil
naṭukkaṭal naṉṉīr cuvittiṭum orupāl;
marakkalam vantiṭum vayaṟkari orukāl"1



14. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum poruḷilakkaṇamum
"kumariyoṭu vaṭavimayat torumoḻivit tulakāṇṭa" imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ eṉṉum cērar perumāṉ makkaḷuḷ iḷavalum, vēttu muṉiyumākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ muttamiḻp pulamiyum nirampiya vittakak kaviyaracar eṉpatu avariyaṟṟiya paḻutaṟṟa muttamiḻk kāppiyamākiya cilappati kārattāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭuvatākum, ittoṭar nilic ceyyuḷil iyaṟṟamiḻiṉ pakutiyākiya poruḷilakkaṇatti, aṭikaḷ eṅṅaṉam kiyāṇṭuḷḷaṉareṉac cila eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷāl viḷakkuvatu ic ciṟṟuriyiṉ nōkkam. ikkāppiya muḻutum poruṭ pakutikaḷ palavum viravi amintu kiṭattali itaṉ patikattoṭakkattē aṭiyārkku nallār virittuḷḷa uriyāl iṉituṇaralākum. avar akap poruṭṭiṇi kaḷākiya mulli, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉṉum aintiṇikkuriya nilaṉum, poḻutumākiya mutaṟ poruḷkaḷum, teyvam, uṇā, mā, maram, puḷ, paṟi, toḻil, yāḻ, paṇ, nilamakkaḷ, nīrnili, vārkoṭi, pū eṉṉum karupporuḷkaḷum, iruttalum ataṉ nimittamum mutalākiya uripporuḷkaḷum itaṉkaṇ amintu kiṭattali eṭuttukkāṭṭi, ataṉ muṭivil, "ic collappaṭṭa aintiṇiyiṉ mutal karuuripporuḷum piṟavumākiya akattiṇip poruṭ payaṉ uṭampoṭu puṇartti yuṇarttip puṟattiṇip poruṭpayaṉum piṟavum ivvāṟu uṭampoṭu puṇarttiyuṇarttuṅkāṟ puṉinturikkappaṭṭuk kēṭpōrkku iṉpam payavātu uṇarvu calikkumātaliṉ vantaṉa vantuḻik kāṭṭutalik karutiṟṟu ivvuri yeṉka" eṉak kūṟiṉar. akap poruṭpakuti palavum āṇṭē avarāṟ kāṭṭap peṟṟaṉavākaliṉ avaṟṟi viṭuttu, avvuḻi avar kāṭṭātu viṭutta puṟapporuṭ pakutiyuḷ oru cilavē īṇṭuk kāṭṭa niṟpaṉa. cilappatikāra intira viḻa'd2vūreṭutta kātiyil cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ vaṭaticimēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟa varalāṟu.
"	irunila maruṅkiṟ porunarip peṟāac
ceruveṅ kātaliṟ ṟirumā vaḷavaṉ
vāḷuṅ kuṭiyu mayirkkaṇ muracum
 nāḷoṭu peyarttu naṇṇārp peṟukavim
 maṇṇaka maruṅkiṉeṉ valikeḻu tōḷeṉap
 puṇṇiyat ticimukam pōkiya vannāḷ
acivi lūkkattu nacipiṟak koḻiyap
pakivilak kiyatip payaṅkeḻu maliyeṉa
imiyava ruṟiyuñ cimiyap piṭarttalik
koṭuvari yoṟṟik koḷkiyiṟ peyarvōrkku."1
eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu.
karikālaṉatu ippaṭiyeṭuppāṉatu puṟattiṇiyuḷ etaṉpāṟ paṭumeṉpatu ārāyccikkuriyatu. puṟattiṇi ilakkaṇappaṭi pōr mēṟ cellutaṟkuriya kāraṇattiyēṉum, tiṇip peyari yēṉum, tiṇikkuriya aṭiyāḷap pūviyēṉum āciriyar īṇṭuk kūṟātu, 'vāḷum kuṭiyum mayirkkaṇ muracum, nāḷoṭu peyarttu' eṉṉum tuṟik kuṟippāl iḵtu iṉṉa tiṇiyāku meṉpataṉi uyttuṇara vittirukkiṉṟār. nāḷoṭu peyarttalāvatu nāṭ koḷḷutal. aḵtu iṉṉateṉ pataṉi nāṭkōṭalāvatu nāḷum ōriyum taṉakkēṟpak koṇṭu celvuḻi akkālattiṟku iṭiyūṟu tōṉṟiya vaḻit taṉakku iṉṟiya miyātaṉavaṟṟi attici nōkki akkālattē muṉṉē cella viṭutal" eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar viḷakki yiruppatu koṇṭu aṟika. eṉavē, karikālvaḷavaṉ tāṉ pōrk kōlam, pūṇṭu, vaṭatici nōkkip puṟappaṭumuṉ taṉṉuṭiya veṟṟivāḷ, koṟṟakkuṭi, vīramuracu eṉpavaṟṟi naṉṉāḷiṉ naṉmuḻuttattil vaṭatici nōkkip puṟappaṭa viṭuttāṉ eṉpatu peṟṟām.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam vāḷnāṭ koḷḷutal mutaliyaṉa ettiṇiyiṉ pāṟ paṭumeṉa ārāyvuḻi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttiṉ paṭi uḻiñippāṟpaṭumeṉal pōtarukiṉṟatu. eṉṉi? avar uḻiñit tiṇiyiṉ tuṟippakuti kūṟum nūṟpā oṉṟiṉ māttiram "kuṭiyum vāḷum nāḷ kōḷ" eṉak kuṭināṭ kōḷum vāṇāṭ kōḷum kūṟip piṟitōriṭattum kūṟāmiyiṉ eṉka. āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vañcittiṇikkum ivi uriya veṉṉum karuttiṉarāyiṉum, uḻiñikkuppōla vañcikku ivi iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavalla eṉpatum avar karuttātal "iyaṅkupaṭiyaravam" eṉṉum cūttiravuriyāl aṟiyappaṭum. avar, ivi vañcikku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavaṉmiyiṉ "iyaṅku paṭi yaravam" eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅka vittu, uḻiñikku iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉ āciriyar āṇṭuk kiḷantōtiṉar eṉpar. paṉṉiru paṭalattiṉ vaḻi nūlākiya puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyuṭiyārō vañci, kāñci, uḻiñi eṉṉum mūṉṟu tiṇikkaṇṇum kuṭi nāṭkōḷum, vāṇāṭ kōḷum kūṟuvar. ivaṟṟuṭ kāñciyi vañciyiṉ pāl aṭakkiṉum vañci, uḻiñi, eṉṉum irutiṇikku avviru tuṟiyum uriya veṉpatu avar karuttākum. cilappatikārattiṟ pōnta eḻuttiṉ ṟiṟaṉaṟintum iṉcoṟ poruḷiṉ aḻuttantaṉi; aṟintum ippaṭiyārkku nalluri pālitta aṭiyārkku nallār 'vāḷuṅ kuṭiyum - nāḷoṭu peyarttu" eṉṉun toṭarkkuc ciṟappuri kūṟumiṭattu veṇpāmāliyiṉ vañcip paṭalattil avviru tuṟikkum aminta,
"	ceṟṟārmēṟ celavamarntu
koṟṟavāṇāṭ koṇṭaṉṟu"1
"	peytāmañ curumpimirap perumpulavar pukaḻpāṭak
koytār maṉṉavaṉ kuṭināṭ koṇṭaṉṟu"2
eṉṉum koḷukkaḷiyum,
"	aṟintava rāyntanā ḷāḻittēr maṉṉaṉ
eṟintila koḷvā ḷiyakkam - aṟintikalip
piṉpakalē yaṉṟiyum pēṇā rakanāṭṭu
naṉpakaluṅ kūki nakum.3
"	muṉṉar muraciraṅka mūrik kaṭaṟṟāṉit
tuṉṉarun tuppiṟ ṟoḻuteḻā - maṉṉar
uṭināḷu lantaṉavā lōtanīr vēlik
kuṭinā ḷiṟivaṉ koḷa".4
eṉṉum veṇpākkaḷiyum mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭiyiruppati
liruntu avar karikālaṉatu pōrc celavi vañcittiṇiyiṉ
pāṟ paṭuttaṉar pōlum eṉak karuta iṭaṉuṇṭākiṉṟatu. avar karuttu yātāyiṉum karikālaṉ kūṭār maṇkoḷal karutiyō, aṉṟit taṉatu maṇṇiṉik koḷḷutal karutiya māṟṟārōṭu porutal kuṟittō puṟappaṭṭāṉeṉpatu ciṟitum peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ ataṉi vañci yeṉṟal poruttamuṭittaṉṟu. iṉi, tirumāvaḷavaṉ pōrilēmiku viruppuṭiyaṉākaliṉ vaṭatici nōkkip pōyiṉaṉ eṉpatu koṇṭu ataṉit tumpittiṇi eṉṉalāmeṉiṉ, āciriyar oruvarum vāṇāṭ kōṭal mutaliyavaṟṟi attiṇikkuk kūṟāmiyiṉ aṅṅaṉamurippatum poruntuvataṉṟām. ivvāṟṟāl, karikālaṉatu vaṭaticip paṭiyeṭuppu uḻiñit
tiṇiyā meṉṟalē cevvitu.
āyiṉ, 'muḻumutalaraṇam muṟṟalum kōṭalum' ākiya uḻiñiyilakkaṇantāṉum ataṉkaṭ peṟappaṭṭilatēyeṉiṉ, imayamaliyāṉatu kuṟukkiṭṭu vilakkiṟṟeṉṟu avaṉ muṉintu ataṉ piṭarilē taṉatu puliyip poṟittu mīṇṭāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭutaliṉ ataṉiyē pakivaratu araṇmuṟṟiya ceyalākak koṇṭu uḻiñiyilakkaṇam poruntiyuḷḷavāṟu kāṇka. maṟṟu, ataṉi uḻiñi eṉpataṟku vēṟu ētukkaḷum uḷa. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kuṭināṭkōḷum vāḷ
nāṭkōḷum kūṟiṉāraṉṟi, muracu nāṭkōḷ eṉa oṉṟu kūṟiṉārallar. veṇpā māliyuṭiyārum aṅṅaṉamē muracu nāṭkōḷ kūṟiṟṟilarēṉum, uḻiñip paṭalattil vāṇāṭ
kōḷ eṉṉum tuṟiyi aṭuttu muracavuḻiñi eṉpatōr tuṟi kūṟiyuḷḷār. eṉavē, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟiya muracu nāṭkōḷukku veṇpāmāliyiṉ uḻiñip paṭalattil māttiramē oruvāṟu ilakkaṇam kāṇappaṭutalāṉum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu vaṭaticimēṟ celavi vañciyiṉpāṟ paṭutturikkak karutiya aṭikaḷ itaṉōṭu ataṉiṭi vēṟṟumi pulappaṭa
"	irunila maruṅkiṉ maṉṉarel lāniṉ
tirumalart tāmaric cēvaṭi paṇiyum
muḻutta mīṅkitu muṉṉiya ticimēl
eḻuccip pāli yākeṉ ṟētta
mīḷā veṉṟi vēntaṉ kēṭṭu
vāḷuṅ kuṭiyum vaṭaticip peyarkkeṉa"1 
eṉṟu kālkōṭ kātiyil, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṟku vāṇāṭ kōḷum kuṭināṭkōḷum kūṟi, muracu nāṭkōḷ kūṟāmi yāṉum karikālaṉatu pōrccelavu uḻiñittiṇiyā meṉpatu valiyuṟutal kāṇka. iṉṉum muracu nāṭkōḷukku aṭiyārkku nallār eṭuttukkāṭṭiya "mācaṟa vicitta" eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭil,
"	maññi olineṭum pīliyoṇpoṟi maṇittār
polaṅkuḻi yuḻiñiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik
kuruti vēṭki yurukeḻu muracam
maṇṇi vārā vaḷavi"1
eṉa uḻiñimāli kūṟappaṭṭiruttal itaṉi naṉku valiyuṟuttu māṟum kāṇka. itaṉāl aṭiyārkkunallārum itu uḻiñi yaṉṟeṉṉum karuttiṉareṉat tuṇital cellāteṉka.
iṉi, itukāṟum kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiliruntu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tolkāppiyattiyē yaṉṟip paṉṉiru paṭalattiyum taḻuviyuḷḷāreṉpatu pōtarum. eṉiṉum,
"	poṉpuṉi yuḻiñi cūṭi maṟiyaruntun
 tiṇpiṇi muraca niliyurittaṉṟu"2
eṉṉum veṇpāmāliyiṉ muraca vuḻiñik koḷuvāṉum.
"kuruti vēṭki yurukeḻu muracam"3
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭiyāṉum vīramuraciṟkup paliyūṭṭutal peṟap paṭuvataṉṟi, ataṉi nāṭkoṇṭu peyarttal peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ, aṭikaḷ ivviru nūlēyaṉṟi akkālattu vaḻaṅkiya vēṟu nūlkaḷiṉum ilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭō, aṉṟit taṟkālattu vēntaratu vaḻakkiṟkaṇṭō vīramuraciṉi nāṭkoḷḷumāṟum kūṟippōntaṉareṉka. iṅṅaṉamē, tolkāppiyam mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ vēṟāka aṭikaḷ taṅkōḷkiyi nāṭṭiccellutal piṟāṇṭum kāṇappaṭum.


15. tolkāppiya ārāycci
nūlāciriyar varalāṟu
1. tolkāppiyar jamatakkiṉi mintareṉpatum akattiyar māṇākkareṉpatum poruntumā eṉṉum ārāycci.
aṟivu cāṉṟa āṉṟōrkaḷē!
ivvārāycciyurikkup poruḷākavuḷḷa tolkāp piyattil,
"	aviyaṭakkilē ariṟavat teriyiṉ
vallā kūṟiṉum vakuttaṉar koṇmiṉēṉ
ṟellā māntarkkum vaḻimoḻin taṉṟē"
eṉa aviyaṭakkattiṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, yāṉum ammuṟi paṟṟi eṉ urikaḷiluḷḷa kuṟikaḷi nuṅkaḷ cīriya matiyāṟ ceppañ ceytu koḷkaveṉa vēṇṭi, eṉatu ārāycciyit toṭaṅkukiṉṟēṉ. ippoḻutu namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum kālattāl muntiyatum,  tamiḻiyal aṉittiyum muṟṟaveṭuttuk kūṟuvatum tolkāppiyamē eṉpatu tamiḻvallār aṉivarkkum oppa muṭinta karuttākum. innūliṉi naṉku ārāytalāṉē tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum, tamiḻakattiṉ paḻiya nilimi kaḷiyum oruvāṟu kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉpatil aiyamilli. yāṉ ivvārāycciyi naṭāttuḻip  paṇṭi nūl, uriyāciriyaṉmār koṇṭa koḷkikaḷukku orōvaḻi māṟākac cellutaluṅ kūṭum aṉṟi, itukāṟum yāṉ koṇṭirunta karuttukku muraṇākavum cila iruttal kūṭum. ārāycciyiṉ peṟṟi ituveṉavaṟinta aṟivuṭiyār ivi kuṟittu eṉṉi muṉiyāreṉṉum tuṇivuṭiyēṉ.
innūliṉ āciriyarākiya tolkāppiyar camatakkiṉi muṉivarkku mintar eṉṟum, akattiyaṉārkku māṇākkar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟār. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiyataṟku ātaravu, uriyāciriya rellāruḷḷum piṉ vantavarākiya āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, tolkāppiyap pāyiravuriyil, "tēva rellāruṅ kūṭi yām cēraviruttaliṉ mēruntāḻntu teṉṟiciyuyarntatu, itaṟku akattiyaṉārē āṇṭiruttaṟ kuriyareṉṟu avari vēṇṭikkoḷḷa, avarum teṉṟicikkaṭ pōtukiṉṟavar kaṅkiyāruḻic ceṉṟu kāviriyāri vāṅkik koṇṭu, piṉṉar yamataṅkiyāruḻic ceṉṟu avar makaṉār tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyāri vāṅkik koṇṭu, pulattiyaṉāruḻic ceṉṟu avaruṭaṉ piṟanta kumariyār  ulōpāmuttiriyāri avar koṭuppa nīrēṟṟu irīip, peyarntu... potiyiliṉ kaṇiruntu, irāvaṇaṉik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatari āṇṭu iyaṅkāmi vilakki, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyārākiya tolkāppiyaṉāri nōkki 'nī ceṉṟu kumariyārik koṇṭu varuka'  eṉak kūṟiṉar" eṉa urittirukkum katiyaṉṟi vēṟilli.
akattiyaṉār eṉap peyariya tamiḻāciriyar oruvar paṇṭu viḷaṅkiyiruntaṉareṉpataṟkum, muttamiḻkkum ilakkaṇamāka avarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatoru virinta nūl akattiyameṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ tikaḻntateṉpataṟkum aḷaviṟanta cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi nāṉ eḻutiya 'akattiyar' eṉṉum uri nūlil virivākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. īṇṭuc cilavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟēṉ.
iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uriyil muccaṅka varalāṟu kūṟu miṭattu, taliccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷum iṭiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷum akattiyaṉār mutalil vittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. akattiyam muccaṅkattārkkum nūlāyiruntateṉavum atiṟkūṟappeṟṟuḷatu. piṉ vanta uriyāḷarkaḷ palarum akattiyaṉārippaṟṟiyum akattiyattip paṟṟiyum āṇṭāṇṭuk kuṟippiṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, akattiyameṉac ciṟcila nūṟpākkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
"taraviṉṟākit tāḻici peṟṟum"
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiravuriyil 'iṉi ivvāṟu vanta koccakaṅkaḷiyellām oru variyaṟip paṭuttup pāttōṟum iṉañcērttip paṇṇiṟkut tiṟam pōlap piṉṉuḷḷa. āciriyar aṭakkuvar.  ataṉi akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum uṇarntu avartam karuttaṟinta āciriyar avvāṟaṭakkāmikkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. avvāciriyar kūṟuṅ kāraṇam iviyeṉa avar piṉpu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavi yellām pērāciriyaruriyāka viruttaliṉ, akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum uṇarntavareṉa avarāṟ cuṭṭappeṟṟavar pērāciriyarēyeṉpatu tuṇipu. "āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ' eṉṉum ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiravuriyil 'iḵtu eṟṟāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, itaṉ mutaṉūl ceyta āciriyaṉ akattiyaṉār collumāṟṟāṟ peṟutum eṉṟavāṟu" eṉap pērāciriyar tāmē kūṟiyiruppatum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
itiliruntu kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṉ piṉ neṭunāḷ vari akattiyam vaḻaṅki vantateṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalākum. eṉiṉum ataṟku yārum uri kaṇṭāreṉat teriyāmiyiṉ, uri yāḷarkaḷ kālattil annūl muḻuvuruvuṭaṉ kāṇak kūṭātavāṟu citintu pōyiṟṟeṉṟu karututal cālum. iṉi, taliccaṅkap pulavarāyiruntu tamiḻilakkaṇam iyaṟṟiya ivvakattiyarum, muṉivar palaruḷḷum ciṟantavarāka vittup purāṇavitikācaṅkaḷ kūṟum akattiyarum, irukku vētappatikaṅkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟi iyaṟṟiya akattiyarum oruvareṉṟō, vevvēṟāvar eṉṟō aṟutiyiṭṭurittal mika aritākum. akattiyar eṉpatu ōr kuṭumpap peyarē eṉṟum, ap peyaruṭiyār palar iruntiruk kiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, mutalil vintamaliyiṉ vaṭakkil ōr akattiyarum, irāmāyaṇa kālattil kōtāvariyāṟṟaṅkariyil ōr akattiyarum, pārata kālattil potiyiṉ maliyil maṟṟōr akattiyarum iruntuḷāreṉṟum oru cārār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟoru cārār akattiyar peyariṉar palareṉak kūṟuvatuṭaṉ taliccaṅkattu akattiyar iruntāreṉpatu tavaṟu eṉṟum, nakkīraṉārkku ilakkaṇam aṟivuṟuttuvarākat tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟa akattiyaroruvar kaṭiccaṅka nāḷil iruntārāvareṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avartam kūṟṟukku yātoru ātāramum kāṭṭappaṭavilli. orē peyaruṭiya palar orupeṟṟiyē nikaraṟṟa perumi vāyn tiruntaṉar eṉpatu putumiyākat tōṟṟiṉum aṅṅaṉam nikaḻtal kūṭāteṉpatilli. eṉiṉum avar oruvarallar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu iṉṟiyamiyātataṉṟō? enta nūlkaḷiṉ vāyilāka akattiyar eṉṉum peyari nām aṟikiṉṟōmō anta nūlkaḷaṉittum akattiyari oruvarākavē vittuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. appeyariṉar palareṉpataṟkut tiṉiyaḷavu cāṉṟum kāṇappaṭavilli. vintattiṟku vaṭakkilirunta akattiyariyum potiyil akattiyariyum oruvarākavē kanta purāṇam mutaliyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kōtāvari maruṅkirunta akattiyarum potiyilakattiyarum vēṟallareṉpatu irāmāyaṇattāl aṟiyappaṭum.
"	nīṇṭa tamiḻālulaki nēmiyiṉaḷantāṉ"
"	taḻaṟpuri cuṭarkkaṭavuḷ tantatamiḻ tantāṉ"
"	eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟami ḻiyampiyici koṇṭāṉ"
eṉa akattiyap paṭalattuk kampar kūṟumāṟuṅ kāṇka. maṟṟum avar 'vintameṉum viṇṭōy nākamatu nākamuṟa nākameṉa niṉṟa' miyum, 'īcaṉika rāyulaku cīrpeṟa virunta' miyum kūṟutalum karutaṟpāṟṟu. akattiyar palareṉpār, muṉṉuḷḷa nūlāciriyar palarum peyarorumiyāl maruṇṭu akattiyaroruvareṉak koṇṭu vaḻuvi viṭṭaṉareṉak kūṟuṅkaṭappāṭuṭiyarākiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu evvāṟāyiṉum ākuka. irāma rāvaṇap pōrukku muṉṉarē akattiyar potiyiliṉ kaṇ iruntaṉa reṉpatiyum, avarē akattiya miyaṟṟiya tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉpatiyum namakkuk kiṭit tuḷḷa ātaravukaḷaṉittum valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattilirunta kāntaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ aracar kulattik kaḷitaliyē nōṉpākak koṇṭa paracurāmaṉ taṉṉoṭu pōrkuṟittu varutaliyaṟintu akattiya muṉivaric caraṇaṭintāṉeṉṟu maṇimēkalikkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatum, akattiyar irāvaṇaṉi veṉṟu pōkkiyatākat tolkāppiya uriyilum maturik kāñciyuriyilum nacciṉākkiṉiyar kūṟuvatum pōlvaṉa vellām ikkarutti araṇ ceytu niṟṟal kāṇka.
iṉi, tolkāppiyaṉār akattiyarkku māṇākkar tāmō eṉpatu ārāytaṟkuriyatu. tamiḻurikaḷil muṟpaṭṭa tākiya iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluriyāṉatu tolkāppiyari iṭiccaṅkattup pulavarākavum, tolkāppiyatti iṭic caṅkattārkkum kaṭiccaṅkattārkkum nūlākavum kūṟuvataṉṟi avari akattiyaṉāroṭu toṭarpupaṭuttu yātum kūṟiṟṟilatu.
"	maṉṉiya ciṟappiṉ vāṉōr vēṇṭat
teṉmali yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaraṉ
ṟaṉpāṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ tāviṉ ṟuṇarnta
tuṉṉaruñ cīrttit tolkāppiyaṉ mutaṟ
paṉṉiru pulavarum pāṅkuṟap pakarnta
paṉṉiru paṭalam paḻippiṉ ṟuṇarntōṉ"
eṉa varum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālip pāyiramē mutaṉ mutalākat tolkāppiyari akattiyar māṇākkar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē piṉvanta uriyāciriyar cilarum kūṟuvārāyiṉar. veṇpā mālikku mutaṉūlākiya paṉṉirupaṭalam kaḷaviyalurip pāyirattil aḷaviṉāṟ peyar peṟṟataṟku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ innūlkaḷ ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟpaṭṭaviyalla veṉṟu karutal poruntum. eṉiṉum tolkāppiyar mutaṟ paṉṉiru pulavarum cērntiyaṟṟiyatu paṉṉiru paṭalam eṉpatil iḷampūraṇa vaṭi kaḷum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum karuttoruppaṭātavarākiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇiyiyaluriyil iḷampūraṇar "akattiṇi ēḻākip puṟattiṇi paṉṉiraṇṭākil moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vittal eṉṉum tantiravuttikkum poruntātāki, miki paṭakkūṟal, taṉṉāṉoru poruḷ karutik kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamum payakkum eṉka" eṉṟum, "paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ 'taṉṉuṟu toḻilē vēntuṟu toḻileṉ-ṟaṉṉaviruvakittē veṭci' eṉa iraṇṭu kūṟu paṭak kūṟiṉā rāyiṉum... avar avvāṟu kūṟal mikipaṭak kūṟalām. ataṉāl paṉṉirupaṭalattuḷ veṭcippaṭalam tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉār eṉṟal poruntātu" eṉṟum, "paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ karantikkaṇ puṇṇoṭu varutal mutalāka vēṟupaṭac cila tuṟi kūṟiṉārāl eṉiṉ, puṇpaṭuttal māṟṟōr ceyta maṟattuṟi yākaliṉ aḵtu ivarkku māṟākak kūṟutal mayaṅkakkūṟutalām; ēṉiyavum ivvāṟu mayaṅkak kūṟalum, kuṉṟak kūṟalum, mikipaṭak kūṟalum āyavāṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉ perukumākaliṉ uyttuṇarntu kaṇṭu koḷka" eṉṟum iṅṅaṉam palaviṭattum paṉṉiru paṭalatti maṟuttiruppatuṭaṉ, veṭcippaṭalam tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyataṉṟeṉat tuṇintum urittuḷḷār. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum paṉṉiru paṭalak koḷkiyip palaviṭattu maṟuttiruppatuṭaṉ, 'koṭuppōrētti' eṉṉum cūttiravuriyil "tattam putu nūl vaḻikaḷāṟ puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṟkut tuṟi kūṟiṉārēṉum, akattiyamum tolkāppiyamumē tokikaḷukku nūlākaliṉ avar cūttirap poruḷākat tuṟikūṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟuṇarka" eṉavum urit tuḷḷār. ivaṟṟiliruntu paṉṉiru paṭalam eṉpatu piṟkālattu yārō oruvarāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟu, akattiyar māṇākkar paṉṉiruvarālum iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉak kaṭṭiyurikkappaṭṭa teṉpatē avarkaḷ karuttātal peṟappaṭum.  'viṉiyiṉīṅki' eṉṉum marapiyaṟ cūttiravuriyiṟ pērāciriyar "paṉṉiru paṭalattup puṉinturi vakiyāṟ pāyirac cūttirattuḷ urikkappaṭṭatu" eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal ūṉṟiyuṇariṉ avar karuttum ituveṉpatu pōtarum. ivvāṟṟāl akattiyarkku māṇākkar tolkāppiyar eṉṉum koḷkiyum valiyaṟṟoḻital kaṇkūṭām. tolkāppiyattilē vaṭacoṟ kaḷi eṭuttāḷutaṟku viti kūṟiyiruppatuṭaṉ ampōtaraṅkam, kuñcaram, viciyaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ payiṉṟiruppatum, ikara aikāravīṟṟu tiṅkaṭ peyarum nāṭpeyarum niṉṟu puṇartaṟku vaḻivakuttiruppatum, vaḻi nūl vakiyuḷ moḻi peyarttiyaṟṟalum oṉṟāka vitittiruppatum, piṟavum ōrntuṇariṉ āriyamakkaḷiṉ kalappup peritum ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarē innūl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatākumeṉil poruttamām. irāmāyaṇa kālattil attuṇi mikutiyāka āriyakkalappu iruntirukka iṭamiṉmiyāl āciriyar akattiyaṉārkku neṭunāḷiṉ piṉpu tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyatākumeṉalē nēritu. ivvāṟṟāl tolkāppiyaṉār akattiyaṉārkku māṇākkarākkutal cellāteṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ākavē, avar camatakkiṉiyiṉ putalvar eṉpatum, akattiyarāl aḻittu varappeṟṟaṉa reṉpatum poruntāmi kūṟal vēṇṭā eṉka. tolkāppiyar akattiyaṉārkku uṭaṉ kālattiṉarallarāyiṉum avaratu koḷkiyip pōṟṟiyōrarukiṉamiyāl avarkku māṇākkareṉap peṟṟaṉar eṉak karututal cālum. iṉi pārata nikaḻccikkup piṉpē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟa teṉpataṟku uṟutiyuṭiya cāṉṟu oṉṟu uḷḷatu. tol
kāppiyattu uyir mayaṅkiyalil,
"	paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kāli
niliyiṉ ṟākum aiyeṉuyirē
ākāram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa."
"	koṭimuṉ variṉē aiyavaṇiṟpak
kaṭiniliyiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti"
eṉṉuñcūttiraṅkaḷāl paṉi eṉpatu koṭi eṉpata ṉōṭu puṇarttup paṉikkoṭiyeṉac ceyki ceytu muṭikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷil it toṭar payiṉṟiruntālaṉṟi āciriyar iṅṅaṉam vitikūṟā reṉpatu tēṟṟam. toki nūlkaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum nampi mūttapirāṉākiya palarāmaṉ palaviṭattuk kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇalākum. avaṟkuk koṭi paṉiyeṉpatum avaṟṟuḷ kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.
"putiyiruḷuṭukkip polampaṉik koṭiyōṟku"
eṉavum,
"niṉṉoṉ ṟuyarkoṭi paṉi"
eṉap paripāṭalilum
"aṭalvēn nāñciṟ paṉik koṭi yōṉum"
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟilum,
"poṟpaṉi veḷḷiyi yuḷḷā toḻukiṉṉā"
eṉa iṉṉā nāṟpatilum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl palarāmaṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiya pārata nikaḻccikkup piṉpē innūl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatātal orutali. pārata kālattip paṟṟi ārāycci ceytuḷḷār palar. pāratap pōr naṭantatu ki.mu.1000-ām āṇṭil eṉṟu oruvarum, ki.mu.1100-ām āṇṭil eṉṟu maṟṟoruvarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ki.mu.1194-ām āṇṭil nikaḻnta teṉṟu cittūr kōpāliyar ārāynturikkiṉṟaṉar.  R.C. tattar kūṟuṅkālam iṉṉuñ ciṟitu muṉṉarc celkiṉṟatu. C.V. vittiyā eṉpavar ki.mu. 3100 tāṉ pāratappōriṉ kālamāmeṉṟu pala cāṉṟukaḷāl niṟuvukiṉṟaṉar. tuvāpara kaliyuka caktiyil pāratam nikaḻntateṉṉum paramparik koḷki vittiyar avarkaḷāl vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷ koṇṭum niṟuvappaṭutaliṉ atuvē pāratappōriṉ kālamākumeṉa yāmum koḷḷaṟpālam. iṉi, māpāratappōriṉ piṉpu tolkāppiyam eppoḻutu tōṉṟiyateṉpatu ārāytaṟ kuriyatu. ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ tamiḻ moḻi tiruntiya niliyiṭinta tiṉṟeṉṉum karuttuṭiya oruvar tolkāppiyam annūṟṟāṇṭiyaṭuttut tōṉṟiyatākum eṉpar. ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa jinēntiramē tolkāppiyappāyirattil ‘aintiram’ eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭateṉṉum karuttuṭiya orucārār aḵtu ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭiyil utittatākum eṉpar. cātavākaṉaṉ eṉṉum āntiramaṉṉaṉukku amiccaṉāyirunta cārvavarmaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa ‘kātantiram’ eṉpatē tirāviṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭiṟṟu eṉakkoṇṭa, maṟṟoru cārār innūl ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉpu eḻuntatākumeṉpar.
iṅṅaṉam airōppiya āciriyar cilarālum intiya āciriyar cilarālum palavāṟākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vellām paḻaṅkatiyāyp pōṉamiyiṉ iṉi avaṟṟiṉ poruntāviyalpukaḷi eṭuttu viḷakkutal vēṇṭā. kaṭic caṅkattiṉ iṟutikkālamāṉatu ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭataṉṟu eṉpatu varalāṟṟaṟivuṭiya tamiḻ vallōr palarālum ippoḻutu naṉku tuṇiyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kaṭiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷē perum pakutiyiṉar ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭilum iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilum iruntōrāvar. avarkaḷāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa ceyyuṭkaḷil cakaṭam, camam, camaḻppu, ñamali, ñamaṉ, yavaṉar mutaliya coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu vantiruppavum, tolkāppiyaṉār,
“	cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟōraṟṟē
a ai oḷaveṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē”
“āe,
o eṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya”
āvō ṭallatu yakaram mutalātu”
eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷāl ca, ña, ya, mutaliya eḻuttukaḷ moḻikku mutalākā eṉa vilakkiyuḷḷār. maṟṟum avar,
“	nāṟcīr koṇṭa taṭiyeṉappaṭumē”
eṉṟu kūṟivittu, eḻuttuvariyaṟi koṇṭu annāṟ cīraṭiyiyē kuṟaḷaṭi, cintaṭi, nēraṭi, neṭilaṭi, kaḻineṭilaṭi eṉa aivakippaṭutturittaṉar. itu kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi eṉappaṭum. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷilō ik kaṭṭaḷiyaṭikku ilakkiyam kāṇu māṟilli. caṅkac ceyyuḷ palavaṟṟiyum ārāyntu tol kāppiyattiṟku uri vakutta pērāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ‘āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ’ eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyil, “ciṟappuṭiya kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuṭ payiṉṟu varal vēṇṭum piṟaveṉiṉ, innūl ceyta kālattil talic caṅkattārum iṭiccaṅkattārum kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi payiṉṟu varacceyyuḷ ceytāreṉpatu ic cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ peṟutum; piṉpu kaṭiccaṅkattārkku aḵtu aritākaliṉ cīrvakiyaṭi payilac ceyyuḷ ceytāreṉṟuṇarka” eṉa urittaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kaṭiccaṅka nāḷilēyē tolkāppiyar kūṟiyaṉa iṟantu paṭṭaṉavum, kūṟātaṉa tōṉṟiyaṉavumāka uḷḷavi cālappala. avaṟṟi virippiṟ perukum. ivaṟṟiliruntu kaṭiccaṅka nāṭkuc cila pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyateṉal peṟappaṭum. kaṭiccaṅka nāḷiṉ muṉpē tolkāppiyar iruntāreṉak koṇṭāruḷ oru cārār, ilaṅkit tīviṉ varalāṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭum kaṭalkōḷ palavaṟṟuḷ ki.mu. 306l nikaḻnta mūṉṟāṅ kaṭal kōṭkuc ciṟitu muṉṉē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉavum, alekcāntar eṉṉum kirēkka vēntaṉ intiyāviṉ mēṟpaṭiyeṭuttu vantapoḻutu uṭaṉpōnta kirēkka vāṉa nūlāciriyarāṟ koṇṭu varappaṭṭa ‘hōrā’ eṉṉum moḻi tol
kāppiyattuḷ ‘ōri’ eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭutaliṉ atu ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉal pōtarum eṉavum kūṟuvar. maṟṟum oru cārār pāṇiṉiyāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟṟa vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇavāciriyar aṟupattu nālvaruḷ mutalvaṉākiya intiraṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ‘aintiram’ eṉṉum nūliṉic cuṭṭi ‘aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ” eṉap paṉampāraṉār pāyirattuḷ ōtutaliṉ, pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālamākiya ki.mu.350kku muṉpē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟap peṟṟatākum eṉpar. ōrieṉṉum tamiḻc collē ‘arici’ mutaliya coṟkaḷ pōṉṟu kirēkka moḻiyiṟ pukuntatāmeṉak karutaṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ iruttalāṉum, tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉikkup piṉṉiruntavarāyiṉ vaṭamoḻiyil mikacciṟap puṭiyatākiya pāṇiṉīyatti naṉku kaṟṟu, ōr pakutiyiṟ piṟanta pala coṟkaḷi ārāyntu ataṉkaṇ aṭakkum annūṉ muṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭu tāmum tamatu nūliṉ kaṇ coṟkaḷi ārāyātu viṭārākalāṉum, pāṇiṉīyam niṟinta eṉṉātu ‘aintiram niṟinta’ eṉak kūṟiyiruppataṉāl avar pāṇiṉīyatti uṇarntavarallar eṉpatu peṟappaṭutalāṉum tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉikku muṉṉiruntārāvar eṉṉum koḷkiyē valiyuṟā niṟkum. pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 300 eṉṟum, 350-eṉṟum, ki.mu.700kku nīṇṭanāḷiṉ muṉṉākum eṉṟum carittira aṟiñar palar palavāṟu kūṟā niṟpar es. kiruṣṇa pēl vālkar eṉṉum aṟiñar tamatu ‘vaṭamoḻi’ ilakkaṇavoḻuṅkukaḷ’ eṉṉum nūlilē’ pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup pintiyataṉṟeṉa ārāyntu niṟuviyuḷḷār. pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālam etuvāyiṉum tolkāppiyaṉār avarkku muṉṉirun tārāvareṉpatu, kaṭippiṭikka tolkāppiyar kālatti ārāytaṟkuriya maṟṟoru cātaṉam kumariyāṟu kaṭalkoḷḷap paṭuvataṟku muṉpu avar iruntāreṉpatu. kalittoki, cilappatikāram, kaḷaviyaluri mutaliya tamiḻnūl, urikaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil nikaḻnta kaṭal kōḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilappatikāravuriyil aṭiyārkku nallār kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṟku nāṟpattoṉpatu tamiḻnāṭukaḷ iruntu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭateṉak kūṟukiṉṟār. erṉasṭ hekkal, skāṭ eliyaṭ mutaliya pēraṟiñarkaḷāṟ kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟa kumarikkaṇṭa (lemūriyā) aḻivu paṉṉūṟāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu nikaḻntatākal vēṇṭum. ‘eliyaṭ’ paṇṭitar kūṟum aintu periya piraḷayaṅkaḷil ki.mu. 9564-l nikaḻnta aintām piraḷayattāl ēḻēḻ tamiḻnāṭukaḷ kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu karututal poruntum. eṉiṉum kumariyāṟum, ataṉ teṟkē ciṟu nilappakutiyum eñciyiruntu piṉṉoru kaṭalkōḷāl aḻintirukka vēṇṭum. kaḷaviyaluriyāṉatu ‘akkālattup pōlum pāṇṭiyaṉāṭṭik kaṭalkoṇṭatu’ eṉa iṭic caṅkattiṟutiyil nikaḻntatākak karutiyurikkiṉṟa kaṭalkōḷāl kumariyāṟu aḻintirukka vēṇṭumātaliṉ ataṟku muṉpē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟatāmeṉka. paṉampāraṉār kumariyāṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku elliyākak kūṟiyiruppatum, ‘kaṭal koḷvataṉ muṉpu piṟanāṭu muṇmiyiṉ ṟātaṟkum elli kūṟappaṭṭatu’ eṉa iḷampūraṇar urittiruppatum pōlvaṉa itaṉi valiyuṟuttāniṟkum. iṉi akkaṭalkōḷ ekkālattu nikaḻntatō eṉiṉ, kūṟuval. yūtavētattiṉ ātiyākamattilē nōvāviṉ kālattil ōr piraḷayam uṇṭāṉa varalāṟu viritturikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu kimu. 2344il nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu yūtanūlāciriyarkaḷ kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. pāpilōṉiyarkkuḷḷum ōr kaṭal kōṭ ceyti vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉ varalāṟu ki.mu.2000-l avarkaḷāl veṭṭappaṭṭa kalveṭṭoṉṟilē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ilaṅkit tīviṉ varalāṟṟilum ki.mu.2387il ōr kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntami kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. manuvaracaṉ kālattil ōr piraḷayam nikaḻntamiyic catapata pirāmaṇam terivikkiṉṟatu. appiraḷayattōṭu toṭarpuṟṟa manuvaracaṉ maliyamalimēl tavam purintu koṇṭiruntāṉeṉa maccapurāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. pākavata purāṇamāṉatu kirutamāliyāṟṟaṅkariyilē tirāviṭa aracaṉāṉa cattiyavirataṉ eṉpāṉ tavañceytu koṇṭirunta poḻutu aṅku mīṉ vaṭivuṭaṉ vanta oru teyvam aṅkē nikaḻap pōkum kaṭalkōḷi avaṉukku muṉṉarē aṟivittu, avvāṟē atu nikaḻntapoḻutu ōr marakkalattil avaṉiyum avaṉic cārntōriyum ēṟṟuvittuk koṇṭupōy uyvitta varalāṟṟi viritturikkiṉṟatu. yūtarkaḷiṉ ākamattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭalkōḷ varalāṟṟōṭu pākavatapurāṇattiṉ varalāṟu peritum ottiruttal karutaṟpālatu. iṅṅaṉam vevvēṟu nāṭṭiṉar kūṟum piraḷayam varalāṟṟālum
