kālattālum peritum ottiruttalāṉum, atu tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻntateṉpataṟkup purāṇaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi paḻiya tamiḻnūl urikaḷum, nilanūl uṇmiyum cāṉṟu pakartalāṉum iṟṟikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 4200-āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu tamiḻakattiṉ teṟkē oru kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntu,  ataṉāṟ kumariyāṟum, ataṉic cārnta nilappakutiyum aḻintaṉaveṉṟum, tolkāppiyam ataṟku muṉṉarē iyaṟṟappeṟṟatākumeṉṟum koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. orukkāl ikkaṭalkōḷāṟ kumariyāṟu aḻiyātirun tiruppiṉ, ilaṅki varalāṟṟilē ki.mu.504il nikaḻntatākak kūṟappaṭum iraṇṭāṅ kaṭalkōḷiṉ muṉpāvatu tol kāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟatākal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟṟāl ilaṅki varalāṟu kūṟum iraṇṭāṅ kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻnta ki.mu.504ām āṇṭukkum, pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālamākiya ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum muṉṉum, pārata kālamākiya ki.mu.3100kkup piṉṉum uḷḷa kālap pakutiyoṉṟilē tolkāppiyar iruntāreṉpatu koḷḷaṟpāṟṟu.
tolkāppiyaratu kalvip perumiyum, 
nūlamipput tiṟaṉum :
tolkāppiyap pāyiramāṉatu tolkāppiyarit tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ārāyntavar eṉṟum, muntu nūl kaṇṭavar eṉṟum, aintiram niṟintavar  eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāciriyarkku muṉpē tamiḻil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ pala iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu ivar tāmē ‘eṉpa’ eṉṟum, ‘eṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉṟum ivvāṟākat tollāciriyar matampaṭap palaviṭattuṅ kūṟic cellutalāl aṟiyappaṭum. muntunūl eṉṟatu akattiyam oṉṟaṉiyē yeṉac cilar koṇṭaṉar. aḵtoṉṟē yāyiṉ ōriṭattēṉum ataṉi vitantōtātu yāṇṭum ‘eṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉṟum, ‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟē āciriyarkku’ eṉṟum potuppaṭak kūṟic cellārākaliṉ, ivarkku muṉpē pala nūlkaḷ iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, avaṟṟiyē ‘muntu nūl’ eṉap pāyiramum kūṟiṟṟeṉavum kōṭal vēṇṭum. muntu nūlāvaṉa akattiyamum, māpurāṇamum, pūtapurāṇamum, icinuṇukkamum eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟiṉar. appoḻutiruntavi iṉṉiṉṉa nūlkaḷeṉa aṟutiyiṭṭurittal cālāteṉiṉum akattiyam uḷḷiṭṭa pala nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa veṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivar avaṟṟiyum, aṟṟināḷ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷiyum naṉkārāyntavar eṉap pāyiram kūṟuvatum, aviyaṉittiyum muṟṟavaṟintavar eṉakkarutaṟkuriya kuṟippukkaḷ tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatum ivaratu kalviyiṉ akalattiyum āḻattiyum, naṉku pulappaṭuttuvaṉavām. eṭuttukkāṭṭākap piṉvarum kuṟippukkaḷ cila nōkkaṟpālaṉa. moḻi marapiṉ kaṇṇē moḻikku iṟutiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷi uṇarttumiṭattu ‘uccakāram irumōḻikkurittē’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl cakaravukaratti īṟṟiluṭiya muṟṟiyalukaramoḻi, tamiḻil iraṇṭē uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum,
‘	uppakāram oṉṟeṉa moḻiya
iruvayiṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē’
eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl pakaravukaratti īṟṟiluṭiya muṟṟukara moḻi oṉṟēyuṇṭeṉṟum aḵtoṉṟumē taṉviṉip poruḷum piṟaviṉipporuḷum uṭittākumeṉṟum kūṟiṉar. piṉṉum nakaravoṟṟi īṟākavuṭiya col uccakāra moḻipōl iraṇṭēyeṉṟum, ñakaravoṟṟi yīṟāka vuṭiya col uppakāramoḻipōl oṉṟēyeṉṟum, āṉāl itu iruporuṭpaṭāteṉṟum kūṟiṉar. maṟṟum nilam nilaṉ, kalam kalaṉ eṉpapōla makarattōṭu mayaṅkuta lillāta ṉakaravīṟṟu aḵṟiṇic coṟkaḷ oṉpatu uḷḷaṉa veṉṟum, tokimarapiṉ kaṇṇē aḷavup peyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalākavuḷḷaṉa kacatapanamavau eṉṉum oṉpateḻuttumē eṉṟum variyaṟuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivi pōlvaṉavum, uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya palatiṟappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiyum eñcāteṭuttu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarttuc ceyki ceytu muṭippatum, vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ poruḷ vikaṟpaṅ kaḷiyum avi mayaṅkivarumāṟṟiyum mikaviritturippatum, iḷamippeyarum āṇpāṟpeyarum peṇpāṟpeyarum ākiya marapup peyarkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum eṭuttu avi iṉṉiṉṉa vaṟṟukku uriyaveṉa uṇarttuvatum piṟavum tamiḻ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ coṟporuṭ parappu muḻutum ivvāciriyarāl naṉku vaṭittaṟiyap peṟṟaṉa eṉpati uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉi yeṉat teḷḷitiṉ viḷakkuvaṉavātal kāṇka.
iṉi ivvāciriyartāmē cūttirattiyalpeṉak kūṟiya cilvakiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭātalum nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmittātalum pōlvaṉavellām oruṅkamin taṉavākat tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ viḷaṅkuvatum, avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ coṟkaḷ poṉpaṇiyāmāṟu pōla ivar karutiya vāṟellām tirintu aḻaku ceyvatum pōlvaṉavaṟṟi nōkkiṉ ivar vaḻivaḻiyākat tamiḻilē ūṟivantatoru kuṭiyiṉ kaṭṭōṉṟi, uṇṇuñcōṟum parukunīrum tiṉṉum veṟṟiliyumellām tamiḻākavē koṇṭu vaḷarntu talimiyeytiya cāṉṟōrāvar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
“	mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum”
“	ovvu maṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭiyē”
“	ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum”
“	uppakāramoṭu ñakāriyumaṟṟē
apporuḷiraṭṭātivaṇiyāṉa
eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷiluḷḷa mutalā, mutalum, alaṅkaṭi, civaṇum, ñakāri, ivaṇi yeṉṉum colvaḻakku
kaḷiyum, innūṟpākkaḷiṉ tiṭpa nuṭpaṅkaḷiyum nōkkuṅ kaḷ. iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyaṟ cūttiram cilavaṉṟi naṉṉūl ciṉṉūl toṉṉūl mutalāya piṟkālattu ennūliṉ kaṇṇum  oru cūttirattāṉum innūṟpākkaḷiṉ aṇimiyilum niṟṟaṟ kuriya taṉṟāyiṉ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉāratu tamiḻp pulamiyiṉ viḻuppatti eṅṅaṉam aḷaviṭṭurittal kūṭum? iṉi ivar vaṭamoḻiyilum perum pulami vāyntavareṉpatu ‘aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉeṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi’ eṉṉum pāyirak kūṟṟāṉē tuṇiyappaṭum.
“	akatteḻu vaḷiyici yariṟapa nāṭi
aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittē”
eṉa ivar kūṟuvatu arumaṟiyiṉkaṇ ivarukkuḷḷa payiṟciyip pulappaṭuttā niṟkum.
iṉi ivvāciriyar innūli amikkum tiṟappāṭukaḷ nōkkaṟpālaṉa. eḻuttum collum poruḷumākiya mūṉṟaṉiyum mūṉṟu atikārattāṟ kūṟaluṟṟa ivar ovvōratikārattiyum oṉpatu oṉpatu iyalkaḷāka vakuttuk koṇṭuḷḷār. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr atikāramum iyalvakiyāl ōraḷaviṉavāka vakuttiruppatu tām kūṟakkarutiya poruḷkaḷi ōrelliyuḷ aṭakka muyaṉṟu iṭarppaṭuvatākumeṉac cilar karutavuṅkūṭum. eṉiṉum avviyalkaḷ yāvum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavum, avvav vatikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyipuḷḷaṉavumātal nuṇṇaṟivāṉ ārāyvārkkup pulaṉākā niṟkum, āyiṉ ōratikāratti oṉpāṉ iyaliṟ kuṟiyavō mikavō vakuttamittal ollātō eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉañ ceytal ollumāyiṉum oru nikarāṉa variyaṟiyuṭimiyiṉ payaṉkaruti aṅṅaṉam iyalkaḷi variyaṟi ceytu, poruḷaḷavukkēṟpac cūttiraṅkaḷi mikuttum kuṟittum iṭarppāṭiṉṟi yāttamittuḷḷāreṉal vēṇṭum. paṇṭiyāciriyarkaḷ tām karutiya poruḷkaḷi eñcāmaṟ kūṟuvatil nōkkuṭiyarāyiruntamipōṉṟē avi piṟkālattil tiripiṉṟi niṉṟu nilavavum, kaṟpōruḷḷattil eḷitiṟpukuntu akalātirukkavum uriya muṟikaḷi iyalumaḷavu kikkoṇṭu avaṟṟik kūṟum karuttuṭiyārāvar. pāṇiṉiyum tamatu nūliṉ eṭṭu attiyāyaṅkaḷiyum nannāṉku pātaṅkaḷāka vakuttuḷḷaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷ, tēvāram, tiruvāymoḻi mutaliya tamiḻ maṟikaḷum, āriya vētap pakutiyākiya aitarēyap pirāmmaṇam mutaliyaṉavum, caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil patiṟṟuppattu mutalāyiṉavum ovvōraḷaviṉavāka iyaṉṟiruttalum kāṇka. iṉi, ivvāciriyar meyppāṭṭiyaliṉ kaṇ eṇvaki meyppāṭum nikaḻuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷi uṇarttumiṭattu,
“	kalvi taṟukaṇ icimi koṭiyeṉac
collappaṭṭa perumitam nāṉkē”
“	celvam pulaṉē puṇarvu viṉiyāṭṭeṉ
ṟallal nītta vuvaki nāṉkē”
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam ovvoru meyppāṭṭukkum nannāṉku kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatum, uvamaviyalilē viṉi, payaṉ, mey, uru eṉṉum nālvaki uvamattiṟkum urupukaḷ taṉittaṉi evveṭṭāka variyaṟutturippatum, peyariyalilē, 
“	avaṉivaṉ uvaṉeṉa varuum peyarum
avaḷivaḷ uvaḷeṉa varūum peyarum
avarivar uvareṉa varūum peyarum
yāṉ yām nāmeṉa varūum peyarum
yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāvar eṉṉum
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappatiṉintum
pālaṟi vanta uyartiṇip peyarē”
eṉavum,
“	atuvitu utuveṉa varūum peyarum
avimuta lākiya āytap peyarum
aviyivi uviyeṉa varūum peyarum
avimuta lākiya vakarap peyarum
yātuyā yāvi eṉṉum peyarum
āvayiṉ mūṉṟōṭappatiṉintum
pālaṟi vanta aḵṟiṇip peyarē”
eṉavum ōriṉamāṉa mummūṉṟu  peyarkaḷi ovvōraṭiyil niṟutti aḻakum teḷivum poruntak kūṟuvatum pōlvaṉa kaṟpōruḷḷattil avi naṉku patiyumāṟu ceyya mēṟ koṇṭa muṟikaḷām eṉka. iṉi ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil potuvum ciṟappum mutaliya vitikaḷ oṉṟaṉiyoṉṟu pōṟṟiyum maṟuttum cellum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaṟpār nūliṉ oru pakuti kaṟṟu amiyātu nūṉmuḻutuṅ kaṟkum kaṭap pāṭuṭiyarāvar. akkaṭappāṭṭiṉiṉṟum avar vilakāmip poruṭṭu ivar nūṟpākkaḷi amittirukkun tiṟaṉ mika viyattaṟkuriyatu. iruḷ eṉṉuñ col niṉṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyil varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟavantavar ‘iruḷeṉkiḷavi veyiliyal niliyum’ eṉṟār. veyil eṉṉuñ coṟku aṅṅaṉam viti kūṟumiṭattō ‘veyileṉkiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum’ eṉṟār. maṟṟu, maḻi eṉṉuñ coṟku viti kūṟuṅkāl ‘maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal niliyum’ eṉṟār. vaḷi eṉṉuñ collukku vitiyum ‘vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum - avviyal niliyal cevviteṉpa’ eṉa ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa cūttirattōṭumāṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār. accūttirantāṉ ‘paṉiyeṉa varūum kālavēṟṟumikkattum iṉṉum cāriyi yākum’ eṉpatu. iṅṅaṉam uyir mayaṅkiyal 39-m cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya vitiyi 161-cūttiraṅkaṭkuppiṉ puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaliluḷḷa ‘iruḷeṉ kiḷavi’ eṉṉum cūttiraṅkāṟum toṭarntu varacceytiruttaliṉ iviyaṉittiyum oruṅku kaṟṟuṇarntālaṉṟi iruḷ, veyil, maḻi, vaḷi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ attuccāriyi yāvatu iṉcāriyi yāvatu peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉpataṉi eṅṅaṉam aṟital cālum. ivi vevvēṟu uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya coṟkaḷātaliṉ ivaṟṟi oruvaḻik koṇarntu oruṅkumuṭittalum poruntāmi kāṇka. ivvāṟu muṭippaṉa maṟṟum palavuḷa. iṅṅaṉam cūttiraṅ kaḷik kōvippaṭutti mutalum muṭivum māṟukōḷiṉṟi ivvāciriyar amittirukkum muṟiyi uṇaralāṟṟāta cilar tammi ilakkaṇam vallārākat tāmē matittuk koṇṭu, ‘tolkāppiyattuḷ iccūttiram īṇṭiruttal poruntātu, āṇṭu iruttal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu tamakkut tōṟṟiyavāṟellām urittal ettuṇip periyatōr aṟiyāmiyākum.
tolkāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintirameṉpār kūṟṟup pōliyātal :
tirukkurukūrc cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar tām iyaṟṟiya tamiḻp pirayōka vivēkavuriyil “ēḻiyaṉ muṟiya tetir muka vēṟṟumi - vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ-ṟōtiya pulavaṉu muṉṉoru vakiyā - ṉintiraṉeṭṭām vēṟṟumi yeṉṟaṉaṉ’ eṉa akattiyattiṉ kaṇṇum, ‘aintira niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ’ eṉat tolkāppiyap pāyirattiṉ kaṇṇum varutaliṉ ivikaṭku mutaṉūl pāṇiṉīyamum aintiramumā meṉka. akattiya niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṉāmiyāṉum, ‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟē yāciriyarkku’ eṉap potuppaṭa vōtiyatallatu peyareṭuttōtāmiyāṉum atuvē poruḷeṉka. piṉṉūl ceytār tattam pāyirattuḷ vēṇṭiya vāṟōtiṉār; atupaṟṟi urikārarum avarurittāṅkurittār; atu  poruḷaṉmiyaṟika” eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. ivar akattiyattiṟku mutaṉūl pāṇiṉīyam eṉak kūṟiyatu pōliyuri yāmeṉa ‘akattiyar’ eṉṉum urinūlil virittu viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. tolkāppiyattiṟku aintiram mutaṉūl eṉṟatu poruntumā eṉpatu īṇṭu ārāytaṟkuriyatu. ettaṉiyō pala nūlāciriyar uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ koḷkikku muṟṟilum māṟākat tām koṇṭa ittuṇiviṟku ivar kāṭṭiya ātāram tol kāppiyap pāyirattil “aintira niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ” eṉak kūṟiyiruppatēyākum. tolkāppiyar tamakku muṉṉuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷēyaṉṟi aintiraviyākaraṇamum kaṟṟuvallavareṉa avar perumi kūṟa vēṇṭiyē pāyirattuḷ avvāṟurikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl aintiratti mutaṉūlākak koṇṭaṉareṉpatu  eṭṭuṇiyum peṟappaṭuvatiṉṟu. pāyirattil, ‘tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakattu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi’ eṉpatum, ‘centamiḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭumuntu nūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa veṇṇi’ eṉpatum nūl iyaṟṟiṉamiyi vicēṭittu niṟṟalum, ‘aintiram niṟinta’ eṉpatu ākkiyōṉ peyari vicēṭittu niṟṟalum nōkkātu tīkkitar iṅṅaṉam vaḻuvuvārāyiṉar. avar piṟitōriṭattil ‘ yām innūluṭ perumpāṉmiyum taṟcamam taṟpavaṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiṉām. ipperumpāṉmiyuṅ kūṟiyatu yātu paṟṟiyeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻ moḻikkum ilakkaṇa moṉṟeṉpataṟiyātu camñāpētattālum pāṭi vēṟṟumi yālum ikaḻntu vēṟeṉpāri nōkkiyeṉka’ eṉak kūṟiyiruppati nōkkuṅkāl, vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻukkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉak koṇṭa tiripuṇarcciyum avar ciṟitum ōrātu tolkāppiyattiṟku aintiram mutaṉūleṉak kūṟak kāraṇamāyiruntateṉal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, cuppiramaṇiyatīkkitarēyaṉṟic cuvāmināta tēci karum tamatu ilakkaṇak kottil 
“	vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi yeṉumiru moḻiyiṉum
ilakkaṇa moṉṟē yeṉṟē yeṇṇuka”
eṉṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. tamiḻiṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṉum nirampiya pulamiyuṭiyarākiya ivviruvarum iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyatu mikunta viyappiṟkuriyatu. ivarkaḷ tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumiyi aṟiyātārallar eṉpatu, cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar tamatu pirayōka vivēkattu 46-ām ceyyuḷuriyil ;‘itu vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻmoḻikkum pētam kōṭi kūṟiṭṭu oru kūṟuṇṭō iṉṟōveṉpatu kūṟukiṉṟatu. tiṇi uṇarttum viṉivikutiyum, āṇpāl peṇpāluṇarttum viṉivikutiyum vaṭamoḻikkilli. tamiḻmoḻikkup piratamā vipattiyum, iliṅkattirayamumilli’ eṉa urittalāṉum, iṅṅaṉamē cuvāmināta tēcikarum ‘irutiṇiyum āṇpāl peṇpāl viṉiyīṟum vaṭamoḻikkilli. mūṉṟi liṅkamum,mutalīṟṟu vēṟṟumikaṭ kurupukaḷum tamiḻiṟkilli. mēlum āṇṭāṇṭuk kāṭṭutum’ eṉa ōriṭatturittu, maṟṟōriṭattu,
‘	cilapeyareccamuñ cila viṉittokiyum
iruvakiyuvamiyum vaṇṇac ciṉiccolum
paṇput tokiyeṉap pakarum vaṭanūl’
eṉac cūttirañ ceytu, ‘pakarum vaṭa nūl eṉavē tamiḻiṟ kaṭāteṉka’ eṉavum urittalāṉum iṉitu viḷaṅkum. ivvāṟu ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷi naṉku uṇarntu vittē ivarkaḷ irumoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉak kūṟiyatu vaṭamoḻiyiṉ mēluḷḷa paṟṟu mikutiyāṉō, vēṟe taṉāṉō eṉpatu terintilatu. iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyilum piṟa moḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇam eṉpatu eḻuttuc collamitiyē yākum. vaṭanūlār ilakkaṇattic cattanūl eṉa vaḻaṅkutaluṅ kāṇka. tamiḻilakkaṇamō eḻuttuñ collumākiya oliyaḷavil aṭaṅkuvataṉṟu; makkaḷ uṟutip poruḷeytu taṟkuriya oḻukalāṟṟiṉiyellām variyaṟi ceytuṇarttum poruṭpakutiyi uṭiyatu. poruḷilakkaṇam vēṟem moḻiyilumiṉṟit tamiḻil māttiramē yuḷḷateṉpatu aṉiva
rālum naṉkaṟiyappaṭṭatu. tamiḻāṉatu piṟa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭiṉum poruḷilakkaṇattiyē ciṟantatākavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākavum koṇṭuḷḷateṉpatu 
‘	eḻuttum collum yāppum ārāyvatu poru
ḷatikārattiṉ poruṭṭaṉṟē, poruḷatikāram
peṟēmēyeṉiṉ ivi peṟṟum peṟṟilēm’ 
eṉṉum kaḷaviyaluriyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. ivvāṟākavum, irumoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉpatum, tolkāppiyat tiṟku mutaṉūl aintiram eṉpatum poruntumāṟu eṅṅaṉam? poruḷilakkaṇatti viṭuttuc collilakkaṇam oṉṟē koṇṭu aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉārāvareṉiṉ, ataṉkaṇṇum irumoḻikkum kōṭipētamuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu avarē kūṟiṉami mēlē kāṭṭappeṟṟatu. tolkāppiyar aintiratti mutaṉūlākak koṇṭaṉa reṉṉum pirayōkavivēka nūlār koḷkiyi irumoḻikkaṭalum nilikaṇṭuṇarnta civañāṉa muṉivar tamatu pāyiraviruttiyuḷ maṟuttirukkum pakuti īṇṭu aṟintu makiḻaṟpālatu. atu, ‘aintiram nōkkit tokut tāṉeṉiṉ, tamiḻ moḻip puṇarccikkaṭpaṭum ceykikaḷum, ‘kuṟiyīṭukaḷum, viṉikkuṟippu viṉittoki mutaliya cila collilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi mutaliya coṟpākupāṭukaḷum, akampuṟam eṉṉum poruṭpāku pāṭukaḷum, kuṟiñci veṭci mutaliya tiṇippākupāṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum, veṇpā mutaliya ceyyuḷilakkaṇamum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāmiyāṉum, iviyellām tāmē paṭittuk koṇṭu ceytāreṉiṉ muntu nūl kaṇṭu eṉpataṉōṭu muraṇutalāṉum muṟkālattu mutaṉūl akattiyam eṉpatūum, ataṉ vaḻittākiya tol kāppiyam ataṉ vaḻinūleṉpatūum tuṇiyappaṭumeṉka’ eṉpatu. itiṟkuṟippiṭap peṟṟaṉavum piṟavumākiya tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ piṉṉarum viḷakkappaṭum. ivvāṟṟāl tol kāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintirameṉpār kūṟṟuc ciṟitum poruttamillāta pōliyuriyāyoḻital kāṇka.
tolkāppiyattiṉ pākupāṭu
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūl cūttiram, iyal, atikāram (paṭalam) eṉṉum mūṉṟu uṟuppukkaḷi aṭakkiyatōr piṇṭamākum.  innūṟpaṭalaṅkaḷ eḻuttatikāram, collati kāram, poruḷatikāram eṉa mūṉṟām. ivvatikāraṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟum oṉpatu iyalkaḷi uṭiyaṉa. eḻuttati kārattiṉ iyalkaḷ nūṉ marapu, moḻi marapu, piṟappiyal, puṇariyal, toki marapu, urupiyal, uyir mayaṅkiyal, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal, kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉpaṉa. nūṉmarapāṉatu nūṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavākiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarum, muṟiyum, tokiyum, pākupāṭum, aḷavum, uruvum, mey yeḻuttukkaḷ piṟa meyyōṭum tammōṭum mayaṅkumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
moḻimarapāṉatu kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ oru moḻik kaṇṇum puṇar moḻiyakattum varumāṟum, ōreḻuttoru moḻi mutaliya moḻip pākupāṭum, makarak kuṟukkamum, aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum, moḻikku mutalām eḻuttukkaḷum, īṟām eḻuttukkaḷum piṟavum uṇarttukiṟatu. piṟappiyalāṉatu uṭampiṉakattu eḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkum nilikkaḷaṅkaḷum, uṟuppukkaḷum, avaṟṟāṉ avi vēṟu vēṟu piṟakkumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. puṇariyalāṉatu meyyum, uyirum iṟutiyum mutalumākavuḷḷa coṟkaḷ niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumāy alvaḻi vēṟṟumip poruḷil iyalpum tiripumeytip potu vakiyāṟ puṇarumāṟum, peyaruṭaṉ ai mutaliya vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷum, iṉ mutaliya cāriyikaḷum puṇarumāṟum, eḻuttukkaḷuṭaṉ kāram mutaliya cāriyikaḷ puṇarumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. toki marapāṉatu mēl uyir mayaṅkiyal mutaliyavaṟṟil īṟukaṭōṟum virintu muṭiyum puṇarcci vitikaḷit tokuttu muṭikkiṉṟatu. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumiyum mēl vēṟṟumikkuk kūṟum muṭivu peṟātu tiripeyti niṟkumāṟṟiyum iḵtu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
urupiyalāṉatu peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷuṭaṉ puṇarum poḻutu cāriyip pēṟeytumāṟṟi virittuṇarttukiṟatu. uyirmayaṅkiyalāṉatu uyirīṟṟu moḻikaḷ niṉṟu perum pāṉmi vaṉkaṇattōṭum, ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇaṅ kaḷōṭum alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum puṇarumāṟṟi virittuṇarttukiṉṟatu. puḷḷimayaṅkiyalāṉatu meyyīṟṟu moḻikaḷ niṉṟu perumpāṉmi vaṉkaṇattōṭum, ciṟupāṉmi ēṉikkaṇaṅkaḷōṭum puṇarumāṟṟi virit tuṇarttukiṉṟatu. kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalāṉatu kuṟṟiya lukarattiṉ vakiyum, avi niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇaru māṟum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. oṉṟu mutalākiya kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyarkaḷ niṉṟu eṇṇup peyarōṭum piṟa peyar kaḷōṭum puṇarum muṟimiyi itu virittuṇarttu kiṉṟatu. eḻuttatikārattil niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumāka vittup puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ iviyeṉ pataṉiyum iḵtuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
collatikārattiṉ iyalkaḷ kiḷaviyākkam, vēṟṟumi yiyal, vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, viḷimarapu, peyariyal, viṉi yiyal, iṭiyiyal, uriyiyal, eccaviyal eṉpaṉa. kiḷaviyākka
māṉatu collum poruḷum irutiṇi aimpālākap pākupaṭumāṟum, cila tiṇi pāl iṭam marapu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaḻānili, vaḻuvamitikaḷum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. vēṟṟumi yiyalāṉatu eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum mutaliyaṉa kūṟi, viḷiyoḻinta ēṉi vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ poruḷ vikaṟpaṅ kaḷi virittuṇarttukiṉṟatu. vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyalāṉatu vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ tamporuḷiṟ ṟīrātu piṟavaṟṟiṉ poruḷiṟ ceṉṟu mayaṅkum poruṇ mayakkamum, tamporuḷiṟ ṟīrntu piṟavaṟṟiṉ poruḷiṟ ceṉṟu mayaṅkum urupu mayakkamum, viṉi nikaḻcciyiṉ eṭṭuk kāraṇaṅkaḷum, āku peyariṉ vakikaḷum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. viḷimarapāṉatu viḷiyiṉ potu vilakkaṇamum, uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar, viravup peyarkaḷil ivvīṟu ivvāṟu viḷiyēṟkumeṉpatum, viḷiyēlāp peyarkaḷ iviyeṉpatum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. peyariyalāṉatu nālvakic coṟkumuriya cila potu vilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, peyarccolliṉ pākupāṭum, ilakkaṇamum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. viṉiyiyalāṉatu viṉic colliṉ pākupāṭum, ilakkaṇamum, kālavaḻānili vaḻuvamitikaḷum, oru cār marapu vaḻuvamitiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. iṭiyiya lāṉatu iṭiccolliṉ pākupāṭum, ‘maṇ’ mutaliya iṭic coṟkaḷ poruḷ kuṟikkumāṟum, iraṭṭittu niṉṟu aci niliyāvaṉavum poruḷ kuṟippaṉavum iviyeṉpatum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uriyiyalāṉatu iciyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟi oru col pala poruṭku urimi yātalum pala col oru poruṭku urimiyātalum uṭiya uric coṟkaḷ palavum, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷum, coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ uṇarumāṟum, ataṉiyuṇarttu muṟimiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. eccaviyalāṉatu muṟkūṟiya eṭṭiyaluḷḷum uṇarttutaṟku iṭamiṉṟi eñci niṉṟa collilakkaṇaṅ kaḷit tokuttuṇarttukiṉṟatu. ceyyuṭkuriya iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭa col eṉpavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷum, valikkum vaḻivalittal mutaliya ceyyuḷ vikāraṅkaḷum, niraṉiṟi, cuṇṇam, aṭimaṟi, moḻimāṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷ kōḷkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, vēṟṟumit toki mutaliya aṟuvakit tokikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, pirinili mutaliya pattu vaki eccaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ muṭivukaḷum, cila viṉic coṟkaḷ aciniliyāmāṟum, cila vaḻānili vaḻuvamiti kaḷum, piṟavum iḵtu uṇarttukiṉṟateṉka.
poruḷatikārattiṉ iyalkaḷ akattiṇiyiyal, puṟat tiṇiyiyal, kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal, meyppāṭṭiyal, uvamaviyal, ceyyuḷiyal, marapiyal eṉpaṉa. akattiṇiyiya lāṉatu akattiṇi ēḻaṉ peyarum, akaṉintiṇiyiṉ mutal karu uripporuḷkaḷum, akapporuṭṭali makkaḷāvār ivareṉpatum, ōtaṟpirivu mutaliya pirivukaḷiṉ variyaṟikaḷum, naṟṟāy, tōḻi, kaṇṭōr, talivaṉ eṉpavarkkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷum, uḷḷuṟiyuvamamum, kikkiḷi peruntiṇi kaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. puṟattiṇiyiya lāṉatu  puṟattiṇi ēḻaṉ peyarum ilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṉ pakutiyākiya tuṟikaḷum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. pāṭāṇṭiṇiyil orupakutiyākak kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu vakikaḷi uṇarttukiṉṟatu. kaḷaviyalāṉatu kaḷavu iṉṉateṉpatum, talimakkaḷ etirp pāṭṭiṉ kāraṇamum, kāṭci mutaliyaṉa nikaḻumāṟum, iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum kaḷavuk kūṭṭam nāṉkum, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭa vakikaḷum, eṇvaki maṇamum akattiṇiyēḻaṉuḷ iṉṉavāṟu aṭaṅku meṉpatum, kaḷaveṉṉum kikōḷil talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, cevili eṉpōrkkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷum, kaḷavu veḷippaṭumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. kaṟpiyalāṉatu kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyi maṇattalākiya kaṟpiṉ ilakkaṇamum, muṉivar karaṇam yāttavāṟum, kaṟpeṉṉum kikōḷil talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, cevili, aṟivar, kūttar, pārppār mutaliyōrkkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷum, ōtaṟ pirivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kālavariyaṟiyum, talivaṉum taliviyum tuṟavaṟam nikaḻtti vīṭupeṟumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. poruḷiyalāṉatu akapporuṭkuriya vaḻuvamitikaḷ palavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. meyppāṭṭiyalāṉatu akattiṟkum puṟat tiṟkum potuvākiya eṇvaki meyppāṭukaḷum, avi ovvoṉṟu nāṉkāmāṟum, avi pōl vēṟu muppattiraṇṭu meyppāṭukaḷum, akapporuṭkēyuriya meyppāṭṭiṉ vaki kaḷum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uvamaviyalāṉatu uvamattiṉ ilakkaṇamum, vakiyum, nilikkaḷaṉum, cila uvama vikaṟpaṅkaḷum, uvamavurupukaḷum, talivi mutalāyiṉār iṉṉiṉṉaviṭattu uḷḷuṟiyuvamam kūṟaṟkuriyāreṉpatum, piṟavum uṇarttu kiṉṟatu. ceyyuḷiyalāṉatu nallicip pulavar ceyyuṭkaḷil taṉinilic ceyyuṭku uṟuppāka varum māttiri mutalāya irupattāṟum, perumpāṉmiyum toṭarnilic ceyyuṭku uṟuppāka varum ammi mutaliya eṭṭu vaṉappum, kūṟu mukat tāṟ ceyyuḷilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu. aṭivariyillāta aṟuvakic ceyyuḷum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. marapiyalāṉatu iḷamip peyar, āṇpāṟ peyar, peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷil iṉṉiṉṉavi iṉṉiṉṉavaṟṟukku uriyaveṉpatum, ōraṟivuyir mutaliya uyir vakikaḷum, pul, maram eṉpavaṟṟuḷ varum marapup peyarkaḷum, antaṇar mutaliyōrkkuriya cila muṟimikaḷum, iruvaki nūl, kāṇṭiki, uri eṉpavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷum, nūṟkuṟṟam pattum, utti muppattiraṇṭum uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyataṟku nīṇṭa nāḷiṉ muṉpē tamiḻ moḻiyum, tamiḻarum uyariya niliyil iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpataṟku atiṟ kāṇappaṭum ātaravukaḷ.
tolkāppiyattilē palaviṭattum ‘eṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉṟu muṉṉi yāciriyarkaḷiṉ tuṇivu kūṟappaṭutali nōkkiṉ ataṟku muṉpē tamiḻilakkaṇa nūl pala iruntiruttal tēṟṟam. ‘nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē’ ‘nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē’ eṉa varupavi pōlvaṉavaṟṟāl muṉṉi nūlāciriyar kaḷiṉ perumiyum pulaṉākum. ‘tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu’ eṉavum, ‘tōleṉa moḻipa toṉmoḻip pulavar’ eṉavum varuvaṉa tolkāppiyam tōṉṟutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē tamiḻ moḻiyum tamiḻāciriyarum ciṟantiruntami kāṭṭum. iṅkē kuṟikkap peṟṟōr ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷeṉpataṉiyum, ōr moḻiyil viḻumiya ilakkaṇan tōṉṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉ ataṟku muṉpē vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ciṟantiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum uṇmiyiṉiyum koṇṭu nōkkiṉ. tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu toṭakkam kaṇṭuṇaralākāta attuṇip paḻamiyuṭiyateṉpatu peṟappaṭum. tol kāppiyattiṟ payiṉṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, atu kūṟum ceyyuṭkaḷ, poruḷkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟi uyttuṇariṉum avi attakiya tiruntiya nili yeytutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum uṇmi pulaṉākum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat toṇṇūṟu, toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarkaḷik kāṇka. oṉpatu pattu toṇṇūṟu eṉavum, oṉpatu nūṟu toḷḷāyiram eṉavum āyiṉamikku āciriyar kūṟiyirukkum tiripu viti moḻinūliyaṟkiyōṭu poruntu vataṉṟāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟaravu uṇaralākāvāṟu avarkku neṭu nāḷiṉ muṉpē accoṟkaḷ annilimiyeyti viṭṭaṉa eṉpatu pulaṉāmaṉṟō? tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ paḻiya uyariya nili ivvārāyccikaḷiṉ aṟutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭumākaliṉ, īṇṭu iraṇṭu kuṟippukkaḷ māttiram eṭuttukkāṭṭuval. ‘pōnti vēmpē ār eṉa varūum, māperun tāṉiyar malinta pūvum’ ‘vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ’ eṉṉun toṭarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarkaḷiyum, avarkaḷuṭiya māli, tāṉi, nilam eṉpavaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. mūvēntar ‘vaṇpukaḻ mūvar’ eṉavum, tamiḻnilam ‘taṇpoḻil varippu’ eṉavum pukaḻnturikkappaṭṭiruttal cintikkaṟpālatu. vaṇpukaḻuṭiyareṉavē ataṟkuk kāraṇamāya koṭi aḷi ceṅkōl kuṭiyōmpal mutaliya iṟimāṭciyaṉittum uṭiyareṉpatum peṟappaṭum. oru nāṭṭiṉ araciyal tiruntiya niliyil iruppatu annāṭṭiṉatum, annāṭṭu makkaḷatum uyarvik kāṭṭum kuṟiyākum. ataṉāṟṟāṉ,
“	āṅkami veytiyak kaṇṇum payamiṉṟē vēntami villāta nāṭu”
eṉat teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum kūṟiṉar. cēkkiḻār, periyapurāṇattilē cōṇāṭṭiṉ ciṟappukaḷik kūṟivantu, muṭivil,
“	naṟṟamiḻ varippi ṉīṅku nāmpukaḻ tirunā ṭeṉṟum
poṟṟaṭan tōḷāl viyam potukkaṭin tiṉitu kākkum
koṟṟava ṉanapāyaṉ poṟ kuṭiniḻaṟ kuḷirvarutēṟā
maṟṟataṉ perumi nammāl varampuṟa viḷampa lāmō”
eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkum ceyyuḷiṉ karuttum īṇṭu aṟintu makiḻtaṟkuriyatu. vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa vētam mutalāya kali kaḷiyum, tamiḻ nūlkaḷaṉittiyum naṉkuṇarnta parimēlaḻakar tirukkuṟaḷuriyilē ‘cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṟāṟpōlap paṭippuk kālantoṭaṅki mēmpaṭṭu varuṅkuṭi’ eṉac ciṟappittōtiya mūvēntar pukaḻ kūṟappaṭavē tolkāp piyarkku muṉpu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uyarvum tāṉē peṟappaṭum eṉka. iṉi, oru nāṭṭiṉ uyarvellām annāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ kuṇavoḻukkac ciṟappēyākum. makkaḷuḷḷē makkaḷiṉ paṇpu aṟiyappaṭiṉ atu koṇṭē ēṉiyar paṇpum aṟiyap paṭṭatākum eṉpa. paṇṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭu makaḷir paṇpu eṅṅaṉam iruntateṉpatu
“	uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum
ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpuc ciṟan taṉṟeṉat
tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu”
eṉat tolkāppiyarum kūṟuvataṉāṟ pulappaṭum. piṟa paṇpukaḷum piṉṉark kāṭṭappeṟum. iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiliruntē tolkāppiyan tōṉṟutaṟku neṭunāḷiṉ muṉṉarē tamiḻum tamiḻarum uyarnili yeytiyiruntami aṅki nelliyeṉa naṉku viḷaṅkum.
eḻuttārāycciyum collārāycciyum
tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tokiyum muṟiyum uruvamum:
“	eḻutteṉappaṭupa
akaramutala
ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵ teṉpa
cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē”
eṉṉum tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttirattāl tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ toki muppattu mūṉṟu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. akara mutal oḷakāram īṟākavuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭum uyireḻut tukkaḷ. kakara mutal ṉakaram īṟākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭum mey yeḻuttukkaḷ. immuppateḻuttukkaḷiyum mutaleḻut teṉpa. kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṉum mūṉṟum cārpeḻuttukkaḷ. immūṉṟum uyirpōlat taṉittum meypōla akarattōṭu kūṭiyum iyaṅkutaliṉṟi oru moḻiyic cārntē varum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ cārpeḻut teṉappaṭṭaṉa. itu paṟṟiyē ivi tōṉṟu māṟṟi āciriyar moḻi marapiṉkaṇ kūṟiṉāreṉka. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpaṉa ikara ukaraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkamākaliṉ ivaṟṟi vēṟu kūṟa vēṇṭiya teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ivi muṉṉar oru māttiriyāy niṉṟu piṉṉar oru kāraṇampaṟṟi ari māttiriyākātu eṉṟum oru māttiriyāyē niṟkum oru taṉmiyiṉavākaliṉ ivaṟṟi vēṟu eḻuttukkaḷāka āciriyar vēṇṭiṉāreṉka. ivaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟṟikaram, kuṟṟukaram eṉṟu peyar kūṟiyoḻi yātu, ‘iyal’ eṉpatu cērttu ‘kuṟṟiyalikaram’, ‘kuṟṟiyalukaram’ eṉak kuṟippiṭṭiruttalum karutaṟpālatu. uyirmey, uyiraḷa peṭi, oṟṟaḷapeṭi, aikārak kuṟukkam, oḷakārak kuṟukkam, makarak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam eṉpavaṟṟi viṭuttuc cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉṟatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ kūṟuval. ‘āl’ eṉpuḻi uyirmuṉṉum meypiṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉāṟpōl ‘lā’ eṉpuḻiyum meymuṉṉum, uyir piṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉa vēyallatu’ uyirum meyyumākiya tam taṉmi tirintu vēṟāyiṉavalla: “meyyōṭiyiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā” eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷum ataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. tiṉi eṉpatu mey mutal uyirīṟṟuc col eṉavum, varaku eṉpatu uyirttoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉavum koḷ vataṉṟi, uyir mey mutal uyirmey īṟṟuc col eṉavum, uyir meyt toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukara meṉavum koḷḷāmiyuṅ kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl uyir mey ōreḻuttākāmi peṟappaṭum. aḷapeṭiyiraṇṭum oru kāraṇam paṟṟi nīṇṭolippaṉavē ākaliṉ avi vēṟeḻut tākā. aikārak kuṟukkam mutaliyaṉavum iyaṟkiyākak kuṟuki yicippaṉavalla vākaliṉ ōreḻuttākā. aṅṅaṉamākaliṉ, piṉṉuḷōr koṇṭa āytak kuṟukkameṉpatoḻittu ēṉiya vellāvaṟṟiyum āciriyar eṭuttōtiṉarāyiṉum avaṟṟi vēṟeḻuttākak koṇṭu cārpeḻutteṉa vēṇṭiṉārallar. vaṭanūlārum uyirmey, uyiraḷapeṭi, aikārak kuṟukkam mutaliyavaṟṟi vēṟeḻutteṉak koḷḷāmi yuṇarka. piṉvanta naṉṉūlār mutalāyiṉār eḻuttiṉiyalpi nōkkātu vēṟek kāraṇattāṉō avaṟṟiyum cārpeḻut teṉpārāyiṉar. iṉi oru cārār teṅku, iñci, vaṇṭu, pantu, cempiyaṉ eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōla melleḻutti aṭuttuḷḷa valleḻuttukkaḷiṉ ōcikkum, cekutta, pacitta eṉpavaṟṟil iyalpāka meṉmiyāy olikkum valleḻuttōci mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum vēṟu taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷ illāmiyāl tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkāṉatu eḻuttuk kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu eṉpar. kakaram mutaliya valleḻuttukkaḷ taṉittum, piṟaveḻuttuk kaḷōṭu kūṭi olikkum oliyum vevvēṟu iyalpiṉavāyiṉ piṉṉatu iyaṟkiyoliyaṉṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. mel leḻuttōṭu kūṭātē vēṟupaṭa olippatuṇṭāyiṉ atu ataṉip pēcum makkaḷiṉ cōrvu mutaliyavaṟṟāl uṇṭā vatēyām. avvoli evviṭattum oru peṟṟiyē nikaḻ vatākak koḷḷutal amiyātu. iṅṅaṉam piṟaveḻuttuk kaḷiṉ cērkkiyāleḻum ceyaṟkiyolikkum, cōrvu mutaliyavaṟṟāleḻum pōliyolikkum taṉittaṉi eḻuttukkaḷ amippatāyiṉ nāṭkunāḷ eḻuttukkaḷ varampiṉṟip palki marapunili tirintu muṉ muṉṉuḷḷa vaḻakkum ceyyuḷu mellām poruḷaṟiya vārāvāyk kaḻiyum eṉpataṉi aṟital vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum teṅku, iñci mutaliyavaṟṟiluḷḷa olikaḷ vēṟeḻuttukkaḷ vēṇṭāmalē yātoru iṭarp pāṭumiṉṟi iṉitiṉ iyaṉṟu varavum avaṟṟukku vēṟu eḻuttukkaḷ vakukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu poruttamākumō? tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku vakutta mutalāciriyar tamatu pēraṟiviṉ tiṟattāl tamiḻiluḷḷa olikaḷiṉ peṟṟiyi yellām nuṉittuṇarntu eḻuttukkaḷ vakutta aṭivi ōrntuṇarātu piṟar tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟellām eḻuttukkaḷi mikuttalum kuṟittalum ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvatu periyatōrēta mākumeṉka. tamiḻ meyyeḻuttukkaḷ vali, meli, iṭi yeṉa mūṉṟu iṉamāka, avi ovvoṉṟum orēyaḷavāka avvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷ peṟṟiruppatum, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiyalum, piṟavum nōkkiṉ avi paranta ārāycciyum ciṟanta kāraṇamum koṇṭu aṅṅaṉam vakukkap peṟṟaṉavā meṉpatu pōtarum.
iṉi, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ kiṭakki muṟi ippoḻutu pōṉṟē tolkāppiyar kālattum, ataṟku muṉṉum iruntatu. aṅṅaṉam iruntateṉpatu tolkāppiyattu nūṉmarapilum piṟappiyalilumuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi aṇukavittu nōkkiṉ iṉitu pulappaṭum. accūttiraṅkaḷ ‘eṉpa’ ‘eṉamoḻipa’ eṉa āciriyar tuṇivāka urittaliṉ tolkāppiyarkku muṉṉum aṅṅaṉamiruntami tēṟṟam. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku ammuṟiyāl eppoḻutu yārāl vakukkappeṟṟateṉa aṟutiyiṭutal aritu. eṉiṉum, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi vippum vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟivippum irumoḻi yiṉum ciṟappeḻuttoḻiya nōkkiṉ yātum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi amintiruttaliṉ, tamiḻ vaṭa moḻi iraṇṭum naṉkuṇarnta āciriyaroruvarāl oru moḻiyi nōkki maṟṟoru moḻik kaṇṇum ammuṟiyāṉē eḻuttukkaḷ amikkap peṟṟaṉavā meṉṟu karututal poruntum. eḻuttukkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟu vaḻaṅkutaṟkaṇṇum ‘pakāram makāram’ eṉṟāṟpōla iru moḻiyilum oṟṟumiyuḷatu. vaṭamoḻiyilē ‘talakāram’ eṉṟum, tamiḻilē ‘laḷaḵkāṉ’ eṉṟum iraṇṭeḻuttukkuc cāriyi yoṉṟē varutalum ciṟupāṉmiyuṇṭu.
vaṭamoḻiyi nōkkiyē tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ muṟip paṭuttap peṟṟaṉavāyiṉ āciriyar akattiyaṉārāl tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku aṅṅaṉam vakukkap peṟṟatāmeṉak karututalum iḻukkaṉṟu. āṉāl vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttukkaḷ tolkāp piyarkkum akattiyaṉārkkum muṉṉarum ammuṟiyāl vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉaveṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭiṉaṉṟi vaṭamoḻi nōkkit tamiḻeḻuttu muṟi koḷḷap peṟṟateṉal aṭātu. vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷi uṇarttum mākēccura cūttiram eṉpavaṟṟil uyireḻuttukkaḷiṉ kiṭakki muṟi māttiram ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkumāṟē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. mutalum īṟuṅ kūṭṭip pirattiyākarittu ‘ac’ eṉavum, ‘hal’ eṉavum vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷ peyariṭappaṭṭiruppatu pōlaṉṟi, tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ avaṟṟiṉiyalpāl uyir, mey eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ ottiruttal kaṇṭu uyir eṉavum, mey eṉavum peyariṭap paṭṭiruttali nōkkiṉ piṟarik kāṭṭiṉum viḻumiya tattuva uṇarvu cāṉṟōrāl tamiḻneṭuṅkaṇakku vakukkappeṟṟa tāmeṉal pōtarum ākaliṉ tamiḻi nōkkiyē vaṭaveḻuttu muṟi tōṉṟiṟṟāmeṉak karututalum ēṟpuṭittē. ivaṟṟuḷ yātu uṟutiyuṭitteṉpatu iru moḻiyiṉum vallunarāl ārāyntu veḷiyiṭaṟ pālatu. iṉi, tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kiṭakki muṟikkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttira viruttiyil civañāṉa muṉivarāl naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
iṉi, ivveḻuttukkaḷ oli vaṭivum vari vaṭivum eṉa iruvaki vaṭivuṭiyaṉa. cilar eḻuttukkaḷi uṇarveḻuttu, oliyeḻuttu, taṉmiyeḻuttu, uruveḻuttu eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum pākupaṭutturippar. ivaṟṟuḷ uṇarveḻut tāvatu civākamaṅkaḷil uṇarttappaṭum cūkkumi, picanti, mattimi, vikari eṉṉum nālvaki vākkuḷ mattimit tāṉattu uṇarcciyaḷavāka irukkum eḻutteṉṟum, oli yeḻuttāvatu taṉ cevikkē kēṭkum vikariyeṉṟum. taṉmi eḻuttāvatu piṟar cevikkuk kēṭkum oliyuṭiya vikari yeṉṟum, uruveḻuttāvatu cevippulaṉām oliyeḻutti aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāka ōviyam pōl eḻutappaṭuvatāmeṉṟum koḷka. ivaṟṟuḷ uṇarvu, oli, taṉmi eṉṉum mūṉṟum oliyeṉṟē koḷḷaṟpālaṉa. eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷ oliyeḻuttu eṉavum, uruveḻuttu eṉavum iru vakiyākum. oliyeḻuttāvatu uyiriṉ muyaṟciyāl untiyiluḷḷa utāṉaṉ eṉṉuṅ kāṟṟu neñcu, miṭaṟu, tali eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu nilipeṟṟu avaṟṟuṭaṉ pal, itaḻ, nā, mūkku, aṇṇam eṉṉum uṟuppuk kaḷuṭaṉ kūṭutalāl vēṟu vēṟu oliyākat tōṉṟuvatām. tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ yāvum iṅṅaṉam piṟakkumāṟu tol kāppiyattilē piṟappiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvoli vaṭivētāṉ nūlkaḷiṟ perumpālum eḻutteṉṟu vaḻaṅkap paṭuvatākum. uruveḻuttāvatu kīṟṟum puḷḷiyuṅ koṇṭu kaṭpulaṉākat tōṉṟumāṟu eḻutappaṭuvatām. oliyeḻuttiṉṟi itaṟku nilipēṟiṉmiyāṉum, iḵtiṉṟiyē oliyeḻuttiṟku iyakkamuṇṭākalāṉum, ōreḻuttolikkē vevvēṟu moḻiyāḷar vevvēṟu vaṭivamittuk koḷvarākalāṉum iḵtu attuṇic ciṟappuṭittaṉṟu. ataṉāṟṟāṉ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkum vaṭivu kūṟātu cilavaṟṟukkuc ciṟupāṉmi vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. nūṉ marapilē meykaḷum, ekara okaraṅkaḷum puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟum, makarattiṟku uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvākum eṉṟum, āytatti muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatum, meyyum uyirum kūṭiyaviṭattu avi tiriyātum tirintu niṟkumeṉap potup paṭak kūṟiyiruppatu maṉṟi, vēṟu vaṭivu kūṟappaṭāmi kāṇka. cilar kuṟṟiyalikara, kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaṭkum puḷḷiyuṇṭeṉpar. tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ cila avvāṟu karututaṟkum iṭañceytu niṟṟalē avar aṅṅaṉam kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākum. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟiṟkup puḷḷi uṇṭeṉṟu tuṇinturikkat tolkāppiyattilē pōtiya cāṉṟilli. āciriyar iṅṅaṉam ciṟupāṉmiyē vaṭivu kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, kūṟiyavaṟṟiliruntē akkālattil tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ vari vaṭivil eḻutap peṟṟu vantaṉa eṉṉum uṇmi peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. oliyeḻutti nōkka vaṭiveḻuttuc ciṟappilateṉṟu muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa tāyiṉum vaṭiveḻuttiṉ payaṉ ciṟiteṉṟu karutalākātu. moḻi vaḷarccikkum, ammoḻiyip pēcum makkaḷiṉ nākarika vaḷarccikkum vaṭiveḻuttuc ciṟanta karuviyākum. tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ oli vaṭivum, vari vaṭivum ‘eḻuttu’ eṉṉum oru peyarālēyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iccol ‘eḻu’ eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟantiruppiṉ olivaṭivik kuṟippatu poruntum. aṉṟēl eḻuttu variyaṟiyum avi eḻutappaṭutalum oruṅku tōṟṟiya kāraṇattāl vari vaṭivik kuṟikkum appeyarē oli vaṭiviṟkum āyiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. tol kāppiyattilē ‘antaratteḻutiya eḻuttiṉmāṉa’ eṉṉun toṭaril ‘eḻutiya’ eṉṉum viṉi koṭukkappaṭṭiruttaliṉ eḻutu eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiliruntu eḻuttu eṉṉum peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpatē peritum poruttamām.
iṉi, tamiḻ vaṭiveḻuttukkaḷiṉ varalāṟākap piṟar kūṟiyiruppaṉa cila ārāytaṟkuriyaṉa. moḻi nūl ārāycci yāḷarkaḷ paṇṭu kalveṭṭukkaḷilum ceppup paṭṭayaṅ kaḷilum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukku vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟu peyar koṭuttu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. airōppiyap pulavarkaḷuḷḷē ‘ṭākṭar kālṭuval’, ‘ṭākṭar pūlar’ mutaliya cilar tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ intiya nāṭu muḻutum vaḻaṅkiya pirāmi eṉṉum acōka eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu vantaṉavām eṉpar. tamiḻ ekara okaraṅkaṭkum ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭkum vaṭivu vēṟṟumiyillāmi avi paḻiya vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu vantaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum eṉṟu ‘kālṭuval’ kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. misṭar i. tāmas ‘ṭākṭar ris ṭēviṭs’, ‘ṭākṭar parṉal’ eṉṉum airōppiya āciriyarkaḷ intiyāvil vaḻaṅkum eḻuttukkaḷ evvitattilum āriyaruṭiyaṉavalla veṉṟum, ki.mu. 7 allatu 8vatu nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ varttakarkaḷāl intiyāvil vaḻaṅkac ceytaṉavākumeṉṟum kiṭittuḷḷa ellā ātaravukaḷum uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉkiṉṟaṉar. intu āriya ilakkaṇakkārarkaḷiṉ varukikku muṉpē tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku tiruntiya niliyil irun tateṉṟum, tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻuttu acōka eḻuttiṉiṉṟum vantataṉṟeṉṟum avarkaḷ uṟutiyāka moḻikiṉṟaṉar. ikkoḷkiyē tiru. cuvāmi vētācalam avarkaḷāl tamatu nūṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟil valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. tiru.em. cīṉivāciyaṅkāravarkaḷ ‘tamiḻārāyccikaḷ’ eṉṉum tam nūliṉ kaṇṇē vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻarkkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟi vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya acōka eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tiripalla veṉṟum, kālṭuvel kūṟukiṟapaṭiyaṉṟi ēkāra ōkāraṅ kaḷōṭu vēṟṟumi teriya ekara okaraṅkaḷukkup puḷḷiyuṇ ṭeṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟutalālum, pa,ma, eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷ pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷilum vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷilum vēṟupaṭṭiruttalālum kālṭuvel mutalāyiṉōr koḷki poruntātu eṉṟum, ki.mu. 15 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē tamiḻarkaḷ piṉīṣiyar, ekiptiyarkaḷuṭaṉ vāṇikattāl kalantiruttalāl ekiptiyaruṭiya eḻuttu muṟiyit tamiḻar annāḷilēyē koṇarntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, intiya āriyar ivveḻuttu muṟiyit tamiḻariṭamiruntu peṟṟi ruttal kūṭumeṉṟum kūṟi, tāmas,ṭēviṭs, parṉal eṉpavar kaḷiṉ koḷkiyē valiyuṭittāmeṉa vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. tiru.pā.vē. māṇikka nāyakkar avarkaḷ tamatu ‘tamiḻ maṟi viḷakkam’ eṉṉum nūlilē, vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ eṉpaṉa pallava maṉṉar kālattil cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷē vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum, avi acōkaṉuṭiya ‘pāli’ moḻi aṟintavarkaḷum, tamiḻ naṉku aṟiyātavarkaḷumāṉa kaṟṟaccarkaḷāl poṟikkap paṭṭamiyiṉ ciṟcila tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷukkup piratiyākap pāli eḻuttukkaḷik kalantu poṟittu viṭṭaṉareṉṟum, pallavar kālattukku muṉṉum piṉṉum uḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukkum vaṭṭeḻuttukkum toṭarpu illiyeṉṟum, ippoḻu tuḷḷa tamiḻiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ ki.mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muntiyatākiya tolkāppiyak kālantoṭaṅki oru peṟṟiyē uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyattiṟku muṉpu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉum peyarkku uriyavāyiṉum allavāyiṉum avi acōkaṉuṭiya pāli eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu uṇṭāṉaviyalla veṉpatu tēṟṟam. ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭaṅkiyē vaḻaṅki vanta pāli (pirāmi) eḻuttiṉiṉṟu ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa veṉṟu kūṟuvatu eṅṅaṉam poruntum? iṉi, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ pirāmi acōka eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu vantaṉavallavāyiṉum, avi tamiḻa rālēyē mutalil uṇṭākkap peṟṟaṉavō allatu cila airōppiya āciriyar mutalāyiṉār karutumāṟu vēṟṟu nāṭṭavarākiya ekiptiyariṭamiruntu aṟintu koḷḷap peṟṟaṉavō eṉpatu tuṇinturikkaṟpālataṉṟu. tamiḻar vēṟṟunāṭṭavariṭamiruntē eḻuttiṉi aṟintāreṉpataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ illāta variyil tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻar tāmākavē aṟintu uṇṭākkiyaṉa veṉṟu koḷvatē muṟiyākum.
iṉi, uyirmeykaḷiṉ vaṭivu vēṟṟumi kuṟittut tol kāppiya urikārarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppaṉa īṇṭu aṟitaṟkuriyaṉa. ‘uruvutirintuyirttal mēlum kīḻum vilaṅku peṟuvaṉa vilaṅku peṟṟuyirttalum, kōṭu peṟuvaṉa kōṭu peṟṟuyirttalum, puḷḷi peṟuvaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟuyirttalum, puḷḷiyum kōṭum uṭaṉē peṟuvaṉa uṭaṉ peṟṟuyirttalum eṉakkoḷka’ eṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟiṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya taṉōṭu mēlum itaṉi viḷakki ‘ki, kī’ mutaliyaṉa mēl vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ‘ku, kū’ mutaliyaṉa kīḻ vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ‘ke, kē’ mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa. ‘kā, ṅā’ mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyi ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyi vaḷitteḻutiṉār. ‘ko, kō, ṅō, ṅo’ mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyum kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟaṉa eṉa urittār. ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vaṭivu kāṭṭiyatu tamakku muṉṉirunta kalveṭṭukkaḷik koṇṭō piṟa ātaravukaḷik koṇṭō ātal vēṇṭum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiyiruppatiliruntu ‘kā’ mutaliyaṉa avar kālattiṟku muṉpu arukiṟ puḷḷi peṟṟum, avar kālattil kāl peṟṟum iyaṅkiyami pulaṉākum. ‘ke, kē’ mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl muṉpu nēr kōṭāka iruntatē piṉpu vaḷitteḻutappeṟṟatu eṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷ ‘ki ku’ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivu vēṟṟumi kāṭṭātu viṭṭataṉ kāraṇam pulappaṭavilli. ki mutaliyaṉa muṉpu irukōṭu peṟumeṉak koḷka. ivi ivvāṟākavē kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ paḻiya vaṭivu iṉṉum ārāyntu kāṇaṟpālatu.
iṉi, peyar, viṉi, iṭi, uri eṉat tamiḻccol nālvakippaṭum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
“	col leṉappaṭuva, peyarē viṉiyeṉ
ṟāyiraṇṭeṉpa aṟinticiṉōrē”
“	iṭic coṟ kiḷaviyum uric coṟkiḷaviyum
avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa”
eṉak kūṟiyiruppatu peyar viṉikaḷiṉ ciṟappiyum, iṭi urikaḷiṉ ciṟappiṉmiyiyum kāṭṭum. peyar viṉikaḷi yiṉṟi iṭiurikaṭku iyakkamiṉmi uṇarka. eṉiṉum avar,
“ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟit taṉavē” 
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu ‘acinili’ mutaliya iṭic coṟkaḷum poruḷuṇarccikku oruvāṟu tuṇiyāvaṉa eṉpati viḷakkum. peyarc collāvatu irutiṇi aimpāṟ poruḷi uṇarttum collām. payaṉili koḷḷaṟkum urupēṟṟaṟkum urittātalum, kālantōṟṟāmiyum peyarc colliṉ ilakkaṇamākum. uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar, viravup peyar eṉap peyarccol mūvakippaṭum. viṉic collāvatu poruḷiṉ puṭi peyarcciyākiya toḻili uṇarttum collām. toḻil nikaḻccikku viṉi mutal, karuvi, iṭam, kālam eṉpaṉa iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. vēṟṟumi koḷḷāmiyum, kālam tōṟṟalum viṉic colliṉ ilakkaṇamākum. kālamum ceyalum kuṟippiṟ peṟappaṭuvaṉa kuṟippu viṉiyum, veḷippaṭiyāka vuḷḷaṉa terinili viṉiyum ākum. 
iviyum uyartiṇi viṉi, aḵṟiṇi viṉi, potu viṉi eṉa mūvakippaṭum. iviyellām mūṉṟu kālamum, mūṉṟu iṭamum, aintu pālum paṟṟiyum, muṟṟu, peyareccam, viṉiyeccam eṉpaṉa paṟṟiyum palavākap pākupaṭumāṟuṅ kāṇka.
iṭic collāvatu peyarum viṉiyum iṭamāka avaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟum collām. iṭi eṉpatu iṭam eṉṉum poruṭṭu. iṉi, peyar viṉikaṭku muṉṉum piṉṉum varumāyiṉum perumpālum iṭiyē varutaliṉ iṭiccol eṉappaṭṭatu eṉpārum, peyarccol viṉiccoṟkaḷu mākātu avaṟṟiṉ vēṟumākātu iṭinikaratāy niṟṟaliṉ iṭiccollāyiṟṟu eṉpārum uḷar. iṭiccol cāriyiyum, viṉiyurupum, vēṟṟumiyurupum, aciniliyum, iciniliyum, kuṟippiṟ poruḷuṇarttuvatum, uvamavurupum eṉa eḻuvakippaṭum. kuṟippiṟ poruḷuṇarttutal ‘maṉ’ mutaliyaṉa ‘kaḻivu’ mutaliya poruḷiyuṇarttutal. naṉṉūlār poruḷuṇarttuvatum kuṟippum eṉa iraṇṭākak koṇṭu iṭiccolli eṇvakiyākkuvar. uriccollāvatu ici, kuṟippu, paṇpu eṉpavaṟṟi uṇarttum col. iḵtu oru poruḷuṇarttum oru collum, oru poruḷuṇarttum pala collum, pala poruḷuṇarttum oru collum eṉa mūvakippaṭum. naṉṉūlār ‘piṅkala mutalā nallō ruric coliṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu koṇṭu collellām uric collē eṉpar pirayōka vivēka nūlār. avar,
“	veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā veḷippaṭa vārā vuriccoṉmēṉa”
eṉṉum cūttirattiyum tamakkātaravākak koṇṭu ‘veḷip paṭavārāvuriccol’ eṉṟamiyāl ēṉiyaṉa veḷippaṭa vanta uriccolleṉpatu peṟṟām eṉpar. aṅṅaṉam kūṟuvatu, colleṉappaṭuvaṉa peyarum viṉiyumākiya iraṇṭumē eṉṟu avaṟṟic ciṟappittuk kūṟi iṭiccollum uric collum avaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟumeṉak kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukkum, ēṉiyāciriyaṉmār karuttukkum māṟākaliṉ pōliyeṉṟotukkaṟpāṟṟu.
iṉi uriccol eṉṉum peyarpporuḷ uṇart tuvār ici kuṟippu paṇpu eṉṉum poruṭku uriya collā kaliṉ uriccollāyiṟṟu eṉpārum, ceyyuṭkuriya collā kaliṉ uriccol eṉpārum, poruṭku urimi pūṇṭu varutaliṉ uriccol eṉpārum, poruṭkup paṇpu urimi pūṇṭu niṟṟaliṉ ataṉi uṇarttuñ col uriccol eṉappaṭṭateṉpārum āka urikārar pala tiṟattavarāvar. vaṭa nūlāciriyar ‘tātu’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa coṟkaḷē ivi yeṉpar teyvac ciliyār. ikkaruttu orucār porutta muṭittākum eṉka.
iṉi tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu mikka iṉimiyuṭiya teṉpatu palarum aṟintatoṉṟu. paṇṭināḷ toṭṭu vanta pulavar perumakkaḷ palarum itaṉ cuviyi nukarntu viyantu itaṉik kuṟikkumiṭamellām centamiḻ, pintamiḻ, vaṇṭamiḻ, oṇṭamiḻ, taṇṭamiḻ, meṉṟamiḻ, iṉṟamiḻ, tīntamiḻ, cuvittamiḻ, naṟuntamiḻ, ceḻuntamiḻ, naṟṟamiḻ, coṟṟamiḻ eṉṟiṅṅaṉamāka  aṭikoṭuttu vaḻaṅki varuvāyiṉar.
‘oṇṭīntamiḻ’ eṉa māṇikkavācakarum, ‘tēṉuṟi tamiḻ’ eṉak kallāṭarum, ‘cevviya maturañ cērnta naṟṟamiḻ’ eṉṟu tañcivāṇaṉ kōviyuṭiyārum kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam palarum iṉimiyuṭiyateṉap pārāṭṭutaṟkut tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ iṉiya ōci nalamum, iyaṟki neṟi vaḻuvāta cantiyamitiyum peritum kāraṇamātal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷellām urappalum kaṉittalum vēṇṭātu eḷiya muyaṟciyāl eḻuvaṉa. avaṟṟuḷḷum cilaveḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalil vārāmiyum, cilaveḻuttukkaḷ iṟutiyil vārāmiyum, cilaveḻuttuṭaṉ cilaveḻuttu mayaṅki vārāmiyum nōkkiṉ tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ ākkappāṭu arumuyaṟciyiṉṟi kūṟat takkavāṟu iyaṟki muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉaveṉpatu pulap paṭum. taṉi meykaḷum, uyir meykaḷum ṭakaram mutaliyaṉavum mutalil variṉum, valloṟṟukkaḷ iṟutiyil variṉum, mayaṅkā veṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ mayaṅkumāṟu niṟpiṉum avi meṉmic cevviyuṭiya nāviṟku varuttam viḷittal kaṇkūṭu. māṇavarkaḷ prapu, ṭampam, tvok, utpatti eṉpavaṟṟi ōtik kāṇka.
iṉi iccoṟkaḷē iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol, vaṭacol eṉa nālvakippaṭum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
“	iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacoleṉ ṟaṉittē ceyyu ḷīṭṭac collē”
eṉṉum eccaviyaṟ cūttirattāl tamiḻcceyyuṭkaḷukkuriyaṉa innālvakic collum eṉṟami kāṇka. peyar viṉi iṭi uri eṉṉum nāṉkum iṅṅaṉam pākupaṭum eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar karuttākum. peyar viṉi iṭi uri eṉpaṉa iyaṟcolliṉ pākupāṭu eṉpatum, tiricollum ticic collum vaṭacollum perumpāṉmi peyarāyum ciṟupāṉmi viṉiyākavum varum eṉpatum cēṉāvariyar, teyvac ciliyār, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṉṉum uriyāḷarkaḷiṉ karuttām. ivarkkuḷḷum ciṟitu vēṟṟumiyuṇṭu. ataṉi uri nōkki yuṇarka. iyaṟcollāvatu, centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkuṭaṉ poruntit tam poruḷ vaḻuvāmal icikkum collām. eṉavē yāvarum eḷitil poruḷuṇarum centamiḻ nāṭṭuc col iyaṟcol eṉappaṭum eṉka. itaṉi, centamiḻ nilattum koṭuntamiḻ nilattum oppa vaḻaṅkum col leṉṟum, iyalpākiya colleṉṟum kūṟuvar. itaṉic ceñcol eṉiṉum amiyum eṉpar teyvacciliyār. tiri collāvatu iyaṟcolliṉ vēṟupaṭa varuñcol. oru poruḷ kuṟitta pala collākiyum pala poruḷ kuṟitta oru collākiyum aritiṟ poruḷuṇarappaṭum collē tiricol eṉka. iyaṟcollē tirintu tiri collākum eṉpatum, kiḷi kiḷḷi yeṉṟāṉatu uṟupputtirintatum, tatti eṉṟāṉatu muḻuvatum tirintatum ām eṉpatum cēṉā variyar karuttu. iḷampūraṇar karuttum itupōlumeṉak koḷḷak kiṭakkiṟatu. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar colliṉ tirivi oruvāṟu koṇṭārāyiṉum, muḻuvatum tirittal eṉpataṉip  piṟa coṟkoṇarntu tirittal eṉpar. iyaṟ colliṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvateṉpatē piṟa āciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttākum. kiḷi eṉṉum collē tatti eṉṟum, mali eṉṉum collē veṟpu eṉṟum tirintaṉa veṉpatu tamiḻiyalukku muṟṟilum māṟākiyāl cēṉāvariyar uri koḷḷaṟpālataṉṟākum. ticic collāvatu centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻntu kiṭanta paṉṉiru nilattumuḷḷār karutiya poruḷiyuṭiyatākum. ellā nāṭṭiṟkum potuvātaliṉṟik koṭuntamiḻ nāṭu ovvoṉṟaṟkēyuriya col ticic collām eṉka. vaṭa collāvatu vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tamiḻiṟ koḷḷappaṭuñ col. aḵtu iru moḻikkum potuvāṉa eḻuttālāyatum, vaṭamoḻikkēyuriya eḻuttuc citintu varuvatum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. itaṟku āriyac coṟ pōlum eṉak kūṟiya iḷampūraṇaruri poruntuvataṉṟu. iṉi centamiḻ nilam yātu, centamiḻic  cērnta paṉṉiru nilam yāvi eṉa ārāytal vēṇṭum. iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, nacci ṉārkkiṉiyar mūvarum centamiḻ nilamāvatu viyi yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum maruta yāṟṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkum uḷḷa nilam eṉpar. ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam koṇ ṭamikku yātum ātāram kāṭṭavilli. nāmum kaṇṭa tilli. ivvuriyāḷarkaḷ kālattiṟku ivvariyaṟi poruttamuṭittup pōlum! kaṭiccaṅka nāḷilum, piṉvanta cēkkiḻār, kampar mutalāṉōr nāṭkaḷilum pāṇṭināṭē centamiḻ nāṭeṉak koḷḷap peṟṟateṉṟu ‘kapilar’ eṉṉum uri nūlil pala cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. āciriyar tolkāppiyar nāḷilē kiḻakkum mēṟkum kaṭalum, vaṭakkum teṟkum vēṅkaṭamum, kumariyum elliyāka vuṭiya nilamuḻutum centamiḻ nāṭātal vēṇṭum.
“	vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
āyiṭit tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi”
eṉappāyirattum,
“	vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
nāṟpeya relli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉac ceyyuḷiyaliṉum varutal itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum. eṉavē paṉṉiru nilam eṉpaṉa itaṟkup puṟampākavuḷḷa tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷātal vēṇṭum. iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar mutalāyiṉār poṅkar nāṭu, oḷināṭu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, cītanāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu, maliyamāṉāṭu, aruvānāṭu, aruvāvaṭatali eṉpaṉa paṉṉiru nilam eṉpar. itu piṟkālattiṟkup poruntumāyiṉum tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkup poruntuvatākātu. aṟṟi nāḷil intiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭa pakutiyil āriyamaṉṟip piṟa moḻikaḷum, teṉ pakutiyil tamiḻaṉṟip piṟa moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅkiyiruppiṉ āriyattiṟkē vaṭamoḻi eṉṟum, tamiḻukkē teṉmoḻi eṉṟum peyar vaḻakkuṇṭākak kāraṇamilli. aṉṟiyum, ciṅkaḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku mutaliyaṉa ap
poḻutu taṉi moḻikaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppiṉ tolkāppiyar ticic coṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟumiṭattil ammoḻikaḷi viṭuttu, ‘centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum taṅkuṟippiṉa’ eṉṟu māttiram kūṟutal poruttamuṭittaṉṟu. ātaliṉ vaṭakkē āriyattiṉ citivukaḷum, teṟkē tamiḻiṉ citivukaḷum taṉi moḻikaḷiṉ nilimiyi aṭiyāmalē akkālattil vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉṟu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. yāṉ kūṟuvatē teyvac ciliyārkkum vēṟucilarkkum karutteṉpatu avaruriyāl aṟiyappaṭum. ‘vēṅkaṭamaliyiṉ teṟkum, kumariyiṉ vaṭakkum, kuṇa kaṭaliṉ mēṟkum, kuṭa kaṭaliṉ kiḻakkumākiya nilam centamiḻ nilam eṉpa’ ‘paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum , tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teliṅkum kaliṅkamum eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum’ eṉṉum avvurippakutikaḷ kāṇka. itaṟku mēṟkōḷākaavvuriyiṟ koḷḷap peṟṟatoru cūttirappakuti naṉṉūl mayili nātar uriyilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu, 
“	kaṉṉit teṉkarik kaṭpaḻan tīvam
ciṅkaḷam kollam kūviḷa meṉṉum
elliyiṉ puṟattavum īḻam pallavam
kaṉṉaṭam vaṭuku kaliṅkam teliṅkam
koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟam
eṉpaṉa kuṭapāl irupuṟac ciyat
tuṭaṉuṟipu paḻakun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum
muṭiyuṭi mūvarum iṭunila vāṭci
aracumēm paṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭikaḷ
patiṉmarum uṭaṉirup piruvarum paṭitta
paṉṉiru ticiyiṟ coṉṉaya muṭiyavum”
eṉpatu; itaṉ piṉ ‘eṉṟār akattiyaṉār’ eṉṟu kūṟiyirut taliṉ iḵtu akattiyac cūttiramē eṉak koḷḷap peṟṟami pulaṉākum. ippaṉṉiru nilaṅkaḷum muṭiyuṭi vēntar mūvariṉ āṇi vaḻi kuṟunila maṉṉar patiṉmarālum ēṉi iruvarālum āḷap peṟṟaṉa veṉa iccūttiram kūṟuvatum karutaṟpālatu.
kallāta ciṟuvar pēcciluḷḷa cantiyum, tamiḻilakkaṇaṅ kūṟum cantiyum oṉṟēyākaliṉ iyaṟkiyiṉ vaḻuvāmalē  tamiḻ nūlōr cantikaḷi amittuḷḷaṉar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. utāraṇamāka māvili eṉa vakaravuṭampaṭumey peṟuvatum, vāḻiyili eṉa yakaravuṭampaṭumey peṟuvatum, maravēr eṉa makaravoṟṟukkeṭuvatum, vāḻikkāy eṉak kakaravoṟṟu mikuvatum, puḷiyaṅkāy eṉa amcāriyi peṟṟu makaram ṅakaramāyt tirivatum tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟumāṟē ciṟuvar pēccil amintiruttal kāṇka. poruḷuṇarttu muṟi yilum tamiḻc coṟkaḷ mikac ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. tamiḻiluḷḷa viṉic coṟkaḷ tiṇi, pāl, eṇ, iṭam, kālam, ceyal eṉpavaṟṟi oruṅku viḷakkutal pōl vēṟu emmoḻic coṟkaḷ viḷakkuvaṉavākum? paṭittāṉ eṉṉum viṉi muṟṟilē pakutiyāṟ ceyalum, iṭiniliyāṟ kālamum, vikutiyāl tiṇi, pāl, eṇ. iṭaṅkaḷum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutal kāṇka. tiru. celvak kēcavarāya mutaliyār em.ē. avarkaḷ tām eḻutiya ‘tamiḻ’ eṉṉuṅ kaṭṭuriyil āṅkilat tōṭu tamiḻi oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyiruppataṉ ōr pakuti piṉ varuvatu. ‘nām vantilaṉ, ceṉṟilaṉ eṉa oru collāy vaḻaṅkum etirmaṟi muṟṟukkaḷi āṅkilattil urippataṟku mūṉṟu coṟkaḷ vēṇṭum. teriniliyum kuṟippumāṉa ovvoru viṉiyālaṇiyum peyariyum āṅkilattil pala coṟkaḷiṉṟi amikkalākātu. ‘vantāy’kku eṉpati vantava ṉākiya uṉakku eṉap poruḷ paṭuvatāy nāṉku coṟkaḷāl amikka vēṇṭum. aṭiyēṉukku eṉṉum kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyari amikka āṟu coṟkaḷ vēṇṭum. nammuṭiya peyarecca viṉiyeccaṅkaḷum coṟ curuṅkutaṟku anukūlamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. vanta maṉitaṉ eṉpati amikka avarkaḷukku nāṉku coṟkaḷ vēṇṭum. avaṉ vantu pōṉāṉ eṉṉum oru vākkiyatti avarkaḷ iraṇṭu vākkiyamiṉṟi urikka iyalātu. avarkaḷukku vēṟṟumit toki, viṉit toki, uvamit toki, aṉmoḻit tokikaḷ illi.’ itiliruntu tamiḻc coṟkaḷum coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷum curuṅkiya vuruvil virinta poruḷik koṇṭirukkum ciṟappu naṉku pulaṉākum.
collamiti, puṇarcci, vēṟṟumi mutaliyavaṟṟil tamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum vevvēṟu iyalpiṉavātal:
ulakil vaḻaṅkum ellā moḻikkum āriya moḻiyē tāyākumeṉṟum, ammuṟiyē tamiḻum āriyattiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatākumeṉṟum koḷḷum ārāyntu tuṇiyāta koḷkiyiṉiyuṭiyār cilar ciṉṉāḷ muṉpu toṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar, moḻiyārāycci eṉpatu putiya kali muṟikaḷil oṉṟāki moḻikaḷiṉ uṇmit taṉmiyi viḷakki varum innāḷil akkoḷki arukiviṭṭatēṉum uṇmiyuṇarātār cilar iṉṉumiruttaliṉ avar poruṭ ṭāka vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻikaḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa iyalpiṉi viḷakkutal kaṭaṉākiṉṟatu. moḻi nūṟpulavarkaḷ moḻikaḷi avvavaṟṟiṉ paṇpukaḷ koṇṭu vevvēṟu iṉam allatu kuḻuuvākap pākupaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. pakutikaḷē coṟkaḷāka allatu collellām ōraciyēyākavuḷḷa iṉam (Isolating) oṉṟu. pakutiyuṭaṉ vikuti mutaliyaṉa oṭṭiniṉṟu, ataṉāṟ colliṉ urupu tiriyātirukkum iṉam (Agglutinative) oṉṟu. vikuti mutaliyaṉa kūṭutalāl   colliṉ uruvantirintum māṟutalaṭiyum iṉam (Inflectional)  oṉṟu. moḻikaḷ palavum im mūṉṟiṉattilē aṭaṅkāniṟkum. cīṉam mutaliyaṉa mutal iṉattiyum, tamiḻ mutaliyaṉa iraṇṭāvatu iṉittiyum, vaṭamoḻi mutaliyaṉa mūṉṟāvatu iṉattiyum cērntavi. tamiḻāṉatu  ciṟupāṉmi mutal iṉattiyum mūṉṟām iṉattiyum ottirukkumāyiṉum perumpāṉmi ovvāmi yuṭiyatu. moḻikaḷ oru nilimiyiliruntu maṟṟoru nilimiyi oru kālattil eytu meṉpataṟkuriya iyipum cāṉṟum iṉmiyāl immūviṉamum ekkālattum mūvēṟu taṉittaṉmikaḷi uṭiyaṉavēyākum. inta aṭippaṭiyāṉa vēṟupāṭṭiliruntē tamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum oṉṟiyoṉṟu cārntirāta taṉimoḻikaḷ eṉpatu naṉku pulappaṭum. maṟṟu ivviru moḻikaḷumilakkaṇa vakiyāl eṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum kāṇaṟpālatu.
tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumikaḷik kūṟuṅkāl ivviru moḻikkum oṟṟumiyē illiyeṉak karutuvatākak koḷḷalākātu. tiruttamuṟṟa moḻikaḷ pala vaṟṟilum potuvāka amintirukkak kūṭiya paṇpukaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. uyir mey, peyar viṉi, āṇpāl, peṇpāl, orumi, paṉmi, eḻuvāy, payaṉili, mūviṭam, mukkālam pōlvaṉa ellā moḻikaṭkum potuviyalpukaḷām. tamiḻ vāṇarum vaṭamoḻivāṇarum neṭuṅkālamākak kalantu vāḻtalāl akkalappup paṟṟiyum ivviru moḻik kaṇṇum otta paṇpukaḷ cila amital iyaṟkiyē. moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟukku avaṟṟiṉ potuviyalkaḷiṉum ciṟappiyalpukaḷē mikka valimiyuṭiyaṉa vākum. ākaliṉ tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalkaḷ iviyeṉpatu kāṭṭappaṭiṉ atukoṇṭu aḵtu ōr uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyāmeṉpatu pōtarum.
muṉpu, tolkāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintiram  eṉpār kūṟṟi maṟutturitta vaḻiyum civañāṉa muṉivar tol kāppiyap pāyiraviruttiyalil tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷi viḷakki ataṉi maṟutturittamiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭiya vaḻiyum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ māṇpukaḷi viḷakka nērnta vaḻiyum tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ pala pōntaṉa. mēlē potu vakiyāṟ kūṟiyaṉavum, kūṟātaṉavumāya cila ciṟappiyalpukaḷ iṅkē viḷakkappaṭum.
āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷi uyartiṇi eṉṟum, ēṉit tāḻnta uyirkaḷiyum uyirillāvaṟṟiyum aḵṟiṇi eṉṟum pākupāṭu ceytiruppatu tamiḻilaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻi mutaliya vēṟemmoḻiyilum illi. eḻuttukaṭku uyir, mey eṉap peyariṭṭiruppatu pōṉṟu iḵtum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tattuva vuṇarvu mēmpāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟanta cāṉṟākum. pāl vakuppu muṟiyilum tamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭavi. tamiḻ muṟiyāṉatu poruṇōkkattāl āṇi āṇ eṉṟum peṇṇip peṇ eṉṟum kūṟum ciṟappuṭiyatu. vaṭamoḻi muṟiyō coṉṉōkkaṅkoṇṭu āṇip peṇṇākavum, peṇṇi āṇākavum, iraṇṭiyum aliyākavum, uyirillāta poruṭkaḷiyum āṇ peṇṇāka vum piṟaḻak kūṟum kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu. oru poruḷē col vēṟṟumiyāl āṇākavum, peṇṇākavum aliyākavum māṟuvatum oru poruḷik kuṟitta oru collē aṭi moḻiyāka varuṅkāl āṇ peṇ aliyāka māṟuvatum ākiya kuṟikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷaṉa. ulakattup poru ḷellām cāttuvikam irācatam tāmatam eṉṉum kuṇaṅ kaḷōṭu kalantaṉavākaliṉ cāttuvikamuḷḷatu āṇākavum, irācatamuḷḷatu peṇṇākavum, tāmatamuḷḷatu aliyākavum, oru poruḷē akkuṇaṅkaḷiṉ mikuti kuṟi oppup paṟṟi āṇ peṇ aliyākavum kūṟappaṭum eṉṉum vaṭa nūlār kūṟṟum ārāyuṅkāl uṇmiyuṭitteṉat tōṉṟavilli. takkiṇam eṉṉum ticip peyarāṉatu ataṉāl vicēṭikkappaṭṭataṟ kēṟpat takkiṇa tēcam eṉpuḻi āṇpālum, takkiṇāmūrtti eṉpuḻip peṇpālum ākumeṉiṉ, tēcam cāttuvikamākavum mūrtti irācatamākavum kōṭaṟkuriya iyiputāṉ eṉṉiyō? tamiḻ nūlkaḷilē ‘teṉṟalañ celvaṉ’ ‘nāṇeṉṉu nallāḷ’ eṉṟiṅṅaṉam uyiril poruḷum kuṇamum mutalāyiṉ āṇ pālākavum peṇpālākavum kūṟappaṭutal vaṭanūl muṟi yaṉṟō veṉiṉ, ivi uruvaka mātaliṉ vaṭanūl muṟiyā māṟu yāṅṅaṉam eṉka. ‘kaṅkiyāḷ’ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum virittu uruvakamākkutal vēṇṭum. ciṟupāṉmi vaṭanūṉmuṟi taḻuviṟṟumām. 
tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷum nūlkaḷum
ceyyuḷ eṉpatit tolkāppiyar potup peyarākak koṇṭirukkiṟār. pāṭṭukkum urikkum cūttirattukkum akkālattil ceyyuḷeṉṟē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantatu. pāṭṭu eṉpataṟkup pā eṉappaṭuvatē ciṟappup peyarākum. avar uṟuppukkaḷ 34 eṉavum pirittirukkiṟār. ceyyuḷ yāvum poruḷ ilakkaṇattukku ilakkiyamāṉavi. māttiri eṉpatum avvuṟuppukaḷil oṉṟu. ceyyuḷil toki nilic ceyyuḷ eṉṟum toṭarnilic ceyyuḷeṉṟum pākupāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. ‘puṟanāṉūṟu’ toki nilic ceyyuḷākum. ‘cīvaka cintāmaṇi’ toṭarnilic ceyyuḷākum. tolkāppiyattil kūṟiyuḷḷa acikkum, maṟṟa nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa acikkum vittiyācamirukkiṟatu. tolkāppiyar ‘nāṟ cīr koṇṭatu aṭiyeṉappaṭumē’ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.ittaṉi eḻuttukkaḷ mutal ittaṉi eḻuttuvariyuḷḷatu iṉṉiṉṉa aṭiyeṉṟum avar vakuttirukkiṟār. 4 mutal 6 vari uḷḷatik kuṟaḷaṭiyeṉṟum, 7 mutal 9 variyuḷḷati cintaṭiyeṉṟum,   10 mutal 14 variyuḷḷati nēraṭiyeṉṟum, 15 mutal 19 variyuḷḷati neṭilaṭiyeṉṟum 18 mutal 20 variyuḷḷati kaḻi neṭil eṉṟum pirittirukkiṟār. itutāṉ kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi eṉappaṭuvatu. inta kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi eṉpatu kaṭiccaṅka kālattilēyē illātu pōyviṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. itaṉip pērāciriyar mutaliyōr taṅkaḷ uriyil viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. 
oru kālattil vaḻakkattiliruntu piṟaku maṟinta oru kāriyattip piṟkālattilum vaḻakkiliruppatu pōlak kūṟuvatu kūṭāteṉa āciriyar ‘marapu’ eṉṟa nuṇukkattālē kuṟippiṭṭiruk kiṟār. utāraṇamāka taḻi eṉpatu muṟkālattil kiyuṟiyākak koṇṭu koṭukkum oruvita taḻiyālāṉa uṭi. ippōtu anta taḻi koṭukkum vaḻakkamilli. ippaṭiyirukka ikkālattilum taḻi koṭuttatāka eṅkāvatu colluvōmāṉāl atu tavaṟākum. tūraviruntu oruvar pāṭukiyil atu ettaṉmiyāṉa pāveṉak kaṇṭaṟivatē pā eṉṟa uṟuppiṉ laṣaṇameṉac collappaṭukiṟatu. pā eṉṟa col uṟuppiṉ peyarāka viruppattōṭu ceyyuḷiṉ peyarākavumirukkiṟatu. pākkaḷi āṟuvakiyākap pākupāṭu ceytirukkiṟār. ceyyuḷil iruvaki uṇṭeṉavum, avaṟṟil oṉṟu aṭi variyaṟi uḷḷateṉṟum, maṟṟoṉṟu aṭivariyaṟiyaṟṟateṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. nūl eṉpatu aṭivariyaṟi illātatu. paṇṭināḷil nūl eṉṟatu ilakkaṇam eṉṟa oṉṟi maṭṭum kuṟittatu. ippōtu nām ellāvaṟṟiyum nūleṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟōm. akkālattil ceyyuḷ eṉṟa peyarāl maṟṟavi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
nūlukku aṭivariyaṟi illātatu pōlavē cūttirattukkum aṭivariyilli, urikkum illi.
ceyyuṭkaḷi āciriyar 7 vitamākap pākupāṭu ceytirukkiṟār. vaṇṇam eṉṟa oru uṟuppi maṭṭum 20 vitamākap pirittirukkiṟār. vallici vaṇṇam mellici vaṇṇam mutaliyaṉa antap pākupāṭṭil aṭaṅkiyavikaḷē. itu ōcimuṟiyik kuṟikkiṟatu. itiliruntu ippōtu ( Rhetoric ) eṉṟu collap paṭum ōcimuṟi akkālattum iruntatākat terikiṟatu. nūli, mutal vaḻiyeṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭuttik kūṟiyirukkiṟār.
naṉṉūlār cārpunūl eṉṟum oṉṟik kuṟikkiṟār. maṟṟum cilar etir nūl eṉṟa oṉṟiruntatākavum collu kiṉṟārkaḷ. cārpunūl eṉṟa oṉṟiruppatākak koḷḷuvatu tavaṟeṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟukiṟār. moḻi peyarppum vaḻinūlākavē koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.
oru poruḷōṭu maṟṟoru poruḷi oppumi kūṟuvatu uvamamākum. itu veḷippaṭi uvamameṉṟum uḷḷuṟi uvameṉṟum pirikiṟatu. uḷḷuṟi uvamam perumpālum ceyyuḷukkum akapporuḷukkumē uriyatu. paṇṭināḷil caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil uvamiyi mikap poruttamākac colliyirukkiṟārkaḷ.
oru āciriyar naṇṭiṉ kaṇkaḷukku uvami kūṟukiyil “ vēppunaṉiyaṉṉa neṭuṅkaṭkaḷvaṉ” eṉak kūṟi naṇṭiṉ kaṇkaḷukku vēppampūvi uvamiyākak kūṟukiṟār. uḷḷuṟi uvamiyiṉāl nām akkāla makkaḷiṉ nākarīka mēmpāṭṭi uṇarntu koḷḷalām.
meyppāṭu eṉṟatu uḷḷa nikaḻcci puṟattilē veḷip paṭṭut tōṉṟuvatik kāṭṭuvatu. itu eṭṭu vakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. meyppāṭṭiyal maṉōtattuva cāstiram pōṉṟirukkiṟatu.
marapu eṉpatil āciriyar iḷamip peyar, āṇpāṟ peyar, peṇpāṟ peyar eṉa mūṉṟu pirivukaḷ ceytu koṇṭu iṉṉiṉṉavaṟṟi iṉṉiṉṉavāṟu kūṟavēṇṭumeṉa virivāka ārāyntirukkiṟār.
iḷamip peyar - pārppu, kuṭṭi, kuḻavi, kaṉṟu, kuruḷi mutaliyaṉa. āṇpāṟ peyar;- ēṟu, kaḷiṟu, cēvu, cēval mutaliyaṉa; peṇpāṟ peyar- pēṭi, peṭṭi, piṭimutaliyaṉa. kuḻavi eṉpatu kuñcarattiṉ iḷamip peyar eṉa ataṟkut taṉi cūttiram colliyirukkiṟār. ataṉāl avvaḻakku atikamāka akkālattil pirayōkattiliruntirukka vēṇṭum. āvukkum erumikkum avvitamē collavēṇṭumeṉṟum kūṟiyirukkiṟār. ippōtu palar vinōtamāka niṉikkakkūṭiya cila viṣayaṅkaḷum āciriyarāl itu cammantamākac collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṭṭiṉ āṇi appar eṉac collavēṇṭumeṉṟum, nariyiṉ peṇṇi narippāṭṭi eṉac colla vēṇṭu meṉṟum kūṟiyirukkiṟār. 
marapiyalil uyirkaḷip palavitap pākupāṭu ceytu makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟivuyirē eṉṟum kuṟittirukkiṟār.
tāvaravarkkatti maram pul eṉa iraṇṭu vitamākap pirittu teṉṉi, paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟip pulliṉattil cērttup puḷiya maram mutaliyavaṟṟi marattiṉattil cērttum kūṟiyirukkiṟār eṉa muṭittār.
poruḷārāycci (maṇamuṟikaḷ)
tamiḻariṉ maṇamuṟi
nāṭṭār ip piracaṅkattil paṇṭi nāḷit tamiḻariṉ nākarīkac ciṟappukaḷip paṟṟi vivarittār. mutalil avar tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattiliruntu tamiḻariṉ maṇamuṟiyi ārāya ārampittār.
poruḷatikārattilē āciriyar akapporuḷiyalip paṟṟiyum puṟapporuḷiyalip paṟṟiyum naṉku ārāyntu pala viṣayaṅkaḷ colliyirukkiṟār. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷilē akapporuḷē mikuntirukkiṉṟatu. tolkāppiyarum akap poruḷip paṟṟiyē mutalil kūṟiyirukkiṟār. ulakiyal eṉpatu mūṉṟu vakippaṭum.avi: iṉpam, poruḷ, aṟam eṉap piriyum. nālvakip puruṣārttaṅkaḷik kūṟum vaṭamoḻi nūlār aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉa aṟatti mutalil vittirukka tamiḻ nūl vallārkaḷ maṭṭum iṉpatti mutalil vittiruppataṉ kāraṇatti caṟṟu kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. makkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi ulakiluḷḷa jīvarācikaḷaṉittumē iṉpattittāṉ virumpukiṉṟaṉa. vāḻkkiyiṉ muṭivē iṉpamākum. poruḷum aṟamum iṉpattip peṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷākavirukkiṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya iṉpattip pōl avi payaṉāka māṭṭā. iṉpamākiya payaṉiyaṭiya poruḷum aṟamum tuṇiyāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ātalāltāṉ tamiḻ vallār iṉpatti mutalil vittirukka vēṇṭum. iṅkum iṉpam eṉa āciriyar colvatu ivvulaka iṉpattiyēyākum. vīṭu allatu vīṭṭiṉpam eṉpatu ulakiyal mūṉṟiyum viṭṭa niliyākum. aimpulaṉ koṇṭum oruṅkē nukarum taliyāya iṉpattiyē āciriyar akapporuḷiṉkaṇ viḷakkiṉār. maṉamottu kātalikkum oru āṇmakaṉiyum peṇ makaḷiyum āciriyar uyarnta oḻukkattiṉarākavum mikkac ciṟantavarākavum kuṟittirukkiṟār. avarkaḷukkut talimakaṉ - talimakaḷ eṉap peyariṭṭaḻittirukkiṟār. peyariṭṭirukkum pāṉmiyi liruntē avarkaḷi ellāvaṟṟilum atāvatu naṭi, kuṇam mutaliya ellāvaṟṟilum ciṟantavarākavē āciriyarkaḷ koṇṭiruk kiṟārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. kiḻavaṉ - kiḻatti eṉṟum kātalar aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. māṟāta urimi uṭiyavarkaḷ eṉpatik kāṭṭavē ivvitam peyariṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
talivaṉum taliviyum oruvari oruvar kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. kaṇṭavuṭaṉē oruvari oruvar kātalikka ārampikkiṉṟaṉar.ituvē taliyaṉpākum. talivaṉ ōriṭattiliruntu vantirukkakkūṭum talivi vēṟōriṭattiliruntu vantirukkakkūṭum. iruvarum teyvacaṅkaṟpattāl oṉṟākac cantittapōtē avarkaḷ neñcam kalappārkaḷ. atuvē vāḻkkikku ārampamākum. itu teyvattāl vanta naṭpu eṉṟē āciriyarkaḷ uṇartti irukkiṉṟaṉar. kaṇṭavuṭaṉum kēṭṭavuṭaṉum aṉpu koḷvatiyē taliyaṉpāka avarkaḷ koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāḷattiyil tōṉṟiya kaṇṇappaṉ āṇṭavaṉiṭam koṇṭa aṉpum taliyaṉpēyākum. kāḷattimali mītu nātaṉiruppatākak kēṭṭavuṭaṉē avar aḷavilā aṉpukoṇṭār. taliaṉpu vayappaṭṭa talivaṉ taliviyar taṅkaḷukkiṭiyē tōṉṟiya naṭpit taṅkaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭatākak koḷvatilli. kaṭavuḷē aṅṅaṉam kūṭṭi vittatāka niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. tali makkaḷukkuḷ nēriṭum kūṭṭam teyvattāl ākiṉṟa kūṭṭamākum. “ oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟirupāl āyiṉ ... otta kiḻavaṉum, kiḻattiyum kāṇpē” eṉṟār āciriyarum. mutalilē cantikkaluṟṟa talimakkaḷ neñcam kalantu eṉṟum piriyāt taṉmiyuṭiya varākiṉṟaṉar. atu mutal avarkaḷiṉ uyir oṉṟākavum uṭampiraṇṭākavum māṟukiṟatu. oruvar uṭampil maṟṟoruvar vāḻa ārampikkiṉṟaṉar. inta apippirāyattik kuṟaḷāciriyarum vaṟpuṟuttiyirukkiṟār. ivarkaḷiṉ kalappi iṉi iṟappilētāṉ pirikka vēṇṭum. anta iṟappum pirikka muṭiyāteṉat tamiḻ nūlār koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. vevvēṟiṭaṅ kaḷiliruntu ōṭivanta nīr oṉṟu kalantapiṉ eppaṭip pirikka muṭiyāta taṉiya aṭikiṟatō aṅṅaṉamē ivarkaḷum pirivuṟā niliyi aṭikiṉṟaṉar. inta apippirāyattiyē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum “pey nīr pōla” eṉṟa cūttirattil amittirukkiṟār.iruvarum oṉṟupaṭṭa piṉ avarkaḷukkut taṉittaṉiyē iṉpatuṉpaṅkaḷilli. oruvarukku vanta tuṉpatti maṟṟoruvarum koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. oruvar aṭiyum iṉpatti maṟṟoruvarum nukarukiṉṟaṉar.
‘iruvar ākattuḷ ōruyir kaṇṭaṉam’ eṉṟa tirukkōvi yāṟṟiṉ aṭikaḷ itaṉi valiyuṟuttum. ippaṭit talivaṉum taliviyum neñcaṅkalanta piṉ meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṉṟi aṉpoṭu oḻuki varuvatiyē kaḷavu eṉa vakuttārkaḷ. piṟaku uṟṟār peṟṟōr aṟiya maṇaviḻikoṇṭu oḻukum vāḻkkikku kaṟpu eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. kaṟpu eṉpati viṭa kaḷavē ciṟantateṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. “kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu kaḷaviṉ vaḻittē” eṉa iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷil kūṟappaṭṭiruk kiṟatu. kaḷavu eṉṟa peyari nōkki ati iḻivāka niṉikkak kūṭātu. kaḷavu vāḻkkiyi cīvaka cintāmaṇiyār “iṉṟamiḻ iyaṟkiyiṉpam” eṉak kūṟukiṟār.
talivaṉ taliviyariṭiyē ēṟpaṭum ivvitac cērkkiyi kaḷavu-kaṟpu eṉap piritta āciriyar maṟṟum avaṟṟi mūṉṟu uṭpirivukaḷākavum pirittirukkiṟār. aviyāvaṉa:- kikkiḷi peruntiṇi, aintiṇi, akaṉantiṇi eṉpaṉavām. kikkiḷiyum peruntiṇiyum caṟṟu tāḻntaṉavākavum aintiṇiyē uyarnta tākavum koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. kikkiḷi eṉpatu ciṟumiyuṟa vākum. itu oru pakkattuṟavu. peruntiṇi eṉpatu paruvamnīṅki oṉṟupaṭuvatu. ākavē ivviraṇṭiyum viṭa aintiṇiyē ciṟantateṉa āciriyar koṇṭār. iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷilum “aṉpiṉ aintiṇik kaḷaveṉappaṭuvatu” eṉak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
kaṟpukku laṣaṇam collukiyil āciriyar,
“ kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarakoḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik koṭaṟkuri marapiṉōr koḷvatu” eṉak kūṟukiṟār.
karaṇam eṉpatu caṭaṅku eṉṟākum. itu caṭaṅkukaḷuṭaṉ cuṟṟattār aṟiya maṇañ ceytukoḷvatik kuṟikkiṟatu. cuṟṟattāriṉṟi oruvar koṭuppataṉṟi talivaṉum taliviyum tāṅkaḷē kūṭuvatum uṇṭu. maṇameṉṟatil talivaṉum taliviyumē mukkiya puruṣarkaḷ. itu niṟka , “mēlōr mūvarukkum puṉarttakaraṇam kīḻōrkku ākiya kālamum uṇṭu” eṉṟa oru cūttiramum, “poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiyapiṉṉar aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa” eṉa maṟṟoru cūttiramum kāṇappaṭuvatiliruntu nāṉku varuṇattārukkum, orē vita caṭaṅkukaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum piṟakē nāṉku varuṇattārukkum vevvēṟāṉa karaṇaṅkaḷ (caṭaṅkukaḷ) ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum terikiṉṟatu. āciriyar iṅkē kuṟippatu āriyarkaḷuk kuḷḷirunta varuṇa pākupāṭukaḷiyēyākum. ivvitap pākupāṭukaḷ tamiḻariṭam akkālattē iruntatākat teriyavilli. tamiḻar maṇamuṟikkum āriyar maṇamuṟikkum vittiyācaṅkaḷiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. āriyar maṇamuṟi eṭṭu vakippaṭum. avitāṉ piramam kāntarvam mutaliyaṉa. tamiḻar maṇamuṟi mūṉṟu vakiyāṉateṉa muṉṉamē coṉṉēṉ. avitāṉ kikkiḷi, peruntiṇi, aintiṇi eṉpaṉa. āriyar maṇamuṟiyil ācuram,irākkatam, picācam eṉṟa mūṉṟuvita maṇaṅkaḷum maṟṟum nāṉkuvita maṇaṅkaḷum tamiḻmaṇa muṟikaḷākiya kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷilēyē aṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. ācuram mutaliya maṇaṅkaḷ kūṭa vaṭamoḻiyil pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟit tamiḻar otukkit taḷḷiṉar. tamiḻariṉ aintiṇi maṇamē āriyariṉ kāntarva maṇattōṭu oṉṟiyirukkiṉṟatu.
maṇaccaṭaṅkukaḷ
karaṇam eṉpateṉṉa? tamiḻ makkaḷ avaṟṟi eppaṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpati iṉi ārāyvōm. ippōtu namatu nāṭṭil anuṣṭikkappaṭum kaliyāṇac caṭaṅkukaḷ paṇṭināḷil tamiḻarkaḷāl anuṣṭikkappaṭṭatēyilli. aḻakiya pantal amittup pacuntaḻikaṭṭip puṣpālaṅkāram ceytu putu maṇal parappuvatum, piṟaku cumaṅkalikaḷāviruppavar naṉṉīrāṭit talivaṉ taliviyari oṉṟu cērppatumuṇṭu. iviyē akkālac caṭaṅkukaḷ. ippōtu anuṣṭikkappaṭum caṭaṅkukaḷukku ilakkiya nūlkaḷil ātāramēyilli. ivvitam cērkkappaṭṭa kātalar iruvar māṟā aṉpuṭaṉ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.vayatu uyara uyara aṉpu kuṟiyumeṉa niṉip pataṟkilli. talivaṉ taliviyip viṭṭup piriyāmalum talivi talivaṉiviṭṭup piriyāmalumirukkiṉṟaṉar. akkālattil muṉṉōr vitta poruḷik koṇṭu kālam kaṭattuvati viṭṭut taṉatu tāḷāṇmiyāl paṇam tēṭi vāḻvatē nalameṉa niṉittaṉarākiyāl talimakaṉ poruḷ tēṭavum, kalvi payilavum pōril kalantukoḷḷavum taliviyi viṭṭup pirivatuṇṭu. ittakiya pirivit talivi tāṅkamāṭṭātu araṟṟuvāḷ. tōḻi avaḷukku āṟutal kūṟi āṟṟuvāḷ. talivaṉum taṉatu puṟappāṭṭi nēril taliviyiṭam collātu cila kuṟippukaḷāl uṇarttuvāṉ. allatu tōḻi mūlam ceyti viṭuppāṉ. talivaṉ varaviyē etirnōkkit talivi uṇṇal, pūccūṭal eṉpaṉa etiyum koḷḷātu kāttiruppāḷ. talivi pirivi āṟṟātavaḷākiyiruppati uṇarnta tōḻi talivikku varunta vēṇṭāmeṉṟu colli talivaṉ taṉṉiṭam cīkkirattil varuvatākac collip pōṉatākak kūṟit tēṟṟuvāḷ. kalittokiyilē inta niliyi “aṭitaṅku aḻalaṉṉa vemmiyāka” eṉṟa ceyyuḷ mūlam viḷakkiyuḷḷārkaḷ. atil tōḻi taliviyi nōkki vemmiyāl ularntu coṟpa nīruḷḷa oruciṟu kuṭṭiyil āṇyāṉiyum peṇyāṉiyum nīraruntac ceṉṟapōtu aṅku aruntuvataṟkup pōtiya nīr illātiruntum āṇ yāṉi muṉṉatāka kuṭikkātu “piṭiyūṭṭi piṉ uṇ'99ṇum kaḷiṟu” eṉak kuṟippiṭṭiruppatupōla peṇ yāṉiyik kuṭikkumpaṭic ceytuviṭṭup piṟaku kuṭippatip pārttu viṭṭum aṅku uṉ talivaṉ nīṇṭa nāḷ taṅka māṭṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāḷ. kuṟuntokiyilum inniliyi viḷakka iṉṉoru ceyyuḷirukkiṟatu. cuṉi nīlik kuṭikka vanta āṇmāṉum peṇmāṉum aṅku iruvarum kuṭippataṟkup pōtiya nīrillāmiyāl āṇ māṉ peṇ māṉ kuṭittāl pōtumeṉa niṉittatām. āṉāl āṇmāṉi viṭṭup peṇmāṉ muṉṉatākak kuṭikka maṉam koḷḷātu. ākiyāl āṇmāṉ vāyi jalattil vittuk kuṭippatu pōl pācāṅku kāṭṭi viṭṭup piṟaku peṇmāṉi kuṭikkac ceyyumām. talivaṉ taliviyarkkiṭiyē uḷḷa uḷḷaṉpiyum iṇipiriyāt taṉmiyiyum paṇṭiya āciriyarkaḷ ittakiya muṟiyil viḷakkiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. jātip pākupāṭu eṉpatu tamiḻarukkuḷ iruntatākat teriyavilli. varuṇam eṉpatu vaṭa col, tamiḻil itaṟku uriya collē kiṭiyātu. tolkāppiyar tamatu nūlilē vaṭa coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟi upayōkittirukkiṟār. kuñcaram eṉṟa colli avar tārāḷamākak kiyāṇṭiruk kiṟār. jāti pākupāṭṭik kuṟikkum tamiḻccol iruntiruntāl avar ataṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟiyiruppār. avar nilappeyar kuṭippeyar... eṉak kuṟikkum cūttirattil ‘kuṭippeyar’ eṉṟu āciriyar colliyuḷḷa iṭattil uriyāciriyarkaḷ cilar jāti pākupāṭṭip pukutta muyaṟcikkiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟum cilar anta cūttirattilē “tiṇi nilippeyar” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷa iṭattilē atip pukuttap pārkkiṉṟaṉar. piṟappu, tiṇi, kuṭi eṉṟa  vārttikaḷ jātiyik kuṟikkumā eṉṟu pārttālum avvitam koḷḷuvataṟkilli. āriyar kalappukkup piṉ tamiḻariṭam ēṟpaṭṭa jāti pākupāṭukaḷukkum āriyarkaḷukkuḷḷēyirukkum varuṇa pētaṅkaḷukkum kūṭa vittiyācamirukkiṟatu. anta nāṉku varuṇattilē tamiḻarkaḷ yāvarum aṭaṅkavilli. pirāmmaṇa ṣatriya, viciya, cūttirar eṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭiruppatil nāṉkāvatākavuḷḷa cūttirar eṉṟa patattukku ēval ceyvōr eṉap poruḷpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṟa pākupāṭṭil kaṭiciyākavuḷḷa vēḷāḷar eṉṟa patattukku aṭimi eṉṟa poruḷilli. vēḷāṇmit toḻil ceypavar eṉavē ataṟkup poruḷ. attoḻilum ataṉic ceypavarkaḷum tamiḻarkaḷāl mutaṉmiyākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟār. tolkāppiyar kālattilum piṟakum āriyakkoḷkikaḷ kalakka ārampittaṉa. ivaṟṟil mukkiya māṉavi vētam vēḷvi varuṇam ākiya mūṉṟē. vētam tamiḻmakkaḷālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṉāl vēta vēḷviyit tamiḻmakkaḷ oppuk koḷḷavilli. tamiḻ aracar cilar vēḷvi purintatākat terikiṉṟatāṉālum āriyarkaḷ ceytatu pōla avarkaḷ ceyyavilli. ivikaḷi yellām pārkkumpōtu āriyarkaḷ mikap paḻiya nāḷilēyē tamiḻarkaḷōṭu kalakka ārampittārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu.
tamiḻariṉ pōrmuṟikaḷ
tamiḻarkaḷ taṟukaṇ āṇmiyuṭiyavarkaḷ, añcātavarkaḷ eṉpataṟkup pala ātāraṅkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa. “viḻittakaṇ” eṉpatit toṭakkamākak koṇṭa kuṟaḷaṭi tamiḻariṉ taṟukaṇ muṟiyi veḷiyākkukiṉṟatu.
pōrmuṟiyi veṭci, vañci, tumpittiṇi, umiññittiṇi eṉappala pirivukaḷākap pirittirukkiṉṟār. veṭci eṉpatu mutalil āṭkaḷi ēvi, vēṟṟaracaṉiṉ pacuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷip piṭittu varac ceytāl avaṟṟi mīṭka avvaracaṉ vara iruvarum poruvatu.
vañciyeṉpatu nāṭṭik kippaṟṟap pōr ceyvatu, tumpittiṇi eṉpatu vīrattik kāṭṭap pōriṭuvatu, uḻiññittiṇi yeṉpatu matil vaḷittu muṟṟuki yiṭṭup pōriṭuvatu.
akkālattil aracarkaḷ nālvakip paṭikaḷiyum vittiruntatākat terikiṟatu. “muḻumutal araṇam” eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyiruppatāl akkālattil uyarnta kōṭṭikaḷiruntaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. nalla nāḷ kaṇṭu kuṭi, vāḷ mutaliyaṉavaṟṟi veḷiyē puṟappaṭac ceytu piṟaku aracarkaḷ pōrmuṉikkuc cellum vaḻakkamuṭiyavarkaḷāka viruntatākavum terikiṟatu. pōriṭuvataṟku muṉ paṭiyāḷarukku viruntiṭṭu aracaṉum uṭaṉiruntu uṇṇuvatum vaḻakka muṇṭeṉṟu terikiṟatu. pōriṭuvatil cila niyāyaṅkaḷi anuṣṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟum nirṇayam ceyyappaṭṭiruk kiṟatu.pakivar valiyiḻanta kālattil avarkaḷit tākkāmal avarkaḷukku utavi ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟum atuvē yuttatarumameṉṟum collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟi
tamiḻmakkaḷ mikka muyaṟciyuṭiyavarkaḷ. nāvāykaḷ  mūlam veḷināṭukaḷukkum ceṉṟu vāṇipam naṭatti  vantirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṟku anēka ātāraṅkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa.  illaṟam naṭattum muṟiyil avarkaḷiṉ viruntōmpum taṉmi mikac ciṟantatākum. avarkaḷ ciṟanta oḻukkattiṉarākavum vaḷḷaṉmi vāyntavarkaḷākavum, piḻittār tāṅkum kāval pāṉmiyōrākavum, meyyuṇarvu peṟuvatil karuttuk koṇṭārākavum iruntaṉareṉṟam muṭivākap paṟṟaṟat tuṟakkum taṉmiyiṉarāyiṉar eṉavum kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
ulaka iṉpatti naṉku anupavitta piṉ avarkaḷ ivviṉpam niliyātatu eṉpati aṟintu vīṭu peṟa virumpum taṉmiyiṉar eṉpatik kāṭṭavum ātāraṅkaḷuṇṭu.

16. tolkāppiyar kalvip perumiyum nūlamipputtiṟaṉum
tolkāppiyap pāyiramāṉatu, tolkāppiyarit tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ārāyntavar eṉṟum, muntunūl kaṇṭavar eṉṟum, aintiram niṟintavar eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāciriyarkku muṉpē tamiḻil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ pala iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu ivar tāmē 'eṉpa' eṉṟum 'eṉmaṉār pulavar' eṉṟum ivvāṟākat tollāciriyar matampaṭap palaviṭattum kūṟic cellutalāl aṟiyappaṭum.  muntunūl eṉṟatu  akattiyam oṉṟaṉiyēyeṉac cilar koṇṭaṉar. aḵtoṉṟēyāyiṉ ōriṭattēṉum ataṉi vitantōtātu, yāṇṭum 'eṉmaṉār pulavar' eṉṟum, kaṭinili yiṉṟē āciriyarkka' eṉṟum potuppaṭak kūṟic cellā rākaliṉ, ivarkku muṉpē pala nūlkaḷ iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉavum, avaṟṟiyē 'muntunūl' eṉap pāyiramum kūṟiṟṟeṉavum kōṭal vēṇṭum. muntunūlāvaṉa akattiyamum, māpurāṇamum, pūtapurāṇamum, ici nuṇukkamum eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟiṉar. appoḻutiruntavi iṉṉiṉṉa nūlkaḷeṉa aṟutiyiṭṭurittal cālāteṉiṉum, akattiyam uḷḷiṭṭa pala nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa veṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivar avaṟṟiyum aṟṟināḷ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷiyum naṉkārāyntavar eṉap pāyiram kūṟuvatum aviyaṉittiyum muṟṟavaṟintavar eṉak karutaṟkuriya kuṟippukkaḷ tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatum ivaratu kalviyiṉ akalattiyum āḻattiyum naṉku pulappaṭuttuvaṉavām. eṭuttukkāṭṭākap piṉvarum kuṟippukkaḷ cila nōkkaṟpālaṉa.
moḻi marapiṉ kaṇṇē moḻikku iṟutiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷi uṇarttumiṭattu 'ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē' (42) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl cakaravukaratti īṟṟiluṭiya muṟṟiyalukaramoḻi tamiḻil iraṇṭē uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum,
'	uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa
iruvayi ṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē'1
eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl pakaravukaratti īṟṟiluṭiya muṟṟukara moḻi oṉṟē yuṇṭeṉṟum, aḵtoṉṟumē taṉviṉip poruḷum piṟaviṉip poruḷum uṭittāmeṉṟum kūṟiṉar. piṉṉum nakaravoṟṟi īṟākavuṭiya col, uccakāra moḻi pōl iraṇṭē eṉṟum, ñakaravoṟṟi yīṟākavuṭiya col uppakāra moḻipōl oṉṟē yeṉṟum, āṉāl iḵtu iru poruṭpaṭāteṉṟum kūṟiṉar. maṟṟum, nilamnilaṉ, kalam kalaṉ eṉpaṉa pōla makarattōṭu mayaṅkutalillāta ṉakaravīṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarccol oṉpatu uḷḷaṉa veṉṟum, toki marapiṉkaṇṇē aḷavup peyarkkum niṟip peyarkkum mutalāka vuḷḷaṉa kacatapa namava au eṉṉum oṉpateḻuttumē eṉṟum variyaṟuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivipōlvaṉavum, uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya palatiṟappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiyum eñcā teṭuttu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarttuc ceyki ceytu muṭippatum, vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ poruḷ vikaṟpaṅkaḷiyum avi mayaṅki varumāṟṟiyum mika viritturippatum, iḷamip peyarum āṇpāṟ peyarum peṇpāṟ peyarum ākiya marapup peyarkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum eṭuttu avi iṉṉiṉṉavaṟṟukku uriyaveṉa uṇarttuvatum, piṟavum tamiḻ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ coṟporuṭ parappu muḻuvatum ivvāciriyarāl naṉku vaṭit taṟiyap peṟṟaṉa eṉpati uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉi yeṉat teḷḷitiṉ viḷakkuvaṉavātal kāṇka.
iṉi ivvāciriyar tāmē cūttirattiyalpeṉak kūṟiya cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭātalum, nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmittātalum pōlvaṉavellām oruṅkamin taṉavākat tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ viḷaṅkuvatum, avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ coṟkaḷ poṉpaṇiyāmāṟu pōla ivar karutiyavā ṟellām tirintu aḻaku ceyvatum pōlvaṉavaṟṟi nōkkiṉ, ivar vaḻivaḻiyākat tamiḻilē ūṟivantatoru kuṭiyiṉ kaṭṭōṉṟi, uṇṇuñcōṟum parukunīrum tiṉṉum veṟṟiliyu mellām tamiḻākavē koṇṭu vaḷarntu talimi yeytiya cāṉṟōrāvar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
"	mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum"1
"	ovvu maṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭiyē"2
"	ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum"3
"	uppa kāramoṭu ñakāriyu maṟṟē
 apporu ḷiraṭṭā tivaṇi yāṉa"1
eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷiluḷḷa mutalā, mutalum, alaṅkaṭi, civaṇum,  ñakāri, ivaṇi eṉṉuñ col vaḻakkukaḷiyum, innūṟ pākkaḷiṉ tiṭpanuṭpaṅkaḷiyum nōkkumiṉkaḷ. iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaṟ cūttiram cilavaṉṟi, naṉṉūl ciṉṉūl toṉṉūl mutalāya piṟkālattu ennūliṉ kaṇṇum oru cūttirantāṉum innūṟpākkaḷiṉ aṇimiyilum niṟṟaṟkuriya taṉṟāyiṉ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉāratu tamiḻp pulamiyiṉ viḻuppatti eṅṅaṉam aḷaviṭṭurittal kūṭum? iṉi ivar vaṭamoḻiyilum perumpulami vāyntavar eṉpatu 'aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi' eṉṉum pāyirak kūṟṟāṉē tuṇiyappaṭum.
"	akatteḻu vaḷiyici yariṟapa nāṭi aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittē"2
eṉa ivar kūṟuvatu arumaṟiyiṉkaṇ ivarukkuḷḷa payiṟciyip pulappaṭuttā niṟkum.
iṉi, ivvāciriyar innūli amittirukkum tiṟappāṭukaḷ nōkkaṟpālaṉa. eḻuttum collum poruḷumākiya mūṉṟaṉiyum mūṉṟu atikārattāṟ kūṟaluṟṟa ivar, ovvōratikārattiyum oṉpatu oṉpatu iyalkaḷāka vakuttuk koṇṭuḷār. iṅṅaṉam ovvōratikāramum iyalvakiyāl ōraḷaviṉavāka vakut tiruppatu tām kūṟakkarutiya poruḷkaḷi ōrelliyuḷ aṭakka muyaṉṟu iṭarppaṭuvatākumeṉac cilar karutavuṅkūṭum. eṉiṉum avviyalkaḷ yāvum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavum, avvav vatikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyipuḷḷaṉavumātal nuṇṇaṟivāṉ ārāyvārkkup pulaṉākā niṟkum. āyiṉ ōratikāratti oṉpāṉ iyaliṟ kuṟiyavō mikavō vakuttamittal ollātō eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉañ ceytal ollumāyiṉum, oru nikarāṉa variyaṟi yuṭimiyiṉ payaṉ karuti aṅṅaṉam iyalkaḷi variyaṟi ceytu, poruḷaḷavuk kēṟpac cūttiraṅkaḷi mikuttum kuṟittum iṭarppāṭiṉṟi yāttamittuḷḷā reṉal vēṇṭum. paṇṭiyāciriyarkaḷ tām karutiya poruḷ kaḷi eñcāmaṟ kūṟuvatil nōkkuṭiyarāyiruntami pōṉṟē, avi piṟkālattil tiripiṉṟi niṉṟu nilavavum, kaṟpōruḷḷattil eḷitiṟ pukuntu akalātirukkavum uriya muṟikaḷi iyalu maḷavukikkoṇṭu avaṟṟik kūṟum karuttuṭiyārumāvar. pāṇiṉiyum tamatu nūliṉ eṭṭu attiyāyaṅkaḷiyum nannāṉku pākaṅkaḷāka vakuttuḷḷaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷ, tēvāram, tiruvāy moḻi, mutaliya tamiḻ maṟikaḷum, āriya vētappakutiyākiya aitarēyap piramāṇam mutaliyaṉavum, caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil patiṟṟuppattu mutalāyiṉavum ovvōraḷaviṉavāka iyaṉṟirut talum kāṇka. iṉi, ivvāciriyar meyppāṭṭiyaliṉkaṇ eṇvaki meyppāṭum nikaḻuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷi uṇarttu miṭattu,
"	kalvi taṟukaṇ icimi koṭiyeṉac
collap paṭṭa perumitam nāṉkē"1
"	celvam pulaṉē puṇarvu viḷiyāṭṭeṉ
ṟallal nītta vuvaki nāṉkē"2
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam ovvoru meyppāṭṭukkum nannāṉku kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatum, uvamaviyalilē viṉi, payaṉ, mey, uru eṉṉum nālvaki uvamattiṟkum urupukaḷ taṉittaṉi evveṭṭāka variyaṟutturippatum, peyariyalilē,
"	avaṉivaṉ uvaṉeṉa varūum peyarum,
avaḷivaḷ uvaḷeṉa varūum peyarum,
avarivar uvareṉa varūum peyarum,
yāṉyām nāmeṉa varūum peyarum,
yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāva reṉṉum
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
pālaṟi vanta uyartiṇip peyarē"3
eṉavum,
"	atu vitu utuveṉa varūum peyarum,
avimuta lākiya āytap peyarum,
avi yivi uviyeṉa varūum peyarum,
avimuta lākiya vakarap peyarum,
yātu yā yāvi eṉṉum peyarum,
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
pālaṟi vanta aḵṟiṇip peyarē"4
eṉavum ōriṉamāṉa mummūṉṟu peyarkaḷi ovvōraṭiyil niṟutti aḻakum teḷivum poruntak kūṟuvatum pōlvaṉa kaṟpōruḷḷattil avi naṉku patiyumāṟu ceyya mēṟkoṇṭa muṟikaḷām eṉka.
iṉi, ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil potuvum ciṟappum mutaliya vitikaḷ oṉṟaṉi yoṉṟu pōṟṟiyum maṟuttum cellum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaṟpār nūliṉ orupakuti kaṟṟu amiyātu nūṉ muḻutuṅ kaṟkum kaṭappāṭuṭiyarāvar. ak kaṭappāṭṭiṉiṉṟum avar vilakāmip poruṭṭu ivar nūṟpākkaḷi amittirukkuntiṟaṉ mika viyattaṟkuriyatu. iruḷ eṉṉuñ col niṉṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyil varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟavantavar.
"	iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliyal niliyum"1
eṉṟār. veyil eṉṉuñ coṟku aṅṅaṉam viti kūṟumiṭattō
"	veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum"2
eṉṟār. maṟṟu; maḻi eṉṉuñ coṟku viti kūṟuṅkāl
"	maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal niliyum"3
eṉṟār.
vaḷi eṉṉuñ collukku vitiyum
"	vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
avviyal niliyal cevvi teṉpa"4
eṉa ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa cūttirattōṭu māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār. accūttirattāṉ
"	paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumik
kattum iṉṉum cāriyi yākum"5
eṉpatu. iṅṅaṉam uyirmayaṅkiyal 39ām cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya vitiyi 161 cūttiraṅkaṭkuppiṉ puḷḷimayaṅkiyaliluḷḷa 'iruḷeṉkiḷavi' eṉṉuñ cūttiraṅkāṟum toṭarntuvarac ceytiruttaliṉ iviyaṉittiyum oruṅku kaṟṟuṇarntālaṉṟi, iruḷ, veyil, maḻi, vaḷi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ attuccāriyiyāvatu iṉcāriyiyāvatu peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉpataṉi eṅṅaṉam aṟital cālum? ivi vevvēṟu uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya coṟkaḷātaliṉ ivaṟṟi oruvaḻik koṇarntu oruṅku muṭittalum poruntāmi kāṇka. ivvāṟu muṭippaṉa maṟṟum palavuḷa. iṅṅaṉam cūttiraṅkaḷik kōvippaṭutti mutalum muṭivum māṟu kōḷiṉṟi ivvāciriyar amittirukkum muṟiyi uṇaralāṟṟāta cilar 'tolkāppiyattuḷ iccūttiram īṇṭiruttal poruntātu, āṇṭu iruttal vēṇṭum' eṉṟu tamakkut tōṟṟiyavāṟellām urittal naki eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiṟkuk karuviyām. attuṇiyaṉṟivēṟeṉ payaṉuṭittu?


17.paraṇip peyarkkāraṇam
tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷ taṉinili, tokinili, toṭarnili eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ toṭarnili, coṟṟoṭar nili, poruṭṭoṭar nili eṉa iruvakippaṭum. poruṭṭoṭar nilic ceyyuḷ nāṟporuḷ payakkum naṭineṟittākip palliyalpāṉum virintu naṭappatum, avvāṟiṉṟik kuṟi pāṭuṭiyatum eṉa iraṇṭām. avaṟṟuḷ muṉṉataṉip peruṅkāppiyameṉpar. piṉṉatiyum atupōṉṟa coṟ ṟoṭarnilic ceyyuḷiyum cila taṉic ceyyuṭkaḷiyum pirapantam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. tamiḻp pirapantaṅkaḷit toṇṇūṟṟāṟāka vakuttirukkiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ paraṇi eṉpatoṉṟu. atu poruṭṭoṭar niliyākum. pōriṉ kaṇ āyiram yāṉiyiveṉṟa vīraṉmēl atu pāṭappaṭuvatu eṉpa,
"	āṉiyāyiram amariṭiveṉṟa māṉavaṉukku vakuppatu paraṇi"1
			    (ilakkaṇa viḷakkappāṭṭiyal)
paraṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇam,
"	kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuk kaṭitiṟap purittal
kaṭumpāli kūṟal koṭuṅkāḷi kōṭṭaṅ
kaṭikaṇa murittal kāḷin ticolal
aṭupēyk kavaḷ cola lataṉāṟ ṟalivaṉ
vaṇpuka ḻuritta leṇpuṟat tiṇiyuṟa
vīṭṭa laṭukaḷam vēṭṭa livimē
laḷavaṭi mutalā vaṭiyiraṇ ṭāka
vuḷamakiḻ paraṇi yurikkap paṭumē"2
			   (ilakkaṇa viḷakkappāṭṭiyal)
eṉpataṉāl uṇarka.
iṉi, paraṇiyeṉap pirapantap peyar pōnta kāraṇam palarāl palavāṟu kūṟappaṭum. aviyellām, īṇṭurippiṉ viriyum, kālañceṉṟa vi.kō.cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār, pi.ē., avarkaḷ tām acciṭṭa kaliṅkattup paraṇi mukavuriyil paraṇiyeṉṉum peyarkkāraṇam vicārippāṉpukkup palaruṭiya koḷkikaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭi iṟutiyil tam koḷkiyip piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar:-
"īṇṭik kūṟiya yāvarum paraṇi yeṉṉum collit tām nāṭōṟum vaḻakkamāyp pārkkum akarātikaḷi
leṭuttup pārttu, ataṉkaṇ accolliṟkuk kūṟappaṭṭa ovvoru poruḷiyum ivvāṟeṭuttamittup peritu
miṭarp paṭṭuc camarppittut tamakkuṭṭāmē maṉappāl kuṭittu makiḻuṟuvārāyiṉār. ituniṟka, iṉit tolkāppiyam poruḷatikārattuc ceyyuḷiyaliṟ koccakavorupōkiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuñ cūttiravuriyil tēvapāṇi mutalāyiṉa taṉakkiṉamākiya vaṇṇakattiṟkōtiya taraviṉṟit tāḻici peṟumeṉavum  pāṇiyuḷellā miraṇṭaṭiyāṉē tāḻampaṭṭa
vōci virāay varutalum muṭukivarutalu muḷaveṉavum,
"	uḷiyāḻi yōrēḻu moru celavu ḷaṭaṅkutalāṉ
viḷiyāṭa nīrpeṟā miṉṉuruvam parāvutumē."
eṉṟāṟ pōlum pāṇic ceyyuḷuṭ payiṉṟu varum pala tāḻici toṭar poruḷavākaliṉ avaṟṟip palavaṭukki variṉum tāḻiciyā meṉka veṉavum, maṟṟup pāṇiyuṭ puṟattiṇi palavum virāay varutaliṉ atu tēvapāṇiyā
meṉṟateṉṉi yeṉiṉ, aviyellāṅ kāṭukeḻu celvikkup pāṇi nāṭ kūḻum tuṇaṅkiyuṅ koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvatōr vaḻakkup paṟṟi ataṉuṭ pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉip peytu collappaṭuvaṉa ātalāṉ aviyellā vāṟṟāṉun tēvapāṇiyēyām eṉpateṉavum kūṟāniṟṟaliṉ īṇṭuyām ārāyap pukunta paraṇi yeṉpatu pāṇiyeṉṟē yiruttal vēṇṭumeṉal peṟutum. aṉṟiyum muṟkālattu ōlic
cuvaṭikaḷiṉ varivaṭiviṉkaṇ rakāra maṉittuñ cila vuyirmeyn
neṭi lākārattōṭiyalum kāleṉa veḻutappaṭṭiruttalum oruvāṟu ematu kūṟṟi valiyuṟuttikiṉṟami kāṇka."
piṟariyellām maṉappāl kuṭippavar eṉṟu kūṟi viṭṭuc cāstiriyār niṟuviyirukkum koḷkiyi īṇṭārāyvōm. avar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya tolkāppiya vuriyiṉi nuṇṇitiṉ ārāyvārkkut "tēvapāṇi" eṉpatu tavira, maṟṟup pāṇi yeṉṟuḷḷaṉa vellām paraṇiyeṉṟē yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu pulappaṭum. ōliccuvaṭikaḷiṉ varivaṭivil rakarattiṟkum neṭṭeḻuttiṉ kālukkum vēṟṟumi kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ piṟar iṅṅaṉam vaḻuviṉar eṉak karutiya cāstiriyār tāmē atil vaḻuvi yirukkiṉṟami viyappākum! 'pāṇi nāṭ kūḻum tuṇaṅkiyuṅ koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvatu' eṉpataṟkuyātum poruḷillāmiyi avar nōkkiṟṟilar. paraṇippāṭṭellām tēvapāṇiyām eṉpatē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu. ataṟkuk kāraṇamum avar kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivvāṟṟāl paraṇi vēṟu, pāṇivēṟu eṉpatu teḷika.
