'paraṇiyāvatu kāṭukeḻu celvikkupparaṇi nāṭ kūḻum tuṇaṅkiyum koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvatōr vaḻakkup paṟṟiyatu' eṉa avar eḻutiyatu paraṇiyeṉṉum pirapantap peyariṉ kāraṇattip puvappaṭuttukiṉṟatu. koṟṟavi yeṉpāḷ veṟṟit teyvamākaliṉ veṉṟi vēṇṭi avaḷi vaḻipaṭuvār veṟṟikkuriya paraṇi nāḷil kūḻiṭṭut tuṇaṅkiyāṭi vaḻipaṭutal marapākum. veṉṟapiṉ kaḷakkūḻaṭṭup  pēykaṭku iṭuvatuṇṭu.  pēykaḷ koṟṟaviyiṉ kaṇaṅkaḷākaliṉ, akkūḻum itaṉ kaṭ kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, paraṇiyeṉap peyar vantateṉka. kaliṅkattup paraṇi, kaḷampāṭiya pakutiyil 'kaḷapparaṇik kūḻpoṅki' eṉavum, 'paṇṭu mikumō paraṇik
kūḻ' eṉavum, 'varukūḻp paraṇik kaḷaṅkaṇṭu vantapēyi muṉṉūṭṭi, yorukūḻpparaṇi nāmirukku mūrkkaṭ pēykku vārīrē' eṉavum varutal kāṇka. nāṭpeyarāl paliyiṉpeyar vaḻaṅkutal 'tiruvātirikkaḷi' mutaliya peyar vaḻakkukaḷālum uṇarka. ivvāṟṟāl paraṇik kūḻpaṟṟiyē pirapantattiṟkup paraṇiyeṉap peyar pōntateṉpatu tuṇipu.

18. kapilar eṉṉum nūliṉ ārāycci
eṉ naṇpar vittuvāṉ tiru. vē. vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭi yāravarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟṟu, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tiṉarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa 'kapilar' eṉṉum urinūl ceṉṟa yāṇṭilē anuppappeṟṟu eṉakkuk kiṭittatu. kiṭittum, yāṉ cilayāṇṭukaḷāka nōyāl nalivuṟṟu avvap poḻutu ceyyaṟ pālavāya iṉṟiyamiyā viṉikaḷaṉṟip piṟitoṉṟum āṟṟakilātiruntu varutaliṉ ataṉip paṭittup pārātiruntuviṭṭēṉ. ip paṅkuṉittiṅkaḷiṉ iṟutiyiṟ piṟitoru vēliyum muṉṉillāmiyiṉ cevvi vāyttu ataṉip paṭittaṟiyalāṉēṉ. reṭṭiyāravarkaḷatu ārāycci yaṟivi muṉṉamē 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūlāl aṟintu ḷēṉākaliṉ, ippoḻutu putuvatāka viyappoṉṟum tōṉṟavilli. reṭṭiyār tamatunūliṉ muṉṉuriyil, iṟṟikkup patiṉāṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ eṉṉāl eḻuti veḷiyiṭap peṟṟa 'kapilar' eṉṉum urinūliccuṭṭi, aḵtirukkavum iḵtu eḻutiyatu mikiyākāteṉaṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvārāy, "nāṭṭāravarkaḷ perumpālum kallūri māṇavariṉ payaṉikkaruti annūli eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. iḵtu aṉṉatōr nōkkattāl eḻutappaṭṭataṉṟu" eṉṟurittu, iraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu cilavaṟṟik kuṟittiruppatuṭaṉ, nūliṉakattum cila viṭaṅkaḷil annūṟkaruttukkaḷit tām vēṇṭumāṟṟāl āṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu eṉpāl avar vittuḷḷa aṉpiṟkum matippiṟkum aṟikuṟiyām eṉṟē eṇṇukiṟēṉ. eṉiṉum, eṉatu nūliṉ karuttukkaḷi āṇṭuḷḷa vakimiyiṟ kaṟpārkkut tiripuṇarcci uṇṭākāvāṟu cilavaṟṟi viḷakkutal kaṭaṉeṉak karutiyum, ataṉōṭu eṉakku amitiyilavākat tōṉṟum annūṟporuḷkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟi yēṉum veḷippaṭuttal aṉṉavar ārāycci piṉṉun tūymiyuṟutaṟkum, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum uriyatāmeṉa ōrntum aviyiṟṟic ciṟu kaṭṭurikaḷāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭat tuṇintēṉ. icceyal annūliṉ āciriyar pāluḷḷa naṭpiṉ kiḻamikkum, matippiṟkum, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum poruntiya tākumeṉpatē eṉatu tuṇipu.
annūliṟ putumiyākak kāṇappaṭum cila collāṭci muṟiyi mutalil ārāytaṟku eṭuttuk koḷkiṉṟēṉ. mukittal, aṅkaṉam, kāṇkiṉṟatu, naṟkīrar eṉpaṉa muṟiyē ārāyappaṭum.
"paraṇar eṉṉum nūli yāṉ eḻuti mukitta piṉṉar" eṉa annūliṉ muṉṉuri toṭaṅkumiṭattē 'mukitta' eṉṟoru col āḷappaṭṭuḷatu.  'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉ muṉṉuriyilum "avarkaḷaṉivaratu varalāṟṟiyum oruṅkē eḻuti mukippatu aritākumākaliṉ" eṉavum, "tolkāppiyattiṟku ōr uri eḻutakkaruti; atil eḻuttatikārattiṟku ippōtu uri eḻuti mukittirukkiṉṟēṉ" eṉavum īriṭattil ivviṉiyiṉ āṭci uḷatu. itaṉi vaḻaṅkuvatu vaḻuvākātu eṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟ poruṭṭu "mukintatu eṉṉuñcol, vaḻakkilum paḻiya ēṭukaḷilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, 'āṟṟiṟ pālatti mukittē ṉallēṉ' (kuṟṟālattala, kaṇṭakacētaṉa. 39) eṉṟu ceyyuḷilum vantuḷḷataṉāṉum, 'mukitutu' eṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattilum, 'mukiseṉu' eṉṟu teluṅkilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, mukieṉṉuñcol toṉmiyatē eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum" eṉakkīḻkkuṟippil viḷakkam eḻutappaṭṭuḷatu. (kapilar-muṉṉuri.) tamiḻ lekcikaṉilum iccoṟkup poruḷ kūṟumiṭattē 'āṟṟiṟpālatti' eṉṉum immēṟkōḷum, kaṉṉaṭattilum teluṅkilum itu vaḻaṅku māṟum kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caṅkappulavarkaḷippaṟṟi ārāycciyuri eḻutum āciriyaroruvar piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvataṉṟik kaṭalpōṟ parantuḷḷa tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil yāṇṭum vaḻaṅkappaṭātatum, uriyāciriyaṉmār evarāṉum tīṇṭappaṭātatum, evviṭattu ettiṟattu makkaḷālō aruki vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatumākiya ticiccol oṉṟi mutaṉmiyāka nāṭṭuvatum, ataṉip paḻiya tamiḻc col eṉṟu niṟuttappukuvatum nērmiyuṭiyavākat tōṉṟa villi. 'mukitta' eṉṟu eḻutiyavuṭaṉ ataṉi viḷakkuvāṉ pukuntatē atu tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ vaḻakkilillātatu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. aṉṟiyum, mukittal viṉiyi nūṉ muḻutum koṇṭuykkātu muṉṉuriyaḷavil muṭittiruppatu eḻutiṉōrukkum aḵtu aruvaruppu viḷittirukku meṉpataṉik kāṭṭā niṟkum.
iṉi, 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliyavākat tamiḻaṟiñar palarāṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭuñ coṟkaḷ itaṉakattum, 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉakattum aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam, eṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉamē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkuvatē cevvitu eṉpatu ivaṟṟi eḻutiṉōr āyntukaṇṭa muṭipākum.
"	āṅkaṉam taṇikuva tāyiṉ yāṅkum"	   (naṟ.322)
'	īṅkaṉañ celka tāṉeṉa eṉṉi yāṅkaṟin taṉaṉō tāṅkaruṅ kāvalaṉ'	   (puṟa.208)
'yāṅkaṉam ottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam'    (puṟa.8)
eṉṟu, āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttalāṉum, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟ kaṇ vārāteṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutalāṉum, aṅkaṉam āṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam eṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻamiya eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. ātaliṉ, yāṉ innūlil aṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟ kaḷiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ." (kapilar-muṉṉuri) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
kuṟṟālat talapurāṇattil ōriṭattu vantu viṭṭatu eṉpatu koṇṭu 'mukittal' viṉiyi āḷat toṭaṅkiṉōr pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap palarāṉum vaḻakkiṉum ceyyuḷiṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varuvatum, oru ciṟanta āciriyarāl ilakkaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatum ākiya colli avvaṭivil vaḻaṅka maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷḷatu. naṉṉūlāciriyar iṟṟikku eḻunūṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpiruntavar eṉpatu yāvarkkum oppa muṭintatu. avar
"	cuṭṭiyā vekara viṉāvaḻi yavvi
oṭṭi ṅavvum mutalā kummē"
eṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyiruttaliṉ avar kālattiṉ muṉpē aṅṅaṉam mutaliya colvaḻakku avvaṭivuṭaṉ nilaviya
teṉpatu peṟappaṭum. avvāṟu payila vaḻaṅkuvataṉi aṟavē yotukkip putiyatoru vāypāṭukoṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu marapu nili tirittup poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟu ceyvatēyākum.
	"marapunili tiriyiṟ piṟitupiṟi tākum"
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiramum, 'marapiṉi nilitirittuc collupaveṉiṉ, ulakattuc collellām poruḷiḻantu piṟitu piṟitākum' eṉa ataṟkup pērāciriyar kūṟiya uriyum nōkkaṟpālaṉa.
iṉi, paḻiya ceyyuṭkaḷil 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliyaṉa vaḻaṅkāmiyiṉ uṇmiyum, tolkāppiya vitiyiṉmiyum āytaṟkuriyaṉa. ṅakara voṟṟu akaravuyiruṭaṉaṉṟip piṟavuyirkaḷōṭu tamiḻil yāṇṭum varutal illi. avvuṭaṉ kūṭiya 'ṅa' eṉṉum uyirmey tāṉum aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam uṅṅaṉam eṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉṉum aintu coṟkaḷilaṉṟi vēṟiṭattu uṭaṉili yāka mayaṅki varuvataṉṟu. ākaliṉ, ceyyuḷil etukit toṭiyil accoṟkaḷi amittal ariteṉpatu kūṟal vēṇṭā. ṅakarattuṭaṉ mayaṅkutaṟkuriya vēṟumey ataṉōṭu piṟappoṟṟumiyum oliyoṟṟumiyumuṭiya kakaram oṉṟumēyām. ākaliṉ, ceyyuṭ ceyyum pulavar etukit toṭiyil ṅakarattik kakaramākat tirittu vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē. "yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam" eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etuki yātaṟkiḻukkiṉṟām. iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyaliṉ mūṉṟāñ cūttiram "āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ" eṉṟē paṭikkap paṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum vaḻaṅkutaṟku arumiyuṭiya coṟkaḷē eḷiyavākat tirital iyalpu. marūuc coṟkaḷiṟ pala avvāṟu vantaṉavē. aṅkaṉam eṉpatē paḻiya vuruvāyiṉ vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷimiyuṭiya accol aṅṅaṉam eṉattirital poruttamiṉṟām. āyiṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ vārā teṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutaliṉ 'ṅaṉam' eṉpatu toṉmiyatātal eṅṅaṉam eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi aṅṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarttuvatu tolkāppiyar karuttaṉṟām. ākaliṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ varumeṉa avarvitittilar.
"	pakuti vikuti iṭinili cāriyi
canti vikāram āṟiṉum ēṟpavi
muṉṉip puṇarppa muṭiyum eppataṅkaḷum"
eṉṟu naṉṉūlār oru colliyē mutaṉili mutaliyavākap pirittu muṭikkumāṟu koṇṭāṟ pōlat tolkāppiyar koṇṭilar. toṭarmoḻikaḷiluṅkūṭap paṇputtoki, viṉittoki mutaliyavaṟṟi niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap puṇarkkalākāteṉal avar karutteṉpatu "uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki" eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ aṟiyap paṭum. 'ṅavvum mutalākum' eṉak kūṟiya naṉṉūlārum cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ varumeṉṟārēyaṉṟit taṉittu mutalāku meṉak kūṟiṉārallār. ākaliṉ, tolkāppiyaṉār ṅakaram moḻi mutalil varumeṉak kūṟāmiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu paḻiya vaḻakkākāteṉat tuṇital poruntāmi ōrka. āyiṉ, atu paḻiya vaḻakkē eṉat tuṇītaṟkuttāṉ cāṉṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, kūṟuval. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattiṉ mutal iyalākiya nūṉ marapil uṭaṉili meymmayakkam kūṟum nūṟpā
"	meynnili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē"
eṉpatu. itil, ra, ḻa eṉṉum iraṇṭum oḻinta patiṉāṟu meyyum tammuṉ tām vantu mayaṅkumeṉa āciriyar vitit tiruttaliṉ ṅakara oṟṟiṉmuṉ ṅakaramvantu mayaṅkum col iruntatākal vēṇṭumaṉṟē? tamiḻilē cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ ṅaṉam eṉpatu iyintuvarum aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷaṉṟi ṅakaram uṭaṉiliyāka mayaṅkaṟkuriya vēṟu col yātuḷatu? tiru. vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ pala yāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattiṟku uriyeḻuti muṭittuḷār eṉpatu 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉ muṉṉuriyāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉkaṇ, mēl eṭuttukkāṭṭiya "meyn nili cuṭṭiṉ" eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkum uriyum, utāraṇamum eḻutap paṭṭirukkavē vēṇṭum. aviyiṟṟi iṭiyē orukāl niṉikkavu muṭiyāmaṟ ceytamaṟatiyiṉ vaṉmiyē vaṉmi. am maṟatiyāl avar tāmeḻutiya puttakaṅkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ yāvaṟṟiṉum piḻiyuḷḷa tōr muṭipiṉi mēṟ koṇṭārāvar.
tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttoki mutaliya caṅkavilakkiyaṅ kaṭkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarttōṉṟiyateṉpatu reṭṭiyāravarkaṭkum uṭaṉpāṭē. annūliṉālē aṟutiyiṭap paṭutaliṉ aṅṅaṉam mutaliya col vaḻakkum paḻamiyateṉpatu pōtarum. ataṉiyaṟintē naṉṉūlārum ataṟkumāṟiṉṟit tamatu matamumtōṉṟa ilakkaṇam kūṟippōntāreṉka.
iṉi, kapilar eṉṉum annūliṉkaṇ "purāṇak katikaḷum pala kāṇkiṉṟaṉa; cilavaṟṟil ovvoru collē kāṇ kiṉṟatu; ciṟitum māṟupāṭillāta aṭikaḷum parakkak kāṇutaliṉ; aivar pulavar iyaṟṟiyaṉa eṉak kāṇkiṉṟaṉa vākaliṉ; kapilar pāṭiya kalippāṭṭukkaḷuḷḷum nāṭaka vaḻakkuk kāṇkiṉṟatu; nālaciccīrippaṟṟiya ilakkaṇamum kāṇkiṉṟatu; ñāṉa veṭṭiyāṉ eṉṉum nūlilum kāṇkiṉṟatu; oṟṟu eṉa ṟakara ukaram kāṇkiṉṟatu; ḻakarattukku ṭakaram kāṇkiṉṟatu. kakara ḻakaram māṟik kāṇkiṉṟatu." (pakkam-33, 34, 35, 37, 38, 44, 49, 116, 117.) eṉṟiṅṅaṉam palaviṭattum 'kāṇ' eṉpataṉaṭiyākavarum viṉiccoṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi palaviṭattum parakkak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam eḻutuvatē ilakkaṇam eṉakkaruti eḻutappaṭṭavātal vēṇṭum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār
"	viṉiyē ceyvatu ceyappaṭu poruḷē
 nilaṉē kālam karuvi yeṉṟā
iṉṉataṟ kitupaya ṉāka veṉṉum
aṉṉa marapi ṉiraṇṭoṭun tokii
āyeṭ ṭeṉpa toḻiṉmuta ṉiliyē"
eṉpataṉāl, viṉi tōṉṟutaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷik kūṟi,
"	avitām,
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṉṟuva kuṉṟum"
eṉpataṉāl, cila viṉikkaṇ avaṟṟuṭ kuṉṟat takuvaṉa cila kuṉṟivarum eṉṟār. cēṉāvariyar "kuṉṟattakuvaṉavāvaṉa ceyappaṭu poruḷum ēṟpatum payaṉumām" eṉa ataṉi viḷakki, koṭiyāṭiṟṟu, vaḷivaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉa avi kuṉṟi varutaṟku utāraṇaṅkāṭṭiṉar. eṉavē cila viṉikaḷiṟ ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟivarutal iyalpu. kāṇṭal viṉiyō ceyap paṭuporuḷ kuṉṟātatu. ceyappaṭu poruḷi viritturikkum "kāppiṉ oppiṉ" eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattil "nōkkaliṉ" eṉa varutal kāṇka. kāppu mutalāyiṉavellām poruḷpaṟṟi ōtappaṭṭaṉavākaliṉ, nōkkal eṉpatil apporuḷ paṟṟivarum kāṇṭal, pārttal eṉpaṉavum aṭaṅkum. eṉavē, kāṇ eṉpataṉaṭiyākavarum viṉiccoṟ kaḷi, purāṇak katikaḷum pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, cilavaṟṟil ovvoru collē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟiṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkutalē marapākum. uriyāciriyar palarum ammarapu piṟaḻāmalē vaḻaṅki varuvārāyiṉar.
"	ceyappaṭu poruḷic ceytatu pōlat
toḻiṟpaṭak kiḷattalum vaḻakkiyaṉ marapē"
eṉṉuñ cūttiram vaḻakkiṉkaṇ orōvaḻic ceyappaṭu poruḷ viṉimutal pōlak kūṟappaṭum marapu vaḻuvi amit taṟkeḻuntatē. avvaḻuvamitiyiyē mēṟkoṇṭu nūl urikaḷil yāṇṭum eḻututal muṟimiyaṉṟām. iṉi, vēṟṟumoḻip payiṟci oṉṟē koṇṭu tamiḻiṉ iyalpiṉi viḷakkaluṟuvār cilar tolkāppiyattilē ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyippaṟṟiya kuṟippu illi eṉavum, ākaliṉ tamiḻiṉ kaṇ ceyappāṭṭuviṉi iṉṟeṉavum kūṟāniṟpar. ceyappaṭu poruḷ eṉṉum peyariliruntē ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyuṇmi peṟappaṭuvataṉiyum, tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttirattiṉ toṭakkamē "eḻutteṉappaṭupa" eṉa ataṉik kuṟippiṭu taliyum,
"	collap paṭṭaṉa ellā māṇpum
maṟutali yāyiṉum maṟṟatu citivē"
eṉap piṟāṇṭum aḵtu āḷappaṭutaliyum avar aṟiyār. reṭṭiyār ceyappāṭṭuviṉi palavaṟṟit tām vaḻaṅkutaliṉ akkoḷkiyiṉar allareṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ōr arip
pakkattilē (pak.137) "kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa, tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa, kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa" eṉṟiṅṅaṉam eṭṭiṭattil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷami kāṇka. aṅṅaṉamākavum kāṇṭal viṉi oṉṟiṉmaṭṭil avar karuttu māṟupaṭṭatu eṉṉiyō? āyiṉ, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉa ōriṭattum avar vaḻaṅkāmaṟ pōkavilli. eṉiṉum muṉṉatu peru varaviṟṟākavum, piṉṉatu ciṟuvaraviṟṟākavum iruttali nōkkiṉ, kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpataṉiyē avar niṉivu kūrntu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār eṉpatum, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatu payiṟci vayattāl avaraṟiyāmalē pōntatām eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. kaṟpōr putuvatākavuḷḷa taṉiyē viyappuṭaṉ nōkku varātaliṉ kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpatē avaruḷḷattil nilipeṟuva tākum. oru palkalikkaḻakattiṉ ārāycci viṉiñarāl eḻutappaṭṭu, uyarnta tāḷiṟ pariya uruviṉatākap polivuṟa acciṭappaṭṭuḷḷa puttakattiṟ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ ettuṇiyar ivviḻi vaḻakkiṉi viḻumiya vaḻakkākak koṇṭaṉarō eṉa uṉṉa accamuṇṭākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil yāṇṭum nakkīrar eṉa vaḻaṅkuñcol itil naṟkīrar eṉa āḷappaṭṭuḷatu. itaṉ āciriyar muṉṉar eḻutiya 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉum nakkīrar eṉṟē palaviṭattum vantuḷatu. annūliṉ kīḻk kuṟippukkaḷuḷ oṉṟilētāṉ "natkīrar eṉpatu naṟkīrar eṉa vaḻaṅkiya colliṉ tiripākalām eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu" eṉa vaṭanūl oṉṟilē kaṇṭa 'natkīrar' eṉṉum vaṭivukku viḷakkam eḻutuḻi naṟkīrar eṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷatu. innūliṉkaṇ nakkīrar eṉṉum vaḻakku aṟavē kiviṭappeṟṟu, naṟkīrar eṉpatē vaḻaṅkumāṟu mēṟkoḷḷap paṭṭuḷatu. "iṉi, nallantuvaṉār nalliṟiyaṉār nalluruttiraṉār eṉa uyir mutaṉmoḻikaḷ vantaviṭattum, naṉṉākaṉār naṉṉākiyār eṉamelleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻikaḷ vanta viṭattum, nalviḷakkaṉār nalveḷḷiyār nalvēṭṭaṉār eṉa iṭiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻikaḷ vanta viṭattum nal eṉṉum aṭimoḻiyē vantiruttaliṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻikaḷ varumiṭattum naleṉṉum aṭimoḻi niṟṟalē muṟiyāmāka lāṉum, naṟkāṭci nalloḻukkam naṉñāṉam eṉṟu ciṟappuṭip poruḷkaḷik kūṟumiṭattum nal eṉṉum aṭimoḻiyē vantiruttalāṉum, piṟavāṟṟāṉum naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ collē amivuṭiyateṉṟum, nakkīrar eṉṉuñcol marūumoḻi eṉṟum tērntu, naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ colli mēṟ koṇṭuḷḷēṉ" eṉa attuṇipiṟku ētuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ collē nakkīrar eṉat tirintatākal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉkaruttiṟkum icintatē. nāṉkāṇṭu kaḷiṉ muṉ karantit tamiḻc caṅkattiṉ oru ciṟappukkūṭṭattil, 'nallantuvaṉār' eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟiyāṉ pēciyapoḻutu, navvantuvaṉār eṉa vaḻaṅkutal poruntāteṉa viḷakkuḻi itaṉi viritturikkalāṉēṉ. eṉiṉum cāṉṟōr vaḻakkoṭu muraṇi naṟkīrar eṉa vaḻaṅkutal takutiyaṉṟu eṉa eṇṇi yāṉ avvaḻakkiṉi mēṟkoṇṭilēṉ. valliṉa mutaṉmoḻikaḷ varumiṭattu māttiramē nakkaṇṇiyār, nakkīraṉār, nacceḷḷiyār, nattattaṉār, nappacaliyār, nappaṇṇaṉār, nappālattaṉār eṉa nal eṉpatu tirintu varutaliṉ ataṟku ētu eṉṉeṉa ārāytalē muṟimiyām. vaḻaṅkutaṟku eṇmiyatu ātalē mutalil ataṟkuk kāraṇamātal vēṇṭum. valleḻuttu āṟaṉuḷ kacatapa eṉṉum nāṉku meyyum uṭaṉiliyākavaṉṟi vēṟu meyyuṭaṉ mayaṅkutal illi. ataṟkuk kāraṇam arumuyaṟciyuṭaṉ ataṉikkūṟa vēṇṭiyiruttalē. tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ mayakka vitiyellām avi perumpālum eḷiya muyaṟciyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkutaṟ payattavē. ṭa ṟa eṉṉum iraṇṭu meyyum ka ca pa eṉpavaṟṟōṭu mayaṅkumāyiṉum ataṉi vaḻaṅkutaṟkuc ciṟitu arumuyaṟci vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟatu.
"	kappat tintiraṉ kāṭṭiya nūliṉ
meyppāṭ ṭiyaṟki viḷaṅkak kāṇāy"
eṉa oru moḻiyilum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ṟakarattip pakaramākat tirittiruppatu kāṇka. makaḷir pēccil nappeṇṭu, nappiḷḷi, naccol eṉpaṉa kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟṟāl, oru kuṟiliṉpiṉ varum ṟakaravoṟṟu vēṟu meykaḷōṭu mayaṅkiṉ ciṟitu vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatātalpeṟappaṭum. aḵtu uṭaṉiliyāka mayaṅkuḻiyum attakiyatē. iḷampirāyat tiṉarip pēcuvittu ippoḻutum ataṉi aṟital cālum; eṉiṉum atukoṇṭē attakiya coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tirittu vaḻaṅkutal karuttaṉṟām.
nakkīrar mutaliya vaḻakkup paṇṭutoṭṭu nilipeṟṟu varuvatoṉṟu. iraṇṭu tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇaṅkaḷilum, cīkāḷattip purāṇam mutaliyavaṟṟilum nakkīrar eṉṉuñcol palaviṭattu vantuḷatu. curuṅkavurikkiṉ paḻiyavum putiyavumāṉa nūl, urikaḷ yāvaṟṟiṉum naṟkīrar eṉpatu aṉṟu, nakkīrar eṉpatē kāṇappaṭuvatu eṉṉalām. eṭṭuttoki, pattup pāṭṭukkaḷilē pāṭiṉōr peyarip pulappaṭuttum kuṟippuk kaḷil nakkīraṉār, nacceḷḷiyār mutaliya peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅki yiruttaliṉ avi tokukkappeṟṟa attuṇip paḻaṅkālan toṭaṅkiyēāṉṟōr palarāṉum avi aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkap
peṟṟaṉa eṉpatu pōtarum. muṟkālattu nallicip pulavarkaḷ oruvar oruvarip pārāṭṭum iyalpiṉar eṉpatu
"	pulaṉaḻuk kaṟṟa antaṇāḷaṉ
parantici niṟkap pāṭiṉaṉ"
"	iṉṟum paraṇaṉ pāṭiṉaṉ"
"	antuvaṉ pāṭiya cantukeḻu neṭuvari"
eṉṉum ivimutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. avarkaḷ oruvar ceyyuḷiluḷḷa ciṟappuṭiya toṭarkaḷiṉ cuviyi nukarntu iṉpuṟutalum, avaṟṟiyum, avaṟṟi iyaṟṟiṉōriyum pārāṭṭutalum uṭiyar eṉpatu oruvar pāṭṭiluḷḷa coṟ
ṟoṭar aṅṅaṉamē palaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷil varutalāṉum, patiṟṟuppattiluḷḷa ovvoru pāṭṭukkaḷum maṟam vīṅkupal pukaḻ, cāṉṟōr meymmaṟi eṉṟivvāṟu avaṟṟil vantuḷḷa ciṟappuṭiya toṭarkaḷāṟ peyar kūṟappaṭutalāṉum, cila pulavarkaḷ aṇilāṭu muṉṟilār, irumpiṭarttaliyār eṉṟiṅṅaṉam aṉṉōr pāṭṭiluḷḷa toṭarpaṟṟi eḻunta ciṟappup peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkap peṟutalāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam ciṟanta pulavaroruvar peyariṉ muṉṉuḷḷa nal eṉṉum aṭimoḻiyit tirittu nakkīraṉār eṉṟāṟpōla oruvar vaḻaṅkā niṟka ēṉōrum ataṉip pārāṭṭi avvāṟu vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriya piṟapulavar peyarkaḷiyum avvaṇam vaḻaṅkiṉārātal vēṇṭum. avvaḻakkē piṉṉarum pāṭṭu urikaḷiṟpayiṉṟu varuvatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam payiṟcimikka valiyuṭiya vaḻakkiṉip puṟakkaṇittu 'naṟkīrar' eṉa aḻivaḻakkut toṭarvatu muṟiyaṉṟām. ippeyariṉi iṅṅaṉam tirittōr iṉi nakkaṇṇiyār, nacceḷḷiyār mutaliya ēṉip peyarkaḷiyum tirittu vaḻaṅkum iyalpuṭiyarāvar. iṅṅaṉam ivartām niṉittapaṭiyellām coṟkaḷiṉ uruvimāṟṟi vaḻaṅkuvarēl, atu teluṅku, maliyāḷam pōlum piṟitoru moḻiyāvataṉṟit tamiḻākātu eṉka.

19. ceppamuṭiyārkkuc ceyyum viṇṇappam
vittuvāṉ tiru vēṅkaṭarācalu avarkaḷ, 
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam, ceṉṉi.
yāṉ eḻutiya 'kapilar' eṉṉum ārāycci nūlippaṟṟit tiru. na.mu.vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭāravarkaḷ 'tamiḻppoḻi'lil (13-2) eḻutiyirukkuṅ kaṭṭuriyip pārttēṉ. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ, eṉatu ārāycci tūymiyuṟutaṟ poruṭṭum tamiḻ vaḷartaṟ
poruṭṭum annūlil (kapilar) taṅkaṭku amitiyilavākat tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟi veḷiyiṭuvatākak kūṟi, mutaṟkaṇ, cila coṟkaḷik kuṟittu atil eḻutiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. naṇpaṉatu māciṉip pōkkit tūymip paṭuttuvatu naṉmakkaḷ ceyalēyaṉṟō? atu karuti nāṭṭāravarkaḷiṉ peruntakimiyip pārāṭṭutalōṭu avarkaḷ pāl naṉṟiyaṟitalum uṭiyēṉ. avarkaḷ ārāyntirukkiṉṟa coṟkaḷāvaṉa:
1. 	mukittal
2. 	āṅkaṉam aṅkaṉam, īṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṅkaṉam
3. 	kāṇkiṉṟatu, kāṇkiṉṟaṉa
4. 	naṟkīrar
	eṉpaṉa.1 ivaṟṟuḷ, mukittal eṉṉuñ collik kuṟittu avarkaḷ 	eḻutiyiruppataṉ curukkam:
1. 	mukittal eṉpatu ticiccol.
2. 	atu tamiḻilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil (oru  tala purāṇattil ōriṭattilaṉṟi)  		vaḻaṅkappaṭavilli.
3. 	caṅkap pulavarippaṟṟiya ārāycci nūlil, piṟkālattu mika arukivantuḷḷa 	colli vaḻaṅkutal nērmiyaṉṟu.
4.	 mukittal eṉṉuñcol aruvaruppiṉi viḷippatu. iḵtu, ataṉi 	nūlmuḻuvatum vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉē aṟiyappaṭum.
ivaṟṟi muṟiyē ārāytum:1
1.	mukittal eṉṉuñcol ticiccol eṉpatu etaṉāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatō, aṟikilēṉ. atu teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉ ticiccollākum eṉiṉ, (avvāṟāyiṉ) tamiḻccoṟkaḷuṭ pallāyirañ coṟkaḷ ticiccoṟkaḷē
yākum. paḻiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātataṉāl, atu ticiccollākum eṉiṉ, avaṟṟil vaḻaṅkātaṉavellām ticic coṟkaḷēyākumaṉṟō? āyiṉ, nāṉ nīṅkaḷ, anta2, inta, enta, cari, aṉuppu, ciri mutaliyaṉavellām paḻiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātaṉavākaliṉ ticiccoṟkaḷēyākum. avvāṟāyiṉ collārāyccit tiṟaṉillāta pulavar, nāṉ, nīṅkaḷ mutaliya coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉum, ataṉiyuṭiya nāṭṭāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal takātaṉṟē! aṟṟaṉṟu. nāṉ eṉpatu yāṉ eṉpataṉ marūu; nīṅkaḷ3 eṉpatu nīyir eṉpataṉ marūu; anta, inta, enta eṉpavi a, i, e eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ marūu. ākaliṉ, avi tamiḻc coṟkaḷē. ciri eṉpatu paṇṭu illāviṭiṉum piṉṉart tamiḻiṟṟōṉṟiyatuvē eṉṟu kūṟuvārkaḷpōlum. iṉi, aṉuppu eṉpatu viṭukkum eṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟum, ciri eṉpatu naki eṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟuṅ kūṟiviṭalāmpōlum.
mukittal eṉpatu ticiccol eṉṟē koḷḷiṉum, ceyyuḷilum vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, ataṉi urinaṭiyil vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟātal yāṅkaṉam? ticiccel vaḻaṅkalākātu eṉṟu ilakkaṇam uḷatō? āyiṉ 'aṉuppu' eṉṉuñ colli nāṭṭāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal takātaṉṟē?4 accol (aṉuppu) paṇṭic ceyyuṭkaḷil yāṇṭum vantilāmiyāṉum, teluṅkil maṭṭumē uṇmiyāṉum atu teluṅkiṉiṉṟu vantatātal kūṭum.5
2. mukittal eṉṉuñcol talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattu maṭṭum vantuḷatu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. aḵtu aruṇakirinātar tiruppukaḻil palaviṭattil vantuḷatu. tamiḻleksikaṉil iccolli nōkkiya nāṭṭāravarkaḷ atiṟkuṟittirukkun tiruppukaḻaṭikaḷik kaṇṭiruttal kūṭumē. aṅkaṉamākavum avarkaḷ, 'piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvatu' eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppatu viyappiṉi viḷikkiṉṟatu. aruṇakirinātarkālam 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟu teritaliṉ iṟṟikku 500 yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iccol tamiḻil vaḻaṅki vantuḷatu teḷivākum. paḻiya ōliyēṭukaḷiṉ iṟutiyil, muṟṟiṟṟu eṉṟātal, mukintatu eṉṟātal eḻutiyiruttali ōlic cuvaṭi nōkkiṉār naṉkaṟivar. ākaliṉ, iccolvaḻakkup putiyataṉṟu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭu maṉṟē? niṟka. tamiḻilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil tamiḻmoḻic coṟkaḷ ellām vantuḷḷaṉavō? vantilātavaṟṟiyum vaḻaṅkumpōḻtu, vantuḷḷataṉi vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu eṉṟal amiyumā ṟeṉṉiyō!
3. caṅkappulavarippaṟṟi eḻutum urinūlil, piṟkālattu vanta colli vaḻaṅkutal takātu eṉṟārkaḷ. avvāṟu caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vantilāta coṟkaḷi mēṟkoḷḷāteḻutiṉ aḵtu ikkālattu urinūlātalē illi. niṟka. caṅkappulavar ceyyuḷukku eḻutiyirukkum urikaḷil, acceyyuṭkaḷil vantilātaṉavākiya uppukkaṇṭam, kaṇṭa carukkari, kiṭāram, kuttalarici, cumaṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷi uriyāḷar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karuttiṟkuc ciṟitum amiyātaṉṟē? caṅkap pulavar tañceyyuṭkaḷiṉ uriyilē ittakiya piṟkālattuc coṟkaḷ vantirukka, avarik kuṟittu ikkālattil eḻutum urinūliṟ piṟkālattuc colli -caṅkakālattu vaḻaṅkāta colli - eḻututal kūṭātu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ colla villi. vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu eṉpatu amivuṭiyatākumō?
4. mukittal eṉṉuñ colli nūṉ muḻuvatilum vaḻaṅkāta taṉāl accol eḻutiṉōrukkē aruvaruppiṉi viḷit tirukkum eṉṟārkaḷ. mukintatu eṉpatu muṟṟiṟṟu - pūrttiyāyiṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷatu. iccolli nūṉ muḻuvatilum evvāṟu vaḻaṅkutal kūṭum?
āṅkaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ:
āṅkaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷik kuṟittu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppataṉ karuttukkaḷ:
1. naṉṉūlār aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi vitikūṟi amittuḷār.
2. aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟi avvaṭivil vaḻaṅka maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷḷatu.
3. 'putiyatoru vāypāṭu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu marapu nili tirittup poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟuceyvatē yākum'.
4. yāṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉi yāṅkaṉam eṉa etukit toṭiyil pulavar tirittaṉar.
5. aṅkaṉam eṉpatu vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷimiyuṭiyatu; aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu arumiyuṭiyatu. ākaliṉ, aṅkaṉam eṉpatu aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu.
6. 'aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi a-ṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarttuvatu tolkāppiyaṉār karuttaṉṟām. ākaliṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum eṉa avar vitittilar'.
7. 'meynnili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colliyē tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟittār.
8. āṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam eṉpatu piḻiyuḷḷatōr muṭipu.
9. aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colvaḻakkup paḻamiyatē-eṉpaṉa.
ivaṟṟi muṟiyē nōkkutum:
1. naṉṉūlār aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷukku vitikūṟiyiruttalāṉ avi vaḻaṅkutal kūṭum eṉpatuvē pōtarutalaṉṟi, āṅkaṉam, īṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu eṉpatu evvāṟu pōtarum? avar aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟi amittilarāyiṉ, avaṟṟi mēṟkoḷḷātu viṭuttal kūṭum. amit tuḷḷārākaliṉ avaṟṟi vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟaṉṟu. ituvēyaṉṟi, āṅkaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟi vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭātu.
2. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi avvaṭivil vaḻaṅkutali yāṉ yāṇṭum maṟuttilēṉ. yāṉ maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷatu, eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppatuvē viyappākavuḷḷatu. māṇavarporuṭṭu yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpavaṟṟiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ.
	3. aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam mutaliyavi putiyavāypāṭā, āṅkaṉam....yāṅkaṉam mutaliyavi putiya vāypāṭā? caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷilum tolkāppiyattilum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷa āṅkaṉam.....yāṅkaṉam mutaliyavi putiyavāypāṭum piṟkālattu nūlkaḷilē vantuḷḷa aṅṅaṉam......yāṅṅaṉam mutaliyavi paḻiyavāypāṭum ākumāyiṉ, piṉṉi yātutāṉ yātākātu?
caṅkacceyyuṭkaḷil āṅkaṉam.....yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi aṟinta aṟiñarkaṭku, yāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa 'kapilaril vantirukkum āṅkaṉam....yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ viḷaṅkātō? atu poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟu ceyvatātal eppaṭi? aṅṅaṉam.......yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatē marapu eṉṟum avaṟṟi aṅkaṉam.....yāṅkaṉam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu marapaṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟi, 'marapunili tiriyiṟ piṟitupiṟitākum' eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattiyum, ataṟku, 'marapiṉi nili tirittuc collupa eṉiṉ, ulakattuc collellām poruḷiḻantu piṟitu piṟitākum' eṉṉum pērāciriyaruri yiyum nāṭṭāravarkaḷ ātāramākak kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. accūttiravuriyiṉiṟutiyil, 'cevippulaṉāya ōcikēṭṭuk kaṭpulaṉāyaporuḷ uṇarvatellām marapupaṟṟāka allatu maṟṟilli eṉṟavāṟu' eṉṟu pērāciriyarē eḻutiyiruppatil nāṭṭāravarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭam ceṉṟilatupōlum. ivvuriyiṉiyum mēliyiraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷiyum nōkkuvōrkku īṇṭuk kūṟiya marapu eṉpatu iṉṉateṉat tāṉēpōtarum. 'irutiṇi aimpāṟ poruḷkaḷit tiṇiyum pālum vaḻuvāmal taḻuvutal vēṇṭum' eṉṟu, 'nilantī nīrvaḷi' eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkūṟi, am marapu tirital ceyyuṭkaḷukku illi eṉpataṉi aṭutta cūttirattiṟ kūṟi, am marapu tiriyil poruḷ piṟitākum eṉpataṉi 'marapu nilitiriyiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattil āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. uriyāḷarum ivvāṟē poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. iṉi, nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvatuvē iccūttirattiṉ poruḷ eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, āṅkaṉam..........yāṅkaṉam eṉpaṉavē paḻiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttaliṉ avaṟṟi āṅṅaṉam, aṅṅaṉam.........yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu eḻutiṉōraṉṟē marapunili tirittavarāvar? avi (aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavi) yaṉṟē poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟu viḷittirukkum?
4. yāṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉiyē etukit toṭiyil yāṅkaṉam eṉṟu pulavar tirittaṉar eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppatu uṇmiyākumā? paṇṭik kālattil āciriyappākkaḷil etuki yiruntē tīravēṇṭum eṉṉum niyati uṇṭō? piṟkālattilum - ikkālattilum - āciriya vaṭikaḷi etuki yillāmal amittuppāṭum vaḻakkāṟu uḷatē. tolkāppiyam centoṭiyik kūṟiyiruttalāṉaṉṟē paṇṭippulavar tañceyyuḷil etuki mōṉikaḷi niyatiyākak koḷḷātu viṭuttaṉar. piṟkālattup pulavarēpōṉṟu etukikkākac coṟkaḷit tirikkum vaḻakkam paṇṭip pulavarpāl uṇṭeṉpatu nāṭṭāravarkaḷāl yāṉaṟinta putucceytiyē.
'yāṅṅaṉam ottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam' eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukiyātaṟku iḻukkiṉṟām. aṅṅaṉamākavum etukimōṉikaḷiṉ nirppantam illāta kālattil - illāta pāvil, pulavaroruvarallar- palarum 1-accolli vaḻaṅkiya pulavaraṉivarumē-ṅakarattik kakaramākat tirittē vaḻaṅkiṉār eṉpatu ettuṇi amiviṉatu! ṅakarattōṭu piṟappoṟṟumiyum oliyoṟṟumiyum uṭiyatu kakaram oṉṟē eṉṟu eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. kakaram ṅakarattōṭu oliyoṟṟumi uṭiyatu eṉpatum eṉakkup putiyatē. 'ka'ṅakkaḷ muyaṟciyāṉum māttiriyāṉum kuḷakkari kuḷaṅkari eṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭiyottu iṉamāyiṉa' eṉṟē eḻutiyuḷḷār mayili nātar. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ oliyoṟṟumi yuṭiyatu eṉṟatu eppaṭi eṉaviḷaṅkavilli. ṅakaramum arimāttiriyatu, kakaramum arimarattiriyatu. ākaliṉ avi oliyoṟṟumi yuṭiyavākum eṉiṉ, cakaramum arimāttiriyatē; ēṉ ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum arimāttiriyavē. ākaliṉ ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum ṅakarattōṭu oliyoṟṟumi uṭiyavēyākum. ṅakaramum kakaramum olivēṟṟumiyuṭiya vākalāṉaṉṟē vēṟu vēṟu iṉaṅkaḷāyiṉa. 'oḻinta melleḻuttiyum iṭiyeḻuttiyum nōkkit tām valleṉṟicit talāṉum valleṉat talivaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, melleṉṟicit talāṉum melleṉṟu mūkkiṉvaḷiyāṟ piṟattaṉāṉum mel leḻuttāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiyirut taliṉ kakara ṅakaraṅkaḷ olivēṟṟumiyuṭiyavātal aṟiyap paṭumē.
iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyal mūṉṟāñ cūttiram 'āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ' eṉṟē paṭikkap paṭṭu varukiṉṟatu eṉṟārkaḷ. accūttiram muḻuvatum īṇṭut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
'	āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ ṟaṉvayiṟ
pāṅki ṉōriṟ kuṟitalip peytalum
pāṅkilaṉ ṟamiyō ḷiṭantalip paṭalumeṉ
ṟāṅka viraṇṭē talippeyaṉ marapē'
eṉpatu accūttiram. cūttirattiṉ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉkām aṭikaḷi nōkkiṉ, mutalaṭi, 'āṅkaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ ṟaṉvayiṉ' eṉṟē amintiruntirukkum eṉpatu eḷitiṟ pulaṉākum. uṇmip pāṭam atuvēyātal ōliyēṭukaḷi nōkkiṉ aṟiyalākum. eṉṉeṉiṉ, paḻiya patippukkaḷil,
'	āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ aḷaviṟan taṉavē
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarntōr paṉṉuṅ kāli'
eṉṟu patikkappaṭṭirunta tolkāppiyac cūttiram, ippoḻutu tiru. vē. turicāmi aiyaravarkaḷik koṇṭu paricōtittut tiru. kaṉakacapip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa patippil,
'	āṅkaṉam virippiṉ aḷaviṟan taṉavē,
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarntōr paṉṉuṅ kāli'
eṉṟu patikkap paṭṭuḷatu. turicāmiaiyaravarkaḷ pāṭattit tiruttiviṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiviṭuvārkaḷ pōlum.
akanāṉūṟṟil, 'yāṅṅaṉam' eṉṟu patippittuḷḷa mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum (ceyyuḷ 27, 90, 378) tiru es. viyāpurip piḷḷiyavarkaḷiṭamirukkum paḻiya ōliyēṭṭil 'yāṅkaṉam' eṉṟē irukkiṉṟatu. iḵtu ēṭeḻutiṉōr tavaṟu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ eṉceyvatu!
accupputtakaṅkaḷil āṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu patit tiruttaṟkuk kāraṇam avarkkuḷḷa naṉṉūṟpayiṟciyē yākum. mahāmahōpātyāya, ṭākṭar srīmat aiyaravarkaḷiṉ  caṅkanūṟpatippukkaḷil ivvāṟu vantilāmi nōkkat takkatu.
5. vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ col, vaḻaṅkutaṟku aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu eṉṟārkaḷ. itaṉi aṟiyiṉ, melloṟṟut taṉ iṉavalleḻuttōṭu kūṭi varuñcol vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyatu eṉpatum melleḻuttu iraṭṭittu varum col vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatu eṉpatum avarkaḷ karuttātal naṉkupulaṉākum. ākavē, vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya 
oṉṟu eṉpatu vaḻaṅkutaṟ karitākiya oṉṉu eṉṟum,
aṉṟu	    "		"	       aṉṉu eṉṟum,
kaṉṟu	    "		"	       kaṉṉu eṉṟum,
eṉṟu	    "		"	       eṉṉu eṉṟum,
vaḻaṅkutal eṉṉi? aṉṟu mutaliyavi maliyāḷattil aṉṉu mutaliyavāyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟiyum, maṅkalam kañci cantaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ ammoḻiyil muṟiyē maṅṅalam kaññi cannaṉam eṉṟu iruvaḻakkilum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. oṉṉu aṉṉu kaṉṉu eṉṉu eṉpaviyē vaḻaṅkutaṟ kariyavākaliṉ oṉṟu aṉṟu kaṉṟu eṉṟu eṉat tirintaṉa eṉpatum, avvāṟē, maṅṅalam kaññi cannaṉam eṉpaviyē vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyavākiya maṅkalam kañci cantaṉam eṉṟu tirintaṉa eṉpatum nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karuttātal vēṇṭum. vaḻaṅkutaṟku aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉpatuvē vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉat tirintatu eṉṟavarkaṭku, vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyavākiya maṅṅalam cannaṉam eṉpaviyē vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyavākiya maṅkalam cantaṉam eṉṟāyiṉa eṉpataṉṟē amitiyākum. iṉi, māṅṅā tēṅṅā eṉṟu maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkutal pōṉṟu tamiḻilum mikappaḻiya kālattil vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum avi vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyavākaliṉ, māṅkāy tēṅkāy eṉṟu tiriyalāyiṉa eṉṟum nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷ. eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ṅṅoli vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatum ṅka oli vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyatum ākum eṉpatu avarkaḷ koḷkiyākaliṉ eṉka.
6. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi a+ṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarppatu tolkāppiyarkkuk karuttaṉṟākaliṉ, avar, ṅakaram moḻimutal varum eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar eṉṟārkaḷ. āyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭa poruḷ itu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟavilli. attaṉmi, appaṭi eṉpatu avar koṇṭa poruḷ eṉpārkaḷāyiṉ, āṇṭu, akaram cuṭṭupporuḷ tarutaliṉ ṅakaram moḻimutal vantatākum. ākavē, aññālam annāḷ ammaṇi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷip pirittu, 
'	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ña na ma t tōṉṟiṉ
oṭṭiya oṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum'
eṉṟu vitikūṟiya āciriyar,
'cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ṅa ña na ma t tōṉṟiṉ'
eṉṟu vitikūṟi aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉiyum amittiruppārallavō? accam aññi aṇṇaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiluḷḷa akarattip pōlavē aṅṅaṉam eṉpatilum akarañ colluṟuppāy amintuḷatu eṉṟālaṉṟi, aṅṅaṉam eṉpatil ṅakaram moḻimutal vantatākak karutappaṭāmi amiyātu. avvāṟu koḷḷiṉ, aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉ poruḷ yātu?
viṉittoki paṇputtokikaḷip pirittup puṇark kalākātu eṉṉuṅ karuttāl ataṟkup puṇarcciviti kūṟātatu pōṉṟu aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṟkum puṇarcciviti kūṟiṟṟilar eṉṟārkaḷ. kolyāṉi aṭukaḷiṟu ōṭukāl eṉṉum viṉit tokikaḷil uḷḷa yāṉi kaḷiṟu kāl eṉpaṉavum, karuṅ kutiri karumpārppāṉ centāmari veṇkuṭi eṉṉum paṇputtokikaḷil kutiri pārppāṉ tāmari kuṭi eṉpaṉavum taṉittum vaḻaṅkutalpōṉṟu, aṅṅaṉam eṉpatilum ṅaṉam eṉpatu taṉittu vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭumē. taṉittu vaḻaṅkiṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutalil varumeṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭumē. viṉittoki paṇputtokikaṭku āciriyar puṇarcciviti kūṟātataṉ kāraṇam- 'kol eṉat toḻiṉmi uṇara niṉṟatu kolyāṉi eṉat tokiyāyavaḻi koṉṟa eṉak kālaṅ kāṭṭi niṉṟamiyiṉ puṇarkkappaṭātāyiṟṟu.' 'karum eṉap paṇpuṇara niṉṟatu karuñcāṉṟāṉ eṉat tokiyāyavaḻi, karum eṉpatu kariyāṉ eṉap pālkāṭṭi niṟṟaliṟ puṇarkkappaṭātāyiṟṟu' eṉṟu uriyāciriyarum, 'āṭaraṅku.........eṉa nilam mutalākiya peyareccan tokka viṉittokikaḷi virikkuṅkāl āṭiṉavaraṅku eṉac ceyta eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟuvirinta akarīṟu iṟappuṇarttiyum, āṭāniṉṟavaraṅku, āṭumaraṅku eṉac ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum avaṟṟāṉāya puṭi peyarcciyit tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum oru coṟkaṇ oruṅku tokkuniṟṟaliṉ ataṉi oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakkip puṇarkkalākāmiyiṉ, puṇark kalākātu eṉṟār.' 'karumpārppāṉ karumpārppaṉi karum pārppār karuṅkutiri karuṅkutirikaḷ eṉa varum ivaṟṟuṭ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ  kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi, kariyarākiya pārppār kariyatākiya kutiri kariyaṉavākiya kutirikaḷ eṉa aimpāliṉiyum uṇarttum paṇpukoḷ peyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka. ivaṟṟuṭ karumi eṉṉum paṇpuppeyar tokkatēl karumiyākiya pārppāṉ eṉa virittal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam viriyāmiyiṟ paṇpukoḷ peyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka....... iṅṅaṉam aimpālun tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉiliyātaliṟ puṇarttalākāmi kūṟiṉār' eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟiyiruppavaṟṟāṉ naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. iṉṉatō riṭarppāṭu aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colliluḷḷa akarac cuṭṭiṉkaṇ iṉmiyiṉ, ataṟku vitikūṟutal eḷitiṉiyalumaṉṟē.' ivaṟṟāl, viṉittoki paṇputtokikaḷukku vitikūṟa iyalāmal viṭuttatupōla aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉiyum vitikūṟātu viṭuttār eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiyatu ciṟitum poruntāmi naṉkaṟiyappaṭum.
7. 'meynili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl, aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷit tolkāppiyaṉār amit tuḷḷār eṉṟārkaḷ. 'ra ḻa oḻinta maṟṟiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷ tammoṭu tām mayaṅkum' eṉpatuvē cūttirattiṉ poruḷ. itaṉāl, ṅakarattoṭu ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiyē āciriyar karutiṉār eṉṟu tuṇital amiyātu. āyiṉ, uriyāciriyar, eṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colliyē utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉārāl eṉiṉ, ataṉi nōkkutum;
'	ya ra ḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum'
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uriyil, ya ra ḻakkaḷukku muṉ ṅakaram varutaṟku utāraṇamāka avar (uriyāciriyar,)
vēyṅaṉam
vērṅaṉam
vīḻṅaṉam
eṉpavaṟṟik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ivvutāraṇaṅkaḷil, vēy, vēr, vīḻ eṉpavi nilimoḻikaḷākaliṉ, ṅaṉam eṉpatu varumoḻi eṉa naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. ākavē, ṅakaram moḻimutal vantatākumaṉṟē? ivvutāraṇamē āciriyar karutiṉārāyiṉ, 'mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum' eṉṟu ṅakarattip pirittuk kūṟutaliṉṟi, 'mutalākeḻuttut tōṉṟum eṉpa' eṉpatu pōlaṉṟē cūttirañ ceytiruppār? ṅakaram moḻi
mutal vārātataṉāṉaṉṟē ataṉi mutalākeḻuttōṭu cērkkātu pirittuk kūṟiṉār? iccūttirattiṟku uriyāciriyar kāṭṭiya 'vēyṅaṉam' mutaliyavi utāraṇamākumō? itupōṉṟē, 'meynili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uriyil avar kāṭṭiya 'eṅṅaṉam' eṉṉum utāraṇamum amivuṭiyataṉṟu. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil vaḻaṅkiyirukkumēl tolkāppiyattilum caṅkanūlkaḷilum āṅṅaṉam īṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉavaṉṟē kāṇappaṭal vēṇṭum. avvāṟiṉṟi, āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉpaṉavē kāṇappaṭutaṟku niyāyam yātu? āyiṉ, ṅakarattiṉmuṉ ṅakām varutaṟku utāraṇam yātō eṉiṉ, 'piṭiyūṭṭip piṉṉuṇṇuṅṅ kaḷiṟeṉavu murittaṉarē' eṉṉum aṭiyil ṅakaram iraṇṭu vantiruttalpōlvatu utāraṇamākum. aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu pōlat taṉimey muṉṉar uyirmey vantatuvē amiyum; iḵtu amiyātu eṉiṉ, taṉimeyyōṭu taṉimeymayaṅku
tali iccūttirattāṟ koḷḷākkāl 'kaṇṇ karuviḷi,' 'poṉṉ poṟi cuṇaṅku' eṉpaṉapōla varupavaṟṟilellām meykaḷ mayaṅki niṟṟaṟku ilakkaṇamiṉṟākum. aṉṟiyum, taṉimeyyōṭu uyirmey mayaṅkiya coṟkaḷi utāraṇaṅ
kāṭṭiṉum āṇṭu meyyoṭu meymayaṅkiya tuṇiyē utāraṇamākak koḷḷappaṭutal karutattakum. meymayakkam eṉpatu meyyoṭu meymayaṅkutalēyākum. iṉi, ṅakara
meyyōṭu ṅakaruyirmey mayaṅkiya col iruntiruppiṉ aḵtu utāraṇamātaṟkut taṭṭilli.
8. āṅkaṉam..........yāṅkaṉam eṉpavaṟṟip piḻiyuḷḷatōr muṭipu eṉṟārkaḷ. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum caṅkappulavar palarum vaḻaṅkiyirukkuñ collē piḻiyuḷḷatāyiṉ, piḻi yillāta colyātō, aṟiyēṉ.
9. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakkup paḻamiyatē eṉṟārkaḷ. paḻamiyatāyiṉ caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vantirukkumē āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉum vaṭivaccoṟkaḷē caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ōriṭattilātal āṅṅaṉam aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟila. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil āṭciyuṭiya coṟkaḷ putiya vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷātalum, avaṟṟil vantilāta coṟkaḷ paḻamiyavātalum kālattiṉ kōlamē.
mukittal eṉṉuñ col paṇṭiyilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantilātataṉāl ataṉi vaḻaṅkiyatu tavaṟu eṉṟārkaḷ. āṅkaṉam..........yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ paṇṭiyilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantuḷḷaṉavākaliṉ avaṟṟi vaḻaṅkiyatum tavaṟāyiṟṟu, nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟumanniyāyam niyāyamē!
III. kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉṉuñ collik kuṟittu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ - eḻutiyiruppataṉ curukkam:-
1. kāṇṭalviṉi ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟātatākaliṉ, kāṇ eṉpataṉ aṭiyiṟ piṟakkuñ coṟkaḷik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟiṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkutalēmuṟi.
2. vēṟṟumoḻip payiṟci oṉṟē koṇṭu tamiḻiṉ iyalpiṉi viḷakkaluṟuvār cilar............tamiḻiṉkaṇ ceyap pāṭṭuviṉi iṉṟu eṉavum kūṟāniṟpar.
3. kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpataṉiyē avar ('kapilar' āciriyar) niṉivukūrntu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār; kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatu payiṟcivayattāl avaraṟiyāmalē pōntatu.
4. itu (kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpatu pōṉṟu ceyviṉiyāka eḻutuvatu), iḻi vaḻakku.
5. ettuṇiyar ivviḻivaḻakkiṉi viḻumiya vaḻakkākak koṇṭaṉarō eṉa uṉṉa accam uṇṭākiṉṟatu.
ivaṟṟi muṟiyē ārāytum :-
1. kāṇ eṉpataṉ aṭiyiṟ piṟakkum viṉic coṟkaḷic ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyākavē vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku,
‘	viṉiyē ceyvatu ceyappaṭu poruḷē
......... ....... .....	........ ........	..
āyeṭ ṭeṉpa toḻiṉmuta ṉiliyē’
eṉṉum cūttiratti vitiyākak kuṟittārkaḷ. oru toḻiṟku mutaṉili (kāraṇam) eṭṭu eṉpatum, avi iviyeṉpatum iccūttirattāṟ pōtarutalaṉṟic ceyappaṭuporuḷ uḷḷa viṉiyic ceyappāṭṭuviṉi vaṭivil vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭu meṉpatu pōtarutal illi. ceyappaṭuporuḷuḷḷa viṉikku utāraṇaṅkāṭṭiya uriyāḷarellārum vaṉintāṉ eṉṟu ceyviṉiyākak kāṭṭiṉarēyaṉṟi, vaṉiyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyākak kāṭṭiṉārilli. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉiyic ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyāka vaḻaṅkutalvēṇṭum eṉpatu iccūttirattil evvāṟu pulaṉākiṉṟatō, aṟikilēṉ. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ aṟivippārkaḷāyiṉ avarkaḷpāl eṉṟum naṉṟipārāṭṭuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉāvēṉ.
‘viṉiyē' eṉṉum iccūttirattil vantuḷḷa 'ceyappaṭu poruḷ' eṉṉuñ collāl aṟiyappaṭum eṉiṉ, atu, karuttāviṉ1 ceyali aṭinta poruḷ eṉṟu karumattiṉi viḷakkiyatitaṉi. accollāl, tamiḻil ceyappāṭṭu viṉi vaḻaṅkutal uṇṭu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. attuṇiyēyaṉṟi, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāta viṉiyiyellām ceyappāṭṭu viṉivaṭivil vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu peṟappaṭātē. ceyappaṭuporuḷiyē naṉṉūlār ceyporuḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār 'ceyporuḷāṟun taruvatu viṉiyē' eṉṟatu kāṇka. nāṭṭāravarkaḷum, tamiḻiṟ ceyappāṭṭu viṉi vaḻakku uṇṭu eṉpatik kūṟumiṭattē, 'ceyappaṭu poruḷ eṉṉum peyariliruntē ceyappāṭṭuviṉi yuṇmi peṟap paṭuvataṉiyum' eṉṟu eḻutiyiruttaliṉ, accollāl, ceyappāṭṭu viṉi vaḻakku uṇṭu eṉpatumaṭṭumē aṟiyappaṭum.
2. vēṟṟumoḻip payiṟci oṉṟē koṇṭu, tamiḻil ceyappāṭṭuviṉi illi eṉṟār ivar eṉayāṉ aṟikilēṉ. āyiṉ, ceyappāṭṭu viṉivaḻakkut tirāviṭa moḻikku iyalpāyataṉṟu; vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantatu eṉpāri yāṉ. aṟivēṉ.
‘	karmaṇiprayōkam pāṣāsilikkuc cērntataṉṟu’2
‘	pāṣikkuc contamākak karmaṇiprayōkam illi’3
ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkivaruvatu vaṭamoḻiyiṉ cariyāṉa moḻi peyarppiṟku vēṇṭi iṭikkālattil kiruttirimamāka ēṟpaṭuttiyatoru sampratāyamākum. itaṟku lakṣyam-mutalāvatu, vaṭamoḻi aṟiyātavar karmaṇiprayōkatti orukālum ātarittal illi. pēccumoḻiyil ceyviṉi vaḻakkē kēṭkumāṟuḷatu. paḻiya nūlkaḷilmaṭṭumaṉṟu, eḻuttaccaṉ ḻutaliyōruṭiya nūlkaḷilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarpu illātaviṭattuk karmaṇi prayōkam illi eṉpatu eṉ aṉupavam. "rakuvamsam kāḷitācaṉāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu" eṉpataṟku īṭāka, rakuvamsam kāḷitāsaṉ uṇṭākkiyatu" eṉṟāl, ciṟitum amiyāmi yilli eṉpataṉṟu. immuṟimiyē moḻikku iyalpāyatu eṉṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu..............'
‘karmaṇi prayōkattic cutta tirāviṭaṅkaḷāya viṉic coṟkaḷil atikam oḻittal vēṇṭum.'1
īsvaraṉ ceyyuṉṉa tellām (ceykiṉṟatellām) sahikkayē (poṟuttuk koḷḷutalē) uḷḷu (uḷatu) eṉṟu kūṟāmal, īsvaraṉāl ceyyappeṭuṉṉatellām sahikkap peṭukiyē uḷḷu eṉṟu pirayōkittāl, atu maliyāḷam aṉṟu eṉṟē kūṟutal nērum'
eṉṟu, kēraḷapāṇiṇi, mahāmahimi, srīrājarājavarma kōyiṟṟampirāṉavarkaḷ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
‘kaṉṉaṭattil karmaṇi prayōkam visēṣarūṭamāka villi. vīṭukaṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉpataṟkup patil vīṭukaṭṭiṟṟu (maṉe kaṭṭitu) eṉṉum prayōkam ērāḷamāyirukkiṉṟatu'.2
‘maṇimayatālayaṅkaḷu cametu vikāvutata aḷateyali' eṉṉum aṭiyil, camikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṉum poruḷil camintaṉa (cametuvu) eṉṟu vantiruttal kāṇka.3
ikkāṭṭiyavaṟṟāl, ceyappāṭṭuviṉi vaḻakkut tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku iyalpāyataṉṟu eṉpatum, ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyi vaḻaṅka vēṇṭiyaviṭattuc ceyviṉiyi vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkē porumpālum uḷatu eṉpatum pōtarum. pukaḻuṭam pōṭu viḷaṅkuṅ kā. namaccivāya mutaliyāravarkaḷ karuttum ituvē.
3. kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyilyāṉ eḻutiyiruppatu payiṟcimikutiyāl eṉṉi aṟiyāmalē nērntatu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. avvāṟāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉavum eṉṉi yaṟiyāmaṟ cilaviṭaṅkaḷil pōntiruttal vēṇṭumē. eṉatu ‘kapila'ril ōriṭattātal avvāṟu vantilatē. aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavi eṉakkup payiṟciyillātaṉa eṉpārkaḷ pōlum. āyiṉ, kalācāli māṇavarkkāka yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil ōriṭattilātal aṅkaṉam............yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñcol vantila; aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷē vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl, aṟiyāmal eḻutum vaḻakkam eṉpāl illi eṉpati nāṭṭāravarkaḷ aṟintu koḷvārkaḷāka.
4. kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyāy vaḻaṅkāmal, kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉṟu ceyviṉivaṭivil vaḻaṅkiyatu iḻivaḻakku eṉṟārkaḷ.
‘muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉūlākum' eṉṟu vantuḷatu. ‘muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu' eṉpataṟku, 'muṉṉōṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭatu' eṉṟu pērāciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār.
‘nōkkuva vellām aviyē pōṟal' eṉṉumiṭattum, nōkkappaṭuva eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyil vantilatu. ‘nōkkuva' eṉpataṟku, ‘taṉṉāṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa' eṉṟu uriyāciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār. (nōkkal eṉpatu ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉi yākaliṉ, ataṉi, ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyil vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karuttu. pakkam 48 pārkka.)
‘kaṇṭatu moḻimō' eṉṟu kuṟuntokiyil vantuḷatu
‘kāṇkiṉṟa nilamellām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa vicumpellām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa ventīyelām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa vikkāṟṟellām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa kaṭamellām yāṉē eṉṉum' (tiruvāy 5.6.3.)
eṉṟu oru ceyyuḷil palamuṟi vantuḷatu. iviyellām iḻivaḻakkēyō? ivi ceyyuḷākaliṉ amiyum; uri naṭiyil amiyātu eṉpārkaḷō? avvāṟu ceyyuḷukkum urikkum taṉittaṉiyē ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyiruttali yāṉ aṟintilēṉ. uriyiyuñ ceyyuḷ eṉṉum vaḻakku uḷataṉṟē? niṟka. ikkālattut tamiḻp pulavarkaḷuḷ urinaṭi yeḻututaliṟ ciṟantavar kā. namaccivāyamutaliyā ravarkaḷeṉpatu tamiḻnāṭu aṟinta ceytiyē. avarkaḷ, tam urinūlkaḷil, kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpataṉiyē peritum vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.
‘aṅkē palappala kaṇavāykaḷum koṭumuṭikaḷum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.' 1
‘iṭiyiṭiyē kaṇavāykaḷum nīrōṭikaḷum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.'1
‘kāṭṭuvilaṅkukaḷuṭ ciṟanta yāṉi, puli, karaṭi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ aṭivāraṅkaḷil maṭṭum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.' 2  
‘orukālattil atu ciṟanta nakaramāyiruntatu eṉpataṟku aṟikuṟikaḷ āṅkāṅkē ippōtuṅ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa' eṉpaṉa kāṇka.3 ivi payiṉṟu vantuḷḷaṉa. kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatuvum cilaviṭaṅkaḷil vantuḷatu. yāṉ eḻutiya 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūl muḻuvatum mutaliyāravarkaḷ kaṭik kaṇittut tiruttañ ceytatē. itaṉi yāṉ muṉṉuriyil kuṟittirukkiṉṟēṉ. tiru. celvakēcavarāya mutaliyāravarkaḷum ‘tiruvaḷḷuvar' eṉṉum nūlil ivvāṟu ceyviṉiyāka vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
5. yāṉ eḻutiya kapilar eṉṉum nūl, uyarnta tāḷiṟpariya uruviṉatākap polivuṟa acciṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ atiṟ kāṇum ivvaḻivaḻakkiṉi viḻumiya vaḻakkeṉṟu koṇṭu palar keṭṭoḻivarē eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ añcukiṉṟārkaḷ. itu mēṉmakkaḷiyalpē. āyiṉ, tolkāppiyam kuṟuntoki tiruvāymoḻi mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṟpōr cilar eṉṟu koṇṭu avarikkuṟittu añcātiruppiṉum, tiru kā. namaccivāya mutaliyāravarkaḷiṉ urinūlkaḷip payilum māṇavar pallāyiravarākaliṉ, iḷamiyiṟ kaṟṟatu ciliyileḻuttup pōl nilipeṟumākaliṉ avaraṉivaruṅ keṭṭoḻivarē eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karutuvārkaḷāyiṉ, avarkaḷuḷḷam evvāṟu añcum! antō!!
IV.  naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ collikkuṟittu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppataṉ karuttu.
1. 	nakkīrar eṉpatu paṇṭu toṭṭu nilipeṟṟuvaruvatu
2. 	naṟkīrar eṉpatu aḻivaḻakku
3. nakkīrar eṉpati naṟkīrar eṉṟu māṟṟiṉatu pōla nakkaṇṇiyār nacceḷḷiyār mutaliya peyarkaḷiyum māṟṟi vaḻaṅkuvarēl, atu piṟitoru moḻiyātalaṉṟit tamiḻākātu.
1. nakkīraṉār nappālattaṉār mutaliya peyarkaḷil nal eṉpatuvē aṭimoḻi eṉṟum, naṟkīraṉār eṉpatuvē nakkīraṉār eṉṟu maruviṟṟu eṉṟum yāṉ eḻutiyiruppatu taṅkaṭkum uṭaṉpāṭē eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷē eḻutiyiruk kiṟārkaḷ. itaṉāl naṟkīraṉār eṉpatuvē tiripillātatu-iyalpāyatu eṉpatu avarkaṭkum uṭaṉpāṭākum. aṅkaṉa mākavum, nakkīrar eṉpatu paṇṭutoṭṭu nilipeṟṟu varuvatu eṉṟatu viyappiṉit tarukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟāyiṉ, mañcaṉ, añci(aintu), pōcci, ācci eṉpaviyē paṇṭutoṭṭu nilipeṟṟuvarupavi eṉṟum mintaṉ, aintu, pōyiṟṟu pōyttu, āyiṟṟu āyttu eṉpavi piṟkālattil (eṉṉip pōṉṟavarkaḷāl) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭavi eṉṟum kūṟutal vēṇṭumē. tirintacol paṇṭiyatākumā, tiriyāta col paṇṭiya tākumā? iṉi, paṇṭu eṉṟatu kaṭic caṅkakālattik kuṟippatu eṉiṉ, nakkīrar eṉṉuñ col caṅkacceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruppa tākat teriyavilli. paṇṭita i. vi. aṉantarāmiyaravarkaḷ, cilakālattiṟku muṉvari naṟkīrar eṉpatuvē vaḻaṅkivantatāka eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.1 (avarkaḷ nakkīraṉ eṉpatuvē iyalpāya peyar eṉṟu koṇṭavarkaḷ; nāṭṭāravarkaḷ akkoḷkiyiṉa rallar tamiḻp. 13pakkam. pārkka.) hālāsyamāhātmyattil naṟkīraṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyiruttaliṉ, appurāṇatti iyaṟṟiṉōr kālattil naṟkīrar eṉṟē vaḻaṅkivantatu eṉpatu teḷivākum. akkālattil nakkīrar eṉṟu (vaṭamoḻiyil) poruḷillāta toru collāka ataṉit tirittu vaḻaṅkutaṟku niyāya milli. cīvakacintāmaṇi 482-āñ ceyyuḷiṉ uriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiyirukkum vicēṭavuriyip paṭitta pulavarkaḷāṉē naṟkīrar eṉṉum vaḻakku oḻintirukkum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kālattil nakkīrar eṉṟu vaḻakku vaḻaṅkiyatu eṉpatil aiyamilli. avvaḷaviṟāṉē atu paṇṭutoṭṭu nilipeṟṟu varuvatu eṉal amivuṟātu. naṟcēntaṉār,2 naṟṟāmaṉār3 eṉṉum peyar akanāṉūṟṟilum naṟṟiṇiyilum iṉṉum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. naṟṟamaṉār4 eṉṉum peyaroṉṟu naṟṟiṇiyiṟ kāṇkiṉṟatu. mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, nakkīraṉār eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉavē paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyiṉṟāy, varalāṟṟōṭu muraṇutal kāṇka.
2. naṟkīrar eṉṉuñcol aḻivaḻakku eṉṟārkaḷ. iyalpāya peyari uṇmivaṭivil vaḻaṅkutal aḻivaḻakkāyiṉ, ciṟanta vaḻakku yātō? naṟkīrar eṉpatu aḻivaḻakkāyiṉ, naṟcēntaṉār naṟṟāmaṉār eṉpavi aḻivaḻakkākāvō?
3. naṟkīrar eṉṟu eḻutiyatupōṉṟu naṟkaṇṇiyār naṟceḷḷiyār eṉṟum, eḻutuvēṉāyiṉ avi tamiḻmoḻic coṟkaḷākāmal, teluṅku maliyāḷam pōlap piṟitumoḻi yākum eṉṟārkaḷ. perumpulavar piṉṉattūr a. nārāyaṇacāmi aiyaravarkaḷum macāvittuvāṉ rā. irākaviyaṅkāravarkaḷum naṟcēntaṉār naṟṟāmaṉār eṉṉum peyarkaḷi muṉṉarē eḻutiyiruttaliṉ, avarkaḷē teluṅku maliyāḷam pōlap piṟitorumoḻiyip paṭittuviṭṭārkaḷākaliṉ, putiya torumoḻiyip paṭikkum perumiyum aṭiyēṉukkuk kiṭikkāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eṉmāṭṭuḷḷa aṉpiṉ mikutiyiṉāl apperumiyi eṉakkē koṭukka uṭaṉpaṭṭālum naṟṟiṇiyiyum akanāṉūṟṟiyum kaṇṭa tamiḻulakam ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭātē.
'teṟṟeṉa' eṉpatu teḷ eṉṉum mutaṉiliyaṭiyilum, nākku eṉpatu nāl eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyilum piṟanta coṟkaḷ eṉpatu yāṉaṟintatu. itaṉit tamiḻp poḻililum eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. atukoṇṭu, ‘teṭṭeṉa' eṉṟum, 'nāṟku' eṉṟum eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉkaruttaṉṟu. yāṉ avvāṟu eḻutiṟṟumilēṉ. aṟiñar vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷi - tiripillātavaṟṟi - avvāṟē vaḻaṅkutal nalaṉ eṉpatu eṉkaruttu. kaṉṉaṭamoḻi - paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum putukkaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum iruperumpiriviṉatāy vaḻaṅkutal pōlavum, maliyāḷamoḻi - paḻiya maliyāḷam arvācīṉa maliyāḷam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutalpōlavum, tamiḻmoḻiyum paṇṭittamiḻ piṟkālattamiḻ eṉṟum pirivuṟṟirukkumēl āṅkaṉam.......yāṅkaṉam eṉṟum naṟkīrar eṉṟum eḻututal amitiyākātu. avvāṟu piriviṉṟiyiruttalāṉē, tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ nīyir yāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiyum nummi niṉṉi eṉṉun toṭakkattavaṟṟiyum āṭūu makaṭūu eṉṉuñ coṟ kaḷiyum ivipōṉṟa piṟavaṟṟiyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. pukaḻuṭampōṭu viḷaṅkuñ cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār tam urinūlil aḷapeṭic coṟkaḷiyum vaḻaṅkiṉār. nīvir nīyir nummi eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟkaḷit tiru nāṭṭāravarkaḷum eḻutivarukiṉṟārkaḷ. iviyellām vaḻakkiṟantaṉavāyiṉum paṇṭic ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttalāṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭutaliṉ, aṭiyēṉum āṅkaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟi vaḻaṅkalāṉēṉ. camaṉ ceytu cīrtūkkuṅ kōlpōlamintu viḷaṅkum periyōrkaḷ emmiruvar kūṟṟukkaḷiyum ārāyntu, amivillātavaṟṟi aruḷkūrntu terivikkumāṟu paṇivuṭaṉ vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ.
‘kuṇattik koḷḷārāka. ataṉāl eṉ? kuṟṟattik koḷvārāka. ataṉāl eṉ? kuṇattik koṇṭu kuṟṟattik koḷḷātuviṭuvārāka. ataṉāltāṉ eṉ? kuṇattik kuṟṟamākak koḷḷutaltāṉ poṟuttaṟku aritāyuḷatu'.
eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi mahākaviyoruvar kūṟiyatu niṉikkat takkatā yirukkiṉṟatu.


20. tiru.vē. vē reṭṭiyāravarkaḷiṉ 
ārāycciyiṉ ceppamuṭimi
vittuvāṉ tiru.vē.vēṅkaṭarācalu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ eḻutiya 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūliṉ poruḷkaḷ palatiripuṇarcciyāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉavākat tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ, tamiḻ kaṟkum iḷiñar poruṭṭu avaṟṟic cila kaṭṭurikaḷāka eḻuti veḷip paṭuttal kaṭaṉeṉak karuti, yāṉ eḻutiya mutaṟkaṭṭuri 'tamiḻp poḻil' tuṇar 13 malar 2-l veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu. ataṉkaṇ, mukittal, aṅkaṉam kāṇkiṉṟatu, naṟkīrar eṉa reṭṭi yāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya nāl vēṟu coṟkaḷ ārāyappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya accol vaḻakku eṉṉāl maṟukkap paṭṭamiyiṉ avarkaḷ aṟivārāycci kuṟipāṭuṭiya reṉṟātal, yāṉ niṟivuṭiyēṉeṉṟātal karutiṉēṉāka eṇṇātirukka avarkaḷiyum ēṉōriyum vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ. eṉatu ciṟumiyi eṉṉiṉ aṟintār piṟarilar eṉpatu tēṟṟam. aṅṅaṉam mikac curuṅkiya aṟiviṉēṉāyiṉum, etaṉiyum uṇmi kāṇum nōkkuṭaṉ ārāyumiyalpum, eṉ aṟivu ārāyccik kuṟivāl nērnta vaḻukkaḷip piṟar eṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ ataṟkicintu avaṟṟit tiruttikkoḷḷum viruppamum uṭiyēṉ. tamatu tavaṟuyātāyiṉum piṟar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya vaḻi ataṉi ēṟṟukkōṭal naṉmakkaḷ iyal pākalāṉum, tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉuṇṭāka nūlārāycci ceyvārkku aḵtu iṉṟiyamiyāk kaṭappāṭu ākalāṉum reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ eṉṉāṟ  kāṭṭaluṟṟa piḻikaḷukkāka eṉṉi muṉiyātu avaṟṟit tiruttikkoḷḷa muṟpaṭuvārkaḷeṉa eṇṇiṉēṉ. āyiṉ avarkaḷ 'ceppamuṭiyārkkuc ceyyum viṇṇappam' eṉat talippeyariṭṭu eḻutiya kaṭṭuri 'tamiḻp poḻil' 13-ām tuṇar,9,10,11-ām malarkaḷil veḷivantataṉip paṭittu, eṉ eṇṇam paḻutāyiṉami kaṇṭēṉ. avarkaḷ pala poruntāvurikaḷāl tampiḻipaṭṭa muṭipukaḷiyē cātippāṉ muyaṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ. mīṭṭum aviyiṟṟi maṟuttu uṇmi nili nāṭṭutal kaṭaṉāyiṉamiyiṉ ikkaṭṭuri eḻuta nērntatu. avarkaḷ eḻutiyavaṟṟiyellām peyartteḻuti ārāyiṉ ivvuri mika viriyumākaliṉ iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭum. itaṉip paṭippōr avarkaḷ eḻutiyataṉiyum uṭaṉvittu nōkkutal naṉṟām.
"mukittal eṉṉuñ col ticiccol eṉpatu etaṉāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatō, aṟikilēṉ. atu teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉ ticic collākum eṉiṉ, tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṭ pallāyirañ coṟkaḷ ticic coṟkaḷēyākum" eṉpatu mutalākac cila kūṟi vantu, 'mukittal eṉpatu ticiccol eṉṟē koḷḷiṉum' eṉṟu toṭaṅkic cila kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivaṟṟi nōkkuḻit ticiccol eṉpatu paṟṟit teḷivāṉa karuttoṉṟum avarkaṭkilli yeṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
"	centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattun taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi"1
eṉat ticiccoṟkilakkaṇa muṇarttum tolkāppiyac cūttiravuriyil iḷampūraṇarum, cēṉāvariyarum centamiḻ nāṭṭut teṉkīḻppāl mutalāka vaṭakīḻppāl iṟutiyākavuḷḷa poṅkar nāṭu mutalāya paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷip paṉṉiru nilam eṉpataṟkup poruḷākak koṇṭaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar avaṟṟuṭaṉ, avaṟṟiṉ puṟañcūḻnta ciṅkaḷam mutaliya paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷiyum koṇṭaṉar. teyvacciliyār avviru tiṟattup paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉarēṉum, vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumariyiṭippaṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭip puṟañ cūḻnta paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷik kōṭalē poruttamāmeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉareṉpatu avaruriyāl aṟiyappaṭum. mayilinātarum naṉṉūluriyil ciṅkaḷam mutaliya paṉṉiru nāṭṭiṉ peyarkaḷiyuṇarttum ōr cūttirap pakutiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, aḵtu akattiyaṉār iyaṟṟiyatākavum kuṟippiṭṭār. piṟkūṟiya paṉṉirunāṭṭil karunaṭam, teluṅku eṉpaṉavum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilumiruntu vantu vaḻaṅkuvaṉavē ticic collākā eṉakkarutiṉ, poṅkarnāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kuriyaṉavum ticiccollākā eṉpatu avarkaḷ karuttātal pōtarum. maṟṟu, tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya ticiccol iṟṟeṉṟu avarkaḷ viḷakkuvārkaḷāka.
iṉi, "paḻiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātataṉāl atu(mukittal) ticiccollākum eṉiṉ, avaṟṟil vaḻaṅkātaṉa vellām ticiccoṟkaḷēyākumaṉṟō? āyiṉ, nāṉ, nīṅkaḷ, anta, inta, enta, cari, aṉuppu, ciri mutaliyaṉavellām paḻiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātaṉavātaliṉ ticic coṟkaḷēyākum" eṉavum, "mukittal eṉpatu ticiccol eṉṟē koḷḷiṉum, ceyyuḷilum vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkutaliṉ ataṉi urinaṭiyil vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟātal yāṅṅaṉam? ticiccolli vaḻaṅkalākātu eṉṟu ilakkaṇam uḷatō? eṉavum eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. (yāṉ eḻutiyiruppataṉi iṅkē tarukiṉṟēṉ: "caṅkap pulavarkaḷip paṟṟi ārāycciyuri eḻutum āciriyaroruvar piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvataṉṟik kaṭal pōṟparantuḷḷa tamiḻilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil yāṇṭum vaḻaṅkappaṭātatum, uriyāciriyaṉmār evarālum tīṇṭappaṭātatum, evviṭattu ettiṟattu makkaḷālō aruki vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatumākiya ticiccol oṉṟi mutaṉmiyāka nāṭṭuvatum, ataṉip paḻiya tamiḻccol eṉṟu niṟuttappaṭuvatum nērmiyuṭiyavākat tōṉṟavilli. 'itil, paḻiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅ kātaṉa vellām ticiccollākumeṉṟum, ticiccoṟ kaḷi vaḻaṅkalākātu eṉṟum yāṉ kūṟiyaṉami peṟappaṭumā ṟeṅṅaṉam? yāṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟi nekiḻaviṭuttuk kūṟātavaṟṟik kūṟiyavākak kaṟpittukkoṇṭu eḻutuvatē ceppamuṭimi pōlum.
mukittal eṉṉuñ col tiruppukaḻil palaviṭattil vantuḷatu eṉavum, accollit tamiḻ 'lekcika'ṉil nōkkiya yāṉ atiṟ kuṟittirukkum tiruppukaḻaṭikaḷit kaṇṭiruttal kūṭum eṉavum, avvāṟākavum 'piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvatu' eṉṟuyāṉ eḻutiyatu viyappiṉi viḷikkiṉṟatu eṉavum, aruṇakirinātar kālam 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟu teritaliṉ iṟṟikku 500 yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē accol tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya tākum eṉavum avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. yāṉ eḻutiyatu viyappu viḷikkakkūṭiyateṉpatil aiyamilli. āyiṉ, tām eḻutiyataṉi nōkkiyē mutaṟkaṇ avarkaṭku viyappuṇṭātal vēṇṭum. "āṟṟiṟ pālatti mukittēṉallēṉ" (kuṟṟālattala. kaṇṭakacētaṉa 39) eṉṟu ceyyuḷilum vantuḷḷa taṉālum" eṉa eḻutiya avarkaḷ ataṉiṉum muṟpaṭṭa tirup pukaḻil accol vaḻaṅkiyiruttali eṭuttukkāṭṭāmi eṉṉi? avarkaḷ karuttiṉ āḻam aṟitaṟkariyatākavuḷḷatu. maṟṟu, tiruppukaḻil vantuḷḷataṉāl atu paḻiya tamiḻccol ākumeṉiṉ, calām, capācu mutalāyiṉavum paḻiya tamiḻc collā meṉpatu avarkaḷ karuttiṟkicintatu pōlum. "paḻiya ōliyēṭukaḷiṉ iṟutiyil muṟṟiṟṟu eṉṟātal, mukintatu eṉṟātal  eḻutiyiruttali ōliccuvaṭi nōkkiṉār naṉku aṟivar' eṉkiṟārkaḷ. ōliccuvaṭi nōkkiya perumi kaṟṟār palarukkuṇṭu. nōkkiyatōṭamiyātu eṇṇiṟanta cuvaṭikaḷi ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭavarkaḷ accolli ōriṭattum vaḻaṅkātu viṭṭami eṉṉi? 'mukintatu eṉpatu muṟṟiṟṟu - pūrttiyāyiṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷatu. iccolli nūṉ muḻuvatilum evvāṟu vaḻaṅkutal kūṭum?' eṉa urik kiṉṟārkaḷ. ataṉi vaḻaṅkutaṟku nūliṉkaṇ vēṟiṭamē vāykkavilliyeṉpatu periyatōr viyappē! atu kiṭakka.
paḻiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātaṉa eṉṟu avarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiya coṟkaḷil 'ciri' eṉpatum  oṉṟu. atu kuṟittu avarkaḷ 'ciri' eṉpatu naki eṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟuṅ kūṟiviṭalām pōlum eṉṟu kūṟi nakiyāṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ataṉi naki yeṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟavar yārō? 'vilāviṟac cirittiṭṭēṉē' eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum, 'cirippār kaḷippār tēṉippār' eṉṟu māṇikkavācakarum, 'eṉṟāṉiṟi yōṉatu kēṭṭavar- cirittār' eṉṟu cēkkiḻārum 'cirittatu ceṅkaṭ cīyam' eṉṟu kampanāṭarum ataṉi vaḻaṅki yuḷḷārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻukku vēntarkaḷ eṉattakka paḻiya perumakkaḷālum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya uriyāciriyar kaḷālum payila vaḻaṅkappeṟṟuḷḷa iccolli "nakiyeṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭalām pōlum" eṉa reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ urippataṉ nōkkam ituveṉap pulaṉākavilli.
mukittal eṉṉuñ collik kuṟittut taṭi nikaḻttiya yāṉ 'anuppu', 'nīṅkaḷ' eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkutal takātu eṉṟu cāṟṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ karuttu, yāṉ ivaṟṟi vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟākāmi pōla avarkaḷ ataṉi vaḻaṅkutalum tavaṟākātu eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatō? avarkaḷ ataṉi vaḻaṅkuvatu tavaṟātal pōla yāṉ ivaṟṟi vaḻaṅkuvatum tavaṟākum eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatō? yāteṉa aṟiyakkūṭavilli. mukittal eṉṉuñ coṟ pōlaṉṟi, ivi tamiḻ nāṭeṅkaṇum poruḷaṟintu pēcappaṭuvaṉavātaliyum, takutiyuṭiya pulavar palarālum ivi pēcappaṭṭum eḻutappaṭṭum varutaliyum ōrtal vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum iccoṟkaḷip paḻantamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantuḷḷa paḻiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ eṉa yāṉ ōriṭattum kūṟavilli eṉpataṉiyum avarkaḷ cintittal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, aṟiñar palarum 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliyavāka vaḻaṅki varuñ coṟkaḷi reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ 'aṅkaṉam' mutaliya uruviṉavākattirittu, avarkaḷ eḻutiya 'paraṇar' 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūlkaḷiṉum, 'tamiḻp poḻi' lil veḷiyiṭṭa kaṭṭurikaḷilum vaḻaṅkiyiruppatōṭu, 'kapilar' muṉṉuriyil avvāṟu vaḻaṅkiṉamikkuk kāraṇamum kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. atu, "āṅkaṉam, īṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttalāṉum, ṅakaram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ vārāteṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutalāṉum aṅkaṉam āṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam eṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻamiya eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. ākaliṉ yāṉ innūlil aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ" eṉpatu. yāṉ ataṉi ārāynta piṉṉar, avarkaḷ ceppamuṭiyārkkuc ceyta viṇṇappak kaṭṭuriyil "aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi avvaṭivil vaḻaṅkutali yāṉ yāṇṭum maṟuttilēṉ. yāṉ maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷatu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppatuvē viyappākavuḷḷatu. māṇavar poruṭṭu yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpavaṟṟiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ" eṉa ōriṭattilum, "kalācāli māṇavarkkāka yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil ōriṭattilātal aṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ col vantila; aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷē vantuḷḷaṉa" eṉap piṟitōriṭattilum eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. avvāṟiruppiṉ, avaṟṟiyum, 'paraṇar' mutaliyavaṟṟiyum pārkkum oruvar, māṇavarkkup puttakaṅkaḷ eḻutiya tiru. vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷum vēṟu. periyōrkaḷukkākap paraṇar mutaliya nūṟkaḷ iyaṟṟiya tiru vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭiyār avarkaḷum vēṟu eṉṟutāṉ koḷvar. eṉṉi? muṉṉiya puttakaṅkaḷil yāṇṭum 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliya col vaḻakkum, piṉṉiyavaṟṟil 'aṅkaṉam' mutaliya vaḻakkumē kāṇap paṭutaliṉ eṉka. reṭṭiyāravarkaḷum caṅkap pulavarkaḷākiya paraṇar kapilarum vēṟu, patiṉōrān tirumuṟiyiluḷḷa cila nūṟkaḷiyaṟṟiya paraṇar kapilarum vēṟu eṉac coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakku vēṟṟumi mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu tuṇintuḷḷārkaḷātaliṉ ammuṟi paṟṟip piṟar ceyyum ārāycci muṭipukaḷi avarkaḷ matittalē vēṇṭum! 'paraṇar' 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūṟkaḷiyaṟṟiya reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷē eṉ ārāyccik kuriyavākum.
'yāṅṅaṉam ottiyō vīṅkucelaṉmaṇṭilam' eṉattiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukiyātaṟku iḻukkiṉṟām. aṅṅaṉamākavum etuki mōṉikaḷiṉ nirppantam illāta kālattil - illāta pāvil, pulavaroruvarallar palarum accolli vaḻaṅkiya pulavaraṉivarumē - ṅakarattik kakaramākat tirittē vaḻaṅkiṉār eṉpatu ettuṇi amiviṉatu!' eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itil 'iḻukkiṉṟām' eṉpatu kāṟumuḷḷa pakuti muṉṉar eṉṉāl eḻutappaṭṭatē. pulavarellārumē ṅakarattik kakaramākattirittu vaḻaṅkiṉār eṉal eṉ karutteṉṟu eṅṅaṉam aṟintārkaḷō? yāṉ eḻutiyatu orōvaḻik kakaravuruviṟ kāṇappaṭiṉ ataṟkuk kāraṇam kāṭṭiyapaṭiyēyām. "ceyyuṭ ceyyum pulavar etukit toṭiyil ṅakarattik kakaramākat tirittu vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē" eṉṟu eḻutiya yāṉ, ataṉiyaṭuttu, "yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅku celaṉ maṇṭilam" eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etuki yātaṟkiḻukkiṉṟām" eṉṟum, iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaliṉ mūṉṟāñ cūttiram 'āṅkaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ' eṉṟē paṭikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu eṉṟum eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟi nōkkuvār. ṅakaravuruvil iruppatē iyalpām eṉpatum, kakaravuruviṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa cila ēṭeḻutuvōrāl nērntaṉa vākum eṉpatum aṉṟō eṉ karutteṉak koḷḷā niṟpar? reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ eṉ karuttukkaḷit tamakku vēṇṭiya vāṟellām tiritturippatu muṟiyākumā? 'paṇṭip pulavartañ ceyyuḷil etuki mōṉikaḷi niyatiyākak koḷḷātu viṭuttaṉar" eṉavum, 'yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅku celaṉ maṇṭilam' eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukiyātaṟku iḻukkiṉṟām' eṉavum kūṟiya avarkaḷē, "iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyal mūṉṟāñ cūttiram 'āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ' eṉṟē paṭikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu" eṉa yāṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavaḻi, ataṉi ēṟṟuk koḷḷa maṉamiṉṟi, "cūttirattiṉ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉkām aṭikaḷi nōkkiṉ, mutalaṭi, 'āṅkaṉam-' eṉṟē amintirukkum eṉpatu eḷitiṟpulaṉākum" eṉkiṉṟārkaḷ. ettuṇi muraṇpāṭu kāṇmiṉ! etukiyē vēṇṭuvatiṉṟu eṉṟum, mūṉṟām eḻuttu ṅakaramākavum kakaramākavum māṟi yiruppiṉum etukiyāmeṉṟum paṇṭiyōr karuttiṉik kaṇṭuritta avarkaḷ avviraṇṭaṉiyum kāṟṟilē paṟakka viṭṭu, piṉ mūṉṟaṭikaḷil pāṅkiṉōril, pāṅkilaṉ, āṅka eṉa vantiruttaliṉ 'mutala' ṭiyilum āṅkaṉam eṉṟē irukka vēṇṭum meṉpatu cāla aḻakitu.
"paḻiya patippukaḷil, āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ aḷaviṟantaṉavē pāṅkuṟavuṇarntōr paṉṉuṅkāli' eṉṟu patikkappaṭṭirunta tolkāppiyac cūttiram, ippoḻutu tiru.vē.turicāmi aiyaravarkaḷik koṇṭu paricōtittut tiru. kaṉakacapip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa patippil, 'āṅkaṉam....kāli' eṉṟu patikkappaṭṭuḷatu. turicāmi aiyaravarkaḷ pāṭattit tirutti viṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiviṭuvārkaḷ pōlum' "eṉṟum, "akanāṉūṟṟil ''yāṅṅaṉam' eṉṟu patippittuḷḷa mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum (ceyyuḷ 27,90,378) tiru.es.viyāpuri piḷḷiyavarkaḷiṭamirukkum paḻiya ōliyēṭṭil 'yāṅṅaṉam' eṉṟē irukkiṉṟatu. iḵtu ēṭeḻutiṉōr tavaṟu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ eṉ ceytum" eṉṟum, "accup puttakaṅkaḷil āṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu patittirut taṟkuk kāraṇam avarkkuḷḷa naṉṉūṟ payiṟciyē yākum" eṉṟum eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itiliruntē tolkāpyimum, akanāṉūṟum paḻiya ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiliruntu mutalil acciṭṭavarkaḷ 'āṅṅaṉam' eṉavum, 'yāṅṅaṉam' eṉavumē acciṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu pulaṉām. akanāṉūṟu mayili tiru irājakōpāla aiyaṅkāravarkaḷālum, makāvittuvāṉ tiru rā. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷālum ārāyappaṭṭatu. tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyali nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyuṭaṉ acciṭṭavarkaḷ makāvittuvāṉ tiru rā. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷē. avarkaḷ eṅkeṅku kiṭitta ettaṉi ēṭṭuccuvaṭikaḷi ārāyntu ataṉip patittuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu apputtaka mukavuriyāl aṟiyappaṭum. appatippilē, ceyyuḷiyal 51ām cūttirattil 'āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ' eṉṟum, 213 ām cūttirattil 'āṅṅaṉa maṟipa' eṉṟum, 242ām cūttirattil 'āṅṅaṉa moḻukiṉ' eṉṟum 'āṅṅaṉam' eṉṉuñ col avvuruviṉatākavē acciṭap peṟṟuḷatu. avarkaḷ naṉṉūṟ payiṟciyāl aṅṅaṉam ceytu viṭṭārkaḷeṉpatu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷiṉ karuttā kiṉṟatu. palarālum naṉku matikkappeṟṟa ciṟanta pulavarkaḷit tolkāppiyap payiṟciyillāreṉṟum, naṉṉūṟ payiṟci yoṉṟē uṭiyāreṉṟum kuṟitturippatē tamatu perumiyi mikuppatākumeṉa avarkaḷ karutiyuḷḷārkaḷ pōlum! avvaḷavu perumitamuṭiya avarkaḷ "āṅṅaṉam-(eṉat) turicāmi aiyaravarkaḷ pāṭattit tiruttiviṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiviṭuvārkaḷ pōlum" eṉa, aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉ  eṉatu nā ventuviṭumeṉpatu pōlap pukaṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ. tiru.kaṉakacapip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa poruḷatikārap patippu muṉṉiya patippukkaḷil uḷḷa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa piḻikaḷil mikaccila tiruttappeṟṟum, cila kuṟippukkaḷ cērkkap peṟṟum, aḻakākap patikkap peṟṟuḷateṉpatum, kuṟinta viliyil māṇākkarkaḷ peṟṟup payaṉaṭiyumāṟu utavap peṟṟuḷateṉpatum, uṇmi. āṉāl aḵtu, ēṭupārttu muḻutum ceppañ ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatupōl māṟupāṭu piṟitilli. puttakattiluḷḷa appiḻi kaḷellām ēṭṭilum iruntaṉavāyiṉ, reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ 'ēṭu' 'ēṭu' eṉṟu, ataṉ āṇikku aṭaṅkiyoḻukumāṟu kūṟuvatellām poruḷillā vaṟuṅkūṟṟēyākum. makāmakō pāttiyāya, ṭākṭar u.vē.cāminātiyaravarkaḷiṉ peyariyum patippiyum orōvaḻit tamakku araṇāka amittuk koḷḷum reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ, aiyaravarkaḷ tamatu urinaṭiyil ev viṭattum 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliya coṟkaḷi 'aṅkaṉam' mutaliya vākat tiriyātu vaḻaṅkuṅ kāraṇattit tāmāka ārāyntō, avarkaḷōṭu ucāviyō aṟintu, tāmum ataṉip piṉpaṟṟu vataṉṟō muṟiyākum?
"	iṅṅaṉa miraṇṭu tiṅka ḷēkalu mēka vēlāṉ
aṅṅaṉam puṇarnta vaṉpi ṉavaṇmu lip pōkanīkki
eṅṅaṉa meḻunta tuḷḷa miruḷiṭi yēkaluṟṟāṉ
taṅkiya poṟiyiṉākkantaṉak kortē rāka niṉṟāṉ"1						(cīvakacintāmaṇi . 1359)
eṉpatu aiyaravarkaḷ patippittatu tāṉē? ivviṭattu naṉṉūṟ payiṟciyāṟ patippittārkaḷ  eṉṟē kūṟuvārkaḷ pōlum!
iṉi, avarkaḷ "vaḻaṅkutaṟku. eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ col vaḻaṅkutaṟku aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu eṉṟārkaḷ. itaṉi aṟiyiṉ, melloṟṟuttaṉ iṉavalleḻuttōṭu kūṭivarum col vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyatu eṉpatum, melleḻuttu iraṭṭittu varuñcol vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatu eṉpatum avarkaḷ karuttātal  naṉku pulaṉākum" eṉakkūṟi, oṉṟu, aṉṟu mutalāyiṉa oṉṉu, aṉṉu mutaliya vākattirintu vaḻaṅkutal eṉṉi? eṉa viṉāvukiṉṟārkaḷ. vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉpatu aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu eṉak kūṟiyatiliruntu, avarkaḷ piṟ kūṟiyavellām eṉ karuttākumeṉa eṅṅaṉam aṟintu koṇṭārkaḷō terintilatu. eḻuttilum, collilum, toṭarilum cila tammaḷavil yāvarum vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyaṉavāka
vuḷḷaṉa. ḻa eṉṉum eḻuttu ḷakaramākavum, yakaramākavum ciṟcilarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭutal kaṇkūṭu. cila pakutiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ ataṉi ṣa eṉavum olikkiṉṟaṉar. 'aṅṅaṉam' mutalāyiṉavum avvāṟē tiruntā nāviṉōrkku vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyavātali yāvarum uṇarvar. iḵtu uṇmikku māṟākuvatō? iṉi, maṅkalam, kañci, cantaṉam, māṅkāy, tēṅkāy, eṉpaṉa maliyāḷattil muṟiyē maṅṅalam, kaññi, cannaṉam, māṅṅā, tēṅṅā eṉa vaḻaṅkutaliṉ ṅṅa oli ariteṉpatum' ṅka oli eḷiteṉpatum poruntā eṉkiṉṟārkaḷ. kañciyum cantaṉamum īṇṭikku vēṇṭā. maṅṅalam, māṅṅā, tēṅṅā eṉa maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriya kāraṇatti ammoḻi varalāṟu ārāyvār ārāyka. tiṅkaḷ, poṅkal, paṅkuṉi, kaṅkul, maṅkul, cuṅkam mutaliya coṟkaḷit tamiḻ makkaḷ tiṅṅaḷ, poṅṅal, mutaliyavākat tirittu eṅkēṉum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷō? appaṭi vaḻaṅkiṉ ataṉi eṭuttuk kāṭṭutalum, aṉṟēl vāḷā amintiruttalumaṉṟō nērmiyākum?
iṉi, marapu nili tirital eṉa yāṉ eḻutiyatu kuṟittum, 'ṅakarattuṭaṉ mayaṅkutaṟkuriya vēṟu meyataṉōṭu piṟap poṟṟumiyum oliyoṟṟumiyumuṭiya kakaram oṉṟumēyām' eṉa yāṉ eḻutiyatil uḷḷa oliyoṟṟumi eṉpatu kuṟittum avarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟukkellām māṟu urippiṉ ivvuri veṟitē virivatātaliyum, aṅṅaṉam, aṅkaṉam eṉpavaṟṟuḷ paḻamiyatu yātu eṉṉum ārāyccikku avi iṉṟiyami yātaṉa allavātaliyum karuti, avaṟṟi ikkaṭṭuriyil viṭṭoḻikkiṉṟēṉ. viṉittoki, paṇputtokikaḷip pirittup puṇarttāmikku urikārarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷa kāraṇaṅ kaḷi avarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiyiruppataṉ ārāycciyum īṇṭikku vēṇṭaṟ pālataṉṟu. tolkāppiyar aṅṅaṉam, aṅkaṉam eṉpavaṟṟuḷ yātēṉum oṉṟaṉ āṭci pulappaṭa viti kūṟiyiruppiṉ, aḵtoṉṟē, ataṟku māṟākak kūṟappaṭum kāraṇaṅkaḷi yellām citittoḻittu uṇmiyi nilināṭṭuvataṟ kuriyatākum. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil oru col iruvakiyāka vaḻaṅkiṉ avaṟṟuṭ paḻiyatāya vaḻakku ituveṉat tuṇital perumpālum aritākum. aṭampu, aṭumpu, aṇimi, aṇumi, kuricil, kurucil, pariti, paruti; eṉa iccoṟkaḷ iru taṉmiyākavum paḻiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantuḷḷami kāṇka. ivaṟṟuḷ paḻiya vaṭivu. aṉṟi, uṇmi vaṭivu ituveṉat tuṇitalākāmiyiṉ ōr patippil oru taṉmiyākavum, piṟitoru patippiṟ piṟitoru taṉmiyākavum iccoṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tāḻakkōl eṉap poruḷpaṭum ōr col 'tāḻ' eṉavum, 'tāḷ' eṉavum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ḻakāra vīṟṟil vittu,
"	tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ akkiṭivarutal urittu mākum"1
eṉa viti kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, tāḻ eṉpatē paḻiya uruvāmeṉa aṟutiyiṭappaṭumaṉṟō? ivvāṟṟāl aṅṅaṉam mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ vaṭivu tuṇitaṟku itukāṟum nikaḻttiya taṭi viṭi yāvaṟṟiṉum pārkkil īṇṭeṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭum tolkāppiya viti paṟṟiya ārāycciyē iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappiṟṟākum.
tolkāppiya nūṉmarapil uṭaṉili meymmayakkattiṟku vitikūṟiya
'	meynnili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
tammuṟ ṟāmvaruum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē'2
eṉṉuñ cūttiratti yāṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭi, atil, ra,ḻa eṉṉum iraṇṭum oḻinta patiṉāṟu meyyum tammuṉ tām vantu mayaṅkumeṉa āciriyar vitittiruttaliṉ ṅakaravoṟṟiṉ muṉ ṅakaram vantu mayaṅkum col iruntatākal vēṇṭum eṉavum, tamiḻilē cuṭṭuviṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ ṅaṉam eṉpatu iyintuvarum aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷaṉṟi ṅakaram uṭaṉiliyāka mayaṅkaṟkuriya vēṟu col illi eṉavum kūṟiṉēṉ. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷi a+ṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarttuvatu tolkāppiyar karuttaṉṟākaliṉ ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ varumeṉa avar vitittilar eṉavum urittēṉ.
ivaṟṟiṟku reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ kūṟiya maṟuppuriyāvaṉa.
1. aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyar koṇṭa poruḷ attaṉmi, appaṭi eṉpaṉaveṉṉiṉ, āṇṭu akaram cuṭṭupporuḷ tarutaliṉ ṅakaram moḻi mutal vantatākum. ākavē, aññālam annāḷ ammaṇi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷip pirittu, 'cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ' eṉṟu viti kūṟiya āciriyar 'cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ṅañanamat tōṉṟiṉ, eṉṟu viti kūṟi, aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉiyum amittiruppar.
2. "meynnili cuṭṭiṉ" eṉṉuñ cūttirattil ṅakarattoṭu ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam eṉṉum coṟkaḷiyē āciriyar karutiṉār eṉṟu tuṇital amiyātu.
3. uriyāciriyar ataṟku eṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colli utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiyatum poruntātu.
4. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil vaḻaṅkiyirukkumēl tolkāppiyattilum caṅka nūlkaḷilum āṅṅaṉam īṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉa kāṇappaṭāmal āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉpaṉavē kāṇappaṭutaṟku niyāyam illi.
5. ṅakarattiṉmuṉ ṅakaram varutaṟku utāraṇam 'piṭiyūṭṭip piṉṉuṇṇuṅṅ kaḷiṟeṉavu murittaṉarē' eṉṉum aṭiyil ṅakaram iraṇṭu vantiruttal pōlvatākum. taṉi meyyōṭu taṉimey mayaṅkutali iccūttirattiṟ koḷḷākkāl, 'kaṇṇ karuviḷi', 'poṉṉ poṟicuṇaṅku eṉpaṉa pōla varupavaṟṟi lellām meykaḷ mayaṅki niṟṟaṟku ilakkaṇamiṉṟākum.
ivaṟṟuḷ 4āvatākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa ceyti uṇmikku māṟāyateṉpatu yāṉ mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiliruntu pulaṉākum. maṟṟum, iccūttira ārāycciyiṉāl uṇmi tuṇiya vēṇṭuvatu muṟiyākavum, ataṟku māṟākat tāmē tuṇintu koṇṭatoru piṟaḻcci muṭipukku iccūttirap poruḷi iṉikka muyalvatākavum uḷḷatu. ākaliṉ ataṉiyoḻittu, ēṉiyaṉa iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyaṉa.
1. aṅṅaṉam eṉṟillātu, reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ koḷkip paṭi aṅkaṉam eṉṟiruppiṉum, accol attaṉmi eṉap poruḷpaṭumākaliṉ, a eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu taṉmi eṉap poruḷ paṭum kaṉam eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarnta teṉal vēṇṭum. tolkāppiya uyir mayaṅkiyalil
"	viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum
eṉave ṉeccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum
 āṅka veṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum
ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē"1
eṉaakara vīṟṟilum,
"	ikara viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē"1
"	iṉiyaṇi yeṉṉuṅ kāliyum iṭaṉum
viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭum aṉṉa"2
eṉa ikara vīṟṟilum,
"ukara viṟuti akara viyaṟṟē"3
"cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟ ṟākum"4
eṉa ukara vīṟṟilum āciriyar viti kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, kaṉam eṉpatoru col taṉmi eṉap poruḷpaṭuvatāyiruntu, akaram mutaliya cuṭṭukkaḷōṭu puṇarntiruppiṉ, akkaṉam eṉpatu mutaliyavāka valimikkaṉṟō muṭintirukka vēṇṭum? avvāṟiṉmiyiṉ, tam kūṟṟut tamatu koḷkiyiṉiyē citittoḻippataṉi ōrātu, avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉ uṇmiyi maṟukkap pukuntatu eṉṉiyō?
aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu āciriyar karuttākātu eṉpataṟkum, uriyāciriyar kāṭṭiya utāraṇam poruntātatu eṉpataṟkum maṟṟum avarkaḷ kūṟiyaṉa ārāyaṟpālaṉa.
"raḻa oḻinta maṟṟiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷ tammoṭutām mayaṅkum eṉpatuvē cūttirattiṉ poruḷ. itaṉāl, ṅakarat toṭu ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiyē āciriyar karutiṉār eṉṟu tuṇital amiyātu. āyiṉ uriyāciriyar, eṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colliyē utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉārāl eṉiṉ, ataṉi nōkkutum;
"	yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum"5
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uriyil, yaraḻakkaḷukku muṉ ṅakaram varutaṟku utāraṇamāka avar, vēyṅaṉam, vērṅaṉam, vīḻṅaṉam eṉpavaṟṟik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ivvutāraṇaṅkaḷil vēy, vēr, vīḻ, eṉpaṉa nilimoḻikaḷākaliṉ, ṅaṉam eṉpatu varumoḻi eṉa naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. ākavē ṅakaram moḻi mutal vantatāku
maṉṟē? ivvutāraṇamē āciriyar karutiṉārāyiṉ, 'mutalā
keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum' eṉṟu ṅakarattip pirittuk kūṟutaliṉṟi, 'mutalākeḻuttut tōṉṟum eṉpa' eṉpatu pōlaṉṟē cūttirañ ceytiruppār? ṅakaram moḻimutal vārātataṉāṉaṉṟē ataṉi mutalākeḻuttoṭu cērkkātu pirittuk kūṟiṉār? iccūttirattiṟku uriyāciriyar kāṭṭiya 'vēyṅaṉam' mutaliyavi utāraṇamākumō? itu pōṉṟē, 'meynnili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uriyil avar kāṭṭiya 'eṅṅaṉam' eṉṉum utāraṇamum amivuṭiyataṉṟu" eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
'yaraḻa' eṉṟu toṭaṅkum cūttiram, meynnili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttiratti yoṭṭi muṟpaṭa amintuḷatu. yaraḻa voṟṟiṉmuṉ ṅakaram vantu mayaṅkutaluṇṭeṉpatum, ṅakaram taṉṉōṭu tāṉ mayaṅkutaluṇṭeṉpatum, ivviru cūttirat tālum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉataṟku vēyṅaṉam' mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, piṉṉataṟku 'eṅṅaṉam' eṉpataṉiyum uriyāciriyarum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiṉar. iraṇṭu cūttirat tiṟkum iruvarum kāṭṭiya iruvaki utāraṇamum poruntā eṉṟu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. piṉṉataṟku avarkaḷ kāṭṭum utāraṇam piṉpu ārāytaṟkurittu. yaraḻa muṉ ṅakaram muyaṅkutaṟku uriyāḷarkaḷ kāṭṭiya utāraṇam amiyātu eṉṟavarkaḷ tām vēṟu kāṭṭavumilli. eppaṭiyum 'ṅaṉam' eṉpataṉi ēṟṟuk koḷḷalākātu eṉpatē avarkaḷ kaṭip piṭi. ataṟku avarkaḷ kūṟumkāraṇam 'ṅaṉam' eṉpataṉik koṇṭāl ṅakaram moḻikku mutalākiṉṟatu. ṅakaram moḻi mutalākumēl 'mutalākeḻuttu ṅakaramōṭu tōṉṟum' eṉa mutalākeḻuttiṉ vēṟāka ṅakarattip pirittuk kūṟutal amiyātu eṉpatē nallatu; avarkaḷ vātam evvaḷavu poruntumeṉṟu pārppōm. tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatu niṟka. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷukku naṉṉūl viti kūṟutaliṉ atu tōṉṟutaṟku muṉpu toṭṭē accoṟkaḷ vaḻakkiliruntu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu vāymiyaṉṟō? pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka aṅṅaṉam avi vaḻaṅki varā niṟkavum, ṅaṉam eṉpatu orukālattum ōriṭattum taṉittu vaḻaṅkāmikku avarkaḷ yātu kāraṇam kūṟuvārkaḷ? aḵtu aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkutaṟkum vaḻaṅkāmikkum, avvāṟu vaḻaṅkutalum vaḻaṅkāmiyumē kāraṇameṉṟu tāṉ avarkaḷ kūṟavēṇṭum! ivvāṟṟāl, tolkāppiyattil yaraḻamuṉ ṅakaram mayaṅkum eṉpataṟkum ṅakaram taṉṉoṭu tāṉ mayaṅkum eṉṟataṟkum, tolkāppiyam muḻutukkum uriyeḻutiṉōrākiya uriyāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷē poruntuvaṉavām eṉpatum,  tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ārāyntu nūliyaṟṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār yaraḻa oṟṟiṉ muṉṉum, ṅakara oṟṟiṉ muṉṉum ṅakaram vantu mayaṅkiya coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉamikaṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku viti kūṟiyuḷḷār eṉpatum, avvāṟāyiṉum ṅaṉam eṉpatu yaraḻa vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷi yoṭṭiyum, cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷi yoṭṭiyum vaḻaṅkiyataṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkāmiyiṉ ṅakaram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ varumeṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar eṉpatum, naṉṉūlār kālattil yaraḻavoṟṟiṉ muṉ ṅakaram mayaṅkiya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkāṟaṟṟup pōkac cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ ṅaṉam eṉpatu cērnta coṟkaḷē vaḻaṅkiṉamiyiṉ avvaḻakkiṟku maṭṭil viti kūṟiṉār eṉpatum, ṅaṉam eṉpatu taṉittu vaḻaṅkāviṭiṉum taṉakkeṉa ōr poruḷuṭiyatātal paṟṟit tamatu matampaṭa 'ṅavvum mutalākum' eṉṟār eṉpatum naṉkayaṟiyappaṭum uṇmikaḷākum eṉka. ṅakara meyyuṭaṉ uyir kūṭiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirmey yeḻuttukkaḷil 'ṅā' mutaliya patiṉōreḻuttukkaḷum tamiḻ moḻiyil yāṇṭum varuvatilli. atupaṟṟiyē "ṅappōlvaḷi" eṉpatoru mutumoḻiyum tōṉṟiṟṟu. akaraṅkūṭiya ṅakarattāṉum 'ṅaṉam' eṉpataṟkaṉṟi vēṟu collukku uṟuppāy vara yārum kaṇṭatumilli. kēṭṭatumilli. ippoḻutu ataṉiyum cekuttuviṭa reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ muyalkiṉṟārkaḷ. avveḻuttiṉ mītu avarkaṭku attakiya ceṟṟam uṇṭāyatu eṉṉiyō!
5. ṅakarattuṭaṉ ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku "uṇṇuṅṅkaḷiṟu" eṉṉum oṟṟaḷapeṭiyi avarkaḷ utāraṇaṅkāṭṭiyatu muḻuppōliyām eṉpataṉic ciṟumakārum aṟivar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār nūṉ marapiṉkaṇ meymmayakkaṅ kūṟuvāṉṟoṭaṅki,
"	ṭaṟalaḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark kacapa eṉṉum mūveḻut turiya"1
eṉpatu mutal
"	yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum"2
eṉpatu kāṟumuḷḷa ēḻu cūttiraṅkaḷāl vēṟṟu nili meym mayakkaṅ kūṟi,
"	meynnili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē"1
eṉṉum cūttirattāl meykaḷ tammoṭu tām mayaṅkum uṭaṉili meymmayakkaṅ kūṟuvārāyiṉar. iviyellām meymmayakkamēyāyiṉum taṉi meyyuṭaṉ uyirmey mayaṅku
māṟu kūṟiyaṉavēyākum. ṭakaravoṟṟu mutalāyiṉavaṟṟiṉ muṉ taṉimeyvantu mayaṅkutaṟku iṭamiṉmiyumōrka.  āciriyar taṉi meyyuṭaṉ taṉi meymayaṅkumiyalpiṉi, moḻi marapiṉkaṇ,
"	yaraḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟu muṉ ṉoṟṟak
 kacatapa ṅañanama īroṟ ṟākum"2*
eṉṉum cūttirattāl vitittup pōntaṉar. naṉṉūlārum immuṟi piṟaḻāmalē vēṟṟunili meymmayakkamum, uṭaṉili meymmayakkamum, īroṟṟu mayaṅkum mayakkamum kūṟuvārāyiṉar. uriyāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivvuṇmikku māṟu
pāṭiṉṟiyē uriyeḻuti, utāraṇamuṅ kāṭṭiṉar. piṉ vanta ilakkaṇa viḷakka nūlārum ivvāṟē kūṟippōntaṉar. ivvāṟirukka, oṟṟaḷapeṭiyil ṅakaravoṟṟu iraṇṭu vantiruttal pōlvaṉa 'meynnili cuṭṭiṉ, eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟateṉṟu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ kūṟuvatu ciṟitēṉum poruntuvatākumā? ceyyuṭkē yuriyateṉṉuṅ karuttāl āciriyar poruḷatikārattuc ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiya oṟṟaḷapeṭiyi īṇṭuk koṇarntu porutta muyalvatu periyatōr vintiyē. 'meynnili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttiratti viṭṭuviṭṭāl 'kaṇṇ' 'poṉṉ' eṉa oṟṟaḷapeṭiyil meykaḷ mayaṅki niṟṟaṟku ilakkaṇam iṉṟeṉak kūṟum reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ uyiraḷapeṭiyil neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉ a.mutaliya uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ mayaṅki niṟṟaṟku ilakkaṇam yāṇṭuk kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ? avi iraṇṭeḻuttu mayaṅkiya mayakkam eṉpatē uriyāḷarkaḷukkuk karuttāyiṉ, evviṭat
tilēṉum avaṟṟi eṭuttukkāṭṭi amittiruppārkaḷē? āciriyar yātēṉumoṉṟik kūṟātu viṭṭiruppiṉ ataṉi amittuk kōṭaṟ poruṭṭaṉṟō puṟaṉaṭi, utti mutalāyiṉa
vaṟṟik kūṟi vittuḷḷār? puṟaṉaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟilum uriyāḷar avaṟṟi eṭuttamittilāmiyiṉ, avi īreḻuttu mayaṅkiyamayakkamallaveṉpatē avarkaḷ karuttātal peṟappaṭum. aḷapeṭi eṉpatu eṉṉi? neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉar ataṟku iṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttu vantum, oṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉ avvoṟṟē vantum muṉṉiyavaṟṟiṉ ōciyi mikuvippatē aḷapeṭi yākum. āa" eṉṉum uyiraḷapeṭiyil akaram ākārat tuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭu ataṉi mūṉṟu māttiriyiṉatākac ceyta taṉṟi, vēṟu taṉiyōciyi uṭiyataṉṟām. 'kaṇṇ' eṉṉum oṟṟaḷapeṭiyilum piṉṉuḷḷa ṇakaravoṟṟu muṉṉuḷḷa taṉōṭu orumiyuṟṟu ataṉi oru māttiriyiṉatāka mikuvitta attuṇiyaṉṟi, vēṟu taṉippaṭṭa ōciyiṉataṉṟu. mikūum, tarūum, teṟūum, eṉṉum uyiraḷapeṭikaḷil mikuuum, taruuum, eṉa aḷapu niṟikka vanta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ iraṇṭu taṉittaṉi yeḻuti vaḻaṅkappaṭāmal neṭil oṉṟum kuṟil oṉṟumāka eḻuti vaḻaṅkappaṭutalē aḷapu niṟikkavanta eḻuttukkaḷ muṉṉiyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumiyuṭiyavalla eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. ivvāṟṟāl uyirkaḷil mūṉṟu māttiriyik kuṟippataṟku ōreḻuttum, meykaḷil oru māttiriyik kuṟippataṟku oreḻuttum illāmiyālēyē avi āa, kaṇṇ eṉa vevvēṟeḻuttukkaḷāka eḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu kaṭip piṭikka. ceyyuḷiyalil māttiri vakiyāl aci kōṭaliṉ, aḷapeṭi neṭilum kuṟilumākap pakukkappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatum, āṇṭum oṟṟaḷapeṭiyiluḷḷa oṟṟu iraṇṭum pakukkappaṭutal iṉṟeṉpatum uṇaraṟpālaṉa. eṉavē, ṅṅ, ṇṇ eṉa oṟṟaḷapeṭiyil varuvaṉa iṭiyaṟavu paṭṭicikkum iruvēṟeḻuttukkaḷākāmiyiṉ, 'avi mayaṅkutaṟku vēṟu ilakkaṇam iṉmiyiṉ "meynnili cuṭṭiṉ" eṉṉuñ cūttiram ammayakkattik kuṟikka vanta teṉpatu pōliyātal kāṇka. maṟṟum, accūttirattiṟ kuṟippiṭappeṟum patiṉāṟu eḻuttukkaḷil valleḻuttāṟum kk, cc eṉa īroṟṟāka mayaṅkāmiyiṉ, taṉimeyyuṭaṉ uyirmeyyāka mayaṅkuvaṉavē avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇamākum. aṅṅaṉam taṉimeyyuṭaṉ uyirmey uṭaṉiliyāka mayaṅkutal kūṟum accūttirattil mayakkattiṟkuriya vallāta oṟṟaḷapeṭiyiṉ oṟṟukkaḷik koṇarntu pukutti, avi mayaṅkutaṟku accūttiram vitiyāmeṉak kūṟutalyāmi mayirāṟ kayiṟu tirippatu pōlum viyakkattakka ceytiyē yāmeṉka!
itukāṟum ceytupōnta ārāycciyāl 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliya colvaḻakkik karutiyē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ''meynnili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattil ṅakaram uṭaṉiliyāka mayaṅkumāṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār eṉpatum, ataṟku māṟāka aṅṅaṉam mutaliyaṉa tolvaḻakkiṉa alla eṉṟum, aṅkaṉam mutaliyaṉavē tolvaḻakkiṉa eṉṟum aḻivaḻakkurippār kūṟṟu oru ciṟitum aṭāteṉpatum nilināṭṭappaṭṭami kāṇka. uṇmi ivvāṟākavum, yāṉ muṉpu eḻutiyavaṟṟik kuṟittu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ 'kālattiṉ kōlamē' eṉa ōlamiṭṭatum, 'anniyāyam niyāyamē' eṉac cāpamiṭṭatum ettuṇi nērmiyuṭiyaṉa eṉpati avarkaḷāṟ karutappaṭṭa ceppamuṭiyār ārāyntu kūṟuvārāka.
iṉi, 'meynnili cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttiram 'āṭi niḻaliṉ aṟiyattōṉṟi' nāṭutaliṉṟip poruḷ naṉi viḷaṅka' amintuḷḷatākalāṉum, ataṟku urikaṇṭa iruvarum ataṉ poruḷi naṉku pulappaṭuttiyuḷḷārākalāṉum reṭṭi

yāravarkaḷ accūttirattiṉ poruḷi aṟintilar eṉṟu karutuvatu iḻukkākum eṉa uṉṉiyē, avarkaḷ ataṉi maṟantārkaḷ eṉa muṉpu eḻutiṉēṉ. āyiṉ, avarkaḷ piṉṉar eḻutiyavaṟṟi nōkkiyaviṭattu, accūttirap poruḷi avarkaḷ aṟintilar eṉpatum, atuvēyaṉṟi, ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa "yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar" eṉṉum cūttirap poruḷiyum aḷapeṭiyiṉ iyalpu mutaliya piṟavaṟṟiyum avarkaḷ aṟintilar eṉpatum, avvāṟṟāl, avarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppatākak kūṟum tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāravuri vaḻukkaḷ malintatoṉṟāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatum pulaṉāyiṉa.

21. yāppiyal amiyāc ceyyuḷ
tamiḻ moḻi vaḻakku eṉavum ceyyuḷ eṉavum iru vakiyiṉi uṭiyatu. makkaḷ palarum pēcum col naṭiyē vaḻakku eṉpatu. kaṟṟuvalla pulavarkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭuvatu ceyyuḷ ākum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ceyyuḷi aṭivariyaṟi uṭiyatu eṉavum, aṭivariyaṟi illatu eṉavum iruvakippaṭuttu urikkiṉṟār. muṉṉatil veṇpā mutaliya pākkaḷ yāvum aṭaṅkum. piṉṉati āṟu vakippaṭuttut tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉum nūl, uri eṉṟa iraṇṭiṉuḷ avaṟṟi nām aṭakkik koḷvōm. eṉavē ceyyuḷ eṉpatu pāc ceyyuḷ, nūṟ ceyyuḷ, uricceyyuḷ eṉa mūvakip
paṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ nūṟ ceyyuḷ eṉpatu cūttiram. uricceyyuḷ eṉpatu kaṭṭuri. ippoḻutu ceyyuḷ eṉṟa aḷavil cūttiramō, kaṭṭuriyō uṇarappaṭuvatilli. veṇpā, āciriyappā, kalippā, vañcippā, eṉṉum pākkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ iṉamākiya tāḻici tuṟi viruttaṅkaḷum, maruṭpā, paripāṭal eṉpaṉavumē ceyyuḷ eṉṟu uṇarappaṭuvaṉa. ivi pāṭṭu eṉṉum peyarālum vaḻaṅkaṟkuriyaṉa.
yākki, yāppu eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum yāttal (kaṭṭutal) eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟantaṉa. palvakit tātukkaḷāl yākkappaṭṭatu uṭampu. palvakic coṟkaḷāl yākkappaṭṭatu ceyyuḷ. yāvarum aḻakuḷḷavaṟṟiyē virumpuvar. uṭampu aḻakuṭiyatāvatu ataṉ uṟuppukkaḷ palavum ēṟṟa vaṭivam uruvam uṭiyaṉavātalāl. avvāṟē ceyyuḷum ataṉ uṟuppukkaḷ ēṟṟa aḷavum ōciyum uṭiyaṉavāyiṉ aḻakuṭiyatākiṉṟatu. ceyyuḷ uṟuppukkaḷāvaṉa: eḻuttu, aci, cīr, taḷi, aṭi, toṭi mutalāyiṉa. avi ellāvaṟṟiṟkum aḷavu allatu variyaṟi uṇṭu. avvariyaṟikaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭṭāl vevvēṟu vakiyāṉa ōcikaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. avvōci vēṟupāṭu paṟṟiyē veṇpā mutaliya pākkaḷ vakukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi yāvum cērntatē yāppiyal eṉpatu. eṉavē, yāppiyal muṟṟum amiyāta ceyyuḷ uṇṭu eṉpataṟkē iṭamilli. āṉāl, ōruṭampu evvaḷavu aḻakuṭiya
tāyiṉum, ataṉuḷ irukkum uyiriṉ aṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟpavē atu matippup peṟuvatu pōla, ceyyuḷum ataṉkaṇ aminta karuttiṉ ciṟappiṉālēyē peritum matikkappaṭuva
tākum. cilar ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ variyaṟikaḷ kavi taṉ karutti uḷḷavāṟu veḷiyiṭutaṟkut taṭiyāyiruttaliṉ avi kūṭā eṉpar. avarkku nām kūṟuvatu, urikkum poruḷuk kēṟpavē orucār ceyyuṭkaḷ kaṭumiyāṉa variyaṟi yuḷḷaṉa vākavum, maṟṟorucāraṉa aṅṅaṉam illātaṉavākavum vakukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. kattūriyi ataṉ maṇam keṭātavāṟu tiṭpamuṭiya ciṟiya cimiḻiṟ peytu vippatu pōla, makkaṭku aṟivuṟuttum uyartaṉik karuttiṉi amip pataṟku variyaṟi mikavuṭiya ciṟiya veṇpāvāṉatu karuvi yākak koḷḷappeṟṟatu. uḷḷak kiḷarcciyi uṇṭākkuṅ karuttiṉi veḷippaṭuttat tuḷḷalōciyiṉi uṭiya kalippāviṉik karuviyākak koṇṭaṉar.  oru nīṇṭa karuttu allatu varalāṟṟiṉi orē toṭarcciyākac colvataṟku akavaṟpā karuviyāyiṟṟu. akavalukku mikutiyāṉa variyaṟi allatu kaṭṭuppāṭu illi. akaval allatu āciriyappāvi āyiram aṭivariyil pāṭalāmeṉa vitittuḷḷaṉar āyiram eṉpati aḷaviṉmi eṉṟē nām koḷḷalām. etuki, mōṉi, iyipu mutaliya toṭi vikaṟpaṅkaḷum itaṟku oru taliyāka vēṇṭumeṉṉum niyatiyilli. ivvāṟṟāl, āṅkilattil  Blank - Verse eṉpataṉōṭu iḵtu oruvāṟu, oṟṟumiyuṭiyatu eṉalām. nīṇṭa karuttiyō, varalāṟṟiyō, uriyāṭaliyō taṭiyiṉṟic collic celvataṟku ituvē amiyum. ātaliṉ yāppiyal amiyāc ceyyuḷ eṉṟu putitāka oṉṟu tēṭavēṇṭiyatilli.
iṉi, etuki, mōṉi mutaliya toṭikaḷ karuttiṉit taṭiyiṉṟic colvataṟku iṭiyūṟākum eṉac cilar karutuvar. itu muḻu uṇmiyākātu. ciṟuvarkaḷ iyalpākavē etuki, mōṉikaḷik kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar. potumakkaḷ vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻikaḷilum peṇṭirkaḷ kūṟum oppāri mutaliya vaṟṟilum etuki mōṉi nayaṅkaḷ iyalpāka amitalik kāṇkiṟōm. ātaliṉ itaṉi iyaṟkikku māṟu eṉṟu collutal amiyātu. maṟṟum iviyellām ciṟanta ōcikaḷukkuk kāraṇamākiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ coṟ kaḷāl maṭṭumaṉṟi ōci vēṟupāṭṭālum uṇarttappaṭuvaṉa eṉpati nām karuttil iruttutal vēṇṭum. ōciyāṉatu uṇarcciyi eḻuppāviṭil icik kaliyiṉāl eṉṉa payaṉuṇṭu? palvēṟu vakiyāṉa uṇarccikaḷip pulap paṭuttavum, uṇṭākkavum ōci karuviyāka iruttalāl aṉṟē nam muṉṉōrāya aṟiñar palarum palvaki ōci nalamuṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷālēyē nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟip pōntaṉar? muṉ āciriyappāvip paṟṟi viḷakkiyaviṭattu, etuki, mōṉi ataṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa alla eṉak kūṟiṉōm, āyiṉum cāṉṟōrkaḷ palarum perumpālum avi poruntum paṭiyākavē ceyyuṭkaḷ iyaṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ karuttut taṭiyuṇṭatu eṉpataṟku iṭamilli. āṅkilattil Rythm eṉappaṭum. cantam allatu tāḷavaṟutikku etuki mōṉikaḷ ciṟanta karuvikaḷākum. itaṟku utāraṇamāka,
	kātaliyik kāriruḷiṟ kāṉakattē kiviṭṭa
pātakaṉip pārkkap paṭāteṉṟō - nātam
aḷikkiṉṟa āḻivāy āṅkalava ōṭi
oḷikkiṉṟa teṉṉō vuri.1
	maliyiṭip piṟavā maṇiyē yeṉkō
aliyiṭip piṟavā amiḻtē yeṉkō
yāḻiṭip piṟavā iciyē yeṉkō
