aṉṟiravu piḷḷiyār aṭiyārkaḷōṭum appatiyilēyē taṅkiṉār. tiruvaṟattuṟiyil vīṟṟirukkum civaperumāṉ kāḻip piḷḷiyāriṉ vaḻi varuttattit tiruvuḷat taṭittu, avar ēṟutaṟku muttuc civikiyum muttukkuṭiyum, ūtutaṟku muttuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum koṭuttaruḷattiruvuḷaṅ koṇṭu, tirunelvāyilirukkiṉṟa maṟiyōrellārukkum taṉittaṉiyē kaṉavilē tōṉṟi, 'ñāṉa campantaṉ nampāl aṇikiṉṟāṉ; nīṅkaḷ, muttuc civikiyum muttuk kuṭiyum muttuc ciṉṉaṅ kaḷum nammiṭattil eṭuttu avaṉiyaṭintu koṭuṅkaḷ' eṉṟu aruḷic ceytaṉar. uṭaṉē antaṇarellārum viḻit teḻuntu, oruṅku kūṭi, makiḻntu, aṟputa meytiya cintiyōṭum kōyili vantaṭintu, tirukkōyiṟ paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkum avvaruḷuṇṭāṉami yaṟintu viyantu, tiruppaḷḷi yeḻuccik kālam vara, tirukkāppi nīkki, muttuc civikiyiyum muttuk kuṭiyiyum muttucciṉṉaṅkaḷiyum kaṇṭu, kaḷippu mikutiyāl āravārittu, palavaki vāttiyaṅkaḷum olikka, aṭiyārkaḷuṭaṉ, avaṟṟic cumantu koṇṭu āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyāriṭattiṟkup pōṉārkaḷ. ataṟku muṉ, paramacivaṉ piḷḷiyārkkum kaṉavilē tōṉṟi 'arattuṟiyil vīṟṟirukkum vaḷḷalākiya nām makiḻntu aḷikkum muttuc civiki mutaliyavaṟṟi nī ēṟṟuk koḷḷutal takum' eṉṟu uḷḷavāṟu aruḷicceyya piḷḷiyār viḻitteḻuntu, tām kaṉaviṟkaṇṭa pēraruṭ paṇpiṉit tantiyārkkum aṭiyār kaḷukkum aruḷicceytu, vikaṟiyilē ceyviṉi muṟṟi, veṇṇīṟu aṇintu, tiruvinteḻutti ōtikkoṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiṉār. pariti vāṉavaṉum pukali vēntaric civikiyiṉmēl ēṟṟiṭak kātalittavaṉ pōlat tēriṉmītu vantu tōṉṟiṉāṉ. appoḻutu antaṇarkaḷum civaṉaṭiyār kaḷum araara eṉṉum muḻakkattuṭaṉ muttuc civiki mutaliya vaṟṟit tāṅkikkoṇṭu etirē vantu, 'ivi tiruvaṟat tuṟiyil vīṟṟirukkiṉṟa mutalvar tanta pēraruḷākum, ēṟṟaruḷvīr' eṉṟu kūṟi, iṟivar taṅkaḷukku aruḷic ceytavaṟṟi oṉṟum oḻiyāmal urittārkaḷ. piḷḷiyār avaṟṟik kēṭṭu, akkaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷi "entiyīcaṉem perumāṉ" eṉṉun tiruppatikattiṉālē pāṭi,
"	cōti muttiṉ civikicūḻ vantupār
mītu tāḻntuveṇ ṇīṟṟoḷi pōṟṟiniṉ
ṟātiyā raruḷ ātaliṉ añceḻut
tōti yēṟiṉār uyya ulakelām"1
āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyār muttuc civikiyi valamvantu nilamici viḻuntu vaṇaṅki yeḻuntu, tiruvinteḻutti ōtikkoṇṭu, ulakamelām uyyumpaṭi, atil ēṟiyaruḷiya
poḻutu civaṉaṭiyāḷarkaḷellārum āravārittārkaḷ; maṟiyōrkaḷ vēta muḻakkañceytārkaḷ; vāṉōrkaḷ āravārittu malar maḻi poḻintaṉar; palavakippaṭṭa maṅkala vāttiyaṅkaḷ olittaṉa. vaḷarmatiyāṉatu pāṟkaṭalil utittāṟ
pōla muttuc civikiyil ēṟiyaruḷiya karuṇi vaḷḷalākiya tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyār, mēlē muttukkuṭi niḻaṟṟavum, aṭiyārkaḷum maṟiyōrkaḷum mutalāyiṉārkaḷ neruṅki uvaki mikutiyāṟ kūttāṭit taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum poḻikiṉṟa puṉal veḷḷattiṟ kuḷikkavum, muttuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ "ēḻulakaṅkaḷum aṭiyārkaḷum maṟikaḷum uyyumpaṭi ñāṉa campantar vantār; ulaka muḻutum niṟintirukkiṉṟa umā tēviyār ñāṉappālūṭṭa uṇṭaruḷiya pālaṟāvāyar vantār; vētamutaliya kalikaḷaṉittiyum ōtātuṇarnta muttamiḻ virakar vantār" eṉṟu ūtavum, ceṉṟu, tiruvaṟattuṟiyi aṭintu, kōpura vāyilukkut tūrattilē muttuc civikiyiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅki, vaṇaṅki yeḻuntu, tirukkōyiliṉuḷḷē pukuntu, valañ ceytu tirumuṉpaṇintu, iṟivaṉ ṟiruvaruḷip palavāṟu paravip patikampāṭi, cilanāḷ appatiyilē eḻuntaruḷiyiruntār. irukkum nāḷilē, tirunelveṇṇey mutaliya patikaḷukku aṉparkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟu toḻutu tirumpiṉār. 

9. tiruviḻā
viḻavu eṉpatu koṇṭāṭṭam allatu kaḷiyāṭṭattik kuṟikkum tamiḻccol. itu cāṟu eṉṉum peyarālum vaḻaṅkum. nakkīrar pāṭiya tirumurukāṟṟup paṭiyil 'ūrūr koṇṭa cīrkeḻu viḻaviṉum' eṉṟum, 'cāṟayarkaḷattu vīṟu peṟattōṉṟi' eṉṟum iru collum payiṉṟiruttal kāṇka. makkaḷyāvarum iṉpattiyē virumpukiṉṟaṉareṉpatu kaṇkūṭāya uṇmi. ivvulakilē makkaḷ iṉpa nukarcciyiṉ poruṭṭuc ceyyum koṇṭāṭṭa mellām viḻavu eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkutaṟ kuriyaṉavē. yāṟṟilē putuppuṉal vantuḻi āṭavarum makaḷirum ceṉṟu nīrāṭi iṉpan tuykkum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ putuppuṉal viḻavu eṉak kūṟappaṭum. iṅṅaṉamē kaṭaṟkarik kāṉal viḷiyāṭṭu, uṇṭāṭṭu āya viḻākkaḷ mikkiruntaveṉpatu paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṉpu kāṭṭiya 'ūrūr koṇṭa cīrkeḻu viḻaviṉum' eṉṉum toṭarē akkālattu ovvoru ūrilum viḻāk koṇṭāṭappeṟṟu vantateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. akapporuḷilē ūrtuñcāmi yeṉpatōr tuṟiyuḷatu. ūriluḷḷārellārum viḻāk koṇṭāṭutalāl iravil tuyilātiruppar eṉpatu ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam nakaraṅkaḷilum, ūrkaḷilum viḻācceytal mikkirunta ceytiyāṉatu akkālattu makkaḷ celvattilum iṉpattilum ciṟantiruntaṉa reṉpatip pulappaṭuttāniṟkum. celvamum, iṉpamum nāṭṭiṉi aḻaku ceyvaṉa eṉpatu
"	piṇiyiṉmi celvam viḷiviṉpam ēmam
aṇiyeṉpa nāṭṭiṟkiv vintu"1
eṉṟu teyvap pulavarāṉum kūṟappeṟṟaḷḷatu. paḻiya pulavarkaḷ 'viḻavu mali mūtūr' 'viḻavu mēmpaṭṭa paḻaviṟal  mūtūr' (perumpā.411) eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam akkālattup patikaḷic ciṟappittuk kūṟiyiruppatu viḻā oṉṟiliruntē celvap perukku mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷalākum eṉpataṉik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, teyvaṅkaṭkuc ceyyappaṭum ittiruviḻākkaḷ tāmum, kuṟiñci, mulli mutaliya vevvēṟu nilattu makkaḷāl vēṟu vēṟu peyariṉiyuṭiya kaṭavuḷarik kuṟittu vevvēṟu muṟimiyākac ceyyappaṭuvaṉavākum. aviyellām īṇṭu virittaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟu. iraṇṭoru tiruviḻākkaḷ paṇṭu naṭanta muṟimiyi aṟintu koḷḷutal, ataṉoṭu ippoḻutu tiruviḻā nikaḻum iyalpiṉi oppiṭṭup pārttaṟkum, cīrtiruttam ceyvataṟkum utaviyāyirukkumākaliṉ, atuvē iṅkuk kūṟakkarutiyatākum.
iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷukku nakkīrar iyaṟṟiya uriyil 'maturi āvaṇi yaviṭṭam, uṟiyūrp paṅkuṉi yuttiram, karu'd2vūr uḷḷi viḻā', eṉa mūṉṟu periya tiruviḻākkaḷ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. akanāṉūṟu mutaliyavaṟṟilum ittiruviḻākkaḷ kuṟikkap
paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl ivi ivvivvāṟu naṭantaṉa veṉṟu naṉku aṟintu koḷvataṟku ātaravukaḷ kiṭikkavilli. kārttikit tiṅkaḷilē tirukkārttiki nāḷil iravilē terukkaḷil viḷakkukaḷi niri niriyāka vittu, mālikaḷāl aṇivittup palarum kūṭi makiḻvuṭaṉ viḻāvayarvar eṉṟa ceyti akanāṉuṟṟiluḷḷa nakkīrar pāṭṭu oṉṟiṉāl veḷiyākiṟatu. ivvāṟṟāl ēṟakkuṟiya iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē tamiḻakattil ittakiya tiruviḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟṟu vantaṉa eṉpatu nām peritum pārāṭṭutaṟkum makiḻcci yaṭitaṟkum uriya ceytiyaṉṟō?
iṉip  paḻiya nāḷilē kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil  intiraṉukku viḻāc ceyta varalāṟu cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali eṉṉum iruperuṅ kāppiyattum viḷakkamākak kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. maṇimēkali viḻāvaṟi kātiyāl aṟiyappaṭum ceyti piṉ varuvatu.
'kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattilirunta pala camayavātikaḷum, cōtiṭarum, amiccar mutaliya aimperuṅkuḻukkaḷum, eṇpērāyat
tārum, piṟarum oruṅku kūṭiyiruntu, paṇṭu akattiya muṉivar kaṭṭaḷiyāl tūṅkeyileṟinta toṭittōṭ cempiyaṉ eṉṉum cōḻamaṉṉaṉāl intiraṉatu uṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟup pukār nakarilē irupatteṭṭu nāḷ naṭattiyatum, piṉṉar āṇṭutōṟum aṅṅaṉamē naṭattappeṟṟu varuvatumākiya intira viḻāvi naṭattat tavaṟiṉāl nikaḻakkūṭiya tīmikaḷi
yellām naṉku cūḻntu viḻāviṉi naṭattuvateṉṟu uṟuti ceytu koṇṭu, ataṉi nakarattārukku aṟivikkumpaṭi muracaṟivōṉukkuc colliṉar. muracaṭikkum mutukuṭiyiṟ piṟantōṉākiya avaṉ, vaccirak kōṭṭattil vikkappaṭṭirunta muraciṉi yāṉiyiṉ piṭarilēṟṟi, mutaṟkaṇ ūriṉi vāḻtti, vāṉam tiṅkaṭōṟum mummaḻi poḻika, maṉṉavaṉ kōṭkaḷ nilitiriyāmikkuk kāraṇamākiya ceṅkōliṉaṉ ākuka, aṟiñarkaḷē! intiraviḻā naṭakkum kālattil ellāttēvarum iṅkē eḻuntaruḷuvar eṉpatu periyār tuṇipātaliṉ vīti mutaliyavaṟṟil niṟi kuṭaṅkaḷum, poṟpālikikaḷum, pāvi viḷakkukaḷum ākiya ivaṟṟip parappuṅkaḷ; kulikkamukum, kulivāḻiyum, vañciyum, pūṅkoṭiyum, karumpum ākiya ivaṟṟik kaṭṭuṅkaḷ; tiṇṇikaḷiṉ mēl tūṇkaḷil muttu mālikaḷit toṅkakkaṭṭuṅkaḷ';  vītikaḷilum maṉṟaṅkaḷilum paḻamaṇali māṟṟip putumaṇalip parappuṅkaḷ; palavakiyāṉa tukiṟkoṭikaḷiyum māṭaṅkaḷilum vāyilkaḷilum kaṭṭuṅkaḷ; kaṇṇutaṟ perumāṉ mutalākac catukkap pūtam iṟutiyākavuḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ uṟiyum kōyilkaḷil avvat teyvaṅkaṭku ēṟpa vevvēṟākac ceyyappaṭum kaṭaṉkaḷi aṟintōr ceyyuṅkaḷ; aṟavurikaḷ puritaṟkuriya aṟiñarkaḷē! pantarilum ampalaṅkaḷilum ceṉṟu virivurikaḷ puriyuṅkaḷ; camayavātikaḷē! paṭṭi maṇṭapattiyaṭintu muṟippaṭi vātam puriyuṅkaḷ; yāvarum yāriṭattum pakimiyum vekuḷiyum koḷḷātiruṅkaḷ' eṉṟu kūṟi muracaṟintu, *'paciyum piṇiyum pakiyum nīṅki, vaciyum vaḷaṉum curakka' eṉa vāḻtti iṅṅaṉam viḻāviṉit terivittaṉaṉ.
itiliruntu avviḻāviṉ nōkkam evvaḷavu uyarnta
tākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu, pāruṅkaḷ! ōr periya tiruviḻāc ceyvateṉṟāl nakarattiluḷḷa palavaki māntarkaḷum oruṅku kūṭiyiruntu ārāyntu muṭivu ceyvateṉṟa vaḻakkam akkālattu makkaḷiṉ oṟṟumiyiyum āḷuntiṟattiyum naṉku viḷakku
kiṉṟataṉṟō? tiruviḻā naṭakkum ovvoru nāḷilum nakarattiṉ pala pakkaṅkaḷilum aṟavurik kaṭṭurikaḷum, camaya varalāṟṟu ārāyccikaḷum nikaḻumeṉpatu tiruviḻāviṉ payaṉi evvaḷavu mikutippaṭuttik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu! kalviyum, camaya aṟivum kuṟintuḷḷa ikkālattil tiruviḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟum poḻutu ittakiya ceyalkaḷ nikaḻumēl evvaḷavu perumpayaṉ uṇṭākum. ikkālattil tiruviḻākkaḷi naṭatti vikkum aṟaniliyat talivarkaḷum, aṟap puravalarkaḷum, piṟarum iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu nam muṉṉōr ceytu kāṭṭiya vaḻikaḷi iṉiyēṉum piṉpaṟṟi yoḻukumāṟu perumāṉ tiruvaruḷpālippāṉāka!
'	paṟṟā mākkaḷ tammuṭa ṉāyiṉum
ceṟṟamum kalāmuñ ceyyā takalumiṉ'1
eṉṟu itiṟ kuṟittiruppati ovvoru matattiṉarum cintit tuṇarvarēl tiruviḻā naṭipeṟum tūya nāṭkaḷil kalakamum, koliyum nikaḻutaṟku iṭaṉuṇṭāmō?
iṉi, caṅka kālattiṟkuc ciṟitu piṉṉirunta camaya kuravarkaḷ kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭilē tiruviḻā naṭipeṟṟamikku ōr mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭi niṟuttutum.
civa camaya kuravarkaḷākiya tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyārum tirunāvukkaracarum aṭiyār kuḻāttuṭaṉ tiruppatikaḷ tōṟum ceṉṟu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭut tiruppatikam pāṭi varunāḷil, tiruppukalūril eḻuntaruḷiyirunta piḷḷiyār tirunāvuk
karacaṭikaḷ tiruvārūriṉiṉṟum pukalūr nōkki varuvatu kēḷvi
yuṟṟu aṭiyārkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟu etirkoṇṭu aḷavaḷāvi 'apparē! nīr varunāḷil tiruvārūriṟ ṟikaḻperumi vakutturippīr' eṉṉalum, tirunāvukkaracar tiruvārūril tiruvātirit tiruviḻā naṭanta ciṟappiṉi oru tiruppatikattāl aruḷic ceytaṉar. ataṉ peṟṟiyi aṟintu koḷvataṟkuc cila pāṭṭukkaḷ iṅkē kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa:-
"	muttuvi tāṉa maṇippoṟ kavari muṟiyālē
pattarka ḷōṭu pāviyar cūḻap palippiṉṉē
vittakak kōla veṇṭali māli viratikaḷ
atta ṉārūr ātiri nāḷāvatuvaṇṇam"2
"	vītika ṭōṟum veṇkoṭi yōṭu vitāṉaṅkaḷ
cōtikaḷ viṭṭuc cuṭarmāmaṇika ḷoḷitōṉṟac
cāti kaḷāya pavaḷamu muttut tāmaṅkaḷ
āti yārūr ātiri nāḷā vatuvaṇṇam"3
"	tuṉpam nummit toḻāta nāḷka ḷeṉpārum
iṉpam nummi yēttu nāḷka ḷeṉpārum
numpi ṉemmi nuḻiyap paṇiyē yeṉpārum
aṉpaṉ ārūr ātiri nāḷāvatu vaṇṇam"4

10. cintāmaṇiyiṉ nantāvoḷikaḷ
amiḻtiṉum iṉiya tamiḻ moḻiyākiya aṉṉikkup pēraṇikalaṉkaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākavuḷḷatu cīvakacintāmaṇi yeṉpatu. itaṉiyiyaṟṟiya āciriyar tiruttakkatēvar eṉṉum camaṇa muṉivarāvar. piṟkālattilē virutta yāppiṉāl peruṅkāppiyam iyaṟṟip pōnta kampanāṭar pōlum cīriya kavikaḷukkellām ōr vaḻikāṭṭiyākavuḷḷatu iccintāmaṇi yeṉṟāl itaṉ perumikku vēṟeṉṉa cāṉṟu vēṇṭum? epporuḷiṉiyum meyppāṭu tōṉṟakkūṟum ivvāciriyaratu tiṟam aḷaviṭṭurikkum takittaṉṟu. evvakic cuvikaḷiyum cevvitiṟ peytu vitta koḷkalam pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum ikkāppiyattit tamiḻaṟin
tōr yāvarum kaṟṟu iṉpuṟutal vēṇṭum. umāpati civāc
cāriyār pōlum cila periyōrkaḷ itaṉi yiḻitturittatu matak kōṭpāṭu paṟṟiyēyaṉṟi, ceyyuṭ cuvi mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiyaṉṟeṉka. māṇikkavācakari vīṭṭiṉṟaṉmiyi naṉku uṇarntōreṉak kūṟiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyārtāmē itaṟku uri ceytirukkiṉṟār! vīṭu pēṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi viḷakka nērntuḻi aṟṟatu paṟṟeṉi luṟṟatu vīṭu eṉpatu mutaliya tiruvāy moḻip (1-25) pakutikaḷiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya cīriya viṇavarāṉa parimēlaḻakarum,  (tiruk.349 uri) civañāṉapōtam mutaliya civa cittānta nūlkaṭkup pēruriyum, ciṟṟuriyum mutaliyaṉa vakuttaruḷiya mātavac civañāṉa yōkikaḷum mutalāyiṉār cintāmaṇiyiliruntu mēṟkōḷkaḷ eṭuttāṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉarē! ivvāṟirukkavum ikkālattuc camayappaṟṟiṉar eṉpōriṟ cilar ataṉik kaṇṇāl nōkkuvatum iḻukkuṭitteṉṟu karutuvatu tām ataṉkaṇ nuḻintu cellum mativali peṟṟirāmi paṟṟiyē pōlum!
iṉi, cintāmaṇiyilē kaṟpaṉi nayañciṟantu cuvi keḻumi viḷaṅkuvaṉavum, arumperu nītikaḷi aṟivuṟuttuvaṉavumākiya pāṭalkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟit tiraṭṭi veḷippaṭuttal kaṟpōrkkut tamiḻilē ārvatti yeḻuppi nalam payakku meṉṟu karuti, aṅṅaṉam puriya oruppaṭukiṉṟēṉ. itaṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyār eḻutiyuḷḷa uri mikac cīriyatāyiṉum ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷi naṉku payiṉṟavarkaḷukkē atu payaṉpaṭak kūṭum. ātaliṉ iṅkē tokukkaluṟum pāṭalkaḷi eḷiya poḻippuriyuṭaṉ veḷippaṭuttal kaṭaṉākiṉṟatu. nāmakaḷilampakam.
	kāymāṇṭa teṅkiṉ paḻamvīḻak kamuki ṉeṟṟip
pūmāṇṭa tīntēṉ ṟoṭikīṟi varukki pōḻntu
tēmāṅ kaṉicitaṟi vāḻip paḻaṅkaḷ cintum
ēmāṅ katameṉ ṟiciyāl ticipōya tuṇṭē.1
(uri) kāyttal māṭcimippaṭṭa teṅkiṉ neṟṟu viḻutalālē, kamukiṉ ucciyiluḷḷa polivu mikka tēṉ pōlum iṉiya nīriyuṭiya pākkuttāṟu kiḻintu, palāppaḻam piḷantu, tēmāṅkaṉi citaṟi, vāḻip paḻaṅkaḷ cintum ēmāṅkatam eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭṭup, pukaḻiṉālē tici muḻutum ceṉṟatoru nāṭu uṇṭeṉka.
	collaruñ cūṟpacum pāmpiṉ ṟōṟṟam pōl
mellavē karuvirun tīṉṟu mēlalār
celvamē pōṟṟali niṟuvit tērntanūṟ
kalvicēr māntariṉ iṟiñcik kāyttavē.2
(uri) neṟkaḷāṉavi ariya cūliyuṭiya paccip pāmpiṉ tōṟṟam pōl nērmiyākak karuttaṅki, niṉṟu, kuṭippiṟa vātavar celvam pōṉṟu taliyi nērē nimartti, meyp poruḷi ārāynta nūṟ kalviyiṉiyuṭiya māntarippōl mikavum vaḷintu viḷintaṉaveṉka. (col-nel)
	naṟṟavañcey vārkkiṭam tavañcey vārkkumaḵtiṭam
naṟporuḷ ceyvārkkiṭam poruḷ ceyvārkku maḵtiṭam
veṟṟa viṉpam viḻivippāṉ viṇṇuvantu vīḻnteṉa
maṟṟi nāṭu vaṭṭamāka viku maṟṟa nāṭarō.3
(uri) av ēmāṅkatam eṉṉum nāṭu viṇṇulakamāṉatu veṟṟiyiyuṭiya taṉatiṉpatti ulakiṉi virumpap paṇṇutaṟku makiḻntu vīḻnta teṉṉumpaṭi, taṉṉi yoḻinta nāṭu taṉakku elliyākap poruntiyirukkum. atu vīṭupēṟu karuti nalla tavattiṉic ceyvārkkum iṭam; atu maṟumi karuti illaṟam naṭattuvārkkum iṭam. atu ñāṉamākiya naṟporuḷ payakkum kalviyik kaṟpārkkum iṭam ivvulaka naṭikkuriya poruḷiṉit tēṭuvārkkum iṭameṉka.
	eṇṇeyu nāṉamu mivimūḻki yiruḷtirukkiṭ
ṭoṇṇaṟun tukiṟkiḻi potintuṟi kaḻittaṉapōl
kaṇṇiruṇṭu neṟimalkik kaṭikuḻaṉṟa karuṅkuḻalkaḷ
vaṇṇappō taruccittu makiḻvāṉāt takiyavē.1
(uri) eṇṇeyum mayirccāntu meṉkiṉṟa ivaṟṟilē muḻuki, iruḷip piṭittu nīḷa muṟukki oḷḷiyanaṟiya tukiṟ kiḻiyākiya uṟiyilē potintu, atiṉiṉṟu vākkappaṭṭavi pōla, nōkkiṉār kaṇ iruḷumpaṭi neṟippu mikkuk kaṭi kuḻinta kariya viciyiyiṉ kūntalkaḷ cēṭiyar aḻakiya pūvāl aruccittu vaḻipaṭṭālum varuntum taṉmiyaṉa eṉka.
	ivvuruvu neñceṉṉum kiḻiyiṉmē liruntilakkit
tavvuruvu niṉippeṉṉum tukilikiyāl varuttittuk
kavviyata ṉōkkiṉāṟ kaṇviṭuttuk kātaṉīr
cevvitiṟ ṟeḷintāṉāk kāmappūc citaṟiṉāṉ. 2
(uri) kūṟiya ivvuruvi oruppaṭṭiruntu neñceṉṉum paṭattilē kuṟittu, ataṉi niṉiveṉṉum eḻutukōlāl uṟuppu viḷaṅka eḻuti,  tāṉ kikkoṇṭa tiyāṉattālē nayaṉamōkkam paṇṇi, aṉpeṉṉum nīric cevviyākat teḷittu, amiyāta vēṭkiyākiya pūvic citaṟiṉāṉ eṉka. (caccantaṉ taṉmāmaṉ makaḷākiya viciyiyiṉ pēraḻakil īṭupaṭṭu avaḷi virumpiṉāṉ eṉpatu iṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭṭatu)
	āraṟi vikaḻtal cellā vāyirañ ceṅka ṇāṉum
kūraṟi vuṭiya nīrār coṟporuḷ koṇṭu cellum
pēraṟi vuṭiyi nīyum piṇiyaṉāṭ kavalañ ceyyum
ōraṟi uṭiyi yeṉṟā ṉuruttira tatta ṉeṉpāṉ. 3
(uri) (caccantaṉ nimittikaṉ kūṟṟi ēṟṟuk koḷḷāmi kaṇṭu) uruttira tattaṉ eṉpāṉ piṟarāl ikaḻappaṭāta niṟinta aṟiviṉiyuṭiya intiraṉum amiccar coṟporuḷik koṇṭu naṭakkum ; avaṉippōl nīyum aṟivuṭiyiyāyiṉum ippoḻutu ivaṉ collik koḷḷāmiyāl peṇmāṉ pōlum viciyikku avalañ ceyyum ōraṟivuṭiyi āyiṉāy eṉṟāṉ eṉka.
	kōṇili tirintu nāḻi kuṟipaṭap pakalkaṇ miñci
nīṇila māriyiṉṟi viḷivaḵkip paciyu nīṭip
pūṇaṇi makaḷir poṟpiṟ kaṟpaḻin taṟaṅkaṇ māṟi
āṇiyiv vulakukēṭā maracukōl kōṭi ṉeṉṟāṉ.1
(uri) aracaṉ nīti celuttuvatiṟ piṟaḻvāṉāyiṉ, kōḷkaḷ niṟkum nilikulintu, iraviṉ nāḻiki  kuṟiyap pakaṟ poḻutukaḷ mikku,  maḻiyillātu, nīṇilam viḷivu curuṅki, paci mikkup pūṇaṇinta makaḷiruṭiya aḻakiya kaṟpaḻintu, aṟaccālikaḷum iṉṟi, ivvulaku keṭāniṟkum; aracaṉ āṇiyeṉṟāṉ eṉka. (kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉ taṉṉi aracaṉākkiya caccantaṉik kollak karutiyapoḻutu tarumatattaṉ eṉṉum amiccaṉ iruvar piḻiyiyum uṭkoṇṭu kūṟiyatu itu)
	cātalum piṟattal tāṉum tamviṉip payatti ṉākum 
ātalum aḻivu mellā mavi poruṭ kiyalpukaṇṭāy
nōtalum  parivu mellām nuṇṇuṇar viṉmi yaṉṟē
pētinī peritum pollāy peyvaḷit tōḷi yeṉṟāṉ.2
(uri) vaḷiyalaṇinta tōḷkaḷi yuṭiya naṅkāy iṟattalum piṟattalum ātalum aḻitalum ākiya ellām tam viṉikaḷiṉ payaṉāluṇṭākum avvāṟirukka, iṟattaṟkum aḻitaṟkum tuṉpuṟuvatum, piṟattaṟkum ātaṟkum  iṉpuṟuvatum aṟiviṉmi yaṉṟē? viṉippayaṉuṟutal poruṭku iyalpātali uṇarā miyiṉ nī peritum aṟivuṭiyi yalli eṉṟāṉ eṉka. 	paṟṟā maṉṉaṉ nakarppuṟamāl pāyal piṇañcūḻ cuṭukāṭāl

eṟṟē yitukaṇ ṭēkātē yirutti yāleṉa iṉṉuyirē.3
(uri) ivvulaka muṭiyāy nī kiṭakkiṉṟa iṭam pakivaṉatu nakarp puṟamāy irā niṉṟatu; niṉatu pāyal piṇañ cūḻnta cuṭukāṭāy ōr tuṇiyumillāt tamiyēṉāy irā niṉṟēṉ; niṉṉi appāṟ koṇṭu pōkakkarutiṉ pōkalākāta ceṟinta iruḷāyirā niṉṟatu; mēl nī vaḷarumāṟum aṟiyēṉāyirā niṉṟēṉ eṉṟu piḷḷiyi nōkkik kūṟi, eṉatu koṭiya uyirē! nī iti yellām kaṇṭum pōkātu irāniṉṟāy; itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka:
	aṟiviṉāṟ periya nīrār aruviṉi kaḻiya niṉṟa
neṟiyiṉik kuṟuki yiṉpa niṟikaṭa lakattu niṉṟār
poṟiyeṉum peyara aivāyp poṅkaḻa laraviṉ kaṇṇē
veṟipulaṅ kaṉṟi niṉṟār vētaṉik kaṭaluḷ niṉṟār.1  
(uri) aṟiviṉāṟ periya iyalpiṉi uṭiyarāyiṉār viṉi kaḻiya niṉṟa naṉṉeṟiyiṉik kuṟuki niṟinta iṉpamākiya kaṭalakattē niṉṟaṉar. vēṭki yeṉṉum nañciṉiyuṭiya uṭampeṉṉum pāmpiṭattil poṟi yeṉṉum peyariyuṭiya aintu vāyiṭattē kaḷippic ceykiṉṟa poruḷkaḷilē aṭippaṭṭu niṉṟōr tuṉpamākiya kaṭaluḷē aḻuntiṉār eṉka. (ituvum piṉvarum pāṭalkaḷum cīvakaṉukku āciriyaṉ kūṟiya uṟuti moḻikaḷākum)
	kūṟṟuvaṉ koṭiyaṉākik kolittoḻiṟ karuvi cūḻntu
māṟṟarum valiyi vittāṉ vittati yaṟintu nāmum
nōṟṟavaṉ valiyi nīṅki nukarcciyi lulaka nōkki
āṟṟuṟap pōtal tēṟṟām aḷiyamō periya mēkāṇ.2

	pēraña riṭumpi yellām piḷantiṭum piṟappu nīkkum
āramir taritiṟ peṟṟām ataṉpayaṉ kōṭal tēṟṟām
ōrumim poṟiyu mōmpi yuḷapakal kaḻinta piṉṟik
kūreri  kavarum pōḻtiṟ kūṭumō kuṟittavellām. 3
(uri) periya tuṉpatti yellām nīkkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa piṟappaṟukkum maṉitayākkiyit tavappayaṉāṟ peṟṟām; peṟṟum ataṉ payaṉik koḷḷa aṟikiṉṟilēm. aimpoṟi
kaḷiyum aimpulaṉkaḷālē pēṇi uḷḷa nāḷ ceṉṟāl neruppāṉatu uṭampik kavarum poḻutu niṉitta aṟamellām ceyyak kūṭumō? ōrntu pārum eṉka.
	taḻaṅkural muraciṟ cāṟṟit tattuvan taḻuvan vēṇṭic       
ceḻuṅkaḷi yāḷar muṉṉar iruḷaṟac ceppiṉālum

koḻuṅkaḷiyuṇarvi ṉārik kuṇavataṅ koḷuttalāmō.1
(uri) ellārum tattuva neṟiyit taḻuvutal vēṇṭip pirakirutiyil mayaṅkik kiṭappār muṉ muracaṟintu iruḷ tīrac coṉṉālum atu koḷḷātu, muḻaṅkukiṉṟa aḻal vaṭivāya narakattiṉ mūḻkum muyaṟciyarākum maruṇṭa aṟiviṉarkkuc cīlam koḷuttalākumō eṉka.

pūṭcicāloḻukkameṉṉumvayirattāḻ koḷuvippollā

11. parañcōti muṉivarum 
tiruviḷiyāṭalum
ivar ēṟakkuṟiya 280 āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu, cōḻa maṇṭalattilē, tirumaṟikkāṭṭil, vaḻi vaḻic civarkaḷākiya apiṭēkattar marapil mīṉāṭci cuntara tēcikar eṉpavarkkup putalvarāyt tōṉṟiyavar; tantiyāriṭattil muṟiyāṉē tīkkikaḷ peṟṟu, tamiḻilum vaṭamoḻiyilumuḷḷa palavakiyāṉa ariya nūlkaḷiyuṅ kaṟṟuttuṟi pōyavar; civapatti aṭiyār patti mikkavar; aṅkayaṟ kaṇṇammiyiṉ tiruvaṭikku mikka aṉpu pūṇṭavar; ivar civaperumāṉ tirukkōyil koṇṭirukkum tiruppatikaḷ palavaṟṟukkum ceṉṟu, maturiyi aṭintu kayaṟkaṇ iṟiviyiyum cōmacuntarak kaṭavuḷiyum nāṭōṟum taricittu vaḻipaṭṭuk koṇṭu appatiyilvatiyum poḻutu, mīṉāṭci tēviyār tamakkuk kaṉavilē tōṉṟi 'emperumāṉ tiruviḷi yāṭalkaḷip pāṭuvāy' eṉṟu paṇikka, appaṇiyit tali mēṟ koṇṭu innūlip pāṭi muṭittu, cokkēcar cannitiyil aṟukāṟ pīṭattiliruntu, aṭiyārkaḷum pulavarkaḷum mutalāyiṉār kūṭiya pēraviyil itaṉi araṅkēṟṟiṉar eṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvar. ivar kālam 400 āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpā meṉṟum, 850 āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpāmeṉṟum iṅṅaṉam vēṟṟumippaṭak kūṟuvārumuḷar; oruvar kūṟṟum ātaravuṭaṉ kūṭiyataṉṟu; takka cāṉṟu kiṭitta vaḻiyē itu tuṇitaṟkuriya tākum. ivariyaṟṟiya vēṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ vētāraṇiyap purāṇam, tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ pōṟṟikkaliveṇpā, maturip patiṟṟuppattantāti eṉpaṉa.  tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiluḷḷa māṇikkam viṟṟa paṭalam, kāl māṟiyāṭiya paṭalam, nari pariyākkiya paṭalam eṉpavaṟṟi nōkkuḻi ivvāciriyaratu aruṅkali yuṇarcciyiṉ parappu veḷippaṭum. tolkāppiyam mutaliya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷilum, eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭu mutaliya caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷilum, aimperuṅkāppiyaṅkaḷilum, tēvāram mutaliya paṉṉiru tirumuṟikaḷilum, meykaṇṭa nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟilum ivvāciriyar nalla payiṟciyuṭiyavar.
parañcōti muṉivariyaṟṟiya ittiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇat tiṟku mutaṉūl vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa ālāciyamāṉmiyam eṉṟum, itu patiṉeṇpurāṇaṅkaḷiloṉṟākiya kāntapurāṇattiṉ ōr pakutiyām eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. tala māṉmiyaṅkaḷip patiṉeṇ purāṇaṅkaḷiloṉṟaṉōṭu iyitturippatu avaṟṟiṉ perumiyi mikuttuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉṉum karuttiṉālām eṉpatu nallaṟivu ṭiyāraṉi varkkum uṭaṉpāṭākum. yātāṉum oru tamiḻ nūl āriyattiṉiṉṟu moḻipeyarkkap paṭṭatāṉāl maṭṭum yāvarum ottukkoḷḷattakka perumiyuṭiyatām; atuvum vēta ākamapurāṇa itikācaṅkaḷil oṉṟic cārntatākavum, piṉ nikaḻappōkiṟa varalāṟukaḷi muṉṉarē kūṟi vittatākavum irukka vēṇṭum; eṉṉum ittakiya pōlikkoḷkikaḷ piṟaruṭiya paḻakkaṅ kāraṇamākac ceṉṟa cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilirunta tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟiṉa. avi tamiḻ nūlkaḷ cila maṟintoḻiyumpaṭi vaṭamoḻi mūlam iṉṟeṉṟu puṟakkaṇit totukkumāṟum, cila tamiḻ nūlkaḷi āriyattil moḻi peyarttuk koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu ivi vantaṉaveṉṟu kāṭṭumāṟun tūṇṭak kūṭiyavātal kāṇka. eṉiṉum, akkoḷkikaḷ valiyuṟṟu niṉṟa kālattil oru tamiḻp pulavar anneṟiyē ceṉṟiruppiṉ, avar avaṟṟik kaṭakkum matukiyilarāyiṉāreṉpataṉṟi, avarmītu vēṟu kuṟṟañ cumattutal cālātu. iṟṟi nāḷilum attakiyōriruppatē periyatōr viyappākum. iṉṉōr iṉṉamum irukkiṉṟaṉareṉpataṉit tiruvālavāyuṭiyār tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇa mukavuri 6-ām pakkattil makāmakōpāttiyāya ṭākṭar.u.vē.cāminātiyaravarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷataṉālaṟika. cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu cittānta nūṟpayiṟci vāyntavarāyirunta oruvar kumarakurupara cuvāmikaḷiṉ carittirattit tamiḻil eḻuti vittuk koṇṭu, itaṟku eppaṭiyāvatu vaṭamoḻiyil mūlam kaṟpittuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉat tām eṇṇiyati eṉṉiṭam kūṟiyatumuṇṭu! taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷākiya periyapurāṇam pōlvaṉavaṟṟic cilar āriyattil moḻipeyarttu vittuk koḷḷa, āriyattiṉiṉṟu ivi moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu kūṟum oṉṟiraṇṭupēr iṉṉumirukkiṉṟaṉarē. itiṟ kūṟappaṭum tiruviḷiyāṭal kaḷellām centamiḻp pāṇṭi nāṭṭilē tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē tamiḻ moḻiyāṟ pēciyum eḻutiyum nikaḻttip pōntaṉavākaliṉ, ivvaralāṟukaḷ āriyattil eḻutappaṭu muṉpē tamiḻiṉ iruvaki vaḻakkilum payiṉṟaṉavātal vēṇṭu meṉṟum, ivaṟṟi vaṭanūṟ pulavar ammoḻiyil eḻutuṅkāl tamiḻ vaḻakkukkaḷi naṉku aṟiyāmi mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāṟ cila piṟaḻccikaḷ nikaḻntiruttal  iyalpeṉṟum, innūlāciriyar vaṭanūṉ mēṟkōḷākak katikaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉarāyiṉum paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum toṭar kaḷiyum eṭuttamittē innūli aḻakupeṟac ceytirukkiṉṟaṉa reṉṟum tamiḻ makkaḷ uṇara vēṇṭum. katikaḷiṟ cila piṟaḻcci nikaḻntiruppiṉum, innūlkaḷiṉ nōkkam, carittiravārāycci ceyvataṉṟi, muḻu mutalvaṉākiya iṟivaṉ aṭiyārkaḷukku eḷivantu aruḷ puriyum peruṅ karuṇit tiṟatti uṇartti uyirkaḷi uyvittalē yeṉpati uṉṉiṉ, aḵtōriḻukkākat tōṉṟumāṟilli.


12. aiyaviṉāvukkuviṭi
centamiḻccelvi, 4āñ cilampu, 12-ām paralil oruvar eḻutiya aiyaviṉāvik kaṇṭēṉ. pāṇṭivaḷanāṭṭiṟ pāykiṉṟa āṟu cila nūlkaḷil viyi eṉavum, cila nūlkaḷil viki eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟatu; ivaṟṟuḷ evvāṭci kuṟṟamillatu eṉpatu viṉā.
caṅkap pulavarkaḷ pāṭit tokutta eṭṭuttoki, pattup pāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟilum, cilappatikāram mutaliyavaṟṟilum ivviyāṟu palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu; paripāṭalil itaṟ keṉṟē pala pāṭṭukkaḷ uḷa; evviṭattum viyi eṉṉum peyarē kāṇappaṭuvatu; viki eṉṟu ōriṭattum vaḻaṅkap paṭavilli. paḻiya tamiḻ vaḻakkukaḷi aṟiyāmiyālō, vaṭamoḻiyil eḻuta nērntami mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷālō piṟkālattār cilar cila tamiḻp peyarkaḷit tirittu vaḻaṅku vārum, aṅṅaṉam tirikkappaṭṭō aṉṟi maruviyō vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷukkēṟpak katikaḷ kaṭṭik kūṟuvārum āyiṉar. potiyil eṉṉum tamiḻc collip potiki eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkutaluṅ kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl, paḻiya tamiḻ vaḻakkuṭaṉ porunta viyi eṉṟu kūṟuvatē cālavum porutta muṭittu; eṉiṉum, piṟkālattup periyōr cilar tam kāla vaḻakkit taḻuvi orō vaḻi viki eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppiṉ atu ōr kuṟṟamākāteṉka.


13. mārkkaṇṭēyar allatu
 muyaṟciyiṉ veṟṟi
	uliyā muyaṟci kaḷikaṇā 'd2vūḻiṉ
valicintum vaṉmiyu muṇṭē-ulakaṟiyap
pāṉmuḷi tiṉṟu maṟali yuyirkuṭitta
kāṉmuḷiyē pōluṅ kari.1
tēvarum viḻiyum nāvalam poḻiliṉ teṟkiṉkaṇ kaṭakam eṉap peyariya ciṟanta patiyoṉṟuṇṭu. appatiyilē aḷapparutvattiṉiyuṭiya kuccakaṉ eṉṉum antaṇaṉoruvaṉ iruntaṉaṉ. arumaṟi payiṉṟa nāviṉaṉākiya kavuccikaṉ eṉpāṉ avaṉukku mintaṉāvaṉ. ammintaṉ piṟaviyākiya peruṅkaṭalik kaṭattal karuti ōr vāviyiṉ maruṅkeyti āṉēṟuyartta aṇṇal tiruvuruviyum tiruvinteḻuttiyum uṉṉi aruntavam purintaṉaṉ. avaṉ uḷḷam pēriṉpa aḷakkaril mūḻkiyiruntatu. uṭalam acivu ciṟitumiṉṟi ātīṇṭu kuṟṟipōl niṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam uṇavum uṟakkamum nīttu, uyirppum iṉṟi, ōviyamē yeṉa mēvi nōṟkuṅkāl avvaḻi varum palvaki vilaṅkukaḷum tattam uṭampiṉ kaṇṭūyam (tiṉavu) tīra avaṉmītu urittuc cellā niṟkum. aṉṉāṉ ataṉiyum uṇarātu tavattil uṟittu niṟṟali uvaṇac cēval uyarttōṉākiya tirumāl uṇarntu, vāṉōr kuḻuvuṭaṉ pōntu, avaṉatu tavattip pukaḻntu, taṉ malarkkiyāl avaṉ uṭampiṉi nīvi 'maliyiyotta mātava! iṉṟu mutal niṉ peyar miruka kaṇṭūyaṉ eṉpatākum. piṟimuṭiyōṉ pēraruḷ niṉpāl muṟṟuka' eṉa aruḷpurintu maṟintaṉaṉ.
māyōṉ pōyapiṉṟi ammātavaṉ aṅku niṉṟum virintu ceṉṟu, taṉ tanti muṉpeyti aṭipaṇintu niṉṟaṉaṉ. aṉṉavaṉ makaṉi makiḻvuṭaṉ kaṇṭu taḻuvik koṇṭu 'tavattiṭi uṟṟa peṟṟiyi urippāy' eṉṉa, avaṉum uḷḷavāṟurit taṉaṉ. ataṉik kēṭṭa tanti uvakimikku 'aiya, niṉkulattavarkkuḷ niṉpōlum nōṉmi evarkkuḷatu? niṉakkum ariyatoṉṟuṇṭō?  pāruḷār vicumpiṉ pālār tuṟakkameṉṉum ūruḷār ēṉi ulakuḷār ellāruḷḷum niṉṉiyoppār yāruḷār? kaṟi miṭaṟṟaṇṇalākiya enti yaṉṟi nī purinta nōṉmiyik kāṇavallār yāvar?, eṉap palavāṟu pukaḻntu kūṟi, iṟivaṉaruḷāl ōr niṉivuṇṭāka, ataṉi mintaṉukkurippāṉāy 'aiya, vētam mutaliya kalikaḷ yāvum viḷampiyaṉavum, evarālum taḷḷat takātaṉavumākiya nāṉku kaṭamikaḷ māntarkku uḷḷaṉa. avi āciriyaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟu ōtutalum virataṅkāttalumākiya pirama cariyamum, illiṉkaṇ kaṟpuṭiya maṉiviyōṭiruntu aṟattiṉip puritalākiya kirukattamum, illiviṭṭut tīyōṭu vaṉattiṉkaṭ ceṉṟu maṉiyāḷ vaḻipaṭat tavañceytalākiya vāṉappirattamum, muṟṟattuṟanta yōkavoḻukkamākiya caṉṉi yācamum ām. avaṟṟuḷ mutal niliyākiya piramacariyatti nī iṉṟu kāṟum iyaṟṟiṉi; piṉṉavi iraṇṭum piṉpu ceyyaṟ pālavākalāl avaṟṟi ippoḻutu pēcutal vēṇṭā; iṭiyil vittuc collappaṭṭatākiya ilvāḻkkinili eṉṉum kaṭaṉi āṟṟutaṟku nī iññāṉṟu toṭaṅkutal vēṇṭum' eṉṟaṉaṉ.
ivvuriyiṉik kēṭṭalum kavuccikaṉ 'entiyār eṉakku iṉiyatōr uṟuti kūṟiṉar' eṉa akattē nakittu, 'mācaṟṟa tavattiṉiyuṭiya nīrum putalvaṉākiya oruvaṟku iṅṅaṉam viḷamputal muṟiyō' eṉa moḻintu, piṉṉarum tantiyi nōkkik kūṟuvāṉāy 'muṉṉarē eṉṉip paṟṟiyuḷḷa pācattiṉ kaṭṭiṉiyaṟukka vēṇṭiyayāṉ aḵtaṉṟip piṉṉum oru pācattāṟ piṇikkappaṭuvēṉāyiṉ iṉṉaleṉum āḻiyiṭippaṭṭu iṉital aṉṟi, taṉakkuvamiyillātāṉ tāḷiṉic cērntu maṉakkavali māṟṟalum uḷatāmō? taṉ kiyē paṟṟukkōṭākat tavaḻntu cellum muṭavaṉoruvaṉ piṉ ataṟkum piṇiyuṇṭāka varuntutal pōl yāṉum varuntak kaṭavēṉō? tavattiṉālaṉṟip poyyāya illoḻukkat tāl viṉiyiṉip pōkka muyalvatu uṭampilē poruntiya aḻukki mikka tūya nīrālaṉṟikkalaṅkaṟ ciṉṉīrāṟ kaḻuvutali yokkumaṉṟō? aimpulaṅkaḷākiṉṟa pūtaṅkaḷiṉāl īrkkap paṭṭu allaluṟum puṉmiyēṉākiya yāṉ mātareṉum pēyum vantu paṟṟiyakkāl yātu ceyvēṉ? miṉṉaṟ koṭipōlum makaḷiri virumpiya vēṭkiyāṉatu puviyiṉkaṇ paṉṉāḷum tuṉpattil mūḻkuvikkum; piṉpu nirayamākiya pilattiṉuḷḷē celuttāniṟkum; aḵtu ennāḷil āṇṭakiyōrkku iṉpam payakkumōtāṉ? tuṉpatti nukarum viṉit toṭarcciyuṭiya māntar iṉpam nukarvārpōl ēntiḻi yāriṭattu akappaṭṭaṉar; ñāḷiyāṉatu taṉpal mika naṭuṅku māṟu taciyillāta eṉpiṉik kaṟikkumāyiṉ ataṉāl atu periyatōr cuviyip peṟṟiṭutaluṇṭō? vēl pōlum kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya makaḷir vēṭki oruvar neñciṟ pukumāyiṉ atu piṉpu nīṅkum peṟṟiyatō? aḵtu eñcutaliṉṟi ennāḷum uyiri īrtalāl ōrnāḷ tāṉum nalitalillāta nañcam ataṉiṉ mika iṉiyatākum. ulakilē cātal piṟappeṉṉum taṭañcuḻiyiṟpaṭṭut taṭumāṟukiṉṟavar mātar valippaṭṭu mayakkuṟṟārē yallarō? vāṉavar muṉivaruḷḷum makaḷirāciyāl iṭumpiyuṟṟōr ettuṇiyarāvar? aviyelāṅ kiṭakka, yāṉ ammaṭantiyaruṭaṉ kūṭivāḻum oḻukkatti virumpēṉ; intiraṉ mutaliya imiyavar pataṅkaḷiyum vēṇṭēṉ; vāḻveṉum koṭiya ciṟiyiṉ kaṇṇum vīḻēṉ; pulaṉkaḷāl aḻiyum mayakkiṉi nīkkit tavameṉṉum viḻuccelvatti yaṭintu iṉpuṟṟiruppēṉ' eṉṟaṉaṉ.
immoḻikaḷik kēṭṭalum tantiyākiya kuccakaṉ 'ā'! ulakamelām mīkkūṟum nallaṟamākiya illaṟattiṉi ivaṉ navi eṉa ikaḻukiṉṟāṉ; nāṉmaṟiyiṉ tuṇiviyum koḷḷu kiṉṟilaṉ; tavameṉṉum mayaliṉip pūṇṭāṉ' eṉṟu tuyarameyti, mintaṉi nōkkik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
'pēraṟivuṭiyāy! ōr kaṉṉiyi maṟimuṟiyāl vatuvi pūṇṭu kulattiṉi yōmpak kumararip payanta piṉṉaṉṟō tavam purintu vīṭu peṟutaleṉpatu vaḻakkāṟākum? makaḷir tam uḷḷap parappiṉ orupuṟattē ciṟitu mayakkamum tiyakkamum uṭiyarāyiṉum ataṉ āḻattē aṉpum kaṭippiṭiyum teḷivumē uṭiyarāvar; avar tam uṟutiyi nī uṇarāy; ulakilē uyir vāḻkkiyākiṉṟa peruṅkaṭaluṭ cellum āṭavar neñcamākiya marakkalamāṉatu uṟutiyaṟṟa eṇṇameṉṉum cuḻaṟkāṟṟāl untappaṭṭu aṅku miṅkum alintu keṭāmal aṟamākiya tuṟiyiṉkaṇ nilipeṟa niṟuttum naṅkūramāvatu maṅkiyar nili yeṉpatu kaṭippiṭippāyāka; oruvaṟku aṉpum naṟkuṇamum uṭiya makaḷir vāḻkkit tuṇi varāyiṉ avaṟku illatuyātu? illaṟa meṉpatu taṉakkeṉa vāḻum vilaṅkiṉṟaṉmiyuṭiya maṉakkōḷi nimirttu, maṟṟiyar iṉpamum tuṉpamum taṉṉuṭiyavāka aṉpu pārāṭṭac ceytu, meḷḷa meḷḷa uḷḷam virittu, poṟumiyiyum amitiyiyum paṭippaṭiyākap pukaṭṭi, cintiyitteḷivittu, tāṉ eṉum niṉippum taṉakkeṉum viruppum ōyvuṟap purintu, poṅkit tatumpip pūraṇamāyk kiṭakkum pēriṉpa veḷḷattil muṅki mūḻkumpaṭi yāriyum pakkuvañceyyum nalla paḷḷiccāliyām eṉa ōrvāyāka. ulakiluḷḷa maṉpati pōṉṟu nīyum kāmaviṉpam nukartaṟkēyō itu kūṟiṉēṉ? tām puriyum nalviṉiyālē iṟanta mēlōriṉ tuṉpattit tuṭittaṟkuriya makkaḷip peṟutaṟporuṭṭu ōr mātiṉi maṇattal vēṇṭum; inta naṉmivāynta ilvāḻkki niliyi nī innāḷ kāṟum eṇṇātu mutaliṟ ceyyaṟ pālatākiya kaṭaṉ oṉṟiyumē ceytu muṭittaṉi; paṉṉāḷāka etir nōkkiyirukkum vāṉōrkkum avimutaliya ciṟappukkaḷic ceyyātu nī iṉṉē tavam purital karutaṟpālatō? viruntiṉari yōmpalum, tuṟantārppēṇalum, vaṟiyārkkaḷittalum, cuṟṟantaḻuvalum, oppuravāṟṟalum eṉṉum aṟaṅkaḷ ceyyāvaḻi makkaṭ piṟappāl yārukku eṉṉa payaṉuḷatu? umiyuṭaṉ kūṭi ulakuyirkaḷi yellām payanta civaperumāṉ yāvarum maṭantimārōṭu illaṟattiloḻukap paṇittuḷāṉaṉṟē?
"	tēvarum muṉivartāmumceṅkaṇmāl ayaṉummaṟṟum
yāvarum maṭantimārōṭillaṟattoḻukuntaṉmi
mēvarap paṇittāṉaṉṟēvimaliyōṭaṇukimēṉāḷ
tāvarum puvaṉa māti carācaram payanta tāṇu"1
avviṟivaṉ ceyta varampiṉic citiyātē; uyarntōr palarum oḻukiya vaḻiyi ikaḻātē; eṉatu moḻiyit taḷḷātē; vaciṭṭa muṉiyāṉavaṉ poṉpōlum vaṉappuṭiya aruntati yōṭu eññāṉṟum piriviṉṟiyiruntum uyariya tavattiṉi
yuṭiyaṉāy ulakilē pōṟṟappaṭṭilaṉō? viruppu veṟuppaṟṟa tūyōr nāṭoṟum maṭavaroṭu kūṭi muyaṅkiṉum mātavam puriyiṉum avaratuṇarvu calippiṉṟi oru peṟṟittēyākum; tolli muṟiyāl varintu koṇṭa melliyalōṭu kūṭi vāḻiṉ, tāṉam uḷatākum; aritāya tavamum uḷatākum; avarāl eytalākāta toṉṟilli. pattiṉip peṇṭir irunta nāṭṭilē vāṉam poyyātu; vaḷampiḻippaṟiyātu; nīṇila
vēntaṉ koṟṟañcitiyātu; teyvamum avar ēviṉaceyyu meṉṟāl āṇṭakiyāruḷ avari nikarppār yāvar?
"	kāṇṭakiya taṅkaṇava rikkaṭavu ḷārpōl
vēṇṭaluṟu kaṟpiṉartam meyyuriyil niṟkum
īṇṭiyuḷa teyvatamum māmukilum eṉṟāl
āṇṭakimi yōrkaḷum avarkku nikaraṉṟē"1
āyiḻiyāruṭaṉ kūṭiyiṉpuṟum aṟatti mutaṟkaṇ āṟṟātu tūya tavaneṟiyit toṭaṅkuviyēl vēṭkiyeṉṉum koṭiya nañcu talippaṭiṉ ataṉi nāṉmukaṉālum vilakka
voṇṇātē; illaṟattil niṟpōr oḻukkattiṟ ciṟitu iḻukkuvarāyiṉ ataṉit tīrkkum kaḻuvāyumuḷatu; tavañ ceyvōrkku avviḻukkamuṇṭāyiṉ maliyiṉ ucci
yiṉiṉṟum tavaṟutal pōla uytal aritākum. tuṉpamākiya tiriyāl eṟṟuṇṭu varuntum piṟavik kaṭaliṉiṉṟu kariyēṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉum ataṟkuriya nāḷilaṉṟik katumeṉat tuṟakka
lākumō? nī ippoḻutē uyariya tavattiṉkaṇ cellak karututal nivappumikka maliyiṉ kuṭumiyi aṭiyum viruppuṭiyār ataṉ cāraliyeyti neṟiyāṉē ēṟic cellātu cēṇilē tāvutali yokkum. ātalāl, mintaṉē! nī num muṉṉōr pōla nallaṟamākiya illaṟattiṉi naṭātti, ataṉ piṉ tuṟaviṉi mēṟkoḷka. aṅṅaṉam puriyiṉ niṉakku ētam yātum uḷatākātu. itaṉi maṟutturiyal āṇi namatu' eṉṟaṉaṉ.


14. paṇṭit tamiḻccāṉṟōriṉ 
vāṉanūṟpulami
tamiḻakattilē paḻiya nāḷil vāṉanūl, eṇṇūl, kuṟinūl, maruttunūl mutaliya palakalikaḷum viḷakka muṟṟiruntaṉa eṉpataṟkup paṟpala cāṉṟukaḷ  uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ vāṉanūl eṅṅaṉam vaḷam peṟṟiruntateṉpataṉi īṇṭuc ciṟitu viḷakkutum. uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār eṉṉum nallicip pulavar nalaṅkiḷḷiyeṉṉum cōḻa vēntaṉip pāṭiya puṟappāṭ ṭoṉṟil 
"	ceññā yiṟṟuc celavumañ ñāyiṟṟup
parippum parippuc cūḻnta maṇṭilamum
vaḷi tiritaru ticiyum
vaṟitu niliiya kāyamum eṉṟivi
ceṉṟaḷan taṟintōr pōla eṉṟum
iṉitteṉ pōrum uḷarē"1
eṉṟu kūṟi, ñāyiṟṟiṉ teruvum, iyakkamum, pārvaṭṭamum, ticiyum, vicumpum eṉpavaṟṟi aḷantaṟitaliṉ arumiyiyum, avaṟṟi āṇṭāṇṭuc ceṉṟu aḷantaṟintōrpōla avi ittuṇi aḷaviyuṭiyaṉaveṉṟu vaḻuviṉṟurikkum aṟivuṭiyōrum tamiḻakattilē uḷareṉpataṉiyum pulappaṭuppārāyiṉar. vāṉa nūṟkuriya kuṟiyīṭṭuc coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉkaṇ malkik kiṭattaloṉṟāṉē akkali evvaḷavu vaḷarcciyuṟṟiruntateṉpatu pulaṉākum.
oṉpāṉ kōṭkaḷil ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikka-ñāyiṟu, ceṅkatir, pakalōṉ, pariti, veyyōṉ, eṉṟūḻ, paṉippaki, vēntaṉ, cāṉṟōṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷum; tiṅkaḷik kuṟikka-tiṅkaḷ, ampuli, taṇcuṭar, nilavu, allōṉ, alavaṉ mutaliyavum; cevvāyik kuṟikka-cevvāy, cēy, kuruti, āral, aṟivaṉ, nilamakaṉ, aḻalōṉ mutaliyavum; putaṉik kuṟikka-aṟivaṉ, kaṇakkaṉ, pulavaṉ, punti, māl, pacci mutaliyavum; viyāḻaṉik kuṟikka-viyāḻaṉ, poṉ, antaṇaṉ, aracaṉ, amiccaṉ, āṇṭaḷappāṉ mutaliyavum; veḷḷiyik kuṟikka-veḷḷi, paḷiṅku. pukar, maḻikkōḷ mutaliyavum; caṉiyik kuṟikka-nīlaṉ, kāri, kariyōṉ, katirmakaṉ, mutumakaṉ, muṭavaṉ mutaliyavum; irākuvik kuṟikka-iruḷ, karumpāmpu eṉpaṉavum; kētuvik kuṟikka-koṭi, cempāmpu, eṉpaṉavum tamiḻilē uḷḷaṉa.
irupattēḻu nāṇmīṉkaḷil accuviṉiyik kuṟikkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ irali, puravi, ēṟu, yāḻ, talināḷ, maruttu nāḷ mutaliyavām; paraṇiyik kuṟippaṉa kāṭukiḻavōḷ, tāḻi, aṭuppu, mukkūṭṭu mutaliyaṉa; kārttikiyik kuṟippaṉa aṟumīṉ, aḻal, āral, aḷakkar mutaliyaṉa; urōkiṇiyik kuṟippaṉa paṇṭi, uruḷ, viyam, ūṟṟāl, mutaliyaṉa; mirukacīriṭattik kuṟippaṉa māḻku, māṉṟali mummīṉ, narippuṟam, pāli veyyōṉ mutaliyaṉa; tiruvātiriyik kuṟippaṉa ceṅki, yāḻ, iṟināḷ mutaliyaṉa; puṉarpūcattik kuṟippaṉa kaḻi, karumpu, āvaṇam, ēri mutaliyaṉa; pūcattik kuṟippaṉa koṭiṟu, vaṇṭu, kāṟkuḷam, viyāḻaṉāḷ mutaliyaṉa; āyiliyattik kuṟippaṉa araviṉāḷ, kuvi mutaliyaṉa; vēḷvi, vēṭṭuvaṉ, koṭunukam, vāykkāl mutaliyaṉa makattiyum; eli, kaṇi mutaliyaṉa pūrattiyum; māṉēṟu, katirnāḷ mutaliyaṉa uttarattiyum; kimmīṉ, kaḷiṟu, aiviral mutaliyaṉa attattiyum; neymmīṉ, payaṟu, aṟuvi, naṭunāḷ mutaliyaṉa cittiriyiyum; viḷakku, vīḻkki veṟunukam, marakkāl, kāṟṟiṉāḷ mutaliyaṉa cōtiyiyum; muṟam, muṟṟil, cuḷaku, kāṟṟiṉāḷ mutaliyaṉa vicākattiyum; paṉi, pul, tēḷ, naṭpunāḷ mutaliyaṉa aṉuṭattiyum tuḷaṅkoḷi, taḻal, vallāri mutaliyaṉa kēṭṭiyiyum kuṟikkum tamiḻc coṟkaḷām; mūlattikkuṟikka aṉṟil, vil, kuruku, kokku, tēṭkaṭi mutaliyavum; pūrāṭattik kuṟikka' uṭikuḷam, muṟkuḷam, nīrnāḷ mutaliyavum, uttirāṭattik kuṟikka āṭi, kaṭikkuḷam mutaliyavum, tiruvōṇattik kuṟikka ulakki, mukkōl, māyōṉāḷ mutaliyavum, aviṭṭattik kuṟikkap paṟavi, kākki mutaliyavum, catayattik kuṟikka nīrnāḷ, cekku, kuṉṟu, pōr mutaliyavum, pūraṭṭātiyik kuṟikka muṟkoḻuṅ kōl, nāḻi mutaliyavum, uttiraṭṭātiyik kuṟikka maṉṉaṉ, aṟivaṉāḷ, piṟkoḻuṅkōl mutaliyavum, irēvatiyik kuṟikka nāvāy, toḻu, paḵṟi, āṇāḷ, kaṭināḷ mutaliyavum tamiḻiṉkaṇuḷḷaṉa.
paṉṉiraṇṭu irukki (irāci)kaḷil mēṭattiṟku āṭu eṉpatum, iṭapattiṟku ēṟu eṉpatum, mituṉattiṟku iraṭṭi eṉpatum, kaṭakattiṟku alavaṉ eṉpatum, ciṅkattiṟku maṭaṅkal eṉpatum, kaṉṉikku naṅki eṉpatum, tulāttiṟku niṟikōl eṉpatum, viruccikattiṟkut tēḷ eṉpatum, taṉuviṟku vil eṉpatum, makarattiṟkuc cuṟā eṉpatum,  kumpattiṟkuk kuṭam eṉpatum, mīṉattiṟku mīṉ eṉpatum. tamiḻp peyarkaḷām. āṭṭiṉ maṟupeyarkaḷākiya varuṭi, utaḷ, takar, maṟi, mi, mutaliyavum, āṭu eṉṉum irukkiyik kuṟippaṉavākum iṅṅaṉamē ēṟumutaliyavaṟṟiṉ maṟu peyarkaḷiyum. nāṇmīṉ mutaliyavaṟṟilē kutiri, pari, puravi eṉṟāṟpōl varum maṟupeyarkaḷiyum, mituṉattiṟku yāḻ eṉpatupōl iyipu paṟṟi varum piṟa peyarkaḷiyum koḷka. 
kōṭkaḷik kuṟikkum cēy, pacci, poṉ, veḷḷi, kāri eṉṉum peyarkaḷ niṟam paṟṟiyum, muṭavaṉ eṉpatu iyakkam paṟṟiyum, vēntaṉ, aṟivaṉ, antaṇaṉ mutaliyaṉa ovvōriyalpu paṟṟiyum vantiruttal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē nāṭkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ peyarkkāraṇaṅkaḷum uyttaṟiyaṟpālaṉa. iviyiṟṟiliruntu paṇṭit tamiḻvāṇarkaḷ kōḷ, nāḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷi evvaḷavu naṉkaṟintiruntaṉareṉpatu pulappaṭum. vāṉanūṟ cārpāka malkik kiṭakkum ittakiya coṟkaḷi nōkkuḻi ettakiya kalikaḷiyum vēṟṟumoḻiyiṉ kalappiṉṟiyē tamiḻiṉkaṇ iyaṟṟutal cālumeṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, caṅkattuc cāṉṟōrāya paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷiṉ vāṉa nūṟ pulamiyiṉiyum, aṉṉār annūṟ kuṟippukkaḷi aḻakiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷāṟṟoṭuttamittiruttaliyum cila eṭuttukkāṭṭāl viḷakkutum.
yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañcēralirumpoṟi eṉṉum cēravēntaṉ tuñciyapoḻutu kūṭalūrkiḻār eṉṉum cāṉṟōr iraṅkip pāṭiya aḻakiya pāṭṭoṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷatu. atil, avar 'vāṉattiṉiṉṟum oru mīṉ viḻuntatu; ataṉip pārttu yāmum piṟarumākiya palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa iravalar emmuṭiya malināṭṭukku vēntaṉākiyavaṉ nōyilaṉākap peṟiṉ naṉṟeṉa iraṅkiya neñcattuṭaṉē mika añciṉēm; añciṉapaṭiyē ēḻānāḷ vantatākaliṉ iṉṟu valiyiyuṭiya yāṉi kiyi nilattiliṭṭut tuñcavum, tiṇṇiya vārāṟ piṇikkappaṭṭa muracam kaṇ kiḻinturuḷavum, ulakiṟkuk kāvalākiya veṇkoṟṟakkuṭi kāl tuṇintu ulaṟavum, kāṟṟuppōlu miyalpuṭiya kutirikaḷ katiyiṉṟik kiṭakkavum, pakivarip piṇikkum āṟṟaliyum nacciṉōrkku aḷantu koṭut talaṟiyāta vaṇmiyiyum uṭiya nīlamali pōlum vēntaṉ tēvarulakatti yaṭintāṉ' eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu neñciṉiyurukku kiṉṟatu. ippāṭṭiṉ muṟpakuti avar, ammīṉāṉatu eppoḻutu evvuḻi eṅṅaṉam viḻuntateṉpataṉik kūṟiyiruppatu īṇṭu nōkkaṟpālatu. atu,
"	āṭiya laḻaṟkuṭṭattu
āriru ḷariyiraviṉ
muṭappaṉiyattu vērmutalāk
kaṭikkuḷattuk kayaṅkāyap
paṅkuṉiyuya raḻuvattut
talināṇmīṉ nilitiriya
nilināṇmīṉ ataṉetirērtarat
toṉṉāṇmīṉ ṟuṟipaṭiyap
pāciccellātu ūcimuṉṉātu
aḷakkarttiṇi viḷakkākak
kaṉiyeri parappak kāletirpu poṅki
orumīṉ viḻuntaṉṟāl vicumpi ṉāṉē"1
eṉpatu 'mēṭavirāci poruntiya kārttiki nāḷiṉ mutaṟ kāliṉkaṇ niṟinta iruḷiyuṭiya pātiyiraviṉkaṇ, muṭap paṉipōlum vaṭiviyuṭiya aṉuṭanāḷiṉ aṭiyiṉ veḷḷi mutalākak kayamākiya kuḷavaṭivupōlum vaṭiviyuṭiya puṉar pūcattuk kaṭiyiṉ veḷḷi elliyāka viḷaṅkap paṅkuṉi mātattiṉatu mutaṟpatiṉintiṉkaṇ uccamākiya uttaram avvucci yiṉiṉṟum cāya, ataṟku eṭṭāmīṉākiya mūlam ataṟketirē eḻāniṟka, anta uttirattiṟkumuṉ collappaṭṭa eṭṭā mīṉākiya mīrukacīriṭam tuṟiyiṭattē tāḻa, kīḻt ticiyilum vaṭaticiyilum pōkātu kaṭalāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa pūmikku viḷakkāka mikka tīpparakkak kāṟṟāṟ pitirntu kiḷarntu vāṉattiṉiṉṟum oru mīṉ viḻuntatu' eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. paṅkuṉi patiṉintām nāḷ kārttiki mutaṟkāluṭaṉ kūṭiya iruḷ ceṟinta pātiyiravil oru mīṉ mikka oḷiyuṭaṉ vaṭa kīḻtticiyil viḻuntateṉpatu itaṉ karuttākum. irupattēḻu nāṇmīṉkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya vāṉa vaṭṭattiṉ mēṟpakutiyilē patiṉmūṉṟari nāṇmīṉkaḷ nilavuṟumākaliṉ uttaram ucciyil niṟkumpoḻutu mēl kōṭiyil puṉarpūcattuk kaṭi veḷḷi variyilum kīḻk kōṭiyil aṉuṭattiṉ aṭi veḷḷi variyilum viḷaṅku meṉka. uttaram ucciyiṉiṉṟum cāya, mūlam eḻa eṉṟamiyāl iravu patiṉeṭṭari nāḻikiyaḷavil mīṉ viḻuntami peṟap
paṭum. itaṉkaṇ mēṭattik kuṟikka  āṭu eṉṉuñ collum, kārttikiyik kuṟikka aḻal eṉpatum, ataṉ oru kālik kuṟikka kuṭṭam eṉpatum, aṉuṭattik kuṟikka muṭappaṉi eṉpatum, puṉarpūcattik kuṟikkakuḷam eṉpatum vaḻaṅki yiruttalum, tali nāṇmīṉ mutaliyaṉa uttaram mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟippiṉ uṇarttalum, nāṇmīṉkaḷil iṉṉatu iṉṉuḻiyiruk kuṅkāl iṉṉatu iṉṉuḻiyirukkumeṉa vari ceytalum pōlvaṉavaṟṟiliruntu aṟṟi nāḷilvāṉa nūlāṉatu karutiyavāṟu taṭiyiṉṟic ceyyuḷilamikkum vaṇṇam attuṇip payiṟci uṭittāyiruntateṉpatu pōtarum. mīṉ viḻuntami koṇṭu aracaṟku iṉṉatu nikaḻumeṉa naṉku tuṇiyappaṭṭatit teritalāṉē nimitta nūṟpayiṟciyum mikkiruntami pulaṉākiṉṟatu. itiluḷḷa pāci eṉpataṟkuk kiḻakku eṉpatum, ūci eṉpataṟku vaṭakku eṉpatum poruḷām.
iṉi, viyiyāṟṟic ciṟappittuk kūṟum patiṉōrām paripāṭṭiṉkaṇ kār kālattu maḻi peytal kūṟum vaḻi appoḻutu kōṭkaḷ niṉṟa nilimiyi āciriyar nallan
tuvaṉār viḷakkiyuḷḷār. atu, 
"	virikatir matiyamoṭu viyalvicumpu puṇarppa
ericaṭi yeḻilvēḻam taliyeṉak kīḻiruntu
teruviṭip paṭuttamūṉ ṟoṉpatiṟ ṟirukkiyuḷ
urukeḻu veḷḷivan tēṟṟiyal cēra
varuṭiyip paṭimakaṉ vāyppap poruṭeri
punti mituṉam poruntap pularviṭiyal
aṅki yuyarniṟpa antaṇaṉ paṅkuviṉ
illat tuṇikkuppā leyta viṟiyamaṉ
villiṟ kaṭimakara mēvappām polli
matiya maṟiya varunāḷil vāynta
potiyiṉ muṉivaṉ purivarik kīṟi
mituṉa maṭiya virikatir vēṉil
etirvaravu māri yiyikeṉaviv vāṟṟāṟ
purikeḻu ciyam poḻimaḻi tāḻa
neṟitarūum viyip puṉal"1
eṉpatu, itaṉ mutal mūṉṟu aṭikaḷil irupattēḻu nakkattiraṅ kaḷālākiya paṉṉiraṇṭu irācikaḷum iṭapavīti, mituṉa vīti, mēṭavīti eṉamūvaki vītiyāka vakukkappaṭṭiruttal kūṟap paṭṭuḷatu. nāṇmīṉkaḷum matiyamum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu neṭuñ cēymiyil uḷḷavāyiṉum vāṉiṉkaṇ matikku nēr poruntum nāṇ mīṉē aṟṟi nāḷākak kuṟikkappaṭutaliṉ 'matiyamoṭu vicumpu puṇarppa' eṉappaṭṭatu. eri eṉpatu kārttikiyiyum, caṭi eṉpatu tiruvātiriyiyum, vēḻam eṉpatu paraṇiyiyum kuṟikka, avi muṟiyē kārttikiyiṉ mukkāliyuṭiya iṭapattiyum, ātiriyiyuṭiya mituṉattiyum, paraṇiyi yuṭiya mēṭattiyum kuṟippaṉavāyiṉa. vīti eṉpatu teru eṉavum, irāci eṉpatu irukki eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu terukkaḷāka vakukkappaṭum paṉṉiraṇṭu maṉikaḷil iṉṉa maṉiyuḷ iṉṉakōḷ irukkum poḻutil maḻi poḻinta teṉpatu mēṟkūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āvaṇittiṅkaḷ aviṭṭa nāḷil veḷḷi iṭapattiyum, cevvāy mēṭattiyum, putaṉ mituṉattiyum, ātittaṉ ciṅkattiyum, viyāḻam mīṉattiyum, caṉiyum matiyum irākuvum makarattiyum kētu kaṭakattiyum cēra, irāku matiyiṉittīṇṭa, akattiyaṉ eṉṉu mīṉ taṉṉiṭattik kaṭantu mituṉattip porunta, mutuvēṉiṟkup piṉṉatākiya kār kālattilē ciyamalikkaṇ maḻipeyya ataṉāṟ perukivarum viyippuṉal eṉpatu ataṉ tiraṇṭa poruḷām. kārttiki ucciyiṉiṟka viṭivatu āvaṇittiṅkaḷiṉ toṭakkattēyākaliṉ, ātittaṉ ciṅkattic cēra eṉpatu 'pular viṭiyal aṅkiyuyar niṟpa' eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. caṉiyiṉ irumaṉikkum uppāl eṉṟatu mīṉam eṉṟapaṭi. 'pāmpu matiyamaṟiya varunāḷ' eṉṟatu, āvaṇi mātattu mati niṟi nāḷākiya aviṭṭatti. eṉavē, matiyum irākuvum makarattu niṟka eṉpatum, kētu ataṟku ēḻāmiṭa mākiya kaṭakattu niṟka eṉpatum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. oṉpāṉ kōṭkaḷil matiyamum pāmpukaḷum oḻinta āṟukōṭkaḷ tattam maṉikaḷil niṟṟal karutaṟpālatu.  ēṟakkuṟiya āyirat teṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ āvaṇittiṅkaḷ niṟiyuvāvil iṅṅaṉam kōṭkaḷ niṉṟatu evvāṇṭileṉak kaṇikkum vaḻi icceyyuḷ tōṉṟiya kālam teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum. itaṉkaṇ, veḷḷi paṭi makaṉ (cevvāy), antaṇaṉ (viyāḻam) eṉak kōṭkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum, ēṟu (iṭapam), varuṭi (mēṭam), vil(taṉucu) eṉa irācikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum tamiḻc coṟkaḷāy niṟṟal kāṇka. punti, yamaṉ, mituṉam, makaram mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ itaṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭutal.
"	vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē"1
"	citintaṉa variṉum iyintaṉa variyār"2
eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil varumāṟu kūṟiyataṟkēṟpa, vaṭamoḻi vāṇar tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ paraviya kālattē tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷ ciṟcila vaṭacoṟkaḷi mēṟkoḷvārāyiṉamiyāṉ eṉka. iṅṅaṉam orō vaḻik kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻccol iṉmiyāṟ pōntaṉavalla eṉpatu ivaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷ piṟāṇṭut tamiḻccoṟkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappeṟutal koṇṭu aṟiyappaṭum. piṟkālattuc cōtiṭa nūl iyaṟṟiya pulavarkaḷum.
"	maṟiyuṭaṉ erutu māṉpeṇ varukuḷir mīṉkōl ēḻum
eṟikatir mutalā vuccam"
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam kōṭkaḷum maṉikaḷum mutaliyavaṟṟi iṉiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷāl eḷitiṟ kūṟippōtaraluṅ kāṇka.


15. aḷavikaḷ
camaya nūlkaḷilē poruḷ uṟutiyiṉ poruṭṭup pala vaki aḷavikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aḷavi eṉpataṟku vaṭamoḻiyil piramāṇam eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru camayattiṉarum cila pala aḷavikaḷip paṟṟuk kōṭākak koṇṭu tam tam koḷkikaḷi niṟuvuvar. ākavē aḷavi yeṉpatu ellā matattiṉarkkum potuvātal peṟappaṭum. eṉiṉum cila matattiṉar cila aḷavikaḷi oppuk koḷvatilli. vēṟu cilar cila aḷavikaḷi variyaṟuttuk koṇṭu piṟavellām avaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar. potuvil ivvaḷavi kaḷi āynturippōr aḷavi vātiyar eṉappaṭuvar. kaṭic caṅkap pulavaruḷ oruvarākiya maturik kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār iyaṟṟiya maṇimēkali yeṉṉum tamiḻkkāppiyattil camayak kaṇakkartan tiṟaṅkēṭṭa kāti eṉa ōr pāṭṭu uḷatu. atil, pāṭṭuṭit taliviyum, putta camayattic cārntiruntavaḷu mākiya maṇimēkali yeṉṉum naṅki ōr maṟimoḻiyāl muṉivaṉ uruttāṅkic cēra maṉṉaratu vañcimānakaratti yaṭintu, aṅkē pala camayavātikaḷum kūṭiyirunta aviyiṉ kaṇpukuntu, avaravar camayak koḷkikaḷi yellām kēṭṭu aṟinta varalāṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aḷavivāti, civavāti, piramavāti, viṇavavāti, vētavāti, ācīvakavāti, nikaṇṭa vāti, cāṅkiyavāti, vicēṭikavāti, pūtavāti eṉṉum ivarkaḷuṭiya kōṭpāṭukaḷi atil oruvāṟu kāṇalākum. avaruḷ aḷavi vātiyāṉavaṉ kāṇṭal, karutal, uvami, ākamam, aruttāpatti, iyalpu, aitikam, apāvam, pāricēṭam, campavam eṉṉum ippattaḷavikaḷāl poruḷiṉ meymmiyi aṟintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉkiṉṟaṉaṉ. aḷavikaḷ pattukku mēlum kūṟuvār uṇṭeṉṟum, aviyellām kāṇṭal, karutal, uri eṉṉum mūṉṟiṉuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉṟum civa nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉi,
"	aḷavi kāṇṭal karutaluri yapāvam poruḷop pāṟeṉpar
aḷavi mēlum oḻipuṇmi aitikattō ṭiyal peṉanāṉ
kaḷavi kāṇpar aviyiṟṟiṉ mēlumaṟiva raviyellām
aḷavi kāṇṭal karutaluri yeṉṟim mūṉṟiṉaṭaṅkiṭumē"1
eṉṉum civañāṉacittit tiruviruttattāl uṇarka.
iṉi, ap pattaḷavikaḷiṉ iyalpi iṅkē curukkik kūṟuvōm. kāṇṭal allatu kāṭciyaḷaviyāṉatu vaṭamoḻiyil pirattiyaṭcap piramāṇam eṉappaṭum. itu aintu vakiyiṉatākum. kaṇṇāl niṟattiṉik kaṇṭum, ceviyāl ōciyik kēṭṭum, mūkkāl nāṟṟatti (maṇatti) uyirttum, nāvāl cuviyi uṇṭum, meyyāl ūṟṟiṉi (sparicatti) uṟṟum iṉpamum tuṉpamum taṭiyaṟa uṇarvatu kāṭciyākum. itaṟku uyir, poṟi, neñcu eṉpaṉa ūṟupāṭiṉṟiyum, oḷi, poruḷ, iṭam eṉpaṉa paḻutillāmalum iruttal vēṇṭum. poruḷuṇmi aḷavu cuṭṭalum, oṉṟi maṟṟoṉṟākak kāṇkiṉṟa tiritalum, iraṇṭupaṭak kāṇkiṉṟa aiyamum iṉṟi, poruḷ, peyar vakuppu, taṉmi, toḻil eṉṉum vēṟupāṭukaḷ tōṉṟak kāṇpatē mayakkaṟṟa kāṭciyākum.
kāṭciyi vāyiṟkāṭci, uḷakkāṭci, taṉ nukarvuk (vētaṉi) kāṭci, maṉorumik(yōka) kāṭci eṉa nālvakip paṭutturik kiṉṟaṉar civar. vāyiṟ kāṭciyāvatu intiyap pirattiyaṣam; aḵtu uyiriṉ aṟivu kaṇ mutaliya poṟikaḷiyum, tī mutaliya poruḷkaḷiyum, (pūtam) uruvam mutaliya taṉ pulaṉkaḷiyum (taṉmāttiri) paṟṟi niṉṟu, aiyam tiripu eṉṉum kuṟṟamiṉṟiyum, peyar, iṉam mutaliya vēṟupāṭiṉṟiyum, uruvam mutaliya aimpulaṉkaḷi vēṟupāṭiṉṟi aṟivatām. uḷakkāṭci māṉatap pirattiyakṣam eṉappaṭum. aḵtu aimpulaṉ cārntu aṟinta vāyiṟkāṭciyāṉatu piṉmaṟattaliṉṟi neñcattāl niṉikkap paṭṭu, uṟutiyāki aṟiviṉkaṇ vantu cērum niliyāṉa vuṇarvām. taṉvētaṉik kāṭciyāṉatu uḷakkāṭcip perumi paṟṟi nikaḻum tūya (cattuva) taṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ viḷakkamākiya iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷ uyiriṉ uṇarvukkup pulaṉāmpaṭi varuvatākum. orumik (yōkam) kāṭciyāvatu aṟivit taṭi ceytu niṉṟa aḻukkukaḷi eṭṭuṟup porumi niliyāl (aṭṭāṅka yōkacamāti) vāṭṭutal ceytu oru kālattil ōriṭattil iruntapaṭiyē mūviṭattum mukkālattup poruḷkaḷiyum uṇarvatākum.
karutal aḷavi anumāṉap piramāṇam eṉappaṭum. atu, potu, eccam, mutal eṉa mūvakippaṭum. karutal cātaṉattāl cāttiyatti uṇarvatu. potuvāṉa karutalāvatu mutalum payaṉumāṉa(cātaṉacāttiyam) cērkki. atāvatu muṟiyāṉa uṭaṉikaḻcci yillātiruntum kāṭṭiṉkaṇ yāṉi yoliyik kēṭṭavaṉ aṅku yāṉi uṇṭeṉa uṇartal pōlvatu. eccak karutalāvatu āṟṟil veḷḷam varakkaṇṭu mēṟkil maḻi peytamiyi aṟital pōlvatu. itu payaṉaḷavi (kāriyānumāṉam) eṉavum paṭum. mutal karutalāvatu karukkoṇṭa mēkattikkaṇṭu maḻipeyyumeṉac collutal pōlvatu. itu mutalāṉavi (kāraṇānumāṉam) eṉavum paṭum. immūvaki yaḷavikaḷum muṟiyē nikaḻvu, iṟappu, etirvu, eṉṉum mukkālamum paṟṟi nikaḻum karutalukkum uyir, maṉam, poṟi eṉpaṉa ūṟiṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
civañāṉa cittiyār mutali (cātaṉatti) iyalpu, kāriyam, iṉmi (anupalatti) eṉa mūvakippaṭuttuk koṇṭu, eṭuttukkāṭṭumuṟiyē mā eṉpatu marattik kāṭṭalum, puki aṉalik kāṭṭalum, kuḷiriṉmi paṉiyiṉmiyik kāṭṭalum ām eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. maṟṟum ikkarutal taṉ poruṭṭu, piṟar poruṭṭu eṉavum, uṭaṉpāṭu, etirmaṟi eṉavum palavakiyākap pirikkappaṭum; karutalaḷavi iṉṉum mika viritturikkaṟpālatu; iṅkē curukkappaṭṭatu.
uvami aḷavi upamāṉap piramāṇam eṉappaṭum. iḵtu āviṉippōlum kavayamā allatu āmā(kāṭṭuppacu) eṉṟu oṉṟi maṟṟoṉṟaṉōṭu oppumippaṭuttik kūṟuvatu.
uriyaḷavi eṉpatu cattap piramāṇam eṉavum, ākamap piramāṇam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. itu aṟivaṉ nūlāl tuṟakka narakaṅkaḷ uṇṭeṉa aṟital pōlvatu, ākama aḷaviyit tantiram, mantiram, upatēcam eṉa mūvakippaṭuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu civañāṉacitti.
aruttāpatti eṉpatu tamiḻil poruḷ aḷavi eṉappaṭum. 'eṭuttamoḻi yiṉañ ceppalu murittē' eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷa iṉam cepputalē ivvaḷavikkup poruḷākum. āyarpāṭi kaṅkiyil irukkumeṉṟāl kaṅkik kariyil eṉṟu eṇṇuvatum, koṭuppār uṇṭeṉṟāl koṭātavarum uṇṭeṉak karutuvatum ivvaḷaviyām.
iyalpu eṉpatu yāṉimēliruntōṉ oṉṟu tā eṉṟāl piṟitoṉṟu tarātu aṅkucattit taruvatu pōlvatu.
aitikam eṉpatu ceviyāṟalāka varukiṉṟa ulakuri ākum. aḵtu inta ālamarattil pēy uṇṭu eṉtu pōlvatu.
apāvam eṉpatu iṉmiyākum. aḵtu avviṭattu apporuḷ illi yeṉpatākum. iṉmiyil palavakiyuṇṭu. avi kēṭṭuṇarka.
pāricēṭam eṉpatu oḻipu eṉavum, mīṭciyaḷavi eṉavum tamiḻiṟ kūṟappaṭum. irāmaṉ veṉṟāṉ eṉṟāl irāvaṇaṉ tōṟṟāṉ eṉṟu karutuvatu ivvaḷaviyākum. mūvaril iruvar poyyareṉṟāl oruvaṉ meyyaṉ eṉṟu aṟivatu itu.
campavam eṉpatu uḷḷa neṟi eṉappaṭum. nārācattiṉ aciviṉik kaṇṭu kāntam uḷateṉa aṟivatu ivvaḷaviyākum. 'iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal' (tol.col.kiḷaviyākkam.19) eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattiṉpaṭi kāṟṟu aciyum, tīccuṭum, nīr kuḷirum eṉpaṉavellām ivvaḷaviyām eṉpar.
aḷavi pattum oruvāṟu urikkappaṭṭaṉa.



16. patiṟṟuppattu mūlamum uriyum
iraṇṭām pattu
(11) 	varimaruḷ puṇari vāṉpici ruṭiya
vaḷipāynta taṭṭa tuḷaṅkiruṅ kamañcū
ṉaḷiyirum parappiṉ mākkaṭaṉ muṉṉi
yaṇaṅkuṭi yavuṇa rēmam puṇarkkuñ
cūruṭi muḻumuta ṟaṭinta pēricik
kaṭuñciṉa viṟalvēḷ kaḷiṟūrn tāṅkuc
cevvā yeḵkam vilaṅkuna raṟuppa
varuniṟan tiṟanta puṇṇumiḻ kurutiyiṉ
maṇiniṟa viruṅkaḻi nīrniṟam peyarntu
maṉālakkalavi pōla varaṇkoṉṟu 
muraṇmiku ciṟappiṉuyarnta 'd2vūkkali
palarmocin tōmpiya tiraḷpūṅ kaṭampiṉ
kaṭiyuṭi muḻumuta ṟumiya vēey
veṉṟeṟi muḻaṅkupaṇi ceyta velpōr
nārari naṟavi ṉāra mārpiṟ
pōraṭu tāṉic cēra lāta
mārpumali pintā rōṭiyoṭu viḷaṅkum
valaṉuyar maruppiṟ paḻitīr yāṉip
polaṉaṇi yerutta mēlkoṇṭu polintaniṉ 
palarpukaḻ celva miṉitukaṇ ṭikumē
kavirtati cilampiṟ ṟuñcuṅ kavari
parantilaṅ karuviyoṭu narantaṅ kaṉavu
māriyar tuvaṉṟiya pērici yimayan
teṉṉaṅ kumariyō ṭāyiṭi
maṉ mīk kūṟunar maṟantapak kaṭantē
tuṟi	-	centuṟip pāṭāṇpāṭṭu.
vaṇṇam 	- 	oḻukuvaṇṇam
tūkku 	- 	centūkku.
peyar 	- 	(a) puṇṇumiḻ kuruti.
itaṉ poruḷ:
(7-16.) civanta vāyiyuṭiya vēl māṟupaṭum pakivari
yaṟuttalāl ariya mārpu tiṟanta puṇ umiḻāniṉṟa kurutiyiṉālē nīlamaṇipōluṅ kariya niṟattiṉiyuṭiya kaḻiyiṉ nīr niṟam māṟupaṭṭuk kuṅkumak kalavi pōlumāṟu, araṇiṉiyaḻittu, māṟupāṭu mikka talimiyuṭaṉē uyariya ūkkutali yuṭiyiyāy, palar neruṅkip pātukātta tiraṇṭa pūkkaḷiyuṭiya kaṭampiṉatu kāvaliyuṭiya pariya aṭi tuṇipaṭumāṟu paṭiyiṉiyēvi, veṉṟu aṭikkappaṭukiṉṟa muḻaṅkum muraciṉic ceyta, vellum pōriṉiyum, nārāṉē vaṭikkappaṭṭa kaḷḷiṉiyum, āramaṇinta mārpiṉiyum, pōrilē velluñ cēṉiyiṉiyum uṭiya cēralāta!
maṉālam - kuṅkumam. pōla eṉṟatu pōlumāṟu eṉa viṉiyeccap poruḷpaṭṭu niṉṟatu. 'aruniṟan tiṟanta' eṉa muṉ vanta aṭicciṟappāṉum. 'maṇiniṟa viruṅkaḻi nīrniṟam peyarntu, maṉālak kalavi pōla' eṉap piṉvanta aṭic ciṟappāṉum itaṟkup 'puṇṇumiḻ kuruti' eṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu. māṟṟāratu kāvaṉ marantaṭital marapu. kaṭampaṟuttatu kaṭaliṉ kaṇṇatōr tiṭariṉuḷ eṉpatu ippatti ṉiṟutic ceyyuḷāṉaṟiyappaṭum. veṉṟi muyaṟciyiyaṟṟuvataṉi 'kaṭampaṟuttiyaṟṟiya valampaṭu viyaṉpaṇi' (17) eṉap piṉ kūṟuvatāṉumaṟika.
(21-25.) muṇmurukkamaraṅkaḷ ceṟinta malippakkattē tūṅkukiṉṟa kavarimāṉ parantu viḷaṅkum aruviyiyum, narantam pulliyum kaṉaviṟ kāṇā niṟkum aruntavarmikkuḷḷa perum pukaḻ imayamum, teṟkiṉ kaṇṇatāyakumariyum ākiya avaṟṟukku iṭiyēyuḷḷa maṉṉarkaḷil tammi mīkkūṟuvāratu maṟam keṭumpaṭi veṉṟu,
'kavirtati cilampiṟ ṟuñcu' meṉṟatu āṇṭu uṟiyum muṉivaratāṇiyāṉē murukkeṉṉum muḷḷuṭi maramum, "mayir nīppiṉ vāḻāk kavarimā" (tirukkuṟaḷ, 646) eṉṟu ciṟappikkap paṭṭa taṉmayirkkum varuttañ ceyyāmiyāl, akkavir ceṟinta cilampiṉkaṇṇē iṉitāka uṟaṅkumeṉṟavāṟu. 'aruviyoṭu narantam kaṉavu' meṉṟatu avarāṇiyāṉē piṟavilaṅkāṉum makkaḷāṉum varuttamiṉṟip pakaṟ kālattut tāṉ nukarnta aruviyiyum narantattiyumē kaṉaviṉum kāṇumeṉṟavāṟu. kumariyoṭu veṉṉum oṭu-eṇṇoṭu. avveṉṉum vakaravīṟṟup peyar āyiṭiyeṉa muṭintatu. maṉ eṉpatu araceṉpatu pōl aḵṟiṇic col. maṇkaḷil mīkkūṟu meṉka. 'matan tapa' eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, valikeṭa eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuka.
(1-9) maliyi yokkum alikaḷ periya tivalikaḷāka uṭiyumpaṭi kāṟṟup pāyntu uḻakkiya aciyā niṉṟa periya niṟinta cūliṉiyum, mikkaperiya parappiṉiyum uṭiya kariya kaṭali yaṭintu, piṟari varuttun toḻiliyuṭiya avuṇarkaḷ tamakkup pātukāvalāka uṇṭākkiya accam viḷikkum māviṉatu pariya ariyiṉit tuṇitta periya pukaḻiṉiyum, kaṭiya ciṉat tiṉiyum, veṟṟiyiṉiyumuṭiya murukavēḷ kaḷiṟṟiṉi yūrntāṟpōla,
picir - picirāka. kamañcūl - niṟintanīr; cūlpōṟalāṟ cūleṉappaṭṭatu. 'avuṇa rēmam puṇarkkum muḻumuta'leṉṟatu acurarellārum tammuṭa ṉetirntār valiyilē pāti taṅkaḷ valiyilē kūṭumpaṭi mantiraṅkoṇṭirunta cātitta māmara meṉṟavāṟu. atu kīḻnōkkiya teṉpatu 'kaviḻiṇar māmutal' eṉa murukāṟṟuṭ pōntamiyā ṉaṟiyappaṭum. 'cūruṭi muḻumutal' eṉpataṟkuc cūravaṉmāt taṉakku araṇākavuṭiya māviṉ mutaleṉṟum, cūraṉātaṟ ṟaṉmiyi yuṭiya māviṉ mutaleṉṟum poruḷ kūṟiṉum amiyum. cūravaṉmaṉē māmaramāy niṉṟāṉeṉṟu purāṇaṅ kūṟutaliṉ cūraṉātaṟṟaṉmiyiyuṭiya māveṉal cālumāṟaṟika. muṉṉippuṇarkkum mutal eṉa muṭikka. muṉṉit taṭinta eṉamuṭippiṉum amiyum.
(17-20) mārpiṉkaṇ niṟinta paciya tārum.
ōṭiyum viḷaṅkā niṉṟa veṉṟimikka kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya paḻikkappaṭāta yāṉiyiṉa poṉṉaṇinta eruttattiṉmēl ivarntu viḷaṅkiya niṉatu palarum pukaḻuñ celvatti iṉitākak kaṇṭēmeṉka. 
viḷaṅkum yāṉi eṉavum, niṉ celvam eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
(16) cēralāta, (25) maṟan tapak kaṭantu (6) vēḷkaḷiṟūrn tāṅku (18) yāṉi (19) yeruttamēl koṇṭu polinta niṉ (20) palar pukaḻ celvam kaṇṭiku meṉa māṟik kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka.
iṉi, murukavēḷ mākkaṭaṉ muṉṉi māviṉittaṭintu kaḷiṟūrntāṅkuivaṉum kaṭaliṉkaṇṇē kaṭampiṉit tumittu yāṉi yerutta mēlkoṇṭu polintaṉaṉ eṉa uvamaṅ koḷḷaluñ cālum.
itaṉāl avaṉ veṟṟic celvac ciṟappuk kūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu.

 (12)	
	vayavar vīḻa vāḷariṉ mayakki
yiṭaṅkavar kaṭumpi ṉaracutali paṉippak
kaṭampumuta ṟaṭinta kaṭuñciṉa vēntē
tāraṇi yeruttiṉ vāral vaḷḷuki
5	rarimāṉ vaḻaṅkuñ cāraṟ piṟamāṉ
ṟōṭuko ḷiṉaniri neñcatirn tāṅku
muracumuḻaṅku neṭunaka raracutuyi līyātu
mātiram paṉikku maṟamvīṅku palpukaḻ
kēṭṭaṟ kiṉituniṉ celvaṅ kēṭṭoṟuṅ
10	kāṇṭal viruppoṭu kamaḻuṅ kuḷavi
vāṭāp pimmayi riḷiya vāṭunaṭi
yaṇṇaṉ maḻakaḷi ṟariñimi ṟōppuṅ
kaṉṟupuṇar piṭiya kuṉṟupala nīnti
vantava ṇiṟutta virumpē rokkaṟ
15	ṟolpaci yuḻanta paḻaṅkaṇ vīḻa
veḵkupōḻn taṟutta vāṉiṇak koḻuṅkuṟi
miyūṉ peyta veṇṇel veṇcōṟu
naṉiyami kaḷḷiṉ ṟēṟaloṭu mānti
nīrppaṭu parunti ṉiruñciṟa kaṉṉa
20 	nilantiṉ citāar kaḷinta piṉṟi
nūlāk kaliṅkam vālarik koḷīi
vaṇariruṅ katuppiṉ vāṅkami meṉṟōḷ
vaciyiṉ makaḷir vayaṅkiḻi yaṇiya
vamarpumey yārtta cuṟṟamoṭu
25	nukartaṟ kiṉituniṉ peruṅkali makiḻvē
tuṟiyum vaṇṇamum tūkkum avi
peyar.(8) maṟam vīṅku palpukaḻ.
i-ḷ. 1-3 vīrarkaḷ maṟintu vīḻumpaṭi vāḷiṉ piṇakkattālē kalakki nilattiṉik kavarāniṉṟa cuṟṟattiṉiyuṭiya māṟṟaracu tali naṭukkuṟak kaṭampiṉ aṭiyit tuṇitta mikka ciṉattiṉi
yuṭiya vēntē,  
aril-piṇakkam(piṉṉal). vāḷāṉ arilpaṭa mayakki eṉṉalumām.
4-9. niṉatu celvam tārpōlum puṟamayirip poruntiya kaḻuttiṉiyum nīḷutaṟ ṟaṉmiyuṭiya kūriya ukiriṉiyumuṭiya maṭaṅkal  vaḻaṅkutaliyuṭiya malip pakkattē piṟa vilaṅku kaḷiṉ tokutiyik koṇṭa iṉamākiya niri neñcu naṭuṅkiṉāṟ pōla muracu muḻaṅkum neṭiya nakariṉkaṇ aracukaḷ tuyilāmal tici naṭuṅkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa maṟappukaḻ maṟṟip pukaḻiṉum mikka niṉpal pukaḻum kēṭṭaṟku iṉitāyiruntatu.
tuyilīyātu-viṉittiricol. mātiram aracu tuyilīyātu paṉikkum eṉa iṭattu nikaḻ poruḷiṉṟoḻil iṭattiṉmēl niṉṟatu.  aracarkkuc ciṟanta maṟappukaḻ maṟṟip pukaḻiṉum mikka palpukaḻ eṉṟu ciṟappitta vataṉāṉē itaṟku 'maṟamvīṅku palpukaḻ' eṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu. niṉ celvam palpukaḻ kēṭṭaṟku iṉitu eṉka; palpukaḻuṭaṉ kūṭiya celvam eṉṟavāṟu. 
9-25. ac celvattik kēṭkun tōṟum niṉṉik kāṇa vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppuṭaṉ, vāṭāta pacutta mayiriṉiyum, iḷiya veṉṟi naṭiyiṉiyum, talimiyiṉiyu muṭiya iḷiya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi kamaḻukiṉṟakāṭṭu mallikiyāl ariyākiya vaṇṭiṉi yōṭṭum kaṉṟuṭaṉ kūṭiya peṇ yāṉiyiṉi yuṭiya pala kuṉṟukaḷik kaṭantu vantu avviṭattē taṅkiya kariya periya kaṟṟattiṉatu neṭunāḷ paṭṭiṉi kiṭantamiyā luṇṭākiya paciyāl varuntiya tuṉpam oḻiyumpaṭi, vāḷiṉāṟ piḷantu tuṇikkappaṭṭa veḷḷiya niṇattiyuṭiya koḻuviya tuṇṭaṅkaḷākiṟa āṭṭiṟicciyip peytu aṭappaṭṭa veṇ ṇelariciyāṉāya veḷḷiya cōṟṟi makiḻcci poruntiya kaḷḷiṉ teḷivuṭaṉē uṇṭu, nīrilē mūḻkiya paruntiṉ kariya ciṟakiṉiyotta maṇṇā larikkappaṭṭa pīṟalākiya tuṇiyik kaḷinta piṉpu paṭṭiṉāṟ ceyta tūya uṭiyiṉi ariyil uṭutti, neḷinta kariya kūntaliyum, vaḷinta mūṅkili yokkum melliya tōḷiyum uṭiya paḻippillāta makaḷir viḷaṅkiya aṇiyiṉi aṇiya virumpi meymmiyip poruntiya cuṟṟattuṭaṉē nukartaṟku niṉatu periya āravārattiyuṭiya ōlakkattuc celkiṉṟa makiḻcci iṉitā yirunta teṉka.
kuṉṟu pala nīntip paḻaṅkaṇ vīḻa veṇcōṟu tēṟalōṭu mānti vālarik koḷīi vayaṅkiḻi yaṇiya iṉṉaṇam nukartaṟkiṉitu eṉa muṭikka. kēṭṭōṟum eṉpataṟku accelvatti yeṉa varuvikka. nūlākkaliṅka meṉṟatu oruvar nūlānūlākiya paṭṭu nūl mutalāyavaṟṟāṟ ceyta kaliṅka meṉṟavāṟu. nūliṉ mēlatāya nūlāmi yeṉṉun toḻil ciṉiyoṭu mutaṟkuḷḷa oṟṟumipaṟṟik kaliṅkattiṉmēl ēṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭatu. nūlānūṟ kaliṅkam eṉpāṉ nūleṉpataṉit tokuttuk kūṟiṉā reṉpārumuḷar.
(3) vēntē, (9) niṉ celvam (8) pukaḻ (9) kēṭṭaṟ kiṉitu (25) niṉ peruṅkali makiḻvu nukartaṟ kiṉiteṉ viṉi muṭivu ceyka. itaṉāl avaṉveṉṟic ciṟappum, ōlakka viṉōtac ciṟappum uṭaṉkūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.

17. puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉ ārāycci
puṟanāṉūṟu eṉpatu caṅkap pulavarkaḷāl aruḷic ceyyap paṭṭa eṭṭuttokiyuḷ oṉṟu. pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār mutal kōvūr kiḻār iṟutiyākavuḷḷa pulavar palarāṟ pāṭap peṟṟa nāṉūṟu akavaṟpākkaḷiyuṭiyatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷ ellām tamiḻukkē uriya akam puṟam eṉṉum poruḷilakkaṇa vakiyuḷ puṟapporuḷilakkaṇattiṉ tiṇi tuṟikaḷukku ilakkiyamāka uḷḷavi. innūṟ ceyyuṭkaḷiliruntu, paḻiya nāḷil tamiḻakattiliruntu ceṅkōlōcciya muṭiyuṭit tamiḻ vēntarākiya cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarkaḷil palaruṭiya varalāṟu kaḷiyum, pāri, pēkaṉ, āy, atikaṉ, kāri, ōri, naḷḷi, kumaṇaṉ mutaliya variyā īkiyiṉiyuṭiya vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ varalāṟu kaḷiyum, pulavar vīrar mutalāṉavarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷiyum aṟintu koḷḷalākum. maṟṟum., aracaroḻukkam, oṟṟumi, aṉpu, aruḷ, tuṟavu, vāymi, tavam, kaṟpu, naṭpu mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta aṟaṅkaḷum, paṇpukaḷum ivaṟṟiṭi naṉku viḷaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻariṉ varalāṟukaḷiyum, paḻamperumikaḷiyum, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkuc ciṟanta karuvi yākavuḷḷa nūlkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum itu mutaṉmiyāṉatu. itaṉip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷil antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar, eyiṉar, poṟkollar, nākar, kūttar, pāṇar mutaliya pala vakuppiṉarum irukkiṉṟaṉar. pāṭiya peṇpālarum palarāvar. tamiḻ makkaḷ itaṉip paṭittalāl uyarnta eṇṇaṅkaḷum ūkkamum uṭiyarāvar eṉpatil aiyamilli. mutaṟkaṇ itiluḷḷa aracāṅka oḻukkattip pulappaṭuttum pakutikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭutum. aracarpāl amintuḷḷa oḻukkaṅkaḷip pulavarkaḷ eṭutturippaṉavum, aracarkku avar aṟivuṟuttuvaṉavum, aracarkaḷ tāmē kūṟuvaṉavum ākiya mūṉṟu vakiyāl aracāṭci muṟikaḷ pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
neytalaṅkāṉal iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi eṉṟa cōḻa maṉṉaṉip pāṭiya ūṉpoti pacuṅkuṭiyār eṉṉum pulavar 'nī niṉṉi vaḻipaṭṭu oḻukuvōri virivil aṟintu koḷvi, piṟar kuṟṟaṅkaḷi eṭuttut tūṟṟuvāruṭiya moḻiyi nampamāṭṭāy, nī meyyāka ārāyntu tuṇiyappaṭṭa tīmiyi oruvaṉpāṟ kaṇṭāl ataṉi oḻukka nūlōṭu porunta ārāyntu  attīmikkut taka oṟuppāy, aṅṅaṉam tīmi purintōrum tam kuṟṟatti oppukkoṇṭu vantu paṇintu niṟparēl muṉṉiyiṉum avarmēl aruḷmikuntu avari oṟukkātu viṭuvāy' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
"	vaḻipaṭu vōri vallaṟi tīyē
piṟarpaḻi kūṟuvōr moḻitē ṟaliyē
nīmey kaṇṭa tīmi kāṇiṉ
oppa nāṭi attaka voṟutti
vantaṭi porunti munti niṟpiṉ
taṇṭamum taṇitinī paṇṭiyiṟ peritē"1
eṉpatu kāṇka.
yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañ cēralirumpoṟi eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉip pāṭukiṉṟa kuṟuṅkōḻiyūr kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar, 'teṉṟicikkaṇ kumariyum, vaṭaticikkaṇ imayamum, kīḻtticiyilum mēṟṟiciyilum kaṭalum elliyāka vuṭiya nilattilē kuṉṟum maliyum kāṭum nāṭum uṭiyōr aṉivarum oru peṟṟiyē vaḻipāṭu kūṟak koṭumiyip pōkkic ceṅkōṉmi pūṇṭu, āṟiloṉṟākiya iṟikoṇṭu, naṭuvu nilimiyip poruntit, taṭiyiṉṟi uruṇṭa āṇivaṭṭattāl nilavulakam muḻutiyum āṇṭōruṭiya marapik kāttavaṉē' eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
"	teṉkumari vaṭaperuṅkal
kuṇakuṭa kaṭalāvelli
kuṉṟumali kāṭunāṭu
oṉṟupaṭṭu vaḻimoḻiyak
koṭitukaṭintu kōltiruttip
paṭuvatuṇṭu pakalāṟṟi
iṉituruṇṭa cuṭarnēmi
muḻutāṇṭōr vaḻikāvala."2
eṉpatu atu.
maṟṟum avar avvēntaṉip piṟitōriṭattil, 'periya kaṭaliṉ āḻamum, akaṉṟa ulakattup parappum, kāṟṟu iyaṅkum ticiyum, vaṭiviṉṟi nilipeṟṟa vāṉum eṉṉum ivaṟṟi aḷantaṟiyiṉum niṉ aṟivu aṉpu kaṇṇōṭṭam eṉpaṉa variyaṟuttaṟku  ariyaṉa; ātalāl niṉ kuṭiniḻalil vāḻvōr cōṟu camikkum neruppuṭaṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmi yallatu vēṟu vemmi yaṟiyār; 'intira'vil allatu pakivaratu koli
villi aṟiyār; kalappi allatu vēṟu paṭikkalamum aṟiyār' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avvāy moḻi piṉvaruvatu; 
"	irumunnīrk kuṭṭamum
viyaṉñālat takalamum
vaḷivaḻaṅku ticiyum
vaṟituniliiya kāyamum, eṉṟāṅku
aviyaḷantaṟiyiṉum aḷattaṟ kariyi
aṟivum īramum peruṅkaṇ ṇōṭṭamum;
cōṟu paṭukkun tīyōṭu
ceññāyiṟṟut teṟalallatu
piṟituteṟa laṟiyārniṉ ṉiḻalvāḻ vōrē
tiruvil allatu kolivil laṟiyār
nāñci lallatu paṭiyum aṟiyār"1
iṉip pulavar aracarkaṭku aṟivuṟuttum oḻukkaṅkaḷip pārppōm. nariverūut taliyār eṉṉum pulavar irumpoṟi eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉip pārttu,
"	aruḷum aṉpum nīkki nīṅkā
nirayaṅ koḷpavarō ṭoṉṟātu kāval
kuḻavi koḷpavari ṉōmpumati
aḷitōtāṉē yatupeṟa laruṅkurittē"2
eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
itaṉ poruḷ aruḷiyum aṉpiyum kiviṭṭu narakattiyē tamakku eppoḻutum iṭamākak koḷḷum tīyarōṭu poruntātu, niṉṉāṟ kākkappaṭum nāṭṭiṉik kuḻantiyi vaḷarppār pōṟ pēṇip pātukāppāyāka, aṅṅaṉam kāttal yāvarālum virumpat takkatum, arumiyuṭiyatum ākum.' eṉpatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkuk kuṭapulaviyaṉār eṉṉum pulavar piṉvarumāṟu aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. "kaṭal cūḻnta ulakam muḻutiyumtamatu muyaṟciyāṟ kikkoṇṭu pukaḻi ulakattil niṟutti āṭcipurinta valiyōruṭiya vaḻiyil vantōṉē! nī maṟumiyulakattiṉkaṇ nukarum celvatti virumpiṉum, ēṉi maṉṉari yellām veṉṟu nīyē talivaṉātali virumpiṉum, nalla pukaḻi ivvulakattē nili peṟuttutali virumpiṉum ataṟkut takka ceykiyik kēṭpāyāka. uṭampukaḷellām uṇavi nilikkaḷaṉāka vuṭiyaṉa; ātaliṉ nīri iṉṟiyamiyāta avvuṭampukaṭku uṇṭi koṭuttōr uyir koṭuttōrē; uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu nilattoṭu nīrē; annīriyum nilattiyum oruvaḻik kūṭṭiṉavarkaḷ ivvulakattu uṭampiyum uyiriyum paṭit tavarāvar; vitiyi vitittu vāṉattip pārttuk koṇṭirukkum puṉpulam ettuṇi iṭamakaṉṟa tāyiṉum atu aracaṉatu muyaṟcikkup payaṉpaṭātatu; ātaliṉ ivvuṇmiyik kaṭippiṭittu, ēri, kuḷam mutaliya nīr nilikaḷ mikumpaṭi ceytōrē maṟumic celvam mutaliya mūṉṟiṉiyum tam peyaruṭaṉ ivvulakattil iciyac ceytōrāvar". (puṟam.18)
cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉip pāṭukiṉṟa veḷḷikkuṭi nākaṉār eṉṉum pulavar, 'kaṭal cūḻnta ulakattil mēmpāṭuṭiya tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉip purakkum mūvēntaruḷḷum aracu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭutaṟkuc ciṟappuṭiyatu niṉṉuṭiya aracē; ñāyiṟu nāṉku pakkattilum tōṉṟiṉum, veḷḷimīṉ teṉ pakkattiṟ ceṉṟālum kāviriyāṉatu nīrvaṟṟātu pala kālkaḷāy vantu ūṭṭutaliṉālē vēṟ paṭiyiṉ tokuti pōlak karumpiṉuṭiya veḷḷiya pūkkaḷ acintu koṇṭirukkum nāṭu eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu niṉṉuṭiya nāṭē; iṅṅaṉamākiya celvattiyuṭiya vēntaṉē! niṉakkuriya cilakaruttukkaḷik kūṟuvēṉ, nāṉ colpavaṟṟik kēṭpāyāka! aṟakkaṭavuḷē iruntu aracu purintāṟpōl ceṅkōṉmi yuṭiyarāy, muṟi kēṭka (nīti kēṭka) vēṇṭuṅ kālattuk kāṇpataṟku eḷiyacamayamu muṭiyarākum maṉṉar, maḻivēṇṭuṅkālattup peyyappeṟṟōrāvār; niṉatuveṇ koṟṟak kuṭiyāṉatu veyilimaṟippataṟkuk koṇṭatō veṉiṉ aṉṟu; atu, varuttamuṟṟa kuṭikaḷukku niḻal ceyvataṟkākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu; pōrk kaḷattil māṟṟāruṭiya paṭiyip puṟaṅkaṇṭu niṉpaṭi niṉakkut tarukiṉṟa veṟṟiyum kalappi yuḻuta paṭiccālil viḷinta nelliṉatu payaṉē; maḻi peyyuṅ kālattup peyyā toḻiyiṉum, viḷivu kuṟiyiṉum, ulakilē tīyaceyalkaḷ nikaḻiṉum  ulakattōr kāvalarip paḻitturippar; nī, ataṉi naṉku aṟintaṉiyāyiṉ kuṟaḷi kūṟuvāruṭiya uṟutiyillāta moḻikaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷāmal, uḻu toḻil ceyvāratu kuṭiyip pātukāttu, ataṉālē ēṉik kuṭikaḷiyum kāppā yāyiṉ, niṉ pakivarum niṉ aṭiyip pōṟṟuvar? eṉṟu aḻakupeṟak kūṟiyuḷḷār. atu,
"	aṟampurintaṉṉa ceṅkōl nāṭṭattu
muṟivēṇṭu poḻutiṟ pataṉeḷiyōr īṇṭu
uṟivēṇṭu poḻutiṟ peyal peṟṟōrē;
kaṇpora viḷaṅkuniṉ viṇporu viyaṉkuṭi
veyilmaṟik koṇṭaṉṟō aṉṟē varuntiya
kuṭimaṟip patuvē kūrvēl vaḷava;
(veḷiṟṟuppaṉan tuṇiyiṉ vīṟṟuvīṟṟuk kiṭappak
kaḷiṟṟuk kaṇam poruta kaṇṇakaṉ paṟantali)
varupaṭi tāṅkip peyarpuṟat tārttup
porupaṭi tarūum koṟṟamum uḻupaṭi
ūṉṟucāl maruṅkiṉ īṉṟataṉ payaṉē;
māri poyppiṉum vāri kuṉṟiṉum
iyaṟkiyallaṉa ceyaṟkiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉum
kāvalarp paḻikkum ik kaṇṇakal ñālam;
atunaṉ kaṟintaṉi yāyiṉ nīyum
notuma lāḷar potumoḻi koḷḷātu
pakaṭu puṟantarunar pāra mōmpik
kuṭipuṟanta tarukuvi yāyiṉniṉ
aṭipuṟan tarukuvar aṭaṅkātōrē."1
eṉpatu.
iṉi ilavantikip paḷḷittuñciya naṉmāṟaṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉip pāṭukiṉṟa marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār eṉṉum pulavar, 'civa perumāṉatu mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷil neṟṟiyil viḷaṅkum oru kaṇpōla vēntarkaḷil mēmpaṭṭa māṟaṉē! yāṉi, kutiri, tēr, kālāḷ eṉkiṟa nāṉku paṭiyum māṭcimip paṭṭaṉavāyiṉum aracaratu veṟṟiyāṉatu ciṟanta aṟaneṟiyi mutalāka vuṭiyatu; ataṉāl, ivar nammuṭiyareṉa avar ceyyum koṭun toḻilip poṟuttuk kōl vaḷiyāmalum, ivar namakku ayalāreṉṟu avar iṉiya paṇpukaḷi veṟuttotuk kāmalum, ñāyiṟu pōṉṟa vīramum, tiṅkaḷ pōṉṟa taṇṇaḷiyum, mēkam pōṉṟa vaṇmiyum uṭiyiyāki niṉatu nāṭṭil vaṟiñar eṉpōr illiyāka nī neṭuṅkālam vāḻvāyāka' eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
"	kaṭuñciṉatta kolkaḷiṟum
kataḻpariya kalimāvum
neṭuṅkoṭiya nimirtērum
neñcuṭiyapukal maṟavarum eṉa
nāṉkuṭaṉ māṇṭa tāyiṉum māṇṭa
aṟaneṟi mutaṟṟē araciṉ koṟṟam;
ataṉāl, namareṉak kōl kōṭātu
piṟareṉak kuṇaṅ kollātu
ñāyiṟ ṟaṉṉa ventiṟa lāṇmiyum
tiṅkaḷaṉṉa taṇperuñ cāyalum
vāṉat taṉṉa vaṇmiyum mūṉṟum
uṭiyi yāki illōr kiyaṟa
nī, nīṭu vāḻiya neṭuntaki."1
eṉpatu atu.
aṟivuṭi nampi eṉṉum pāṇṭi vēntaṉiṭam picirāntiyār eṉṉum pulavar peruntaki kūṟum aṟivu moḻikaḷ mikavum pōṟṟaṟpālaṉa. avar 'yāṉikku viḷinta nelliyaṟuttuk kavaḷamākak koṭuttāl orumāviṟ kuṟinta nilattil viḷinta katirum pala nāḷikku ākum; nūṟu ceyyāyiṉum yāṉi taṉittup pukku uṇṇumāyiṉ ataṉ vāyiṟ pukuvatik kāṭṭilum kālāl aḻivatu mikutiyākum. avvāṟē aṟivuṭiya aracaṉ iṟipporuḷ koḷḷum neṟiyi aṟintu koṇṭāl avaṉatu nāṭu kōṭi poruḷit tēṭikkoṭuttu, tāṉum mikavun taḻikkum; vēntaṉ aṟivu kuṟintavaṉāki niliyaṟintu uṟuti kūṟāmal avaṉ virumpuvataṉiyē kūṟum veṟum āravāratti yuṭiya amiccar mutalāṉarōṭu kūṭi aṉpiṉṟik koḷḷum poruḷi virumpuvāṉāyiṉ anta yāṉi pukunta viḷinilampōlat tāṉum uṇṇap peṟāṉ; ulakamumkeṭum' eṉkiṉṟaṉar. piṉvaruvatu avar vāymoḻi.
"	kāynel laṟuttuk kavaḷaṅ koḷiṉē
māniṟi villatum paṉṉāṭ kākum;
nūṟuceṟu vāyiṉum tamittup pukkuṇiṉē
vāypuku vataṉiṉum kālperitu keṭukkum
aṟivuṭi vēntaṉ neṟiyaṟintu koḷiṉē
kōṭi yāttu nāṭuperitu nantum;
melliyaṉ kiḻavaṉāki vikalum
varici yaṟiyāk kalleṉ cuṟṟamoṭu
parivu tapaveṭukkum piṇṭam nacciṉ
yāṉi pukka pulampōlat
tāṉum uṇṇāṉ; ulakamum keṭumē"1
mōcikīraṉār eṉṉum pulavar 'ivvulakamāṉatu vēntaṉākiya uyiri uṭiyatu. ataṉāl iv vulakattārkku nellum uyiraṉṟu, nīrum uyiraṉṟu, yāṉē uyireṉa aṟital paṭiyiṉiyuṭiya aracaṉukkuk kaṭaṉ' eṉkiṉṟār. atu,
"	nellum uyiraṉṟē; nīrum uyiraṉṟē;
maṉṉaṉ uyirttē malartali yulakam
ataṉāl, yāṉuyireṉpa taṟiki
vēṉmiku tāṉi vēntaṟkuk kaṭaṉē."2
eṉpatu.
ivvāṟu aracarkaḷi neruṅkip palatiṟappaṭṭa oḻukkaṅ kaḷiyum eṭuttu iṭitturikkum pulavarkaḷiṉ perumiyi yārāl eṭuttiyampalākum?  aracarāyiṉārum
"	iṭippāri yillāta ēmarā maṉṉaṉ
keṭuppāri lāṉuṅ keṭum."3
eṉṉum mutumoḻiyi yuṇarntu, pulavarkaḷ tammi neruṅki aṟivu, koḷuttutaṟku iṇaṅkiyum, avarkaḷ kūṟum aṟivurikaḷāl tiruntiyum vantaṉar.
iṉip pulavar mutalāyiṉār oḻukkaṅ kūṟuvativiṭa avvaracarkaḷ tāmē kūṟum vāymoḻiyiliruntu avarkaḷ oḻukkattil evvaḷavu karuttuṭiyarāyiruntaṉar eṉa aṟintu koḷvatu uṟuti yuṭittākum. olliyūr tanta pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum maṉṉaṉ pakiyaracar palar oruṅku cērntu pōrukku vantaviṭattu vañciṉaṅ kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ vañciṉamākakkūṟum vāymoḻikaḷil avaṉatu oḻukkattippulappaṭuttum pakutiyi maṭṭum iṅkē nōkkuvōm. 'ciṅkam pōṟ ciṉantu aṭaṅkāta cēṉiyi
yuṭiya maṉṉar palar cērntu eṉṉōṭu pōr ceyvōm eṉṟu colluvar. avarip pōriṉkaṇ alaṟumpaṭiyākap porutu tēruṭaṉ puṟaṅkāṭṭi yōṭac ceyyēṉāyiṉ, aṟam nilitiriyāta aṉpiṉiyuṭiya avikkaḷattil aṟattiṉ taṉmiyi aṟiyāta oruvaṉi ēṟpaṭutti muṟitavaṟik koṭuṅkōl ceytēṉ ākuka' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. 
"	ārama ralaṟat tākkit tēroṭu
avarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṟ ciṟanta
pēramar uṇkaṇ ivaḷiṉum pirika;
aṟaṉili tiriyā aṉpiṉ aviyattut
tiṟaṉi loruvaṉi nāṭṭi muṟitirintu
melikōl ceytē ṉākuka."1
eṉpatu avaṉ vāymoḻi.
itiliruntu akkālattut tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ āṭci naṭattu taṟku aracavi amittuk koṇṭiruntaṉa reṉpatum  avvaviyil aṟam nilitiriyātirunta teṉpatum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟiyum aracaviyil aṟattiṉ kūṟupāṭṭi aṟiyāta oruvaṉi ēṟpaṭuttu taliṉālēyē oḻukkattiṟ piṟaḻcci uṇṭāku meṉpatiyum, atippārkkilum taṉakku iḻivu vēṟilli yeṉpatiyum ammaṉṉaṉ naṉku uṇarntuḷḷamiyum pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
iṉit taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ vañciṉaṅ kūṟumiṭattu, 'eṉatu āṇmiyi matiyāmal ciṟumoḻi kūṟiya paki vēntarkaḷip pōriṉkaṇ citaṟumpaṭiporutu, muracattōṭu avarkaḷik kippaṟṟēṉāyiṉ, eṉkuṭi niḻalil vāḻum kuṭimakkaḷ, eṉatu koṭuṅkōṉmiyāl varuntit tāṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṭiyum niḻal kāṇāmal emmuṭiya vēntaṉ mikavum koṭiyaṉ eṉṟu kaṇṇīr viṭuttup paḻi tūṟṟum taṉmi yuṭiyēṉākuka' eṉkiṉṟāṉ. piṉvaruvatu atu,
"	ciṟucoṟ colliya ciṉaṅkeḻu vēntari
aruñcamañ citiyat tākki muracamoṭu
oruṅkakap paṭēe ṉāyiṟ poruntiya
eṉṉiḻal vāḻnar ceṉṉiḻaṟ kāṇātu
koṭiyaṉem miṟiyeṉak kaṇṇīr parappik
kuṭipaḻi tūṟṟum kōlēṉākuka."2
itiliruntu, kuṭikaḷ varuttamuṟṟup paḻitūṟṟutali im maṉṉaṉ evvaḷavu veṟuttirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu pulaṉākum. ivvēntaṉatu āṭciyilum kuṭikaḷukku ētēṉum tuṉpamuṇṭā
yiruttalkūṭumō? uṇṭāyiṉ ataṉi ivvēntar perumāṉ poṟuttiruttal kūṭumō? iṅṅaṉam tāyokkum aṉpuṭaṉ kuṭikaḷip purantuvarum maṉṉaratu perumikkum ōr elli yuṇṭē? 
iṉik kāñci nakarattiliruntu aracupurinta toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉ 'aracāṭciyākiya vaṇṭiyi naṭāttukiṉṟavaṉ māṭcimi yuṭiyaṉāyiṉ atu iṭiyūṟiṉṟi iṉitāka naṭakkum; avaṉ ataṉi iṉitākac celutta aṟiyā
ṉāyiṉ anta vaṇṭiyāṉatu eppoḻutum pakiyākiya cēṟṟilē akappaṭṭu mikappalavāya tuṉpatti mēṉmē luṇṭākkum' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. piṉvaruvatu avaṉ kūṟiya pāṭṭu, 
"	kālpār kōttu ñālat tiyakkum; 
kāvaṟ cākāṭu ukippōṉ māṇiṉ
ūṟiṉ ṟāki āṟiṉitu paṭumē;
uyttal tēṟṟā ṉāyiṉ vikalum
pakikkū ḻaḷḷaṟ paṭṭu
mikappal tīnōy talittalit tarumē."1 
cōḻaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉi māṟōkkattu nappacaliyār pāṭiyapāṭṭil 'kēṭiṉṟi maṟam poruntiya cōḻaṉatu uṟiyūrilē yuḷḷa avikkaḷattu aṟam niṉṟu nilipeṟṟatu. ātalāl muṟiceluttal eṉpatu niṉakku iyaṟkiyaṉṟip pukaḻumākātu' eṉkiṉṟār.
"	maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar uṟanti aviyattu
aṟamniṉṟu niliyiṟ ṟākali ṉataṉāl
muṟiminiṉ pukaḻu maṉṟē."2
ivvāṟē "aṟaṅkeḻu nallavi yuṟanti yaṉṉa" eṉṟu vēṟu nūlilum (akam.93) kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟṟāl tamiḻ nāṭṭu vēntar yāvarum aṟaneṟi tavaṟāta aracavi amittu āṭci naṭāttiṉami iṉitu pulaṉākum.
iṉi, araciyal naṭāttutaṟkaṇ aṉṟi ulakappotu vāḻvilum akkālattut tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ mika virinta maṉanili yaṭintiruntaṉareṉa avarkaḷ vāymoḻiyālēyē viḷaṅku kiṟatu. kaṭaluḷ māynta iḷamperuvaḻuti eṉṉum pāṇṭi vēntaṉ kūṟiyatorupāṭṭu poṉṉeḻuttāṟ patittu ovvoru varum pōṟṟavēṇṭiya tākum. atu,
"	uṇṭālamma ivvulakam intirar
amiḻtam iyiva tāyiṉum iṉiteṉat
tamiya ruṇṭalum ilarē muṉivilar
tuñcalu milarpiṟa rañcuva tañcip
pukaḻeṉiṉ uyiruṅ koṭukkuvar paḻiyeṉiṉ
ulakuṭaṉ peṟiṉum koḷḷalar ayarvilar
aṉṉa māṭci yaṉiyarākit
tamakkeṉa muyalā nōṉṟāṭ
piṟarkkeṉa muyalunar uṇmiyāṉē."1
eṉpatu.
ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷ, 'ivvulakam uḷatākā niṉṟatuyār iruttalāl eṉil, intirarkkuriya amiḻtamāṉatu teyvat tālō tavattālō arumiyākat tamakkuk kiṭippatāyiṉum ataṉi iṉiteṉṟu taṉittu uṇṭalum ilar; yārōṭum veṟuppiṉṟi yiruppar; piṟar añcattakum tuṉpattiṟkut tāmum añci ataṉit tīrkkātu maṭintiruttalollār; meyyāṉa pukaḻiṉ poruṭṭut tamuyiriyum koṭuppar; paḻi varuvatāyiṉ ataṉāl ulakam muḻutum kiṭippiṉum ēṟṟuk koḷḷār, ettakiya tuṉpattiṟkum maṉaṅkavalār, tām iṭiviṭātu ceyyum valiya muyaṟciyellām tamporuṭṭāka vaṉṟip piṟar poruṭṭākavē ceyvar, ippeṟṟiyuṭiya māṇpuṭiyōr iruttalāl tāṉ eṉka eṉpatu.


18. kuṟiñcippāṭṭiṉ uḷḷuṟi
ippāṭṭu, 261 aṭikaḷiyuṭiya akavaṟpā ākum. tamiḻc ceyyuḷiṉ ōciyamitikkup pā eṉṟu peyar. pāviyuṭiya ceyyuḷum ākupeyarāl 'pā' eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ceppal, akaval, tuḷḷal, tūṅkal eṉac ceyyuḷōci nāṉku vakippaṭum ceppalāvatu, oruvaṉ kēṭpa maṟṟoruvaṉ māṟukūṟuvatupōl iṭiyiṭi niṉṟu cellum iyaṟki yōciyām. akavalāvatu, oruvaṉ tāṉ karutiyavāṟellām variyātu kūṟa, iṭiyiṭi niṟṟaliṉṟik toṭarpupaṭṭuc cellum iyaṟki yōciyām. tuḷḷalāvatu, ici tōṉṟat tuḷḷiccellum ceyaṟki yōciyām. tūṅkalāvatu, cīrtoṟum niṟintu cellum ceyaṟkiyōciyām. ceppalōciyuṭiyatu veṇpā eṉavum, akavalōciyuṭiyatu akavaṟpā eṉavum, tuḷḷalōci yuṭiyatu kalippā eṉavum, tūṅkalōciyuṭiyatu vañcippā eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. iviyaṉṟic ceppalum, akavalum viravivaruvatu maruṭpā eṉavum, ceppalōciyēṉum ellāvōciyumēṉum peṟṟuk kalikkuriya uṟuppukkaḷuṭiyatāy varuvatu paripāṭṭu eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. ippāṭṭu muḻutum orē toṭarcciyāyk cellum akavalōci uṭittāyiruttaliṉ, itu akavaṟ pāvāmeṉpatu aṟika.
kuṟiñcit tiṇikkuriya poruḷ kūṟutaliṉ, itaṟkuk kuṟiñcippāṭṭu eṉṟu peyar kūṟiṉār. ulakattup poru ḷellāvaṟṟiyum mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ, uripporuḷ eṉa mūvakippaṭuttuk kūṟuvatu tamiḻnūlvaḻakku. nilaṉum kālamum mutaṟporuḷ eṉappaṭum. nīr,tī mutaliya maṟṟip pūtaṅkaḷum nilaṉeṉa aṭaṅkum. piṟaporuḷellām ivaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟutaliṉ, ivi mutaṟporuḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. tēvar, makkaḷ, vilaṅku, puḷ, maram mutalāyiṉavum karupporuḷ eṉappaṭum. nilattiṉum, kālattiṉum tōṟṟutaliṉ, ivi karupporuḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. (karu - tōṟṟam). avvan nilattu makkaṭku vēṟu vēṟāyt tōṟṟi niṟṟaliṉ, teyvamum karup poruḷāyiṟṟu, makkaḷ eytaṟpālaṉavākiya aṟam. poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉpaṉa uripporuḷ eṉappaṭum. uriya poruḷ kaḷākaliṉ urip poruḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa ivaṟṟi akapporuḷ puṟapporuḷ eṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkik kūṟuvatu tamiḻ nūṉmuṟi. iṉpam akapporuḷ. aṟamum poruḷum puṟap poruḷ. vīṭu, akam, puṟam iraṇṭiṉum aṭaṅkum. iṉpam tāṉē yuṇarta laṉṟip piṟarkku iṉṉa taṉmitteṉak kūṟalākāmiyiṉ, akam eṉṟum, aṟamum, poruḷum  piṟarkkuk kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, puṟam eṉṟum peyar peṟuvavāyiṉa. iṉpamum palavāyiṉum, orukālattu oru poruḷāl aimpulaṉum nukarum ciṟappuṭiya kāmaviṉpamē iṅkuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. avviṉpantāṉum, cuvi mikuti tōṉṟa ulaka naṭiyōṭu ottalum, ovvāmiyum uṭiyatākkik kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. uruvum, tiruvum, paruvamum, kuṭiyum, kuṇaṉum, aṉpum, mutaliyavikaḷāl tammuḷ oppumi yuṭiyarāya talivaṉum, taliviyum piṇi, mūppātiyaviṉṟi nukarum iṉpamē akam eṉak kūṟappaṭum iṉpameṉka.
avviṉpattukkuk kāraṇamāya oḻukkam akattiṇi eṉappaṭum. atu kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli, peruntiṇi, kikkiḷi eṉa eḻuvakippaṭum. aṟam, poruḷukkuk kāraṇamāya oḻukkam puṟattiṇi eṉappaṭum. atuvum veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki, kāñci, pāṭāṇtiṇi eṉa ēḻuvakip paṭum. ciṟcila oppumipaṟṟik kuṟiñci mutaliya akattiṇi ēḻukkum, veṭci mutaliya puṟattiṇiyēḻum muṟiyē iṉamāka āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyuḷḷār. akattiṇiyuḷ, peruntiṇiyum, kikkiḷiyum allāta aintumē ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. kuṟiñci yeṉpatu, kūṭalum kūṭal nimittamumām mulli eṉpatu, piriviṉkaṇ āṟṟiyiruttalum, iruttal nimittamumām. marutameṉpatu, ūṭalum, ūṭal nimittamumām. neytaleṉpatu, iraṅkalum, iraṅkal nimittamumām. pāli yeṉpatu, piritalum, pirital nimittamumākum kuṟiñci mutaliya tiṇikaḷukku, mutaṟ poruḷākiya nilam, poḻutukaḷum, karupporuḷākiya teyvam mutaliyavum vēṟu vēṟu vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiñcikku nilaṉ maliyum, mali cārnta iṭamum ām.
perum poḻutu aippaciyuṅ kārttikiyumākiya kūtirp paruvamum, mārkaḻiyun tiyumākiya muṉpaṉip paruvamum ām. ciṟu poḻutu apparuvaṅkaḷatu iraviṉ naṭukkūṟākiya yāmam ām. karupporuḷ murukavēḷ ākiya teyvamum, kuṟavar mutaliya makkaḷum, puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum, mayil mutaliya puṭkaḷum, vēṅki mutaliya maraṅkaḷum, maṟṟum innilattiṉum kālattiṉum tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷum, piṟavum ām. ivvāṟṟāl ippāṭṭiṉkaṭ kūṟappaṭum poruḷ iviyātal vēṇṭum eṉṟaṟika. ivaṟṟuḷ uripporuḷ iṉṟēl poruṭpēṟu iṉṟām ākaliṉ, aḵtu orutaliyākak kūṟappaṭuvatām. ataṉic ciṟappikkavē mutaṟporuḷ karupporuḷkaḷum kūṟappaṭum.
iṉi, ippāṭṭil uripporuḷiṉ eppakuti kūṟappaṭuvatu eṉavum, itu yār yārukku eppoḻutu evvāṟu kūṟuvatāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉavum mutaṟkaṇ aṟintu kōṭal vēṇṭum. kuṟiñci mutaliya akavoḻukkamellām kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉa iru vakippaṭuttuk kūṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟiṟ kaḷaviṉṟik kaṟpu nikaḻumā ṟilli yeṉavum, kaḷavē ciṟappuṭitteṉavum kūṟupa. kaḷavāvatu, muṟkūṟiya uruvum tiruvum mutaliyavaṟṟāl tammuḷ oppumiyuṭiyarāya talivaṉum taliviyum, koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟi ūḻvakiyāl tāmē yetirppaṭṭu iṉpam tuyttu varuvatu. mutalil ivvāṟu oru poḻilil etirp paṭṭuc cērvatu iyaṟki eṉavum, ataṉpiṉ avarkaḷ pirintu ceṉṟu muṉpōṉṟu mīṭṭum avviṭattē kūṭappeṟiṉ aḵtu iṭantalippāṭu eṉavum, pāṅkaṉāṟ kūṭappeṟiṉ atu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉavum tōḻiyāṟ kūṭappeṟiṉ atu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭuk kaḷavu nālvakippaṭum. pāṅkaṉum tōḻiyum tali makkaṭku uyirpōṟ ciṟantārākaliṉ, avar kaḷaṟivatu piṟaraṟivatākātu, tali makkaḷaṟivatē pōlum eṉka. ippāṭṭu, iyaṟkiyum, piṉṉikaḻum  kūṭṭaṅkaḷukku nimittamum kūṟuvatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iyaṟkiyiṉ piṉpu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻntu varuṅkāl, iravukkuṟiyiṭi
yīṭupaṭṭut talimakaṉ paṉmuṟi vaṟitē mīḷāniṟpat talivi āṟṟāmi perituṭiyaḷāy uṭal melivuṟalāṉāḷ. taliviyiṉ mēṉi iḷittu vēṟupaṭṭirut talikkaṇṭa ceviliyāṉavaḷ, ivviḷippukkuk kāraṇamāṉa nōy iṉṉateṉa aṟiyum poruṭṭuk, kuṟicolluvāri viṉāvi, avarkaḷ teyvat
tāṉāyiṟṟu eṉakkūṟaliṉ teyvaṅkaṭkuc ciṟappuc ceytu, piṉṉum nōy nīṅkāmi kaṇṭu varuntukiṉṟāḷ. an niliyil, talivi mēlum āṟṟāmiyuṭiyaḷāy, 'emakkuḷ nikaḻnta immaṉṟali nām tāykku aṟivuṟuttalāl paḻiyum varuvatuṇṭō?' eṉṟu tōḻikku uṇartta, avaḷ cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟpatāka, iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷatu ippāṭṭu. aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉpatu, talivaṉukkum talivikkum nikaḻnta kūṭṭatti veḷippaṭuttal. inta nilimiyil itaṉi veḷippaṭuttalē aṟattoṭu poruntiya ceyalākaliṉ,  iḵtu aṟattōṭu niṟṟal eṉappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, ippāṭṭiṟkut tiṇi kuṟiñci eṉavum, tuṟi tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉavum koḷka. tōḻiyāṉavaḷ, uyarkuṭip piṟanta taliviyatu kaḷavoḻukkatti veḷippaṭuttum poḻutu, cevilikkuc ciṉamuṇṭākāvāṟum, taliviyiṭattātal, taṉṉiṭattātal, talivaṉiṭattātal, avaḷiṭattātal ciṟitum kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉa avaḷuṇarumāṟum, iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntatu nalviṉip payaṉēyeṉat tōṟṟumāṟum paṭittu moḻiyum tiṟappāṭu peritum viyakkattakkatākum. ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷuṇaravē, atu naṉku viḷaṅkumātaliṉ, īṇṭu ataṉic curukkik kūṟukiṉṟām.
"tāyē! yāṉ kūṟuvataṉi virumpikkēṭpāyāka! nī tāṉ, oḷḷiya neṟṟiyiyuṭiya eṉ tōḻiyatu iḻiyiṉi nekiḻacceyta poṟuttaṟkariya nōyikkaṇṭu alamantu, kaṭṭiṉālum kaḻaṅkiṉālum aṟintu kuṟi colluvāri viṉāvi, avar teyvattāṉ vanta varuttameṉṟaliṉ palvēṟu teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭum ataṉāṟ ṟīrāmiyiṉ, innōyi aṟiyāta mayakkatti uṭiyiyāy varuntā niṉṟāy. ivaḷō, taṉatu nalla vaṉappukkuṉṟavum, tōḷmeliyavum, kūṟutaṟku arumiyālē taṉmaṉattuḷḷē maṟittirukkum āṟṟutaṟkariya nōyiṉik kuṟittu yāṉ neruṅkik kēṭkaluṟṟa poḻutu, eṉṉi nōkki, 'muttāṉum, maṇiyāṉum, poṉṉāṉumiyaṉṟa aruṅkalaṉ keṭṭatāyiṉum, piṉṉum vantu poruntum. aḵtaṉṟik, kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ amitiyum, mēmpāṭum, oḻukkamum keṭṭāl, uṇṭāya mācupōmpaṭi kaḻuvi, muṉpōlap pukaḻi niṟuttal, kuṟṟamaṟṟa aṟiviṉiyuṭiya muṉivarkkum eḷiya taṉṟeṉṟu toṉṉūlaṟiñar kūṟuvar. yāṅkaḷ iruvēmum āynta maṉṟal ituveṉa nām tāykku aṟivuṟuttiṉ, namakkup pukaḻaṉṟip paḻivaruvatu muṇṭō? aḵtilliyē. iṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭutta piṉpu, talivarkkē nammikkoṭukka nērntilarāyiṉum, nām uyir pōntuṇiyum ivvaruttattip poṟuttiruppiṉ, namakku maṟumiyiṉum ikkūṭṭam kūṭuva toṉṟākumē.' eṉakkūṟi, āṟṟaru nōyiṉi yuṭiyāḷāy miyal nōkkaṅ
koṇṭu, ceyaloḻintu ayarntu meliyā niṉṟāḷ. yāṉuntāṉ, māṟupaṭṭu amarilē mikkuc cellum iraṇṭu periya aracaric cantuceyvikkum toḻilil niṉṟa cāṉṟōr pōla, niṉakkum ivaḷatu varuttattiṟkum añcum iraṇṭu periya accattālē varuntāniṉṟēṉ. koṭuppiṉ ellāvaṟṟāṉum naṉṟāki muṭitalum, kuṭiyum kuṇamum, cuṟṟattutaviyum eṉumivaṟṟi oppittup pārttup palaruṭaṉum eṇṇāmalum, nummiyiṉṟiyum yāṅkaḷē tuṇinta uyirkkup pātukāvalāṉa, ivvariya maṇam muṉpu nikaḻnta vāṟṟi nī naṉṟāka vuṇarumpaṭi kūṟutaṟ kamintēṉ. atu kēṭṭuc ciṉavā tiruppāyāka! tāyē! nī tāṉ,  'viḷinta tiṉiyiṉ kaṇ vīḻum kiḷikaḷi yōṭṭi, mālippoḻutil varuvīrāka'. eṉac colli, emmi aṉuppukiyālē, yāṅkaḷum ceṉṟu maratti ṉucciyil, iravu kāttiruppōṉ paṇṇiṉa, puli yañcum paraṇilēyēṟik, kavaṇum taṭṭiyum mutaliyavaṟṟi muṟi muṟi kikkoṇṭu, kiḷikaḷi yōṭṭiṉēm. piṉpu ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmi mikukiṉṟa pakaṟpoḻutilē, vāṉiṟ paṟakkum paṟavikaḷellām tām virumpum cēkkiyiṟ cellā niṟka, yāṅkaḷ neṭiya maliyucciyiṉiṉṟum kutikkum teḷinta nīriyuṭiya veḷiya tukilpōlum aḻakiya aruviyilē, tavirāta viruppuṭaṉ mēlum mēlum viḷiyāṭi, ataṉ piṉpu, paḷiṅkiṉik karittuc corintu vittāṟpōlum cuṉiyiṟ kuṭintuviḷiyāṭuṅkāl, eṅkaḷ maṉattiṟku viruppamāṉa pala pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉēm. āṉapiṉ, ematu piṉṉiya kūntaliṉ nīrip piḻintu, īrampulartti uḷḷiṭam civanta kaṇṇiṉēmāy, āmpal aṉiccam ceṅkaḻunīr kuṟiñci veṭci mutaliya palvakip pūkkaḷiyum mikka vēṭkiyuṭaṉ palakālum tirintu paṟintu vantu, tūytāṉa akaṉṟa pāṟiyilē kuvittup paṟavikaḷiṉ ōciyākiya palliyaṅkaḷiyuṭiya malippakkattiṟ puṉattē, teḷinta iciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya coṟkaḷāl iṭiyiṭi kiḷikaḷi yōppiyum, puṟavitaḻkaḷik kaḷinteṟintu oḻuṅkupeṟak koytu iyaṟṟiya taḻiyeṉṉum uṭiyiṉi iṭiyilē uṭuttiyum, palvēṟuruviṉ aḻakaminta mālikaḷi emmuṭiya melliruṅ kūntalil aḻakupeṟac cuṟṟiyum, neruppuppōlum niṟattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum aḻakiya taḷiriyuṭiyaacōkiṉatu tātu utirkiṉṟa taṇṇiya niḻalilē mēviyiruntēm. aṅṅaṉam irukkiyil, āṇṭu ōr tōṉṟal paṉṉiṟamuḷḷa palvakip pūkkaḷālum toṭukkappeṟṟa toṭiyiṉiyum veḷḷiya tāḻi maṭaliṟ poruntac ceyta kaṇṇiyiyum ceṉṉiyil aḻakuṟac cūṭiyum, picci malarāṟ ṟoṭutta oru caramākiya māliyic cuṟṟiyum, acōkiṉatu taḷirit tōḷil vīḻntaciyumpaṭi oru kātilē cerukiyum, cantaṉam pūcappeṟṟu akaṉṟu uyarntu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa cempoṟiyiyuṭiya mārpil toṉṟupaṭṭu varum civanta aṇikalaṅkaḷōṭē naṟiya mālikaḷip polivuṟa aṇintum, nuṇṭoḻilaminta kacciṉi uṭiyiṉ mēl iṟukakkaṭṭiyum, kiyilē villi yeṭuttu ampukaḷit terittup piṭittum,  poṉṉāṟceyta vīrakkaḻal, peyarum poḻutellām tiruntiya aṭiyilē ēṟṟiḻivu ceytu viḷaṅkum paṭiyākap pōntaṉaṉ. pōntavaṉ, aṇukutaṟkariya valiyaminta pakivarip puṟaṅkaṇṭa vīrarpōlē cerukkum ciṉamumuṭiya nāykaḷ imiyāta kaṇṇiṉavāy emmi vaḷittukkoṇṭu mēṉmēl neruṅki varutaṟku yāṅkaḷ añci aṭitaḷarntu, varuttam mikka maṉattuṭaṉ vēṟu pulattēṟac cellā niṟpataṉik kaṇṇuṟṟāṉāy, māṟāya viṭikaḷi yellām porutōṭṭiyatum vēṟu pulattirukkum putiya āviṉik kāṇaluṟṟatumākiya ēṟupōlē aḻakupeṟat tōṉṟi, yāṅkaḷ añcutaṟkut tāṉ añci, meṉmiyum iṉimiyumuṭiya coṟkaḷi emakkup poruntumāṟu colli, em kūntaliyum aḻakiyum pukaḻntu, 'oḷḷiya
