toṭiyum, melliya cāyalum, aḻakiya untiyum, matariya kaṇṇum uṭiya iḷiñarē! eṉṉit tappi ivviṭattup pōnta yāṉi mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭīrō?', eṉa viṉāviṉāṉ. ataṟku yāṅkaḷ maṟumoḻi tantilēmākiyāl, avvāṇṭaki neñcaḻintu 'nīvir avaṟṟik kāṭṭittārī rāyiṉum emmuṭaṉ oru colcollutalum numakkup paḻi yāmō?' eṉac colli, vaṇṭu mūcum pūkkaḷiyuṭiya taḻi paranta kompi muṟittum parikkōlik kikaṭanta matayāṉi pōla ōccik, kalleṉṉum ōcipaṭak kattum vēṭṭi nāykaḷiṉ kaṭuṅkurali māṟṟi, yāṅkaḷ pēcuvatōr kālattip pārttu niṉṟaṉaṉ. appoḻutu, vēṟu puṉattilē, kāvalāḷarkaḷ kaḷḷuṇṭu kāvaṉ maṭintirunta vaḷavil, ōr vēḻamāṉatu puṉattiṉit tiṉṟu nokkiviṭukiyiṉālē avarkaḷ varuttammikkut tamatu pāmpu pōlumvilli nāṇēṟṟi ampiṉi yeytu, kāṭellām kalleṉ ōci piṟakka vāyi maṭittu vīḷiyōci yeḻuppi ataṉip puṉattiṉiṉṟum  ōṭṭa, akkaḷiṟu mataccerukkiṉi yuṭittāki, urumēṟu pōla muḻaṅki maraṅkaḷi muṟittuk kiyic curuṭṭi nilattē yeṟintu kūṟṟuvaṉippōla eṅkaḷ mītu kaṭuka vantatu. yāṅkaḷ, uyir koṇṭu piḻippatōriṭam aṅku vēṟuṇarēmāki, ulivuṟṟa maṉattuṭaṉ, uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇiṉip pātukāttali maṟantu, vaḷika ḷolikkumpaṭi virintu ceṉṟu avaṉiccērntu teyvamēṟiṉa mayilpōla naṭuṅkā niṟka, avaṉ kaṭuvicip pakaḻiyi nirampa valittu avviyāṉiyiṉ mukattē yeytamiyāl, ampupaṭṭuruviṉa. puṇṇiṉiṉṟum kuruti yoḻukā niṟka, atu taṉṉi maṟantu āṇṭu niṟkalāṟṟātu puṟaṅkoṭuttōṭiṟṟu, ataṉpiṉ, yāṅkaḷ, murukavēḷukku vari makaḷir kikōttāṭuvatu pōṉṟu, kaṭampiṉ ariyiṉi neruṅkac cūḻnta mālipōl, kikōttuk koṇṭu, yāṟṟiṉ perukkilē kutittu viḷiyāṭaluṟṟu, iṭikariyiṉiṉṟa vāḻipōlē kāltaḷarntu nīriṟ cellaluṟ ṟēmāka, atu kaṇṭu virintu vanteṭuttu aḻakiya mali poruntiṉa nāṭṭiyuṭiyaṉāṉa avvāṇṭaki, 'yāṉ niṉatu aḻakiya nalatti nukarvēṉ. niṉṉi nīṅkuvēṉeṉac ciṟitum añcātē' eṉṟu colli, oḷiporuntiya nutaliṉit tuṭittu, neṭunāḷ ikkaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻavēṇṭumeṉa niṉittu, eṉmukatti nōkkic cirittāṉ. appoḻutu, taṉakku iyalpākiya nāṇamum accamumvantu tōṉṟukiyāl, ivaḷ taṉṉiṭattu  niṉṟu virintu nīṅkavum viṭāṉāy, aṅṅaṉam niṉṟa niliyilē kiyālaṇittu, ivaḷ mārpu taṉ mārpilē yoṭuṅkumpaṭi taḻuvāniṉṟu, piṉ, ivaḷuḷḷak karuttu mēl varintukoṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutalāyirukkumeṉa uṭkoṇṭu ivaḷi nōkki, 'āruyirp pāvāy, palarum vantuṇṇumāṟu katavu akalat tiṟantukiṭakkum vāyiliuṭiyatāy miṭāccōṟṟi varuvārkkellām variyāmaliṭukiṉṟa viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉāṟ pōlum celvattiyuṭiya maṉiyakam polivu peṟumpaṭi pinniṇamoḻiya neymmikka aṭicili nī iṭā niṟkak kuṟṟamillāta uyarkuṭip piṟantōr tamatu cuṟṟattōṭē viruntuṇṭu, eñciyuḷḷa miccili nīyum yāṉum uṇṇutal mēṉmiyuṭittu' eṉṟu colli,  illaṟam taṅkaḷik kari yēṟṟuvatākat teḷivittu, maliyucciyiluṟiyum murukavēḷi vāḻtti vaṇaṅki, avaṉ muṉpu vañciṉaṅkūṟi, aḻakiya iṉiya nīrik kuṭittaṉaṉ. ataṉāl ivaḷ neñcu poruntiyirukkak, kaḷiṟṟiṉāl tarappaṭṭa kūṭṭattit tēvarkaḷum virumpum pūkkaḷ niṟinta cōliyil aṉṟip pakaṟpoḻutellām pōkki, ñāyiṟu maliyiṉkaṇ maṟiya, mālippoḻutu varutalkaṇṭu, 'ilaṅkiḻiyīr! nummuṭiya cuṟṟattār numatu nēriṟi muṉkippaṟṟi emakkuttara, nāṭaṟiyum naṉmaṇattiṉip piṉpu nikaḻttuvōm, yām ikkaḷavoḻukkattāṟ peṟum pēriṉpam nukartaṟkuc ciṟitunāḷ iṅṅaṉam oḻukuvatupaṟṟi neñcu kalaṅkātiruppīrāka' eṉṟu aruḷuṭittākiya naṉmoḻiyi ivaḷ varuttan tīrumpaṭi colli, āvip puṇarnta ēṟu pōlē viṭāmal emmuṭaṉ kūṭavantu kuḻalōciyaṟāta nam ūrvācalil palarum nīruṇṇun tuṟiyil emmi niṟutti mīṇṭupōṉāṉ. atu mutalāka, aṉṟupōlum viruppattōṭē, ennāḷum iravukkuṟiyil varutalit taṉakkiyalpākavuṭiyaṉ. aṅṅaṉam varumpōtellām, ūrkkāvalar kaṭukik kāttārāyiṉum ciṉattiṉiyuṭiya nāy kurittāyiṉum, nī tuyiluṇarntāyāyiṉum, nilavu veḷiyāka eṟitta tāyiṉum, mūṅkiliyotta meṉṟōḷiṟpeṟum iṉṟuyil peṟātu pōvaṉ. aṅṅaṉam vāḷāpeyariṉum ataṟku veṟuttalic ceyyāṉ. avaṉṟāṉ iḷamip paruvattik kaṭantatumilaṉ. celvac cerukkāl ennāḷum taṉ kulattiṟkuriya naṟkuṇaṅkaḷiṉ nīṅkiyatumilaṉ. alar mutaliyavaṟṟāl accattiyuṭiya iv'd2vūriṉkaṇ, taṉakkup poyyāyirukkiṉṟa iravuk kuṟiyil varuntaṉmiyi iḵtoḻukkamaṉṟeṉṟu niṉittu, avaṉ varintukoṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutalē nalloḻukkameṉṟu tuṇikiyiṉālē, ivaḷuṭiya periya matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ peruntuḷiyeṟintu, maḻipeyyappaṭṭa malarpōl aḻaku keṭṭu imicōrntu īrattiṉiyuṭiyavāyk kalaṅkā niṟkum. ataṟku mēlē, ivaḷ, avar varukiṉṟa maliyiṭam puliyum yāḷiyum mutaliya 'iṭumpi ceyvaṉavuṭiyavā yirukkumeṉṟu niṉikkun tōṟum, valiyilakappaṭṭa mayilpōlē taṉatu nalam pōmpaṭi melintu, kaṇṇil arittu vīḻkiṉṟa nīr nāḷ tōṟum mārpilē tuḷippak kalaṅkā niṟkum. itukāṇ nalviṉi nikaḻntavaṇṇam eṉṟāḷ" eṉpatu.


19. cāttaṉār pāttiṟal
nam taṇṭamiḻ moḻiyip paṇṭu vaḷampaṭutta nallicip pulavaruḷḷē maturik kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār vittakak kavittiṟam vāynta mētakkārāvar. "vaḷḷuvar muppālāl talikkuttut tīrvu cāttaṟku" eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum varalāṟu ivar tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷ ettakiya viḻuppamuṭiyavāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum karuttuṭiyā reṉpataṉip pulappaṭuttum, paḻutaṟṟa muttamiḻk kāppiyamākiya cilappatikāramaḷitta cēramuṉiyākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ "taṇṭamiḻc cāttaṉ" (cilampu:patikam.10) eṉavum "taṇṭamiḻācāṉ cāttaṉ" (cilampu: kāṭcikkāti.66) eṉavum tamatu vaṇṭamiḻc ceyyuḷil ivari vāyārap pukaḻvarēl ivaratu tamiḻppulami aṟṟi nāḷil ettuṇi naṉku matikkap paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum? aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāyatum, 'maṇimēkali nūṉuṭpam koḷvateṅṅaṉ' (tiruveṅ:kōvi:107) eṉṟu kaviñar perumāṉākiya tuṟimaṅkalam civappirakāca muṉivarāṟ pārāṭṭappeṟṟatumākiya ivaratu maṇimēkalik kāppiyattiliruntu ivar tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇat tuṟipōyavar eṉpatum, akkālattu nilaviya camayaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum ārāyntu teḷintavareṉpatum, aḷavi (tarukka) nūliṉ elli kaṇṭavareṉpatum, maṟṟum pala tiṟappaṭṭa kalikaḷilum vallunareṉpatum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. paṭippavar nēriliruntu kaṇṭu kaḷippatupōlat tōṉṟumpaṭi mali, kaṭal, vaṉam, nāṭu, nakar mutaliyavaṟṟi iṉiya ceñcoṟ kaḷāl ivar puṉinturikkum aḻaku mikap pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. iṅṅaṉam viḻuppamuṭiya centamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷi yellām paṭittuc cuvittaṉṟō kavipperuvēntarākiya kampa nāṭar "ceñcoṟkaviyiṉpam"  (mitilik:23) eṉac cītiyip pukaḻvārāyiṉar. cāttaṉāriṉ ittakiya kavittiṟatti maṇimēkaliyiluḷḷa ōr kātiyiliruntu ciṟitu viḷakkutum.
maṇimēkaliyil, 'malarvaṉam pukkakāti' eṉa ōrkātiyuḷatu. kōvalaṉ maturiyiṟ koliyuṇṭami kēṭṭu avaṉ kātaṟkaṇikiyākiya mātavi puttacamayañ cārntu tuṟavupūṇṭu taṉmakaḷ maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ toṇṭu ceytu koṇṭiruk kiṉṟaṉaḷ. kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil naṭipeṟṟa intira viḻāviṟkum vaḻakkappaṭi āṭutaṟku avarkaḷ varāmiyāl mātaviyiṉ naṟṟāyākiya cittirāpati maṉam puḻuṅki, avaḷ tōḻiyākiya vayantamāliyi aṉuppa, avaḷ maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ mātaviyirunta malarmaṇṭapattiyaṭintu mātaviyiṉ vāṭiya mēṉikaṇṭu varunti, 'nī uṉ makaḷuṭaṉ intira viḻāviṟku' varāmalum, uṉ marapiṟku ovvāta tava voḻukkam pūṇṭu miruttal paṟṟi ūrār kūṟum paḻimoḻikaḷ iṉṉavi' eṉṟu avaṭkuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉaḷ. ataṉik kēṭṭa mātavi kaṇavaṉ koliyuṇṭa kaṭuntuyarip poṟāḷāyk kāvalaṉ pērūr kaṉiyeriyūṭṭiya māperum pattiṉiyākiya kaṇṇakiyiṉ makaḷ maṇimēkali tavattiṟkuriyaḷaṉṟi iḻinta parattimit toḻilukku uriyaḷallaḷ' eṉṟum, 'iṅkuḷḷa aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷāl nālvaki vāymiyum aivakic cīlamum aṟivuṟuttap peṟṟut tavavaḻiyiṟ pukuntuḷēṉātaliṉ yāṉum aṅku varutaṟku uriyaḷallēṉ' eṉṟum kūṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉaḷ. itaṉ piṉṉarē mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa kāti toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. 
vayantamālikku  mātavi kūṟiya coṟkaḷ vāyilāka maṇimēkalikku ētu nikaḻcci (viṉi payaṉṟaruṅkālam) etirntuḷatākaliṉ, avaḷ taṉ tantiyākiya kōvalaṉukkum, tāyākiya kaṇṇakikkum maturiyil nērnta ventuyariṭumpi ceviyakam vetuppa neñcukalaṅki aḻutu kaṇṇīrukuttut tāṉ toṭuttuk koṇṭirunta māliyi naṉittu viṭṭaṉaḷ. iṅkē maṇimēkalikku ētu nikaḻcci etirntateṉak kūṟappukka āciriyar,
"	māmalar nāṟṟampōl maṇimēkali
kētunikaḻcci yetirntuḷa tātaliṉ"1
eṉa ataṉi ōr aḻakiya uvamiyuṭaṉ paṭuttuk kūṟukiṉṟār. pū arumpāyirunta ñāṉṟu aṭaṅki yiruntu piṉ pōtāy malarum poḻutu maṇam veḷippaṭutali, viṉi payaṉṟaruṅ kālam varuntuṇi maṟintiruntu akkālam vantuḻi veḷip paṭutaṟku uvami kūṟiyatu evvaḷavu poruttamāka virukkiṟatu. iṅkē payaṉṟarumviṉi mikka tūymiyuṭiya toṉṟākaliṉ ataṟkēṟpat tūya malariyē terinteṭuttu uvami kūṟuvārāyiṉar. malar toṭuttuk koṇṭirukkum melliyaliṉ nalla melviṉi veḷippaṭutaṟku meṉmiyuṭiya malar maṇam veḷippaṭutali uvami kūṟiyatu mikka iṉpam viḷikkiṉṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam maṇimēkali varuntuvatik kaṇṇuṟṟa mātavi avaḷ kaṇṇīrit tuṭittu, avaḷeytiya tuyari oruvāṟu māṟṟak karuti 'ittūya māli niṉ kaṇṇīrāl tūymi yiḻantatākaliṉ vēṟumāli toṭuttaṟku nī cōli ceṉṟu putumalar paṟittu varuvāy' eṉkiṉṟaṉaḷ. iṅkē mātavi taṉ ceṅkiyāl maṇimēkaliyiṉ oṇmukak kaṇṇīri māṟṟiṉamiyit
"	tāmari taṇmati cērntatu pōlak
kāmar ceṅkiyiṟ kaṇṇīr  māṟṟi."1
eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār. appoḻutalarnta naṟumalarāl aḻakupeṟat toṭukkappeṟṟa māli pōṉṟu ceñcoṟ kaḷālē teḷivum iṉimiyum porunta yātta ittoṭar kaṟṟārkkuk kaḻipēriṉpam payappatākum. itaṉkaṇ, teḷivu, camanili eṉṉuṅ kuṇaṅkaḷum, cittiravaṇṇamum, il poruḷuvami eṉṉum poruḷaṇiyum iyintuḷḷami kāṇka.
mātavi kūṟiya immoḻiyik kēṭṭalum, maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ malar toṭuttuk koṇṭirunta cutamatiyāṉavaḷ mātavi mukam nōkki 'maṇimēkaliyiṉ kaṇṇīrik kāṇiṉ kāmaṉ taṉ paṭikkalatti yeṟintu viṭṭu naṭuṅkuvaṉ; ittuṇip pēraḻakuṭiyāḷ taṉiyē ceṉṟu malar koyyun taṉmiyaḷallaḷ. ilavantikiccōli, uyyāṉam, campāti vaṉam kavēravaṉam eṉṉum pūñcōlikaṭkuc cella aṟivuṭiyōr añcuvar. aruḷum aṉpumuṭiya pakavaṉatu āṇiyālē paṉmaraṅkaḷum eppoḻutum pūttut tikaḻum uvavaṉam eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu; avvaṉattiṟkaṉṟi niṉmakaḷ cellum takutiyaḷallaḷ; yāṉum avaḷuṭaṉ celvēṉ' eṉṟu kūṟi, aḻakiya pūṅkompu pōlum maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ kūṭi maṇittēr vītiyiṟ cellukiṉṟaṉaḷ. aṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ cellutalik kūṟu mukattāṉē āciriyar tamatu pulamit tiṟattiṉi eṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa reṉakkāṭṭutalē icciṟṟuriyiṉ nōkkam.
maṇimēkaliyum cutamatiyum kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattu maṟukiṟ cellum poḻutu, aṅkē paṟpala kāṭcikaḷikkaṇṭu kuḻām kuḻāmāy niṟkum māntarkaḷ, muṉṉam virāṭaṉ pērūrkkaṇ pēṭi vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu ceṉṟa vicayaṉikkaṇṭu cūḻntamākkaḷip pōla maṇimēkaliyic cūḻntu koṇṭu 'pēraḻakuṭiya ivaḷi aruntavappaṭuttiya tāy mikakkoṭiyavaḷ, avaḷ malarpaṟittaṟku vaṉam pukuvaḷāyiṉ aṅkuḷḷa aṉṉappuḷ mutaliyaṉa ivaḷ naṭi mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭu eṉṉapāṭupaṭā' eṉṟu ivi pōlvaṉa colli avaḷ aḻakip pārāṭṭi varunti niṟka, avviruvarum malarvaṉattiyaṭintaṉar; eṉa ikkātiyi naṭātti muṭikkiṉṟaṉar āciriyar. yātēṉum oru kati nikaḻcciyit talikkīṭākakkoṇṭu ulaka iyaṟki kaḷiyum palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ oḻukalāṟu mutaliya vaṟṟiyum uḷḷavāṟu uṇartti iṉpam viḷippatu nallicip pulavariṉ ciṟanta nōkkamākum. nakaraṅkaḷum, cīṟūr, paṭṭi mutaliyavi kaḷum avaṟṟiṉ amippiṉālaṉṟi aṅku vāḻum makkaḷiṉ naṭiyuṭi mutaliyavaṟṟālum vēṟṟumi yuṟukiṉṟaṉa. celvap perukkum, ceyaṟki vaṉappum, eḻil viḷiyāṭṭum, iṉpac celavum nakaraṅkaḷil viñcik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nām oru nakarap perunteruvilē mālippoḻutil ulā varuvōmāyiṉ iṭiyē ettaṉiyō pala kāṭcikaḷ etirppaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṟṟi nāḷilē mikap periyatoru nakaramāy viḷaṅkiya pukāriṉ maṟukilum palatiṟappaṭṭa kāṭcikaḷ etirppaṭutal iyalpē aṉṟō? aviyiṟṟiyē taṇṭamiḻācāṉākiya cāttaṉārum ōviyam varintu kāṭṭiṉāṟ pōl nam uḷakkaṇṇukkup pulaṉākumāṟu ceḻuntamiḻp pāvāka yāttu vittaṉar.
camaṇ camayattu uṇṇā nōṉpi yoruvaṉ uṟiyiṟ poruntiya kuṇṭikiyum pirappaṅkōlum uṭiyavaṉāyk kaḻuvāta mēṉiyaṉāy varuntutali yuṭiyayāṉi pōṉṟu acinta naṭiyuṭaṉ avvītiyil vantukoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉaṉ. avaṉ uyirkaḷ māṭṭu ettaṉi irakka muṭiyaṉeṉpataṉi,
"kāṇāvuyirkkuṅ kiyaṟ ṟēṅki" 1
eṉṉum ciṟu toṭarāl āciriyar aḻaku peṟa viḷakkiyuḷḷār. kaṇṇukkup pulaṉākāta nuṇṇuṭampuṭiya uyirkaḷum taṉatu naṭattal mutaliya ceyalkaḷāl eṉṉa tuṉpamuṟumō eṉṟu ceyalaṟṟu ēṅkip piyappiya naṭantu cellum annōṉpiyi ōr kaḷimakaṉ kaṇṭu vaḻimaṟittu,
"	vantīr aṭikaḷ! num malaraṭi toḻutēṉ!!
entam aṭikaḷ emmuri kēṇmō" 2
eṉap pīṭiki pōṭṭuk koṇṭu, taṉ kiyiluḷḷa matuc cāṭiyi avaḷ muṉ nīṭṭi, iḵtu immiyum maṟumiyum iṟutiyiliṉpamum taruvatu, eṉ tali makaṉ urittatu, aḻukkuṭiyākkiyiṟ pukunta nummuyir puḻukkaṟip paṭṭōr pōṉṟu uḷam varuntātu itaṉi uṇpīrāka, eṉṉak kaḷḷum ūṉum kaṭintavaṉākiya annōṉpi, cīla millāroṭu vāyāṟ pēcutalum nēritaṉṟākaliṉ avaṉ appuṟañ cellumāṟu kiyiacittuc ciki ceyyavum, avaṉ cellāṉāyt 'teṅkiṉ koḻumaṭalil viḷinta ittēṟaliṉ kaṇkoliyum uṇṭō, itaṉi uṇṭu teḷintu intayōkattiṉ uṟu payaṉik kaṇṭāl piṉpu emmik kiyālakaṟṟutir, itaṉi oru muṟiyēṉum uṇpīr' eṉṟu  cūḷuṟṟu irakkiṉṟaṉaṉ; itaṉip pārttukkoṇṭu niṟkiṉṟatu ōr kuḻu.
ciṟitu appāṟ celvuḻi, miyaluṟṟavaṉ (pittaṉ) oruvaṉ alari māliyāṟ kaṭṭiya tōḷkaḷi yuṭiyaṉāy, erukka māli yaṇintavaṉāy, kantiyuṭaṉ cuḷḷikaḷic cērttuk kaṭṭiya uṭiyiṉiyuṭiyaṉāy, uṭal muḻutum nīṟṟiṉip pūcikkoṇṭu, palarōṭum poruttamil coṟkaḷip pēci, aḻutalum viḻutalum araṟṟutalum kūvutalum toḻutalum eḻutalum cuḻalutalum ōṭutalum otuṅki niṟṟalum niḻaloṭu ciṉattalum pōlvaṉa ceykiṉṟāṉ; ivaṉi irakkattuṭaṉ pārttu niṟkiṉṟatu ōr kuḻu. pittēṟiyavaṉ ceykikaḷ itiṟ paṭṭāṅku pulappaṭa vittiruttal viyakkaṟpālatu.
iṉṉum ciṟitu tūrañ celluṅkāl, pakaṟkūttiyaṟṟuvār peṇṇiyalpu mikku āṇiyalpu viraviya pēṭikkōlam pūṇṭu naṭikka ataṉik kaṇṭu niṟkiṉṟatu ōr kuḻu.
maṟṟum, uyariya niliyiṉiyuṭiya maṉikaḷ tōṟum oḷḷiya cutikoṇṭu vittakar iyaṟṟiya palatiṟappaṭṭa kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷi āṇṭāṇṭuk kaṇṭu niṟkiṉṟa māntarum palar.
piṉṉum, viḻācceytu muṭitta vītiyiṉ kaṇṇē celva makaḷir cilar kūṭi niṉṟu, poṉṉāṇiṟ kōtta naṉmaṇik kōviyum aiyavi yappiya neyyaṇi mucciyum cevvāykku taliyum  meypeṟā maḻaliyum  viḷaṅkum pūṇumuṭiya iḷam putalvari mayiriṭattē cuṟṟiya mūṉṟu kōviyākiya muttuvaṭam piṟi pōlum aṇiyiṉmītu tāḻavum, aṟṟaṅkāvāc cuṟṟuṭip pūntukiluṭaṉ toṭutta maṇikkōvi aciyavum poṟṟēriṉ mēluḷḷa yāṉiyil ēṟṟi vittu, 'ālamar celvaṉ makaṉākiya murukaṉ viḻāviṟkuk kāl koḷḷutalik kāṇpīrāka' eṉṟu kūṟa, ataṉik kaṇṭu niṟpārum palar. civaperumāṉ iṅkē 'ālamar celvaṉ' eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruttal pōṉṟē cilappatikārattum eṭṭut toki, pattuppāṭṭukkaḷiṉum kūṟappaṭṭiruttali nōkkiṉ, āliṉ kīḻē teṉmukamākava marntu nālvarkku oḷineṟi kāṭṭiya ittiruvuruviṉip paṇṭu toṭṭut tamiḻarkaḷ cintittu vaḻipāṭāṟṟip payaṉeyti vantami pulappaṭum. maṟṟum, avaṉ makaṉāvāṉ eṉṟum iḷanala muṭiyaṉākaliṉ 'murukaṉ' eṉappaṭuvāṉ eṉpatum, avaṟkum aṅṅaṉamē viḻāc ceytu vaḻipāṭāṟṟi vantaṉar eṉpatum paṇṭic centamiḻp paṉuval palavaṟṟāṉum peṟappaṭum;
"	ālamar celvaṉ aṇicāṉ makaṉviḻāk
kālkōḷeṉ ṟūkkik katumeṉa nōkki"1
eṉa marutak kaliyuḷ varutaluṅ kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉam kaḷimakaṉ piṉ ṉiṟpār mutal ālamar celvaṉ makaṉviḻāk kālkōḷeṉakkaṇṭu niṟpār iṟutiyākak kūṟiya aṉivarum maṇimēkaliyip puṟañcūḻntu avaḷit  tavaneṟip paṭuttiya tāyi ikaḻntu, 'ivaḷ malarvaṉam pukil āṅkuḷḷa aṉṉaṅkaḷ ivaḷ naṭikku nāṇātu naṭakka vallunakollō, mayilkaḷ ivaḷ cāyalik kaṟpaṉakollō, kiḷikaḷ ivaḷ kiḷavikku eñcalakollō eṉṟivipōlvaṉa colli iṉintuka,
"	kuravamum maravamum kuruntum koṉṟiyum
tilakamum vakuḷamum ceṅkāl veṭciyum
narantamum nākamum parantalar puṉṉiyum
piṭavamum taḷavamum muṭamuṭ ṭāḻiyum
kuṭacamum vetiramum koḻuṅkā lacōkamum
ceruntiyum vēṅkiyum peruñcaṇ pakamum
erimala rilavamum virimalar parappi"2
vittakar iyaṟṟiya cittirappaṭām pōrttatu pōlat tōṉṟiya uva vaṉattic cēvaṭi nilam vaṭuvuṟāmal cutamatiyōṭum maṇimēkali aṭintaṉaḷ; eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷa icciṟu pakutiyiliruntē cāttaṉāriṉ puṉinturi yāṟṟalum,  taṇṭamiḻttiṟalum iṉitu viḷaṅkumāṟu kāṇka. 


20. caṅka nūlkaḷum civamum
pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ caṅkaṅkaḷāl ārāyntu tūymiyuṭaṉ vaḷarkkappeṟṟa perumi tamiḻmoḻikkuriyatu. ataṉālēyē tamiḻik kuṟikkumiṭattuc "caṅkattamiḻ" eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkum ēṟpaṭalāyiṟṟu. muccaṅkattārāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavākiya muttamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum eṇṇiṟantaṉa. vāṉanūl, eṇṇūl, maruntunūl mutaliya kalikaḷum akkālattē ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. ak kalikaḷē yaṉṟi, ici nāṭaka nūlkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉavum iṟantoḻintaṉa. tolkāppiyam, iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal eṉṉum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, eṭṭuttoki, pattup pāṭṭu, patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku eṉṉum ilakkiyaṅkaḷumē ippoḻutu namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa caṅka nūlkaḷākum. ivi yellām iyaṟṟamiḻiṉpāṟpaṭṭavi. tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟamiḻppakuti yaṉittiyum muṟṟa veṭuttu variyaṟi ceyyum viḻumiya muḻu nūlākum. iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal iyaṟṟamiḻiṉ oru pakutiyākiya akapporuḷoṉṟiṉiyē iṉitu viḷakkuvatu. eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭu eṉpaṉa paṟpala pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷic cilamuṟipaṟṟit tokuttu vittavi. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkic cērnta nālaṭiyārum attakiyatē. at tokuppiluḷḷa tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum teyvac centamiḻ nūlit taṉiyāka niṉivu kūrtal vēṇṭum. mēṟkāṭṭiyavi yaṉṟi, maturik kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār eṉṉum caṅkappulavariyaṟṟiya maṇimēkalik kāppiyamum, avarōṭu oru kālattavarum, paraṇar mutaliya caṅkap pulavarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkut tampiyārumākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiya cilappatikāramum caṅkanūlkaḷuṭaṉ cērtteṇṇat takkaṉavākum.
iviyellām tamiḻakattiṉ paṇṭiya varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷeṉa matikkattakkavi. mikap paḻiya nāḷilē tamiḻakattiṉ araciyal nili evvāṟiruntatu, akkālattut tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ vīrattilum, nītiyilum, vaṇmiyilum eṅṅaṉam ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ, aṟṟi nāḷilē camaya nili, camutāya nilikaḷ evvāṟiruntaṉa, vēḷāṇmi, vāṇikam, kittoḻilkaḷ eppaṭi uyarnili aṭintiruntaṉa, āṭavarum makaḷirum eṅṅaṉam kalviyiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavikaḷi aṟintu koḷḷu taṟkuc caṅka nūlkaḷē ciṟanta karuvikaḷākavuḷḷaṉa. ak karuvikaḷ koṇṭu tamiḻakattilē paṇṭu civa camayam enniliyilirunta teṉpataṉi viḷakkuvatē ivvuriyiṉ nōkkam.
iṭik kālattum piṟkālattum tōttiraṅkaḷākavum cāttiraṅkaḷāvum purāṇaṅkaḷākavum camaya nūlkaḷ palappala tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. caṅka vilakkiyaṅkaḷil attakiya nūl oṉṟum kāṇappaṭavilli yeṉpatu uṇmiyē. caṅkakālattil attakiya nūlkaḷ tōṉṟāmikkum, piṉṉar avi tōṉṟiṉamikkum takka kāraṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. oru nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟiyum, annāṭṭu moḻi varalāṟṟiyum nuṇuki ārāyiṉ avviraṇṭum ottiyaṅkutal kāṇappaṭum. eṉavē tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu nāḷaṭivil eytiyirukkum vēṟṟumikaḷi liruntu tamiḻakamāṉatu eṅṅaṉam vēṟṟumiyaṭintu varalāyiṟṟu eṉpatu pulappaṭum eṉka. iṉi, caṅkavilakkiyaṅkaḷilē camaya nūlāka oṉṟum kāṇappaṭāviṭiṉum, akkālattē camaya māṉatu tiṇṇiya niliyilirunta teṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷellām tamiḻukkē ciṟappākavuḷḷa poruḷilak kaṇattiṟku ilakkiyamāka amintavi. aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṉum nāṟporuḷum akamum puṟamu mākiya poruṭ pakutiyil aṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. vētamutaliya kalikaḷellām annāṟ poruḷiyum uṇartatavē yeḻuntaṉa. eṉavē caṅkavilakkiyamaṉittum. camaya cāramākavēyuḷḷaṉa veṉpatu peṟṟām. iṉi, nuṇukiya ārāycci vaḻiyi viṭuttu veḷippaṭiyākavuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷik koṇṭu civa camaya niliyi nōkkutum.
mutukuṭumip  peruvaḻuti eṉṉum  pāṇṭivēntaṉ paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭu muṉ iruntōṉākaliṉ miku toṉmik kālattiṉaṉāvaṉ; avaṉip pāṭiya oru pulavar "niṉkuṭiyāṉatu muṉivarkaḷāṟ paravappaṭum mukkaṭ celvar kōyili valañ ceytaṟkaṇ tāḻakkaṭavatu" eṉṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum cērar peruvēntaṉ civapirāṉ aruḷāṟ piṟantavaṉ eṉṟum, civaperumāṉiyē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭuvōṉ eṉṟum cilappatikāram viḷaṅka vurikkiṉṟatu. cōḻarkula muṉṉōrāṉa maṉu, mucukuntaṉ mutalāṉavarkaḷ civapatti mikkōreṉap purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. karikālaṉ, kōcceṅkaṇṇār eṉpavarkaḷ paṟpala civālayat tiruppaṇikaḷ ceyta civaṉaṭiyārkaḷeṉpatu veḷippaṭi. iṅṅaṉamākat tamiḻakattilē paṇṭu toṭṭuc ceṅkōlōcci vanta muṭiyuṭi vēntarmūvarum vaḻivaḻiyākac civaverumāṉi vaḻipaṭum civa reṉpatu peṟappaṭavē, avarāṭciyuṭ poruntiya tamiḻakattilē taliciṟantu nilaviya camayam civameṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
tokiyilum, kīḻkkaṇakkilum cilappatikāram mutaliya vaṟṟilum civapirāṉ, cevvēḷ, palarāmaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum nāṉku tēvarkaḷ cēraveṭuttuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattilum, maturiyilum, innālvarkkum kōyil kaḷiruntami cilappatikārattāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiliruntu innāṉku tēvarkaḷiyum tamiḻ makkaḷ ciṟappāka vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉareṉpatu peṟappaṭum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum avarkaḷ civaverumāṉiyē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāk koṇṭaṉareṉpatil ettuṇiyum aiyamilli. nālvariyum oruṅkeṭuttuk kūṟumiṭaṅkaḷil piṉmūvariyum vevvēṟu muṟiyākavum kūṟivanta āciriyaṉmāraṉi varum civapirāṉi māttiram yāṇṭum mutaṟkaṇ vittuk kūṟuvatu avarkaḷ koḷkiyi naṉku pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu.
"	piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyilum
aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyilum
vālvaḷi mēṉi vāliyōṉ kōyilum
nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyilum"1
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷōṭu piṟāṇṭu iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa
vaṟṟi ottu nōkki ivvuṇmiyi uṇarka. iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷil āṉēṟu, maḻuppaṭi, mukkaṇ mutaliya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷiyāvatu, niṟattiyāvatu, vēṟu peyariyāvatu koṇṭu civapirāṉik kuṟiyāmal 'piṟavāyākkip periyōṉ' eṉṉum toṭarāl kuṟittiruttal oṉṟē civaperumāṉ oruvarē piṟappillāta mutaṟkaṭavuḷāva reṉṉum uṇmiyi naṉku valiyuṟuttutal kāṇka. vēṟoru teyvam piṟappillāta
tāyiruppiṉ 'piṟavāyākkip periyōṉ' eṉṉun toṭar civaperumāṉukkē celvatākātu. aṅṅaṉam iṉmiyiṉāṟṟāṉ atu civaṉik kuṟippatāyiṟṟeṉka. iṅṅaṉam civapirāṉukkup piṟavāmiyic ciṟappiyalpākak kiḷanteṭuttōtiya āciriyar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ maṟṟōriṭattu iṟavāmiyic ciṟappiyal pākac kūṟiyiruppatum kaṇṭu makiḻtaṟkuriyatu. civa cattiyākiya koṟṟaviyip paravutal kūṟumiṭattu.
"	viṇṇō ramutuṇṭuñ cāva oruvarum
 uṇṇāta nañcuṇ ṭiruntaruḷ ceykuvāy"1
eṉa iṟivaṉ ceyali ēṟṟik kūṟiṉami kāṇka. iṟappiṉit tavirkkum amiḻtuṇṭum ēṉit tēvarellām iṟavāniṟka, iṟappiṉic ceyyum nañciṉiyuṇṭum iṟivaṉ iṟavā tiruntu yāvarkkum aruḷ ceykuvaṉ eṉak kūṟiyirukkum arumippāṭṭi eṅṅaṉam pārāṭṭa vallēm! ivvāṟṟāl piṟavāmiyum iṟavāmiyum ākiya kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyuṭiya mutaṟporuḷ civaperumāṉē eṉṉum uṇmi yātoru taṭiyumiṉṟic caṅka kālattu naṉmakkaḷuḷḷattē nilaviya teṉpatu pōtarum.
"	nīrum nilaṉum tīyum vaḷiyum
māka vicumpō ṭintuṭa ṉiyaṟṟiya
maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ taliva ṉāka
mācaṟa viḷaṅkiya yākkiyar."2
eṉa māṅkuṭi marutaṉār maturik kāñciyilum,
"	nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟiyōṉ mutalāp
pativāḻ catukkattut teyvamī ṟāka"3
eṉak kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār maṇimēkaliyilum civaperumāṉiyē  kaṭavuḷaril mutalvarāka  vittuk  kūṟiyiruttalum kāṇka. itukāṟum kūṟiyaṉavellām oru camayatti uyarttip pēcuṅ karuttuṭaṉ eḻuntaṉa valla eṉpatum, tamiḻakattiṉ paḻiya varalāṟṟuṇmiyi viḷakkavē pōntaṉaveṉpatum nallaṟivuṭiyārkku iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum. camaṇa puttar uḷḷiṭṭa aṉivarum ivvaralāṟ ṟuṇmiyi maṟukkātu ottoḻuki vantami yāṟṟāṉ caṅka kālattilē camayappūcal ciṟitum illātiruntatu. pala camayattiṉarum nērmiyuṭaṉ kalantu vāḻntu vantaṉar. piṟṟi nāḷilē piṟa camayattiṉar paḻiya uṇmikku māṟākat tām tām karutiyavāṟellām urikkavē camayappūcal mikuvatāyiṟṟeṉka.
iṉi, maṇimēkali, camayakkaṇakkar tantiṟaṅkēṭṭa kātiyil, (89-95) civavāti kūṟṟākavuḷḷa,
"	irucuṭa rōṭiya māṉaṉim pūtameṉ
ṟeṭṭu vakiyu muyirum yākkiyumāyk
kaṭṭi niṟpōṉum kaliyuruvi ṉōṉum
paṭittu viḷiyāṭum paṇpi ṉōṉum
tuṭittut tuyartīr tōṟṟat tōṉum
taṉṉil vēṟu tāṉoṉ ṟilōṉum
aṉṉō ṉiṟiva ṉākumeṉ ṟurittaṉaṉ"
eṉṉum pakutiyil iṟivaṉ aṭṭa mūrttiyātalum, muttoḻiṟkum mutalvaṉātalum, eṅkum niṟintavaṉātalum mutaliya civa cittāntak karuttukkaḷ teḷivākak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ caṅka kālattil civa tattuvaṅkaḷum, koḷkikaḷum naṉku uruppeṟṟiruntaṉa veṉpatum peṟappaṭum.


21. iḷaṅkiḷi
aracāṅkattār  veḷiyiṭṭuvarum tamiḻ akarātiyil  (Tamil Lexicon ) iḷaṅkiḷi eṉpataṟkut taṅki eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟkut taṅki eṉpatē poruḷaṉṟu. tampi, taṅki, makaṉ, makaḷ, mittuṉaṉ, tōḻaṉ, māṇākkaṉ mutaliya iḷañcuṟṟattiṉar palariyum itu kuṟippatākum.
"	ēvātu māṟṟu miḷaṅkiḷiyum" 	(tirikaṭukam)1
eṉpatil makkaḷ eṉṉum poruḷilum,
	cīla maṟivā ṉiḷaṅkiḷi		(tirikaṭukam)2
eṉpatil māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum poruḷilum,
	mālavaṟ kiḷaṅkiḷi" 		(cilappatikāram)3
eṉpatil taṅki yeṉṉum poruḷilum,
"	eḻumiyum peṟu viṉṉa iḷaṅkiḷic
cuṟṟameṉṟāḷ" 			(cīvakacintāmaṇi)4
eṉpatil mittuṉaṉ eṉṟa poruḷilum ittoṭar vantuḷḷami kāṇka.
‘ēvātu māṟṟu miḷaṅkiḷiyum' eṉpatil itu makkaḷ eṉṉum poruḷēyaṉṟit tampi yeṉṉum poruḷum payappa tākum. iṉi,
"	iḷaṅkiḷi yārūraṉ" 		(tēvāram)5
eṉa nampiyārūrar tammik kūṟik koḷḷutaliṉ, itu tōḻaṉ, toṇṭaṉ eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum payappatākum. palarālum payilappeṟṟuvarum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil iṅṅaṉam virinta poruḷuṭiyatākap payiṉṟuvarum toṭarik kuṟukiya poruḷuḷḷatākak koṇṭu, perum poruṭcelavāṉum  pala pēraṟivāḷarkaḷiṉ uḻippāṉum veḷivarum aracāṅka veḷiyīṭṭakarātiyil ‘taṅki' eṉṟu poruḷ kuṟittiruppatu mikka viyappit tarukiṉṟatu. kuṟukiya kāla velliyil virintu muṭikkuṅ karuttāl ittakiya tavaṟukaḷ nērvaṉapōlum!



22. puṟanāṉūṟṟil kaṇṭa cila paḻiya 
vaḻakkukaḷum varalāṟukaḷum
caṅkap pulavarkaḷālē pāṭittokukkappeṟṟu namakku eyppiṉil vippākak kiṭittirukkum eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya puṟanāṉūṟṟi ārāyiṉ ettaṉiyō pala vaḻakku
kaḷum varalāṟukaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila iṅkē kāṭṭappaṭum.
kārikiḻār eṉṉum pulavar mutukuṭumipperu vaḻuti eṉṉum paḻiya pāṇṭi vēntaṉi nōkki,
"	paṇiyiya rattiniṉ kuṭiyē muṉivar
 mukkaṭ celvar nakarvalañ ceyaṟkē"1
eṉa aṟivuṟuttatiliruntu, akkālattu vēntarkaḷ kaṇṇutaṟ perumāṉ tirukkōyili valam varumpoḻutu tam kuṭiyit tāḻac ceyvar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. aracarkaḷ paṭikkalaṅ kaḷukku neypūci mayiṟṟōki aṇintu māli cūṭṭi vippa reṉpatu, atiyamāṉ añciyiṭamiruntu toṇṭimāṉuḻit tūtu ceṉṟa oḷaviyār avaṉ paṭikkalakkoṭṭilip pārttu,
"	ivvē, pīliyaṇintu māli cūṭṭik
kaṇṭiraḷ nōṉkāḻ tirutti ney yaṇintu
kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉaka ravvē"2
eṉṟu kūṟiyataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ tam vīramuraciṉi malaraṇi poruntiya kaṭṭilil vittiruppareṉpatum, ammuraciṉi mayiṟ pīliyum uḻiñimāliyum mutaliyavaṟṟāl alaṅkarittu nīrāṭṭi varuvareṉpatum peruñcēralirum poṟiyi mōcikīraṉār pāṭiya ruya-ām puṟappāṭṭil,
"	olineṭum pīli yoṇpoṟi maṇittār
polaṅkuḻi yuḻiñiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik
kuruti vēṭki yurukeḻu muracam
maṇṇi vārā vaḷavi yeṇṇey
nurimukan taṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkki
aṟiyā tēṟiya veṉṉi"1
eṉa varuvatāl aṟiyalākum. aracarkaḷ pōril iṟavātu nōyāl iṟappiṉ avarkaḷ tuṟakkam pukavēṇṭiya aṉṉa varuṭampit taruppiyiṟ kiṭatti, vāḷāṟ piḷantu aṭakkañ ceytal marapeṉpatu oḷaviyār atiyamāṉip pāṭiya 'tiṇpiṇi muracam' eṉṉum pāṭṭil.
"	tiṟampuri pacumpuṟ parappiṉar kiṭappi
vāḷpōḻn taṭakkalu muyntaṉar"2
eṉak kūṟappaṭutalāl aṟiyalākum. pōriliṟanta vīraṉatu peyarum pīṭum eḻutiya naṭukalli niṟuvi ataṟku mayiṟpīli cūṭṭi, avviṭattup puṭiviyālē ciṟiya pantar pōṭuvittaluṇ
ṭeṉpatu, 'vaḷarattoṭiṉum' eṉṉum pāṭṭilē
"	peyarē maṭañcāṉ maññi yaṇimayir cūṭṭi
iṭam piṟar koḷḷāc ciṟuvaḻip
paṭañcey pantark kaṉmici yatuvē"3
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭutalāṟ pōtarum. iṟantōṉukku avaṉ maṉivi kōmayattāl meḻukiya ciṟiya iṭattil taruppiyiṉ mēṟpiṇṭam vippaḷeṉpatu 'nōkōyāṉē'4 'katir mūkkāral'5 eṉṉum pāṭalkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. 'arunariyurumiṉ' (puṟam:58) eṉpataṉāl iṭi veṇṇiṟamuṭiya teṉṉum koḷkiyum,
"	poyyā veḻili peyviṭam nōkki
muṭṭi koṇṭu vaṟpulañ cērum
ciṟunuṇ ṇeṟumpiṉ cilloḻuk kēyppa"6
eṉpataṉāl eṟumpu muṭṭi koṇṭu tiṭṭiyēṟiṉ maḻi peyyumeṉṉum koḷkiyum, 'kaṭavuḷālattu'7 'kaḷḷi nīḻaṟ kaṭavuḷ'8 eṉpavaṟṟāl ālamarattiṉ kaṇṇum kaḷḷi niḻalilum teyvamuṟiyumeṉṉum koḷkiyum annāḷiliruntami peṟappaṭum.
"putalvar pūṅkaṇ mutti"1
eṉpataṉāl kuḻantikaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷil muttamiṭutaluṇ ṭeṉpatum,
"	putalvaṉ - aḻakkaṇṭu
maṟappuliyurittu matiyaṅkāṭṭiyum"2
eṉpataṉāl puli varukiṉṟateṉak kūṟi accuṟuttiyum, ampuli kāṭṭiyum kuḻantikaḷiṉ aḻuki tīrppareṉpatum peṟṟām. cūluṟṟa makaḷir maṇṇiṉi virumpi yuṇpa reṉpatu
"	vayavuṟu makaḷir vēṭṭuṇi ṉallatu
pakiva ruṇṇā arumaṇ ṇiṉiyē"3
eṉpataṉālum, kuṟavarkaḷ naṉṉāḷilē tiṉi vitittalum, muṟpaṭa viḷinta katiri naṉṉāḷiṟ koytu vantu putituṇṭalum uṭiyareṉpatu
"	naṉṉāḷ varupata nōkkik kuṟavar
uḻāatu vittiya parūukkural ciṟutiṉi
muntuviḷi yāṇar nāṭputi tuṇmār"4
eṉpataṉālum viḷaṅkum. iḷañciṟārkaḷ nīrukkaṇittākap paṭinta marak kōṭṭilēṟi āḻamākiya maṭuviṟ pāyntu muḻuki maṇalik koṇarntu kāṭṭi viḷiyāṭuvareṉpatu,
"	nīrnaṇip paṭikō ṭēṟic cīrmikak
kariyavar maruḷat tiriyakam pitira
neṭunīrk kuṭṭattut tuṭumeṉap pāyntu
kuḷittu maṇaṟkoṇṭa kallā viḷami"5
eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. kuṟavarkaḷ kaṭavuṭkup pali tūvi vaḻipaṭṭu maḻiyi vēṇṭum poḻutu aḻittalum vēṇṭāvaḻi oḻittaluñ ceyvareṉpatu
"	malivāṉ koḷkeṉa vuyarpali tūuy
māriyāṉṟu maḻimēk kuyarkeṉak
kaṭavuṭ pēṇiya kuṟavar mākkaḷ
peyalkaṇ māṟiya vuvakiyar"6
eṉpataṉāṟ pōtarum
"	viḷipatac cīṟiṭa nōkki"1
eṉṉum pāṭṭāl puli iṭappakkattu vīḻnta vilaṅkiṉi uṇṇā teṉṉuṅ koḷkiyum,
"	pōḻvāyk kūki
cuṭṭuk kuviyeṉac cettōrp payirum"2
eṉpataṉāl pērānti cettōri aḻikkumeṉṉuṅ koḷkiyum uṭiya reṉpatu peṟappaṭum. paṭikkalap payiṟci ceyvōr murukka marattāṟ ceyta tūṇiṉi ilakkāka niṟutti atiṟpaṭikkalaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkip payilvareṉpatu
"	iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭi kaṉmār
ikaliṉa reṟinta akalili murukkiṉ
perumarak kampam pōla"3
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. iṉṉum vaṭakkiruttal, iṟantōric cuṭutal, tāḻiyāṟ kavittal, kaṇavaṉiyiḻanta makaḷir tīppāytal, kimmi nōṉpu nōṟṟal mutalāya pala vaḻakkaṅkaḷ puṟappāṭṭuk
kaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum varalāṟukaḷ piṉvarum.


23. āciriyarum māṇākkarum
ariya maṉitap piṟappiyaṭintiruppavarkaḷ tām tām piṟanta tēcattāṉum, pēcum moḻiyāṉum, piṟavaṟṟāṉum, vēṟu vēṟu kūṭṭattavarākap pakukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. akkūṭṭat
tavaruḷḷum, tamtam aṟivu, toḻil mutaliyavikaḷāṟ ciṟappu mēmpāṭuṟṟu, avvacciṟappiṟkum, avi nikaḻum avacarattiṟkum takkavāṟu, palvēṟu ciṟappup peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭupavar uṇṭu. pala kalikaḷiyum kaṟṟuṇarntavarkaḷāyt tām aṟin tavikaḷip piṟarum aṟintu payaṉeytumāṟu aruḷ curantūṭṭu pavarkaḷ, 'āciriyar' eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarkku uriyarākiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tām aṟintavikaḷi nūl iyaṟṟutalāṉum, uri eḻututalāṉum, nēriṟ pōtittalāṉum, piṟarkku urimip paṭutti muṟiyē 'nūlāciriyar', 'uriyāciyar', 'pōtakāciriyar' eṉṉum peyarkku uriyarākiṉṟaṉar. 'nūluri pōtakāciriyar mūvarum' eṉpatu ilakkaṇak kottu. ivarkaḷaṉṟi, ulakil tīviṉi taliyeṭukkuṅ kālaṅkaḷiṟṟōṉṟi, ataṉi aḻittut tarumatti niliniṟuttuvōrkaḷum, camayastāpaṉañ ceytu āṉmañāṉam allatu patiñāṉattip paravacceyvōrkaḷum, taṉittaṉi āṉmākkaḷiṉ paripāka kālattil veḷippaṭṭut, tīkṣiyāṉum upatēcattāṉum āṉma cuttiyiyum āṉma lāpattiyum aruḷuvōrkaḷum kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi vāynta 'āciriya'rākiṉṟaṉar.
āciriyar;
yāṉ, ivaṇ āciriyareṉak karutiyatu pōtakāciriyariyē. neṭuṅkaṇakkumutal, peruṅkalikaḷ vari eppāṭattiyum ovvoruvarkkēṉum ovvoru tokutiyāyuḷḷavarkkēṉum pōtippavaraṉivarum, pōtakāciriyarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ 'upāttiyāyar' eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. 'ōtuvittaṟ kaṇ utaviyāyuḷḷavarkaḷ' eṉṉum poruḷ paṭum 'upa - attiyayaṉar' eṉṉun toṭar, collum poruḷum ciṟitu tirintu 'upāttiyār, uvāttiyāyar' eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
parata kaṇṭattavarākiya nam muṉṉōrkaḷ, kalviyē makkaḷukkuk kaṇṇeṉak karutip pōṟṟi vantuḷḷārkaḷ. kuḻantikaṭku mikka iḷampirāyattil vittiyārampañ ceyvittali, iṉṟiyamiyāta oru caṭaṅkākavum nammavar koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉaraṉṟō! 'iḷimiyiṟ kal' (ātti:29) 'vitti virumpu' (ātti:100) 'eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇṇeṉat takum' (koṉṟivēntaṉ:7) eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa namnāṭṭu mūturikaḷākum.
ittamiḻnāṭṭil mikap paḻaṅkālantoṭṭē ciṟupaḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷum, periya kalācālikaḷum, kalvic caṅkaṅkaḷum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. akkālattil upāttiyāyar, 'kaṇakkāyar' eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉar.  'kaṟṟatūumiṉṟik kaṇakkāyar pāṭattāl' eṉpatu nālaṭiyār. (31) caṅkap pulavarkaḷuḷ palarāṉum aṟiyappaṭum perumi vāynta 'nakkīraṉār'kkut tantiyāṉavar maturiyilirunta ōr upāttiyāyarāṉa kāraṇattāl,  'maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār' eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭār. maṇikēlik kāppiyattāl ēṟakkuṟiya iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil vittiyāmaṇṭapa mutaliyaṉa iruntami pulaṉākiṉṟatu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa varuṭaṅkaḷ caṅkamiruntu tamiḻi vaḷarttatu ulakap piracittam. kavic cakkaravarttiyākiya kampar, tamatu irāmāyaṇattil kōcala nāṭṭi varuṇikkumiṭattu.
"	pantiṉi iḷiyavar payiliṭam mayilūr kantaṉi aṉiyavar kaliteri kaḻakam"1
eṉṟu kūṟiyatu, annāṭṭiṟkum poruntuvatāyiṉum, tamiḻakattiṉ ñāpakattāl aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāreṉak koḷḷalum tavaṟākātu. itaṉāl takka paruvam varap peṟṟa vāliparkaḷ ciṟanta kali kaḷip payiṉṟuvanta kalācālikaḷ ipparata pūmiyil akkālattiliruntami pulaṉākiṉṟatu. pala colli eṉ? ayōttiyāṉatu, 'kalviyākiya orē muḷi muḷitteḻuntu, kēḷviyākiya kavaṭukaḷip pōkkit, tavamākiya cākan taḻittu, aṉpākiya arumpu arumpi, tarumamākiya malar malarntu, pōkamākiya paḻam paḻuttatu pōlum, (nakarap:75) eṉparēl, akkālattuk kalviyāṉatu evvaḷavu makōṉṉata niliyil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum?
paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷil mukavuriyāka upāttiyāyar iyalpum avar pāṭam pōtikkum muṟimiyum, māṇākkariyalpum, avar pāṭaṅkēṭkum muṟimiyum, viḷakkamuṟa eḻutappaṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. innāṉkilakkaṇamum cērnta mukavuriyāṉatu 'potuppāyiram' eṉappaṭum. naṉṉūlāciriyar, nūliṉ ilakkaṇattiyum,  potup pāyirattiṟ (nūṟpā:3) cērttiruk kiṟār. avarum, avarkku muṉṉākap piṉṉākavuḷḷavarkaḷum kūṟiyirukkum 'āciriyar-māṇākkariyalpu'kaḷip paṭikkuṅkāl, elliyillāta viyappum uvappum namakku uṇṭākum naṉṉūlāciriyar 'naṟkuṭip piṟappu, cīvakāruṇyamum, kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭum, pala nūlkaḷiyum kaṟṟut tērnta aṟiviṉ teḷivum, pōtaṉācaktiyum, ulakiyalaṟivum mutalākiya uyarkuṇaṅkaḷi uṭiyaṉātalōṭu, poṟi mutaliyavaṟṟāl puviyiṉiyum, uyarcci mutaliyavaṟṟāl maliyiṉiyum, naṭuvunili mutaliyavaṟṟāl niṟikōliyum, mukamalarcci mutaliyavaṟṟāl malariṉiyum ottu viḷaṅkupavaṉē āciriyaṉāvāṉ,' (nūṟ:26) eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
puṟattiṇiyiyalil 'eṭṭu vaki nutaliya aviyattāṉum' eṉpataṟku, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṭṭiyuḷḷa ōr ceyyuḷāṉatu aiyaṉ āritaṉār kūṟiya aviyamāntarilak kaṇattōṭum, āttirēyaṉ pērāciriyaṉ kūṟiya āciriya ilakkaṇattōṭum karuttorumiyuṟṟu, aḻakupeṟa uruvakañ ceyyappaṭṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. 'kuṭippiṟappākiya uṭiyiyuṭuttu, nūluṇar vākiya malaric cūṭi, nalloḻukkamākiya aṇiyip pūṇṭu, cattiyamākiya uṇaviyuṇṭu, tūymiyākiya ātaṉattamarntu, naṭuvu niliyākiya nakariṟṟaṅki, aḻukkāṟiṉmi, avāviṉmi yeṉkiṟa iruperu nitiyattiyum mēṉmēlum īṭṭukiṉṟa tōlā nāviṉiyuṭiya mēlōriṉ pēravikkaṇ orunāḷ cērntiruttal kiṭṭumāyiṉ yāṉ makiḻvuṭaṉ peṟuvēṉāka, pulavu nāṟuvatāy niliyaṟṟatāyuḷḷa yākkiyuṭaṉ porunti, ulakiṉkaṇ cuḻalā niṉṟa ippiṟaviyiṉi." (āciriyamāli-puṟattiraṭṭu-aviyaṟital)
ivaṟṟāṉellām, āciriyareṉpōr, paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyum vāṇikariyum, tām peṟum campaḷattiṟkēṟpa vēli ceyyum kūlit toḻilāḷar allatu uttiyōkastariyum pōṉṟavarallareṉpatum, puṉita cintiyum, puṉitavoḻukka muṭiyavarāyt tammic cārntāriyellām puṉitarākki vāḻvikkum pēraruḷāḷar eṉpatum viḷakkamākiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, āciriyarāvār paṉṉūluṅ kaṟṟaṟiruntavarātalōṭu ulakattōṭu porunta naṭattalilum vallarātal vēṇṭum. tām kaṟṟuṇarnta arum poruḷkaḷip piṟar eḷitiluṇarumāṟu pōtikkun tiṟaṉuṭiyarātal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl, avar kompiṉiṭattu naṉṟākap pūttiruntum, maṇam vīcutalillāta malariyoppāreṉac centāppōtār kūṟukiṉṟār. āciriyaṉ pāṭaṅkaṟpikkuṅkāl, tāṉ kaṟpikkap pōkum viṣayaṅkaḷi maṉattilamittuk koṇṭu, viriyāmalum, vekuḷāmalum, piriyattuṭaṉum, mukamalarcciyuṭaṉum kaṟpittal vēṇṭum. tammuṭiya teḷivum iṉimiyum poruntiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa  coṟkaḷiṉāl māṇākkarkaḷi vacīkarittu, avarkaḷukku mēlum mēlum kēṭka vēṇṭumeṉṉum ārvattiyum maṉaveḻucciyiyum uṇṭu paṇṇutal āciriyarkku iṉṟiyamiyāta ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷil oṉṟu. tamatu pēcuntiṟamiyoṉṟiyē nōkkātu, kēṭpāratu nilimiyaṟintu collit tām collu kiṉṟavikaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṉṟārkaḷāveṉak kavaṉittalum, ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilliyeṉat terintāl, ataṉ kāraṇattiyaṟintu, avarkaḷ ēṟkum vitattāl mīṭṭuñ collalum, māṇākkarkaḷ aiyuṟṟu viṉāviyavikaḷukku iṉitiṉ viṭi kūṟi aiyattip pōkkutalum, āciriyar kaṭami yākum. māṇākkarkaḷ aiyuṟṟu, viṉāviyavikaḷukku uttaraṅ kūṟāmalum, avarkaḷ avvappoḻutu nērum aiyappāṭṭiṉit tārāḷamākat terivittuk koḷḷutaṟku iṭaṅkoṭāmalum, tamatu kōramāṉa tōṟṟattālum vaṉcollālum accuṟutti avarkaṭku ēlāta taṇṭaṅkaḷiyellām vitittu, aṉṉār pāl aṉpiṉṟiyēyoḻukupavarkaḷ, māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivu vaḷarccikkup piratipantamāka vanta picācaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, āciriyarkaḷāka māṭṭārkaḷ. makkaḷukkiyaṟkiyaṟivāṉatu muṟpiṟappi yoṭṭiyiruttaliṉ, ippiṟappiṉ vayataḷaviṟkēṟpa aṟiviruttal vēṇṭumeṉum niyamamilli. immuṟippaṭi māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyariṉum aṟivu mikkuṭiyarāy orōvaḻit tamatu cāturiya aṟivi veḷippaṭuttaluṅ kūṭum;  aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, āciriyarāyiṉār avaratu aṟiviṟku viyantu makiḻntu iṉpuṟutalē takkatākum.
āciriyarāṉavar, kuṟippiṭṭayātāṉumoru pāṭattip pōtippavarāka māttiramaṉṟi māṇākkarkaḷukku uyarnalamuḷḷa potuvuṇarcciyi uṇṭupaṇṇi, avaratu potunala vāḻkkikkuk kāraṇarāyiruttal vēṇṭum. curuṅkac colliṉ, māṇākkarpāl aṉpum anutāpamuṭiyarāy avaratu ikavāḻvu paravāḻvukaḷil tammic campantappaṭuttik koḷvōrē uṇmiyāṉa āciriyarāvar.
upāttiyāyarkaḷ nalloḻukka neṟiyiṉiṉṟum vilakāmal mutalil tammik kāttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. eṉṉi? avar, tamatu pōtaṉiyāl māttiramaṉṟu; tamatu naṭattikaḷiṉālum, tammic cūḻntuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷiyum piṟariyum nalvaḻip paṭutta vēṇṭum. 'māntarkku iṉattiyalpatākum aṟivu' (kuṟaḷ:452) ātaliṉ, palakālum āciriyaṉi aṭuttu iruntu, avaṉatu pōtaṉiyi ēṟṟu varum māṇākkarkaḷukku āciriyaṉatu kuṇamum ceyalum paṟṟāmaṟpōkā. 'cirēṣṭaṉāṉavaṉ etaṉi ācarikkiṉṟāṉō  ataṉi itararkaḷum ācarikkiṉṟārkaḷ,' avaṉ etaṉip piramāṇamākak koḷḷukiṉṟāṉō, ataṉi ulakamum koḷḷukiṉṟatu", eṉak kaṇṇapirāṉ kūṟiyapaṭi, cūḻntuḷḷavarkaḷ avaṉippiṉpaṟṟi naṭakka muyalukiṉṟārkaḷ. āka, oru āciriyaṉ tavaṟi naṭappāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ tāṉ keṭuva taṉṟi, ulakattiyum keṭuppavaṉākiṉṟāṉ.
muṟkālattu āciriyarkaḷ aṉpē uruveṭuttāṟ pōṉṟavar kaḷāyk, kuṉṟā oḻukkamuṭiyarāyirunta kāraṇattālaṉṟō, peṟṟōrkaḷ tam kuḻantikaḷi nampikkiyuṭaṉum vicuvācat tuṭaṉum kurukkaḷiṭam oppittaṉar; kuḻantikaḷum āciriyaṉē tañcamākak kurukulavācañ ceytu vantārkaḷ. kurukkaḷum kuḻantikaṭku uṭaṟpiṇi uṇṭākāmal pātukāttum, uyirp piṇiyākiya aññāṉattik keṭuttum, cuka vāḻvaḷittu, avarkaṭkut tāy tantiyākavum, maruttuvaṉākavum, teyvamē yākavum viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. tacaratarāṉavar, tam kuḻantikaḷāṉa irāmaleṭcumaṇarik kucikariṭam oppuvikkum poḻutu, 'nal tanti nī' taṉittāyum nī ivarkku, enti tantaṉaṉ; iyinta ceyka', eṉak kūṟiviṭuttaṉar. maṟṟum kucikar, irāma laṭcumaṇarkaḷiṉ pūraṇamāṉa yōkkiyatikku anukuṇamāka viruntōr avarkaṭkuk kuruvākiya vaciṭṭarē eṉpataṉi,
"	tiri yōṭum araciṟiñcuñ ceṟikaḻaṟkāl tacarataṉām
poṟiyōṭun toṭarmaṉattāṉ putalvareṉum peyarē kāṇ 
uṟiyōṭu neṭuvēlāy upanayaṉa vitimuṭittu
maṟiyōtu vittivari vaḷarttāṉum vaciṭṭaṉkāṇ"1
eṉṟu jaṉakaṉiṭattiṟ kūṟiyirukkum arumippāṭṭip paṭikkumpoḻutu nam uḷḷam kaḷi tatumpukiṉṟatu.
māṇākkar;
iṉi, muṟkūṟiya colaṟkaruñ ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya nallā ciriyaṉiyaṭuttu, vaḻipāṭu mutaliyaṉa kuṟivaṟap purintu, pāṭaṅ kēṭpōrē māṇākkarāvar. nallāciriyaṉiyaṭuttuk kēṭṭila rāyiṉ, avaratu kalvi, kēṭṭaliṉāl ām attuṇi uṟuti yuṭittāka māṭṭātu. 'kuruvilārkku vitti illi' yeṉpatu  poyyākātu. 'uṭiyārmuṉ illārpōl ēkkaṟṟuṅ kaṟṟār' (kuṟaḷ:595) uyarntōrēyeṉpatu. poyyā moḻiyākaliṉ, āciriyarkku evvaḷavum kīḻppaṭintu ūḻiyañ ceytālum, aviyellām māṇākkaratu uyarccikkuk kāraṇamē tavira vēṟilli. cōmpal, maṟati, māṟupāṭu, vañcam, māṉam, ciṉam mutaliya tīkkuṇaṅkaḷuṭiyōrum, tīt toḻil purivōrum, māṇākkarāka māṭṭārkaḷ. māṇākkarkaḷ, kalviyil niṟinta viruppamum ūkkamumuṭiyarātal vēṇṭum.
maḻittuḷiyāṉatu muttuccippiyiṉ vāyil viḻuntāl muttākap pariṇamikkiṉṟatu. tāmari iliyil viḻuntāl viḻunta aḷaviṉatāka irukkiṉṟatu. paḻukkak kāycciya irumpil viḻuntāl cuvaṟiviṭukiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉamē āciriyaṉ kaṟpikkum nūṟporuḷ, uttama māṇākkaric cārntāl vicēṣa nalattuṭaṉ veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu; mattimaric cārntāl avvaḷavil niṟkiṉṟatu. atamaric cārntāl payaṉiṉṟi aḻintu viṭukiṉṟatu.
nīrip pirittup pālip parukum aṉṉam pōṉṟu, āciriyaṉ kaṟpittavaṟṟil nalamuḷḷa poruḷkaḷip pakuttu maṉattilamittuk koḷvōrum, uṇavu kiṭittaviṭattu vayiṟāra mēyntu, piṉ ciṟitu ciṟitāka vāyiṟkoṇarntu cīraṇippikkum pacuvip pōṉṟu, mikutiyāṉa poruḷkaḷik kēṭṭuk koṇṭu, piṉ avaṟṟic ciṟitu ciṟitākat tam kavaṉattiṟkuk koṇṭu varuvōrum uttamarkaḷāvārkaḷ. pāṭaṅkēṭku muṟiyip paṟṟi naṉṉūlāciriyar kūṟuvatu
"	kōṭal marapē kūṟuṅ kālip,
poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭal muṉiyāṉ,
kuṇattoṭu paḻaki, avaṉ kuṟippiṟ cārntu,
"iru"eṉa iruntu  col eṉac collip,
parukuvaṉ aṉṉa ārvatta ṉākic
cittirap pāviyiṉ attakavu aṭaṅkic,
cevivā yāka, neñcukaḷaṉ ākak,
kēṭṭavi kēṭṭavi viṭātuḷat tamittup,
'pō'eṉap pōtal eṉmaṉār pulavar"1
eṉpatu. māṇākkarkaḷ, kēṭṭa pāṭaṅkaḷic cintittup pōṟṟalum, nūl vaḻakku, ulaka vaḻakkukaḷi ārāyntaṟitalum, tam pōṉṟa māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ payilutalum avaciyamākum. kaṟṟārpāl tām kaṟṟavikaḷi ēṟkumāṟu colli, tammiṉ mikutiyākak kaṟṟavariṭattiruntu ammikutiyāṉa poruḷkaḷik koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. āciriyaṉpāl aṉpum accamum uṭiyārāy akalāmalum, mika neruṅkāmalum, niḻal pōṟṟoṭarntu,  mariyātiyuṭaṉ oḻukutal vēṇṭum. maṟṟum āciriyarkku, 'uṟṟuḻi utaviyum, uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum, piṟṟinili muṉiyātum' kaṟṟal naṉṟē; (puṟam:183) nūṟporuḷēyāyiṉum vaḻipāṭṭumuṟiyāṉaṉṟi, vañcaṉiyāṟ koḷḷakkarututal atipātakamākum. tampāluḷḷa piḻikaḷit tīrttaṟ poruṭṭum, tamakku nallaṟivu koḷuttaṟ poruṭṭum, āciriyaṉ orōvaḻi iṭittuk kūṟutalum, taṇṭittalum kūṭum. appoḻutu avi tam naṉmiyiṉimittameṉak karutit tiruntaluṟuvōrē vivēka muḷḷa piḷḷikaḷāvār.
āciriyaṉ kaṟpikkum arumporuḷiyum eḷitiṉ uṇarntu koḷvataṉālēyē avaṉatu  aṉpip peṟṟuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. "neñcuṟa aruṅkalikaḷ kaṟpavarkaḷ tammaḷavil nēyamilātavarkaḷ yār?"
māṇavarkaḷukkuk kuru paktiyāṉatu, ellāvaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟantatu. 'eḻuttaṟivittavaṉ iṟivaṉākum' aṉṟō? oru kuḻanti iyalpākavē taṉ tāyiyum, piṉ tāyuṇarttat tantiyiyum, tantiyuṇarttak kuruviyum, kuruvuṇarttat teyvattiyum aṟiyum muṟipaṟṟi, 'mātā pitā, kuru, teyvam' eṉa vaḻaṅkuvarāyiṉar. itaṉāl kuruvāṉavar, teyvattiṟkaṭutta paṭiyiluḷḷāreṉat terikiṟatu. aṉṟi, 'mātā, pitā, kuru' eṉṟa mūvarum teyvamāvar eṉa ittoṭarkkup poruḷ colliṉum poruntum, aracaṉum upāttiyāyaṉum, tāyum, tantiyum, tamiyaṉum kuravarkaḷāvārkaḷeṉṟum, ivarkaḷit teyvamākak karutit toḻa vēṇṭumeṉṟum nam nūlkaḷ virikkiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭuc ciṟuvarkaḷ cuvaṭiyiyeṭuttup paṭikkat toṭaṅkum poḻutum, paṭittu niṟuttum poḻutum, 'kuruvāḻka, kuruvētuṇi' eṉṟu kūṟivanta vaḻakkam namatu kurupaktiyi evvaḷavu teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṟatu! kuruninti ceyvōriṉum atipātakar yārumilli.
"	ōreḻut toruporuḷ urikkak kūṭiya
cīreḻut tāḷaric citiyac ceppiṉōr
ūriṭik cuṇaṅkaṉāyp piṟantu aṅ(ku) ōrukam
pāriṭik kirumiyāyp paṭivar maṇṇilē"1
eṉṟu tavarāja yōkikaḷāṉa tirumūlar kūṟukiṉṟār. āciriyaṉ ovvoṉṟiyum taṉakkuk kaṟpikkāviṭiṉum āciriyaṉpāṟ celuttum aṉpu mikutiyāṉē ellāk kaliyuṇarvum eḷitiṉ vantuviṭum. ēkalivaṉ turōṇa vikkirakatti aṉpuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭ ṭaṉṟō oppuyarvaṟṟa villāḷiyāṉāṉ? utaṅkaṉ mutaliyōratu kurupakti, makāpāratattāl evvaḷavu koṇṭāṭappaṭukiṟatu! kuruvākkiya paripālaṉamē māṇākkarkaḷukku uṇmiyāṉa aṟamākum. tāṭakiyiṉ koṭumikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟi, avaḷik kollumāṟu kucikar paṇittavaḷavil, muṉpu avaḷip peṇṇeṉa niṉintu otuṅkiyirunta peruntakiyākiya irāmaṉ, avarkku etir kūṟumāṟu kampar kūṟuvatu,
"	aiyaṉ aṅkatu kēṭ(ṭu) aṟaṉ allavum
eyti ṉālatu ceykaveṉ ṟēviṉāl
meyya niṉṉuri vētam eṉakkoṭu
ceyki yaṉṟōaṟañ ceyyumā ṟeṉṟāṉ,"2
eṉpatuṅ kāṇka,
māṇākkarkaḷ, āciriyaṉ tirumuṉ, vāymiyuṭaṉum ācārat tuṭaṉum oḻukutal vēṇṭum. tām kalvi payilum niliyiṉ nīṅki evvaḷavu uyarnta pataviyi yeytiya kālattum, tam āciriyari niṉittu urukutalum, avarkaḷ etirppaṭiṉ aḷavilaṉpuṭaṉ upacarittalum, tam nalvāḻviṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kaḷākum. vaṉavācaṅ kaḻittuvanta pāṇṭavaṉāṉa arccuṉaṉ, virāṭa nakarppuṟattu nirimīṭkap pōrkkōlam tāṅki vanta poḻutu,  turiyōtaṉaṉ paṭiyil niṉṟa taṉatu āciriyarkaṭku mutalil astira vāyilākat taṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṉit terivittuk koṇṭatum, turōṇar taṉṉuṭaṉ pōr puriya vantaṭutta
poḻutu,
"	yātumoṉ ṟaṟiyāeṉṉi ivaṉalā(tu) iliyeṉṟinta
mētiṉi matikkumāṟu vilmutaṟ paṭikaḷ yāvum
tītaṟat tanta uṇmit teyvamnī eṉṟāl, pañca
pātakan taṉṉiloṉ(ṟu)uṉ patayukam piḻippa(tu) aiyā"3
'eṉa aṉpu mayamāṉa coṟkaḷic collip pōr ceyya maṟuttatum, oppuyarvaṟṟa villāḷiyum civapakti cirōmaṇiyumāṉa avvaruccuṉaṉatu kurupaktiyiṉ mēṉmiyi viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
cila āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ naṭipeṟṟa kalkattā yūṉivarsiṭiyiṉ paṭṭamaḷipput tirunāḷ  oṉṟil vaṅkāḷa kavarṉarākiya lārṭu kārmikkēl, māṇākkarkaḷukkuk kuru nintiyāl varuntīṅkiyum kurupaktiyiṉ mēṉmiyiyum namatu manutarma cāstirattiliruntu eṭuttukkāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttiyatum, namatu irājappiratinitiyā
yiruntu iṅkilāntu ceṉṟa lārṭu hārṭiñcu, pampāy yūṉivarsiṭiyār tamakku 'el.el.ṭi. paṭṭam cūṭṭiyapoḻutu 'māṇākkarkaḷ taṉṉaṭakkamum taṉṉala maṟuppum uṭiyarāy viḷaṅkavēṇṭu' meṉṟu parintu pēciyatum, nam tēyattu māṇākkar ovvoruvar maṉattilum vērūṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ippoḻutu māṇākkarkaḷāyiruppōrkaḷē iṉip periyōrkaḷātaliṉ, ittēcattiṉ uṉṉata vāḻviṟkuk kāraṇarāka iruppavarkaḷ ivarkaḷē.
potu;
āciriyarum māṇākkarkaḷum oruvarukkoruvar aṉpuṭiyarāy, parōpakāra cintiyuṭiyarāy irupparēl, ivvulakam evvaḷavu mēṉili yaṭintuviṭum. "ovvoru ciṟanta muyaṟciyum tam poruṭṭāka illāmal piṟar poruṭṭākavē muyalum perumiyuṭiyōr iruttaliṉāṟṟāṉ, ivvulakam nilipeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu! eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟil" 1 oru periyavar kūṟuvatic cintittoḻukuka.


24. intiyi intiyāvukkup 
potumoḻiyākkutal poruntātu
araciyal kiḷarcci kāraṇamāka intiyanāṭu muḻutukkum intiyip potu moḻiyākka vēṇṭum eṉṉum koḷki yoṉṟu tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭu, ataṉ poruṭṭuc cila āṇṭu kaḷāka valiya muyaṟcikaḷum ceyyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ulakilēyē periyārāka viḷaṅkum kāntiyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ ātaravi ṉāl immuyaṟci mikka valimiyum ākkamum peṟṟu varukiṟatu. innāṭu urimi peṟutaṟkuriya cātaṉaṅkaḷil 'hinti' potu moḻiyāvatum oṉṟākum eṉṉum ciṟanta eṇṇaṅkoṇṭē kāntiyaṭikaḷ pōlvār itaṉit tōṟṟuvittiruppar. avarkaḷ karutumāṟu itaṉāṉum ciṟitupayaṉ viḷiyiṉum viḷiyalām. eṉiṉum itaṉāl viḷiyakkūṭiya nalam tīṅkukaḷiyellām naṉku ārāyntu pārātu, piṟar collum vaḻiyellām oḻukuvateṉpatu peritum ētam taruvatām. 'intiya tēciya makācapi' campantappaṭṭa variyil pārkkiṉum cila āṇṭu kaḷākac ceṉṉi mākāṇattiṉar yātum celvākkillātavar kaḷāy nīrvaḻippaṭṭa puṇi pōl ēṉiyōr vaḻiyil oḻukupavarkaḷāyk kāṇappaṭuvatu iraṅkattakkatākum.
tamiḻ makkaḷukkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka vāḻntu vanta tam muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ aṟivu, āṇmi, koṭi, nīti mutaliya vaṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷavum, immi maṟumi vīṭṭiṉ paṅkaḷip peṟutaṟkuriya vaḻikaḷit terintu koḷḷavum, iṟivaṉi maṉamurukattutittup pēriṉpamaṭiyavum karuviyā yiruppatu tamiḻ moḻiyākum. tamiḻ makkaḷil itaṉip payilātavar yāvarum vāḻkkiyiṉ payaṉi iḻantavar āvar. aṅṅaṉamākavum tamiḻ makkaḷaṉivarum tamiḻ kaṟkavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkuriya muyaṟciyic ciṟitum mēṟkoḷḷātu araciyaṟ kiḷarcci yāḷarkaḷ 'hintiyipparappuvataṟku muyalvatu aṉṉāratu nērmiyiṉmiyik kāṭṭukiṟatu.'
kāci 'intu yuṉivarciṭi'yil paṭikkum tamiḻaṉoruvaṉ kaṭṭāyamākat tamiḻ paṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa viti aṅkuḷḷatu. āṉāl ceṉṉi, aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷilō, tamiḻ nāṭṭuppaḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷilō attakiya vitiyilli. inta nilimiyil hintiyik kallūrikaḷilum paḷḷikkūṭaṅ kaḷilum viruppappāṭamākkum muyaṟci tīviramāka naṭipeṟṟu varukiṟatu. aṅṅaṉamākumāyiṉ tamiḻiṉ nili piṉṉum iraṅkaṟ kuriyatēyākum.
hinti iṉṉum 150 āṇṭukaḷ pōtikkap paṭiṉum ippoḻutu āṅkilam irukkum niliyittāṉ eṭṭa muṭiyum. yāvarum ataṉik kaṟṟal iyalvataṉṟu. appaṭiyē intiyāviṟku atu potumoḻiyāyiṉum ulakap potu moḻiyāka āṅkilam kaṟkappaṭal vēṇṭum. tāymoḻiyōṭu āṅkilam kaṟpatē iyalātatāyiruppa hintiyum kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉpatu poruntumā? eṉavē hintiyip potumoḻiyākkum muyaṟciyāṉatu payaṉilatāvatē yaṉṟik kēṭupayappatumākumeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. yāṉtamiḻik kuṟippiṭṭuc colliya cila ceytikaḷ teluṅku mutaliya nāṭṭu moḻikaṭkum poruntum.

25. tamiḻavēḷ taṉimāṇpu
centamiḻp puravalar, tamiḻavēḷ,tiru.ta.vē.umāmakēcuvaram piḷḷi yavarkaḷ añcāmi, īki, aṟivu, ūkkam eṉṉum aracarkkuriya paṇpukaḷ1 iyalpilē amivarap peṟṟavar. avar, celvam, patavi, āṇi eṉpavaṟṟiṟ piṟar ettuṇic ciṟantōrāyiṉum avi karutiyē avarkku vaḻimoḻitaluṭiya rallar ceytakka ceyyāk kuṟiyō, ceytakka vallaṉa ceyyuṅ kuṟṟamō2 avar pāṟkāṇiṉ avar kaṇṇetirē kaḻaṟṟuri kūṟit teruṭṭu nīrmiyar. muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu millāri aviyiṉmi yētuvāka mīk kūṟutaluṭiyarumallar; avarpāṟ kaṭappāṭaṟiyum paṇpuṭami kāṇiṉ avarkku vaḻimoḻitaluñ ceyvar; maṟṟu, eṉakkitu ceykaveṉa ekkālattum yāroruvar kaṇṇum yātoṉṟaṉiyum irattalaṟiyār; olluva toṉṟaṉip piṟar iranta vaḻi ataṉi maṟuttalu maṟiyār;3 iṉṉavāṟṟāl, puṟappāṭṭilē, nampi neṭuñceḻiyaṉip pēreyiṉ muṟuvalār kūṟiya "valiya reṉa vaḻimoḻiyalaṉ, meliya reṉa mīkkūṟalaṉ, piṟarit tāṉ irappaṟiyalaṉ, irantōrkku maṟuppaṟiyalaṉ"4  eṉṉum ētturikaḷ nam tamiḻavēḷukkum naṟkiyiyumeṉka.
maṟṟu cālpu eṉṉum poṟiyiṉit tāṅkiya tūṇkaḷeṉa vaḷḷuvaṉār teḷḷiyuritta aṉpu, nāṇ, ōppuravu kaṇṇōṭṭam, vāymi eṉṉum aiṅkuṇaṅkaḷum5 tamiḻavēḷi aṇiceyyāniṉṟaṉa", "perumi perumitamiṉmi"6 eṉṉum aṟivuṟūukkaṇṭu avar aṭaṅkiṉarō, avaratu taruk kiṉmiyāṉām perumiyi nōkki avvuri yeḻuntatō eṉṉumāṟu avaratu aṭakkam ciṟantu tikaḻntatu. teyvap pulavar naṭpiyaliṉ maṟimukattāṟ kūṟiyavariviṉ makaḷir, kaḷ cūtu eṉpaṉa avarirunta ticiyiṉum ceṉṟaṟiyātaṉa. maṟṟum,
"uḷḷuva tellām uyarvuḷḷal"1 "aṟivaṟintāḷ viṉiyiṉmi paḻi"2 eṉṉum maṟimoḻikaḷik kaṭippiṭittu, itu arumiyuṭitteṉṟu acāvātu,3 viṉi ceytalāṉām iṉṉāmiyi iṉpameṉak koṇṭu4 iṭiyaṟātu muyalum avaratu mīkkōḷum ūkkamum viḷamputaṟkariyaṉa. avar aṅṅaṉam muyaṟaṟku mēṟkoṇṭa viṉitāṉ taṇṭamiḻ purattalē. iravaṉ maṉiyil eḻunta kuḻaviyām karantit tamiḻccaṅkam ippoḻutu kantaṉiyaṉiyavar kali payil kaḻakamum, pantiṉi yiḷiyavar payiliṭamum,5 maṉṟamum, potiyilum maruttuk kūṭamum, ārtamaraṟṟōr cērtarumillamum, paṭṭimaṇṭapamum, paranta nūl niliyamum, piṟavumām uṟuppukkaḷ tiṟavitiṟpeṟṟa pēraraṇ uṟṟa peṟṟitāṉ centamiḻp puravalar nantā muyaṟciyiṉ vantatōr payaṉēyaṉṟō? avar evvuḻic celliṉum, yāruṭaṉ pēciṉum, yātu puriyiṉum yāvum tamiḻiṉ poruṭṭē yākum.
iṅṅaṉam cintiyum, uriyum ceykiyu mellām centamiḻkkākkiya tiṇṭiṟaṟ kuricil. centamiḻp payaṉām civaneṟi pēṇun tavamuṭiyārumāyiṉar. kaṉinta aṉpiṉ naṉintavuḷḷamoṭu tiruppati tōṟum viruppuṭaṉ naṭantu kaṇṇutaṟ kaṭavuḷi naṇṇi vaḻipaṭum ārvam ivarpāl aḷaviṉṟāyatu. aiyāṟappar tiruvulāppōtum ēḻpativiḻāvil meyppiyiṉṟiyum mēluṭiyiṉṟiyum vemmi mītuṟat taṇmiyiṉ naṭakkum ivaratu eḷiya takimiyip pallāyiravar pārttuḷam nekiḻvar. maṟṟum, yāvarkkum iṉṉā ceyyāmiyum, eḷiyārpāl irakkaṅkōṭalum, kaṟṟārik kātalittalum, naṭṭāri uyarvu kūṟalum,6 periyōrip pēṇiyoḻukalum mutaliya ivar paṇpaṉittum uriyil aṭaṅkuvaṉavalla. curuṅka urikkiṉ avaratu vāḻkki oru kalvikkūṭam, uyarnili virumpumoruvaṉ ataṉiṟ pukuntu payiṟal vēṇṭum.
ā! ipperuntakiyām aruntuṇiyip pirintēyum iruntēm yām! em aṉpirunta vāṟeṉṉē!
26. tamiḻil kaliccoṟkaḷ 
māṇākkarkaṭku aṟivu nūlkaḷi nāṭṭumoḻi vāyilākak kaṟpikkum poruṭṭu araciyalār kaliccoṟkaḷit tokukka virumpiyatu potuvākap pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatē. āṉāl avviruppam payaṉṟarattakka muṟiyil muyaṟci naṭipeṟavilli. teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṟ kaliccol lākkutaṟporuṭṭu 1923ām āṇṭil ceṉṉi aracāṅkattiṉar niyamitta kaḻakattiṉ vēli payaṉaṟṟatāy oḻintataṉ kāraṇatti ārāyntu akkuṟi mīṭṭum nērtaṟkiṭamillātapaṭi ceṉṟa āṇṭilē ceṉṉi aracāṅkattār kaliccol veḷiyīṭṭu ālōcaṉik kuḻuvi amittiruttal vēṇṭum. ceṉṉik kuḻuviṉar kaṇṭa muṭipukaḷum evvāṟṟāṉum payaṉṟarat takkaṉavalla. pala tēya orumippāṭṭiṉ poruṭṭup potu moḻi oṉṟirukkak kūṭumāyiṉ naṉṟē. āṅkilattiluḷḷa kaliccoṟkaḷi appaṭiyē intiya nāṭṭumoḻikaḷil eṭuttuk koḷvatu  pala tēya orumiyi ēṟpaṭuttuvatākātu. ammoḻiyiṉ collōciyi avvāṟē piṟamoḻikaḷil amittal iyalvataṉṟu. amippiṉum avvōciyē uṭiya piṟamoḻic collōṭu vēṟṟumi yaṟiyātu mayaṅkutaṟkiṭaṉākum, Air eṉṉum collit tamiḻil ‘ēr' eṉṟu eḻutiṉāl evvaḷavu kuḻappamuṇṭākum? aṉṟiyum oru moḻiyiluḷḷa kalinūli ammoḻi kaṟṟāraṉṟi ēṉiyōr aṟital iyalātu. avvāṟirukkap pala moḻikaḷilum kalic coṟkaḷimaṭṭil potuvākkutalāl viḷiyum payaṉyātu? teṉṉintiya moḻi allatu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil kalic coṟkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu amippatu eṉpatum, muṟi yaṟṟa ceyki, ticipaṟṟi vaṭamoḻikku etirākat teṉmoḻi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatum, evvakiyilum vaṭamoḻikkut tāḻnta
tallātatum, kalikaṭku vēṇṭiya coṟkaḷi yellām taṉṉakattuk koṇṭiruppatum, taravallatum ākiya tamiḻukku vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu coṟkaḷi ēṉ kaṭaṉ vāṅkutal vēṇṭum? maṟṟum ivviru koḷkiyum nāṭṭumoḻi vāyilākak kalvi kaṟpippatu eṉṉuṅ koḷkikku muraṇāṉavi. moḻip payiṟciyi aṉṟik kalip payiṟciyiyuṅ kuṟikkak kūṭiyavi. ciṟappākat tamiḻukku ūṟu ceyvaṉa. tamiḻ makkaḷ etaṉi iḻantālum taṅkaḷ moḻiyiṉ tūymiyi iḻakka oruppaṭār. ākaliṉ avarkaḷ karuttukku māṟāka valitiṟ pukuttappaṭum immuṟiyāl ettuṇiyum payaṉ viḷiyātu.
ceṉṉi mākāṇat tamiḻc caṅkam, 1934-ām āṇṭil ivvēliyi mēṟkoṇṭu palkalikaḷilum, tamiḻilum payiṟci yuṭiyavarkaḷiṉ utaviyāl, vevvēṟu kalikaṭkuriya coṟkaḷit tamiḻil eḻuti, 1936-ām āṇṭil ōr periya makānāṭṭiyuṅ kūṭṭi, aṟiñar palaruṭiya opputal peṟṟu "kaliccoṟkaḷ" eṉṉum peyarāl ōr puttakavuruvil veḷippaṭuttiṟṟu. ellāk kalvi niliyaṅkaḷum, veḷiyīṭukaḷum avaṟṟi ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi ceyyac ceṉṉi araciyalārē muṉvantaṉar. nūl eḻutuvōrum, kaṟpikkum āciriyarum, mutalāka aṉivarum avaṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭamiyāl avi peritum vaḻakkāṟṟu niliyi aṭintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻip poṟutta maṭṭilāvatu avaṟṟiyē ēṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi ceyvatum, avi pōtāviṭil ammuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi mēlum coṟkaḷ ākkumāṟu ceyvatum aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭamiyākum.

27. toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ
toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ eṉpavaṉ toṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ talinakarākiya kaccippatiyiliruntu aracupurinta ōr ciṟṟaracaṉ; caṅka kālattup pāṭṭuṭit talivarkaḷil oruvaṉ; kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum cāṉṟōr pāṭiya perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi koṇṭavaṉ; caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷiyaṟṟiya nallicippulavar variciyiṟ cērnta tolliciyuṭiyavaṉ.
ivaṉ peyar orōviṭaṅkaḷil toṇṭimāṉ eṉavum, iḷantiriyaṉ eṉavum, tiriyaṉ eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. toṇṭimāṉ eṉṉum peyar toṇṭiyar kuṭiyilē tōṉṟiya aracaṉ eṉṉum poruḷatākum. toṇṭiyar toṇṭi nāṭṭil vāḻnta kuṟumuṭik kuṭiyiṉar. iḷantiriyaṉ eṉpatu am maṉṉaṉ peyar. paṇṭināḷilē aracar mutalāyiṉār peyarkku aṭiyāka varuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ iḷamip paṇpum oṉṟeṉpatu iḷañcēṭceṉṉi, iḷamperuvaḻuti, iḷañcēralirum poṟi, iḷaveḷimāṉ, iḷaṅkīraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭiyilē,
"	irunilaṅ kaṭanta tirumaṟu mārpiṉ
munnīr vaṇṇaṉ piṟaṅkaṭi yannīrt
tiritaru marapi ṉuravō ṉumpal
malartali yulakattu maṉṉuyir kākkum 
muracumuḻaṅkutāṉi mūvaruḷḷum
ilaṅkunīrp parappiṉ vaḷimīk kūṟum
valampuriyaṉṉa vacinīṅku ciṟappiṉ
allatu kaṭinta aṟampuri ceṅkōṟ
palvēṟ ṟiriyaṉ"1
eṉṉum pakuti tiriyaṉatu kuṭippiṟappiyum, peyar varalāṟṟiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. ippāṭṭiṟku urikaṇṭa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷik koṇṭukūṭṭi 'mūvēntaruḷḷum talimivāyntōṉum, tirumāliṉ piṉ vantōṉumākiya cōḻaṉkuṭiyiṟ piṟantōṉ, kaṭaliṉ tiri koṇṭu vantamiyāl tiriyaṉ eṉṉum peyariyuṭiyavaṉ' eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, 'eṉṟataṉāl, nākapaṭṭiṉattuc cōḻaṉ pilattuvārattāl nākalōkattē ceṉṟu nākakaṉṉiyip puṇarnta kālattu, avaḷ,  'yāṉpeṟṟa putalvaṉi eṉ ceyvēṉ' eṉṟapoḻutu,  'toṇṭiyi aṭiyāḷamākak kaṭṭik kaṭalilēviṭa, avaṉ vantu kariyēṟiṉ avaṟku yāṉ aracavurimiyi eytuvittu nāṭāṭci koṭuppal' eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟa, avaḷum putalvaṉi aṅṅaṉam varaviṭat tiri tarutaliṉ tiriyaṉeṉṟu peyar peṟṟa kati kūṟiṉār' eṉa ōr varalāṟum kuṟittuḷḷār. avar, "toṇṭiyi aṭiyāḷa mākak kaṭṭiviṭa" eṉṟamiyiṉ, toṇṭimāṉ eṉṟa peyariṉ kāraṇamum atuveṉa niṉintārākal vēṇṭum. maṇimēkaliyiṉ 24-āvatu kātiyil, neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi yeṉṉum cōḻamaṉṉaṉ iṉṉiḷa vēṉiṟ poḻutiṟ puṉṉiyaṅkāṉalil etirppaṭṭa eḻilmikka iḷiyāḷoruttiyik kaḷaviṟ kūṭi, ciṉṉāḷiṉ piṉ aṉṉavaḷik kāṇātu tēṭuṅkāl, cāraṇaṉ oruvaṉ tōṉṟi, "nākanāṭāḷum vaḷivaṇaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ maṉiviyākiya vācamayili vayiṟṟutitta pīlivaḷi eṉpāḷ piṟantapoḻutu, 'ivaḷ paritikulattoruvaṉi maruvik karuvuṭaṉ varuvaḷ' eṉak kaṇivaṉ kūṟiyataṉik kēṭṭuḷēṉ; avaḷē niṉṉi maṇantu taṇantavaḷāvaḷ; iṉi, avaḷ peṟum putalvaṉ varuvāṉaṉṟi, avaḷ vārāḷ" eṉak kūṟiṉaṉeṉa ōr varalāṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa pīlivaḷiyum, avaḷ putalvaṉumē nākakaṉṉiyum iḷantiriyaṉumāva reṉac cilar karutuvar. (pattuppāṭṭu, pāṭappaṭṭōr varalāṟu kāṇka.)
āyiṉ, muṟkāṭṭiya perumpāṇāṟṟiṉ pakutikku nacciṉārk kiṉiyar urittavuri poruttamuṭitteṉat tōṉṟavilli. 'munnīr vaṇṇaṉ piṟaṅkaṭi annīrttiritaru marapiṉ uravōṉ umpal' eṉpataṟku, 'kaṭal vaṇṇaṉākiya tirumāliṉ piṉ vantōṉum, kaṭal nīrttiriyāl tarappaṭṭa marapiṉiyuṭiya uravōṉumākiya cōḻaṉatu vaḻittōṉṟal' eṉpatē nēriya poruḷākum. tiriyaṉ eṉpatu cōḻaṉuk kuriyatōr peyareṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. 'āyiratteṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muntiya tamiḻar' eṉṉum nūliyaṟṟiya kaṉakacapip piḷḷiyavarkaḷum tiriyar eṉṉum peyaric cōḻarkkē uriyatākkukiṉṟaṉar. toṇṭimāṉ eṉṉum peyarum toṇṭikkoṭiyi aṭiyāḷamākak kaṭṭiviṭṭamiyiṉ iḷantiriyaṉukku vantateṉpatu poruntātu. kuṟuntokiyil, "aṇṇal yāṉi, vaṇṭērt toṇṭiyar" (260) eṉavum, akanāṉūṟṟil, "viṉinavil yāṉi viṟaṟpōrt toṇṭiyar" (213) eṉavum toṇṭiyar eṉpatu ōr kuṭippeyarāka vantuḷḷami kāṇka. perumpāṇāṟṟilēyē iḷantiriyaṉ "toṇṭiyōr marukaṉ" eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ; iḵtoṉṟumē iḷantiriyaṉukku muṉpē toṇṭiyar eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmi niṟuttap pōtiyatākum. ivvāṟṟāl toṇṭiyar eṉṉum peyar kāñci, tiruvēṅkaṭam mutaliyavaṟṟit taṉṉakattēyuṭiya nāṭṭilē toṉṟu toṭṭu āṭci purinta ōr kuṭiyiṉarik kuṟippatāmeṉṟum, iḷantiriyaṉeṉpāṉ cōḻaṉoruvaṉ toṇṭiyar makaḷi maṇantu peṟṟa mintaṉāvāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉē toṇṭi nāṭṭukku aracaṉākit tāyvaḻiyil toṇṭimāṉ eṉṉum peyarum, tanti vaḻiyil tiriyaṉ eṉṉum peyarum,  eytiṉāṉ eṉṟum karututal cālum. iṉi, tiriyar eṉṉum peyar cōḻarkkurittaṉṟāyt toṇṭiyarkkuriyatoṉṟāyiṉum, 'tiritaru marapiṉ uravōṉ umpal' eṉpataṟkut tiriyālē tarappaṭṭa muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiyōṉatu vaḻittōṉṟal eṉpatē poruḷākaliṉ, cōḻaṉ makaṉātalpaṟṟi munnīr vaṇṇaṉ piṟaṅkaṭi eṉṟum, tiriyar kuṭikkuriyaṉātal paṟṟit tiriyaṉ eṉṟum ivaṉ kūṟappaṭṭāṉeṉak kōṭal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, maṇimēkali 29-āvatu kātiyil, neṭumuṭik kiḷḷikkup pīlivaḷi peṟṟa puṉiṟṟiḷaṅ kuḻaviyi nākanāṭṭaracaṉ taṉimarak kalattiṟpōnta kampaḷac ceṭṭiyiṉkiyiṟ koṭukka, avaṉ akkuḻaviyi ēṟṟu marakkalattiṟ koṇṭu mīṇṭu varumpoḻutu pukār nakariṉ kaṭaṟkarikku aṇittākak kappal muḻukiyateṉṟum, ataṉik kēḷviyuṟṟa kiḷḷi makaṉit tēṭutalāl intiraviḻāc ceytali maṟantu viṭṭaṉaṉeṉṟum, intiraṉ kaṭṭaḷiyāl maṇimēkalā teyvam kampaḷac ceṭṭiyip pātukāttateṉṟum, intiraviḻāc ceyyap paṭāmiyāl kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam kaṭalāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa teṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pīlivaḷi makaṉukkut toṇṭik koṭiyi aṭiyāḷamākak kaṭṭiviṭṭa ceytiyō, avaṉ kariyēṟiṉāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyō atiṟ kūṟappaṭavilli. avaṉ kariyēṟi vaḷarntu aracukkuriyavaṉāyiṉum avaṉatu kālam iḷantiriyaṉ kālattiṟku oru nūṟṟāṇṭu pintiyatākal vēṇṭum. cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉip paṭṭiṉappāliyāṟpāṭiya uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉārē āṟṟuppaṭiyāl iḷantiriyaṉip pāṭiyiruttaliṉ, iḷantiriyaṉ kālam ki.pi.mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyākal vēṇṭum. maṇimēkali kāñciyi aṭinta poḻutu aṅkiruntavarkaḷ toṭukaḻaṟkiḷḷi, tuṇiyiḷaṅ kiḷḷi eṉṉum cōḻarkaḷeṉpatu teritaliṉ, pīlivaḷiyiṉ makaṉ vantu aracāṇṭāṉ eṉṉiṉ aṉṉōr kālattiṉ piṉṉark ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilō mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ mutalilō ātal vēṇṭum. eṉavē, evvāṟṟāṉum toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ pīlivaḷiyiṉ makaṉallaṉ eṉpatu peṟṟām.
iṉi, toṇṭināṭṭiliruntu āṭcipurinta pallava maṉṉarkaḷum toṇṭiyarum ōriṉattavarē yeṉap pala ētukkaḷ kāṭṭik kaḷḷar carittirattil yāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ cōḻaṉ makaṉum, muṭiyuṭiya vēntaroṭu cērtteṇṇattakum perumiyuṭiyaṉu mākavum paṇṭic cāṉṟōrkaḷ avaṉik kuṟunila maṉṉaṉākavē karutiyiruntaṉareṉpatu "villum vēlum" eṉṉum marapiyaṟ (nūṟ.84) cūttiravuriyil 'maṉpeṟu marapiṉ ēṉōr' eṉappaṭuvār, aracupeṟu marapiṟ kuṟunilamaṉṉar eṉak koḷka; avi, perumpāṇāṟṟuḷḷum piṟavaṟṟuḷḷum kāṇappaṭum' eṉap pērāciriyar urittamiyāṟ peṟappaṭum. paṇṭiyōr aṅṅaṉam koṇṭamikkuk kāraṇam avaṉ toṇṭiyar kuṭikkuriyaṉāyt toṇṭi nāṭāṭci peṟṟamiyē yākum.
piṟṟināḷilē pallavarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ perum pakutiyi aṭippaṭutti āṭci ceytavaḻiyum aṉṉavar kuṟunilamaṉṉar vakuppilēyē cērtteṇṇap peṟṟaṉareṉpatu periya purāṇattil kūṟappaṭṭa tiruttoṇṭarkaḷil pallavar kulat tutittu nāṭāṇṭa kaḻaṟciṅkar mutalāyiṉār aṅṅaṉam koḷḷappeṟṟamiyāṟ pōtarum. iṉi, perumpāṇ mutaliya vaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum iḷantiriyaṉ paṇpukaḷi uḷaṅ koḷvōmāka.


28. patiṟṟuppattu
patiṟṟuppattu eṉpatu caṅkap pulavarkaḷālē pāṭit tokukkap peṟṟa eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟu, puṟapporuḷ paṟṟiya nūṟu akavaṟpākkaḷāl ākiyatu. pappattup pāṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷa pattup pakutiyāka amintiruttaliṉ itaṟkup patiṟṟup pattu eṉpatu peyarāyiṟṟu. ovvoru pattum taṉittaṉiyē ovvōr aracarik kuṟittu ovvoru pulavar pāṭiyaṉa
vākaliṉ innūl pattup pakutiyuṭiyatāyiṟṟu eṉka. innūl muḻutum cēramaṉṉarkaḷiyē pārāṭṭuvatum, ovvoru pāṭṭiṉ iṟutiyilum tuṟi, vaṇṇam, tūkku, peyar eṉpaṉa kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatum, ovvoru pattiṉ iṟutiyilum varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ aṭaṅkiya patikam ovvoṉṟu amintiruppatum ēṉit tokinūlkaḷil iṉṟi itaṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum ciṟappiyalpukaḷākum. aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟiṉ neytal eṭṭām pattuppōla innūliṉ nālām pattu antātiyāka amintuḷḷatu. eṉavē, antāti pāṭuvataṟkuc caṅkap
pulavarkaḷē aṭikōliyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu pōtarum. ovvoru pāṭṭilum uḷḷa ciṟappuṭiya toṭar oṉṟu avvappāṭṭiṟkup peyarāka amintiruttali nōkkiṉ, akkālattirunta añcol nuṇṭērccip pulavarkaḷ ceyyuṭkaḷilē coṟkaḷiyum poruḷkaḷiyum nuṇṇitiṉ ārāyntu ciṟappuṭiyaṉa kaṇṭu kaḷikūrntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉām. pattuppāṭṭil oṉṟāya kūttarāṟṟuppaṭikku malipaṭu
kaṭām eṉṟa peyar vantatum aṟiyaṟpālatu.
patikaṅkaḷ avvap pattiṉ pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ peyar, avaṉ purinta ariya ceyalkaḷ, pāṭiṉa pulavar peyar, ceyyuṭkaḷ pattiṉ peyar eṉṉum ivaṟṟip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. patika iṟutiyil pāṭiya pulavar peṟṟa paricilum, aracaṉ āṇṭa kālavaḷavum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkāla aḷavāṉatu pāṭiṉōrāl aṉṟip piṉvantōrāl eḻutappaṭṭiruttal orutali. maṟṟum, patikaṅkaḷil pāṭṭuṭittalivar peyarē
yaṉṟi, aṉṉōri īṉṟa tanti tāyar peyarum kāṇappaṭutal viyattaṟkuriyatu. ivvāṟṟāl paḻiya cēra vēntar cilaruṭiya varalāṟu tuṇitaṟku in nūṟpatikac ceyyuṭkaḷ peritum tuṇiceyyāniṟpaṉa. patikaṅkaḷ yāvum muṉṉar āciriya naṭiyum iṟutiyiṟ kaṭṭuri naṭiyum koṇṭiruttaliṉ uric ceyyuṭkaḷām.
innūliṉ mutaṟpattum iṟutippattum iṟantaṉa. ēṉiya iraṇṭu mutaliya eṭṭup pattukkaḷilum muṟiyē pāṭap
peṟṟavarkaḷ imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ, palyāṉic celkeḻukuṭṭuvaṉ, kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭiccēral, kaṭal piṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, āṭukōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ, celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ, takaṭūr eṟinta peruñcēral irumpoṟi, iḷañcēral irumpoṟi eṉṉum cēra
vēntar eṇmarumām. pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ muṟiyē kumaṭṭūrk kaṇṇaṉār, pālik kutamaṉār, kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉār, paraṇar, kākkipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār, kapilar, aricilkiḻār, peruṅkuṉṟūrkiḻār eṉṉum eṇmarumām. ivarkaḷuḷ, kākkipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār peṇpāṟ
pulavarāvar. ippulavarkaḷ pattuppattup pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭiyataṟkāka avvēntarkaḷiṭam peṟṟa paricilkaḷ maruṭciyi viḷikkak kūṭiyaṉa. kumaṭṭūrk kaṇṇaṉār umpaṟkāṭṭu ainnūṟu ūr piramatāyamum, muppatteṭṭu āṇṭu teṉṉāṭṭu varuvāyiṟ
pākamum peṟṟār. kākkipāṭiṉiyār aṇikalaṉukkeṉṟu oṉpatu tulām poṉṉum nūṟāyiram poṟkācum paricilākap peṟṟār. ivvāṟē ēṉōrum peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ. ivvaracar
kaḷuṭiya koṭimaṭam iruntavāṟeṉṉē! ik koṭittiṟattil iṉṉum viyakkattakka nikaḻcciyum uṇṭu. palyāṉic celkeḻukuṭṭuvaṉ taṉṉip pāṭiya pālik kutamaṉāri nōkki 'nīr vēṇṭiyatu koṇmiṉ' eṉa avar, 'yāṉum eṉ pārppaṉiyum cuvarkkam pukal vēṇṭum' eṉṟurikka, avvaracaṉ cāṉṟōrik kēṭṭu oṉpatu peru vēḷvi vēṭpikka, pattām peruvēḷviyil kutamaṉārum avar maṉiviyum cuvarkkamaṭintu viṭṭaṉarām. icceyti, 'nāṉmaṟiyāḷaṉ ceyyuṭ koṇṭu, mēṉiliyulakam viṭuttōṉāyiṉum' eṉac cilappatikārattilum kuṟikkap
peṟṟuḷḷatu. cōḻaṉ paṭiyāḷar cērar paṭiyuṭaṉ porutu tōṟṟup pōkaṭṭa vēl celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉiṭam kapilaṉ peṟṟa ūrilum pala eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattilēyē peruṅ
kuṉṟūr kiḻār pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivvāṟē patikaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷum iruttaliṉ, patikacceytikaḷ perumpālum uṇmi varalāṟukaḷēyātal vēṇṭum. patika ārāycci taṉiyē viritturikkaṟpālatākaliṉ ataṉi, ivvaḷavil niṟutti, nūluṭ pukuntu nōkkuvōm.
innūṟ ceyyuṭkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum perumpālum pāṭṭuṭittalivarkaḷākiya cēramaṉṉarkaḷiṉ vīravāḻkkiyum, koṭicciṟappum, āṭcimuṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvaracarkaḷ tam muṉṉōr oḻukiya neṟiyilēyē tāmum naṭantu, nallamiccarkaḷiyum cāṉṟōrkaḷiyum tuṇiyākak koṇṭu, tam kīḻvāḻum kuṭikaḷi avarkaṭku evvaki iṉṉalum nērāvaṇṇam pātukāttu vantaṉar; tamakkuc ciṉa mūṭṭiya pakivarkaḷip porutu tolittu avarkaḷ nāṭṭiṉi aḻittup pāḻceytu āṇṭuk kavarnta poruḷkaḷi iravalarkku vāri vaḻaṅkiṉar; tam pukaḻip parappum pulavarkaṭku nāṭum ūrum kāṉamum nalkiṉar; āṭalpāṭalkaḷāl tam aviyi aṇiceytu iṉpuṟuttum porunarkkum pāṇarkkum kaḷiṟum pariyum poṟṟāmariyum, viṟaliyarkkup piṭiyum aṇiyiḻiyum vaḻaṅkiṉar; aḻakilum kaṟpilum mikacciṟanta tēviyaruṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpuṟṟu mutunūṉmuṟippaṭi viruntōmputal mutaliyaṉa ceytu illaṟam iṉitu naṭattiṉar; kōṭkaḷiṉ niliyāl paruvam māṟi ulakeṅkum vaṟkaṭamuṟṟakāliyum aracaruṭiya ceṅkōṉmiyāl annāṭṭu makkaḷ vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷik kuṟiviṉṟip peṟṟup paciyum piṇiyumiṉṟi iṉitu vāḻntuvantaṉar; eṉṉum ivipōṉṟa ceytikaḷ maṟṟum pala innūṟ ceyyuṭkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
'āriyar tuvaṉṟiya pēriciyimayam, teṉṉaṅ kumariyōṭāyiṭi, maṉmīk kūṟunar maṟantapak kaṭantē'1 eṉpataṉāl neṭuñcēralātaṉum' 'vaṭaticiyelli yimaya mākatteṉṉaṅ kumariyo ṭāyiṭi yaracar- colpala nāṭṭit tolkavi ṉaḻitta, pōraṭu tāṉip polantārk kuṭṭuva,' eṉpataṉāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum imayamutal kumari variyuḷḷa aracarkaḷi veṉṟu viḷaṅkiṉar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
'accam poyccol aṉpumika vuṭimi, teṟal kaṭumiyoṭu piṟavumiv vulakattu, aṟanteri tikirikku vaḻiyaṭiyākum, tītucē ṇikantu naṉṟumikap purintu'2 eṉpatu, aṟavaḻi cellutaṟkut taṭiyākum accam poy poruṭ paṟṟu kaṭuntaṇṭam mutaliya tīyakuṇañ ciṟitumiṉṟi nallaṉavē purintu celkeḻukuṭṭuvaṉ kuṭiyōmputaliyum, 'uyirpōṟṟaliyē ceruvattāṉē, koṭipōṟṟaliyē iravalar naṭuvaṇ, periyōrp pēṇic ciṟiyōri aḷitti, niṉvayiṟ pirinta nallici kaṉaviṉum, piṟarnaci yaṟiyā,1eṉpatu, peruñ cēral irumpoṟiyiṉ vīram, koṭi, aṭakkam, irakkam, pukaḻ eṉpavaṟṟiyum iṉitu pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. poyyā nāviṟ kapilar eṉṉum pulavar perumāṉ celvakkaṭuṅkōviṉ vāymiyippaṟṟi, 'nilantiṟam peyaruṅ kāli yāyiṉum, kiḷanta colnī poyppu aṟiyaliyē'2 eṉṟum, 'nakiyiṉum poyyā vāymi' 3 eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatum, avaṉatu īkiyik kuṟittu 'īttatu iraṅkāṉ īttoṟum makiḻāṉ'4 eṉṟum, 'maḻiyiṉum perumpayam poḻiti'5 eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷatum aṟintu makiḻaṟpālaṉa. maṟṟum, avar tam āruyir naṇpaṉum vaḷḷaṇmikku eṭuttakkāṭṭākiya
vaṉum āya vēḷpāri tuñciyapiṉ, celvakkaṭuṅkōviṉ uyariya paṇpukaḷāl īrkkappaṭṭu vantārākak kūṟuvatu neñciṉi urukkum nīrmiyatu. neṭuñcēralātaṉatu īkiyiṉ ciṟappu, 'māri poykkuvatāyiṉum, cēralātaṉ poyyalaṉ naciyē'6 eṉavum, 'emakkum piṟarkkum yāva rāyiṉum, pariciṉ mākkaḷ vallārāyiṉum, koṭikkaṭaṉ amarnta kōṭā neñciṉaṉ'7 eṉavum pārāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. maṟṟum, 'vāyp
paṟiyalaṉē veyiṟṟukaḷ aṉittum, māṟṟōr tēettum māṟiya viṉiyē'8 eṉavum   'kaṇṇiṉ uvantu neñcu aviḻpu aṟiyā, naṇṇār tēettum poyppu aṟiyalaṉē'9 eṉavum pōntaṉa avaṉuṭiya viṉit tūymiyiyum vāymiyiyum naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. āṭukōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉatu īkittiṟam, avaṉ taṉṉāṭṭil vaṟumiyiṉmiyāl iravalar vārārāyiṉum, piṟa nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa iravalarkaḷit tēril ēṟṟi varacceytu avarkaṭku uṇavaḷippaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ oṇṇutal makaḷir tuṉitta kaṇṇiṉum pārkkil iravalar puṉkaṇukku añcuvaṉ10 eṉṟum pārāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
iṉi, avvaracarkaḷiṉ tēvimāruṭiya eḻil, iṉkuṇam, kaṟpu mutaliyaṉa eṅṅaṉam pukaḻppaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataṉi nōkku vōm. 'vāṉuṟi makaḷir nalaṉikal koḷḷum, vayaṅkiḻi karanta vaṇṭupaṭu katuppiṉ, koṭuṅkuḻi'1 eṉavum, 'āṟiya kaṟpiṉ aṭaṅkiya cāyal, ūṭiṉum iṉiya kūṟum iṉṉaki, amirtu poti tuvarvāy amartta nōkkiṉ, cuṭarnutal acinaṭi'2 eṉavum, 'olitta kūntal aṟañcāl kaṟpiṉ, kuḻikku viḷakkākiya avvāṅ kunti, vicumpuvaḻaṅku makaḷiruḷḷuñ ciṟanta, cemmīṉ aṉiyaḷ'3  eṉavum, 'aṭaṅkiya koḷki, āṉṟavaṟiviṟ ṟōṉṟiya nallici, oṇṇutal makaḷir'4 eṉavum, 'vēypuripu eḻiliya viḷaṅkiṟip paṇit tōḷ, kāmar kaṭavuḷum āḷuṅ kaṟpiṉ, cēṇāṟu naṟunutaṟ cēyiḻi'5 eṉavum, 'vāḻnāḷaṟiyum vayaṅkucuṭar nōkkattu, mīṉoṭu puriyuṅ kaṟpiṉ, vāṇuta larivi'6 eṉavum, 'āṟiya kaṟpiṟ ṟēṟiya nallici, vaṇṭār kūntal oṇṭoṭi'7 eṉavum vantuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa palavuḷa. ivaṟṟiliruntu, avarkaḷ tēva mātariṉum viñciya aḻakuṭiyar, aruntatiyiṉum mēmpaṭṭa kaṟpuṭiyar, ūṭaṟkālattum iṉiya moḻiyē kūṟupavar, ittakiya paṇpukaḷāl mikka pukaḻ mēvappeṟṟavar eṉa aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ neṟṟi kātaṇiyākiya kuḻikku viḷakkan tarumām! mēṉiyiṉ aḻaku aṇikalaṅkaḷukku oḷiyi nalkumām! avarkaḷ kaṟpāṉatu aṟañcāl kaṟpu eṉavum, āṟiya kaṟpu eṉavum pōṟṟap paṭukiṉṟatu. maṟṟum, aṉṉōruṭiya kūntal, nutal, vāy, tōḷ, mārpu, naṭi mutaliyavaṟṟi nallicippulavarkaḷ curukkamum teḷivum iṉimiyum uṭiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷāl iyaṟkivarampu kaṭavātu pārāṭṭun tiṟaṉ kaṟpōrkkuk kaḻipēriṉpam payappatākum. pulavarkaḷ am mātarāriṉ paṇpukaḷik kūṟi, attakiya āyiḻikaṇava, oṇṭoṭi kaṇava eṉṟiṅṅaṉam aracarkaḷi viḷippatu avvaracarkaḷum pulavarkaḷum, am makaḷiri mika naṉku matittup pōṟṟiṉamikkuc cāṉṟākum.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam kaṟpiṟ ciṟanta poṟpuṟu maṉiviyaruṭaṉ kūṭi illaṟam naṭattum iv vēntarkaḷ vitikavoḻukkam taliniṉṟu, tēvarkaṭaṉ, muṉivarkaṭaṉ, pitirarkaṭaṉ eṉpavaṟṟi āṟṟi vantaṉareṉpatu, 'vēḷviyiṟ kaṭavu ḷaruttiṉi kēḷvi, uyarnili yulakattu aiyar iṉpuṟuttiṉi, vaṇaṅkiya cāyal vaṇaṅkā āṇmi, iḷantuṇip putalvariṉ mutiyarp pēṇit tolkaṭaṉ iṟutta velpō raṇṇal,'1 'kēḷvi kēṭṭup paṭivamoṭiyātu, vēḷvi vēṭṭaṉi uyarntōruvappa,2 'kāvaṟkaminta aracu tuṟipōkiya, vīṟucāl putalvarp peṟṟaṉi, ivaṇarkku, aruṅkaṭaṉ iṟutta ceruppukal muṉpa'3 eṉṟiṅṅaṉam varuvaṉavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. maṟṟum ivvaracarkaḷilē meyyuṇarvum tavavoḻukkamum uṭiyarāy viḷaṅkiṉārum uḷar. aricilkiḻār eṉṉum pulavar takaṭūr eṟinta peruñcēral irumpoṟiyi nōkki, nī muṉṉūliṉ vitiyi aṟintu virataṅkaḷil vaḻuvātu niṉṟu vāṉōruvakkum vaṇṇam vēḷvi vēṭṭal mutaliya kaṭaṉkaḷiyellām āṟṟiyuḷḷāy; āyiṉ, avaṟṟi yāṉ viyantilēṉ; kaliyelāmuṇarnta nari mūtāḷaṉākiya niṉ purōkitaṉukkut tavattiṉ ciṟappi eṭuttuk kūṟi, ataṉkaṇ ēviya niṉ tavavoḻukkattiyum pēraṟiviyum kaṇṭu viyantēṉ eṉpār, 'aṉṉavi maruṇṭaṉaṉ allēṉ niṉvayiṉ, muḻutuṇarntoḻukku nari mūtāḷaṉi, vaṇmiyum māṇpum vaḷaṉum eccamum, teyvamum yāvatum tavamuṭiyōrk
keṉa, vēṟupaṭu naṉantalip peyarak kūṟiṉi, perumaniṉ paṭimiyāṉē'4 eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷami kāṇka.
maṟṟum, itaṉkaṇ, cēravēntarkaḷ nālvakip paṭiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu māṟṟāroṭu porutalum, pakivaruṭiya kāvalmaraṅ
kaḷit taṭintu avaṟṟāl muracu ceytalum, kaḷa vēḷvi vēṭṭalum, makaḷiroṭu tuṇaṅkiyāṭutalum, ayiri maliyil uḷḷa koṟṟaviyi vaḻipaṭutalum mutalāya ettaṉiyō pala ceytikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.pulavaroruvar ōr aracaṉi vāḻttumāṟu kāṭṭi ivvuriyiṉi muṭippōmāka.'niṉṉāḷ, tiṅkaḷaṉiyavāka; tiṅkaḷ, yāṇṭōraṉiyavāka; yāṇṭē, ūḻiyaṉiyavāka; ūḻi, veḷḷavarampiṉavāka, eṉa uḷḷik kāṇku vanticiṉ.'5

29. camaya aṇikalaṉ
tamiḻakattilē paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum camayaṅkaḷ civam, viṇavam eṉṉum iraṇṭumām. caṅka nūlkaḷilellām civattiṟkē mutaṉmi kūṟappaṭiṉum mālvaḻipāṭu yāṇṭum kaṭiyappaṭṭilatu civa naṉmakkaḷellārum tirumāliṭattum aṉpuṭiyarākavē oḻuki vantaṉar atu camayappaṟṟukku melivu taruvatākak karutappaṭavilli. caṅka nāḷilē tamiḻmakkaḷpāṟ camayappaṟṟu aḻuttamāka iruntateṉpataṟkup pala kuṟippukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟē iṅkuk kāṭṭap pukuntatu.
tamiḻarkaḷ tam makkaḷ kuḻavipparuvattu nōy mutaliya
vaṟṟāl naliviṉṟi vāḻtal karuti avarkaṭkukkāppāka aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷ iraṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu civapirāṉōṭiyi
puṭiyatu; maṟṟoṉṟu tirumālōṭiyi puṭiyatu. piṉṉatu piṟkālattiṟ palarāṉum kikkoḷḷap peṟṟu vantamiyiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ peritum payilvatāyiṟṟu. it tirumāliṉ vaḷi, tikiri, taṇṭu, vāḷ, vil eṉṉum aintu paṭikaḷiṉ uruvamintatu. itaṉi aimpaṭi eṉavum, tāli eṉavum, aimpaṭit tāli eṉavum vaḻaṅkuvar. puṟappāṭṭilē, pāṇṭiyaṉ taliyālaṅ
kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉatu iḷami mikutiyik kuṟikkuṅkāl.
"	tāli kaḷintaṉṟu milaṉē; pālviṭṭu
ayiṉiyum iṉṟayiṉ ṟaṉaṉē"1
eṉa iṭikkuṉṟūr kiḻār itaṉi vitantu kūṟiṉar. maṇimēkaliyilē,
"	cevvāyk kutali meypeṟā maḻali 
cintupu ciṉṉīr aimpaṭi naṉippa
aṟṟaṅ kāvāc cuṟṟuṭip pūntukil
toṭutta maṇikkōvi yuṭuppoṭu tuyalvarat
taḷarnaṭi tāṅkāk kiḷarpūṭ putalvari"2
eṉavum,
"	taḷarnaṭi yāyamoṭu taṅkā tōṭi
viḷiyāṭu ciṟutē rīrttumey varunti
amaḷit tuñcum aimpaṭit tālik
kutalic cevvāyk kuṟunaṭip putalvarkku"1
eṉavum iḷamputalvariyalpi viḷakkuḻik kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār ivvaṇikalaṉic ciṟanteṭuttōtiṉār. ivvāṟṟāl itu caṅkanāḷilē payiṉṟami peṟṟām.
"	tāli yimpaṭi taḻuvu mārpiṭi
māli vāyamu toḻuku makkaḷip
pāli ṉūṭṭuvōr ceṅkip paṅkayam
vālni lāvuṟak kuviva māṉumē"2
eṉak kampanāṭar kūṟuvatu piṟkālattilum itu payiṉṟu vantamiyik kāṭṭum.
"	tampirā ṉaruḷi ṉālē tavattiṉāṉmikkār pōṟṟum
nampiyā rūra  reṉṟē nāmamuñ cāṟṟi mikka
aimpaṭi cataṅki cātti yaṇimaṇic cuṭṭi cāttic
cempoṉā ṇariyiṉ miṉṉat teruviṟṟē ruruṭṭu nāḷil"3
eṉṉum periya purāṇat tiruviruttam civa makkaḷum itaṉi aṇintu vantamikkuc cāṉṟākum. parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattu māmaṉāka vantu vaḻakkuritta paṭalattil,
"	aimpaṭi mārpiṟ kāṇēṉ ciṟucilam paṭiyiṟ kāṇēṉ
moympuṭi matāṇi kāṇēṉ mukattaci cuṭṭi kāṇēṉ
miṉpaṭu kuḻikaḷ kāṇēṉ veṟṟuṭal kaṇṭē ṉappā
eṉpeṟu meṉṟu piḷḷip paṇikaḷuṅ kavarntāreṉṉā"4
eṉṟu kūṟuvatu mutaliyaṉavuṅ kāṇka.
iṉi, muṉṉatākiya civaṉōṭiyipuṭiya aṇikalaṉ kalittokiyil palaviṭattuk kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. marutakkaliyil ōr iḷamintaṉuṭiya kālaṇi, iṭiyaṇi, kiyaṇi, taliyaṇi eṉpaṉa kūṟumiṭattil,
"	pūṇṭavi eṟiyā vāḷum eṟṟā maḻuvum
ceṟiyak kaṭṭi yīriṭit tāḻnta
peypula mūtāyp pukarniṟat tukiriṉ
miyaṟa viḷaṅkiya āṉēṟ ṟavirpūṇ"1
eṉa mārpaṇi kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. iṭiyē āṉēṟṟiṉ uruvamum, ataṉ iraṇṭu puṟattum vāḷ, maḻu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ uruvamum amikkap peṟṟa aṇikalaṉ itil kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. vāḷ eṉṟatu muttali vēl (cūlam) ākum. civaperumāṉi muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭum civa makkaṭku ivaṟṟiṉ uruvukaḷ ciṟappuṭiya aṭiyāḷamātal,
"	poṉṉār tiruvaṭik koṉṟuṇṭu viṇṇappam pōṟṟiceyyum
eṉṉāvi kāppataṟ kicciyuṇ ṭēliruṅ kūṟṟakala
miṉṉāru mūvilic cūlameṉ mēṟpoṟi mēvu koṇṭal
tuṉṉār kaṭantiyuṭ ṭūṅkāṉi māṭac cuṭarkkoḻuntē".
"	kaṭavun tikiri kaṭavā toḻiyak kayiliyuṟṟāṉ
paṭavun tiruvira loṉṟuvit tāypaṉi mālvaripōl
iṭapampoṟitteṉṉi yēṉṟukoḷ ḷāyiruñ  cōlitiṅkaḷ
taṭavum kaṭantiyuṭ ṭūṅkāṉi māṭattem tattuvaṉē".2
eṉat tirunāvukkaracu nāyaṉār civaperumāṉi vēṇṭi avaṟṟip peṟṟamiyāl aṟiyappaṭum. ip patikattu, mūṉṟu tiruppāṭṭē iññāṉṟiruttaliṉ, āḷuṭiya aracukaḷ vēṇṭiya ēṉi aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi aṟitaṟkiyalavilli. "tiruvaṭinīṟeṉṉip pūcum" eṉamaṟṟoru tiruppāṭṭil vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉar.



30. piṟkālap pulavarkaḷiṉ 
cuṭucol āṭci
makkaṭku nāviṉāl uṇṭākum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ poy, kuṟaḷi, vaṉcol, payaṉilcol eṉṟa nāṉkumām. ivaṟṟuḷ vaṉcol eṉpatu, uṇmikku māṟākavum, aṉpiṉṟiyum, ceṟṟampaṟṟip 'piṟari ikaḻnturikkumiṭattuk kuṟṟamākumaṉṟi, evvaḻiyum kuṟṟamākātu. piṟar tīmikaḷi meṉcoṟkaḷāl māṟṟa iyalā viṭattup paḻittalum iṭitturittalum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavē.
"	pukaḻpaṭa vāḻātār tannōvār tammi
ikaḻvāri nōva tevaṉ"1
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum. maruttuvaṉ kippum kāramumuṭiya maruntukaḷāl nōy tīrkkumāṟupōlac cāṉṟōrkaḷ veñ
coṟkaḷāl piṟaruṭiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷip pōkkuvar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ikkaruttuṭaṉ itaṟku 'vāyuṟivāḻttu' eṉṟu peyar kūṟi,
"	vāyuṟi vāḻttē vayaṅka nāṭiṉ
vēmpum kaṭuvum pōla veñcol
tāṅkuta liṉṟi vaḻinaṉi payakkumeṉṟu
ompaṭik kiḷaviyiṉ vāyuṟut taṟṟē"2
eṉa ataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampiṉār. tirukkuṟaṭpākkaḷiṟ pala ivviyalpiṉi uṭiyaṉavātaliṉ annūlukkē vāyuṟi vāḻttu eṉavum oru peyaruṇṭu.
piṟari eḷḷiyurikkum col avaruḷḷattik karukac ceytaliṉāl ataṉi veñcol eṉavum, cuṭucol eṉavum kūṟutal poruntum. 'nāviṉāṟ cuṭṭa' eṉṟār vaḷḷuvaṉārum. tīyiṉviṉiyākiya cuṭutalic collukku ēṟṟiyurippatu ōr ilakkaṇi vaḻakku.
piṟar tam piḻiyi niṉintu uḷiyumāṟu ikaḻnturittal eṉṉum karuttu oṉṟāyiṉum, pulavarkaḷ ataṉi urikkun tiṟaṉ palavākum. avi avvakkālattup pulavarkaḷiṉ maṉa niliyālum, moḻinaṭiyālum vēṟupaṭutalum uṇṭu. tamiḻ varalāṟṟil piṟkālattaṉa eṉṟu karutaṟkuriya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil cuṭucol amintuḷḷa muṟiyi ārāyntu kāṇpōm.
cuṭucollāṉatu naṭuniliyuṭiya cāṉṟōrkaḷ makkaḷi naṉṉeṟippaṭuttap potuppaṭak kūṟiyatum, varalāṟṟil iyipupaṭṭa oruvari nōkkik kūṟiyatum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
aṟucuvi yuṇavukaḷi vayiṟāra uṇṭum, tam uṭampiṉip pala vakiyālum oppaṉi ceytum, aṟiñar kūṟum uṟutimoḻi kaḷik kēḷāmalum, matattiṉāl avarkaḷi nōkkāmalum irukkum puṉcelvarkaḷik kuṟittu nītineṟi viḷakka āciriyar kūṟum ceyyuḷ itu.
"	muṭippa muṭittuppiṉ pūcuva pūci uṭuppa uṭuttuṇpa uṇṇā-iṭittiṭittuk
kaṭṭuri kūṟil cevikkoḷā kaṇviḻiyā
neṭṭuyirppō ṭuṟṟa piṇam"1
itaṉ karuttum collum uḷḷattic cuṭum iyalpiṉavātal kāṇka. ivvaḷavu tiṭpanuṭpamiṉṟi, yāvarum aṟiyum eḷiya naṭiyilē cilar pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
"	peṇputti kēṭkiṉṟa mūṭarum, tantitāy
piḻipuṟañ colu mūṭarum,
periyōrkaḷ capiyilē mukaṭēṟi vantatu
pitaṟṟiṭum peru mūṭarum,
- - - - - -  - - - - - -  - - - - - - 
kaṟṟaṟivi lātamuḻu mūṭaruk kivarelām
kālmūṭar arimūṭar kāṇ"2
eṉpatu ataṟku utāraṇamākum. ciṟitum kūcutaliṉṟiyē ciṟcilarik kāl mūṭar, arimūṭar, muḻumūṭar eṉa veḷippaṭiyāka ivar ikaḻnturikkiṉṟār. ivviru mēṟkōḷum potuppaṭa nītiyākak kūṟiyavi.
oḷaviyār oru kālattil tammip pāṭumāṟu vēṇṭiya kayavar cilari nōkki,
"	mūvar kōviyum mūviḷaṅ kōviyum
pāṭiya emtam paṉuval vāyāl
emmiyum pāṭuka eṉṟaṉir nummi
eṅṅaṉam pāṭutum yāam;
kaḷiṟupaṭu ceṅkaḷam kaṇṇiṟ kāṇīr
vutṟilā nalyāḻ virumpik kēḷīr
pulavar vāyccol pōṟṟīr maṟṟum
ilava vāycciyar iḷamuli pullīr
uṭīir uṇṇīr koṭīir koḷḷir
ōvāk kāṉat tuyarmara mataṉil
tūvāk kaṉiyil tōṉṟi ṉīrē"1
eṉṟu kūṟiyatu oru varalāṟṟil toṭarpuṭiyataṟku utāraṇa mākum.
iṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭiyākavaṉṟik kuṟippāl uṇarumāṟu kūṟutalum uṇṭu. oruvarip pukaḻvatupōlap paḻippatu, oruvarip pukaḻvataṉāl maṟṟoruvarip paḻippatu mutaliyaṉa kuṟippil aṭaṅkum. aṇi nūlil itu vañcap pukaḻcci eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. inta nākarika muṟiyāṉatu mēṉmiyuṭiyatākat tōṉṟiṉum uḷḷattil ūṭuruvip pāyum iyalpiṉatu. 'neṭṭiruppup pārikku nekkuviṭāp pāṟi pacumarattiṉ vērukku nekkuviṭum' (nalvaḻi-33) eṉṟāṟpōla, vaṉcollāṟ kalaṅkāta neñcattiyum im meṉcol kalaṅkac ceyyum. 'kayavarkaḷ tammi niyamippāriṉṟittām  virumpiyavāṟu oḻukutalāl tēvari oppar' eṉṟum, 'pētiyār kēṇmi piriviṉkaṇ yātoru tuṉpamum ceyyātiruttalāl mikavum iṉimiyuṭiyatu' eṉṟum teyvappulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārē im muṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭiruttaliṉ itu pulavarkaḷ kiyāḷum ciṟanta neṟi eṉpatu pōtarum.
"	iravaṟiyā; yāvariyum piṉcellā; nalla
taruniḻalum taṇṇīrum pullum-oruvar
paṭittaṉavum koḷḷā; ip puḷḷimāṉ pārmēl
tuṭittaṉavē yaṉṟō tuyar".2
iḵtu oru māṉip pukaḻvatupōl iruppiṉum irattaṟ ṟoḻiliyuṭiya oruvaṉi nintikkum karuttiṉatu.
pāratappōril apimaṉṉaṉ iṟantapoḻutu taṅkaḷ veṟṟiyip pārāṭṭik koṇṭirunta turiyōtaṉātiyari nōkki, 'vīrarāyiṉār oruvarōṭu oruvar nēr niṉṟu pōr ceytal ulakiyaṟki; avvāṟaṉṟi, nīvir ellīrum kūṭip pōr purintum paruvameytāta oru pālaṉukku āṟṟāmal paṟantu pōṉīrkaḷ; piṉpu oruvaṉ piṉṉē niṉṟu tēri aḻikka, oruvaṉ kiyit tuṇikka, oruvaṉ pōr ceyya, ivvāṟu vañcattāl poṉṟu vittīrkaḷ; itukoṇṭō perumitam pēcukiṉṟīrkaḷ' eṉṟivvāṟu vīmaṉ makaṉākiya kaṭōṟkacaṉ kūṟāniṟka, ataṉik kēṭṭa aravakkoṭiyōṉ avaṉi vekuṇṭu nōkki, 'irunta avikkēṟpa urikkāmal tarukkiṉāl iyampukiṉṟa inta arakki makaṉuṭaṉ oṉṟum pēcaṉmiṉ' eṉa aṅkirunta aracar yāvarkkum kūṟiṉaṉ. appoḻutu kaṭōṟkacaṉ kūṟum cuṭucoṟkaḷ aṟiyaṟpālaṉa.
"	antavuri mīṇṭavaṉkēṭ ṭāṅkavaṉi
	nakitturippāṉ arakka rēṉum
cintaṉiyil virakeṇṇār cerumukattil
	vañcakamum ceyyār aiyā
ventiṟalkūr tuṇivarukku viṭamaruttār
	niṟikaḻuvil vīḻac collār
untupuṉa liṭipputiyār ōrūril
	iruppakaṟṟār uriyun tappār"
"	ceḻuntaḻalvāḻ maṉikkoḷuvār ceynnaṉṟi
	koṉṟaṟiyār tīṅku pūṇār
aḻuntumaṉat taḻukkuṟār accamumaṟ
	ṟaruḷiṉṟip poyccū tāṭār
koḻuntiyarit tukiluriyār koṭuṅkāṉam
	aṭivittuk kolla eṇṇār
eḻuntamariṉ mutukiṭār iviyellām
	aṭikaḷukkē ēṟpa eṉṟāṉ"1
ittakiya tīmiyellām arakkarkaḷ ceyyār eṉṟa pukaḻcciyāl, turiyōtaṉaṉ iviyellām ceytavaṉ eṉṟu ikaḻntavāṟu kāṇka.
maṟṟum, oruvari ikaḻvatupōl maṟṟoruvari ikaḻtalum uṇṭu; itu vañcap paḻippaṇi eṉappaṭum. orunāḷ oḷavip pirāṭṭikku oru mātu aṉpuṭaṉ uṇavaḷikka, avaḷ kaṇavaṉ ataṉip poṟātu ikaḻntāṉāka, appoḻutu avar kūṟiyatu, 
"	aṟṟa talipōka aṟātatali nāṉkiṉiyum
paṟṟit tirukip paṟiyēṉō-vaṟṟal
maramaṉiyā ṉukkinta māṉi vakutta
piramaṉiyāṉ kāṇap peṟiṉ"1
eṉpatu. itil piramaṉip paḻippatupōl ōr paṇpuṭiya maṉivikkup poruttamaṟṟa aṉpillāta koṭiyaṉi ikaḻnta vāṟu kāṇka. kuṟṟamuṭiyavarkaḷi aṟiñarkaḷ ikaḻn turikkum tiṟaṅkaḷ maṟṟum palavākum. māyūram vētanāyakam piḷḷi avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nīti nūlil ulōpiyi iḻittuk kūṟiyapāṭṭu oṉṟu aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟpālatu.
"	poṉṉimā nilattilyāṉ putikkum ēlviyil
aṉṉiyē yaṉiyapār aruḷiṉ nōkkinaṟ
coṉṉameṉ vāyiṭic coriyumuṉ naṉvāykku
eṉṉiyē iṭuvaṉeṉṟu icittiṭ ṭāḷarō"2
ippāṭṭiṉi, ulōpiyāṉavaṉ tāṉē kūṟuvatāka amittu, (poṉṉip putittuvittavaṉ vāyil maṇviḻum) eṉṟa karuttic cāturiyamākap pulappaṭuttiyiruppatu nakiccuvi viḷivikkiṉṟatu.
"	kattukaṭal cūḻnākik kāttāṉṟaṉ cattirattil
attamikkum pōtil aricivarum-kutti
uliyiliṭa ūraṭaṅkum ōrakappi aṉṉam
iliyiliṭa veḷḷi eḻum"3
eṉṟa kāḷamēkattiṉ ceyyuḷum nakiyiṉit tōṟṟuvikkum vaciyāka uḷḷatu kāḷamēkap pulavar vacic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟuvatil  vallunar eṉṉum karuttup, paravi vaḻakkāka iruntu varukiṟatu. 'veṇpāviṟ pukaḻēnti' eṉṟu toṭaṅkum ceyyuḷil 'vacipāṭak kāḷamēkam' eṉap piṟkālap pulavaroruvar kūṟiyiruppatum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum. āṉāl kāḷamēkam pāṭiyaviyāka aṟiyappaṭum ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉavum teyvaṅkaḷik kuṟittup paḻippatupōl vaḻuttiṉavākum; uṇmiyiṟ paḻippurikaḷ alla; itaṉiyum vañcap pukaḻcci eṉṟē kūṟuvar. karuṭa'd2vūrtiyil tirumāl ulā varum kāṭciyi,
	- "perumāḷ 
iruntiṭattiṟ cummā irāmiyiṉāl aiyō
parunteṭuttup pōkiṟatu pār."1
eṉṟum, mūttapiḷḷiyār mūṭika vākaṉattil ulā varutali,
"	valimikutta mummatattu vāraṇatti aiyō
eliyiḻuttup pōkiṉṟa teṉ"2
eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. aṭiyārkaḷ ivvāṟu iṟivaṉi ikaḻvatu pōl naki tōṉṟap pāṭi iṉpuṟum iyalpu tēvāram, tiruvācakam mutaliyavaṟṟilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ituvari eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya cuṭucolliṉ vaki pala aṇmik kālattu viḷaṅkiya kaviyākiya cuppiramaṇiya pāratiyiṉ pāṭalkaḷil amintuḷḷaṉa; avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi ivvuriyimuṭippōm.
'muracu' eṉṟa pakutiyil, piṟappāl uyarvu tāḻvu illi yeṉpati avar camatkāramāka vaṟpuṟuttuntiṟam aṟintu makiḻaṟkuriyatu.
"	veḷḷi niṟattoru pūṉi-eṅkaḷ
vīṭṭil vaḷarutu kaṇṭīr;
piḷḷikaḷ peṟṟatap pūṉi-avi
pēruk koruniṟa mākum;
cāmpal niṟamoru kuṭṭi-karuñ
cāntu niṟamoru kuṭṭi
pāmpu niṟamoru kuṭṭi-veḷḷip
pāli ṉiṟamoru kuṭṭi
enta niṟamirun tālum-avi
yāvu morētara maṉṟō?
inta niṟamciṟi teṉṟum-iḵ
tēṟṟameṉṟuñ colla lāmō?
vaṇṇaṅkaḷ vēṟṟumip paṭṭāl-atil
māṉuṭar vēṟṟumi illi;
eṇṇaṅkaḷ ceykika ḷellām-iṅku
yāvarkku moṉṟeṉal kāṇīr."3


31. iḷavēṉiṟ kaviyiṉpam
paritiyañcelvaṉ mēṭa iṭapa irukkikaḷil nilavum perumpoḻutu iḷavēṉil eṉappaṭum. maḻi, paṉi eṉpavaṟṟāṉ eytum kuḷiriṉṟiyum, ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmi murukātum, iḷan teṉṟaloṭu kūṭiyum iruttaliṉ aṉittuyirum amiḻ tuṇṭaṉaveṉat taḷirppataṟkuriyatu ipparuvamākum. yāṟṟiṭik kuṟi, poyki, iḷamarakkā, uyyāṉam mutaliyavaṟṟiluḷḷa maram, putar, koṭi mutaliyaṉa taḷirttup pūttu naṟumaṇam vīcip polivuṟā niṟpatum, kuyilkaḷ kūvuvatum, vaṇṭukaḷ muralvatum, teṉṟal tavaḻvatum, tēṉ pilkuvatum ākiya iṉṉōraṉṉa vaṟṟāl iyalpākavē makkaṭku aimpula iṉpaṅkaḷum viḷitaṟ kiṭaṉāvatu iv virutuvākum. kāmatēvaṉum itaṉiyē takka poḻuteṉak koṇṭu taṉ viṉiyāṇmiyi naṭātti vacantaṉ eṉap peyar ciṟakkiṉṟaṉaṉ.
' 	mācil vīṇiyum māli matiyamum
vīcu teṉṟalum vīṅkiḷa vēṉilum
mūcu vaṇṭaṟi poykiyum pōṉṟatē
īcaṉ enti iṇiyaṭi  nīḻalē. 1
eṉṉum tiruppāṭṭāl iṟivaṉatu tiruvaṭi nīḻaliṉ iṉpat tiṟkum tirunāvukkaracar ivviḷavēṉil nikaḻccikaḷi uvamaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷami itaṉ ciṟappiṉi iṉitu pulappaṭuttāniṟkum.
iḷavēṉiliṉ māṇpayaṉ palavaṟṟiyum nallicip pulavarkaḷ pārāṭṭiyuḷḷa ceyyuṭkaḷ tamiḻil aḷavaṟṟaṉa uḷḷaṉa. caṅkac cāṉṟōrāya taṇṭamiḻp pulavarkaḷ tam ceyyuṭkaḷil iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṉ nikaḻccikaḷiyum taṉmi piṟaḻāmal paṭṭāṅku eṭutturikkum pāṉmiyum, ēṟṟa uvamaṅkaḷōṭu cārtti eḻilpeṟa iyampum iyalpum kaṟpārkkuk kaḻipēriṉpam payappaṉavākum. kalittoki (pālikkali-33) eṉṉum naṟumalarkkāviluḷḷa tōr ceyyuṭ pūṅkompar īṇṭu nam kāṭciyi īrttu niṟkiṉṟatu.
